‘‘sampan‍nasīlā, bhikkhavai, viharatha sampan‍napātimaukkhā; pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutā viharatha ācāragaucarasampan‍nā aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvinau; samādāya sikkhatha sikkhāpadaisū’’ti – iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vutta’’nti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

ākaṅkhaiyyasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. vatthasuttaṃ

70. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, vatthaṃ saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ malaggahitaṃ; tamainaṃ rajakau yasmiṃ yasmiṃ raṅgajātai upasaṃharaiyya – yadi nīlakāya yadi pītakāya yadi lauhitakāya yadi mañ‍jiṭṭhakāya mañ‍jaiṭṭhakāya (sī॰ pī॰), mañ‍jaiṭṭhikāya (syā॰) durattavaṇṇamaivassa aparisuddhavaṇṇamaivassa| taṃ kissa haitu? aparisuddhattā, bhikkhavai, vatthassa| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, cittai saṃkiliṭṭhai, duggati pāṭikaṅkhā| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, vatthaṃ parisuddhaṃ pariyaudātaṃ; tamainaṃ rajakau yasmiṃ yasmiṃ raṅgajātai upasaṃharaiyya – yadi nīlakāya yadi pītakāya yadi lauhitakāya yadi mañ‍jiṭṭhakāya – surattavaṇṇamaivassa parisuddhavaṇṇamaivassa| taṃ kissa haitu? parisuddhattā, bhikkhavai, vatthassa| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, cittai asaṃkiliṭṭhai, sugati pāṭikaṅkhā|

71. ‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, cittassa upak‍kilaisā? abhijjhāvisamalaubhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau, byāpādau cittassa upak‍kilaisau, kaudhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau, upanāhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau, makkhau  cittassa upak‍kilaisau, paḷāsau cittassa upak‍kilaisau, issā  cittassa upak‍kilaisau, macchariyaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau, māyā cittassa upak‍kilaisau, sāṭhaiyyaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau, thambhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau, sārambhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau, mānau cittassa upak‍kilaisau, atimānau cittassa upak‍kilaisau, madau cittassa  upak‍kilaisau, pamādau cittassa upak‍kilaisau|

72. ‘‘sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘abhijjhāvisamalaubhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā abhijjhāvisamalaubhaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahati; ‘byāpādau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā byāpādaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahati; ‘kaudhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā kaudhaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahati; ‘upanāhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā upanāhaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahati; ‘makkhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā makkhaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahati; ‘paḷāsau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā paḷāsaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahati; ‘issā cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā issaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahati; ‘macchariyaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā macchariyaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahati; ‘māyā cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā māyaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahati; ‘sāṭhaiyyaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā sāṭhaiyyaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahati; ‘thambhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā thambhaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahati; ‘sārambhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti  – iti viditvā sārambhaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahati; ‘mānau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā mānaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahati; ‘atimānau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā atimānaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahati; ‘madau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā madaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahati; ‘pamādau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā pamādaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisaṃ pajahati|

73. ‘‘yatau khau yatau ca khau (sī॰ syā॰), bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ‘abhijjhāvisamalaubhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā abhijjhāvisamalaubhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau  pahīnau hauti, ‘byāpādau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā byāpādau cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau hauti; ‘kaudhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā kaudhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau hauti; ‘upanāhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā upanāhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau hauti; ‘makkhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā makkhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau hauti; ‘paḷāsau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā paḷāsau cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau hauti; ‘issā cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā issā cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau hauti; ‘macchariyaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā macchariyaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau hauti; ‘māyā cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā māyā cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau hauti; ‘sāṭhaiyyaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti  – iti viditvā sāṭhaiyyaṃ cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau hauti; ‘thambhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā thambhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau hauti; ‘sārambhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā sārambhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau  hauti; ‘mānau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā mānau cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau hauti; ‘atimānau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā atimānau cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau hauti; ‘madau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā madau cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau hauti; ‘pamādau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti – iti viditvā pamādau cittassa upak‍kilaisau pahīnau hauti|

74. ‘‘sau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti; dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ‘svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau sandiṭṭhikau akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’ti; saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ‘suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, ujuppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, ñāyappaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau  bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau, yadidaṃ cattāri purisayugāni, aṭṭha purisapuggalā| aisa bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau āhunaiyyau pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau, anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’ti|

75. ‘‘yathaudhi yataudhi (aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pāṭhantaraṃ) khau panassa cattaṃ hauti vantaṃ muttaṃ pahīnaṃ paṭinissaṭṭhaṃ, sau ‘buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgataumhī’ti labhati atthavaidaṃ, labhati dhammavaidaṃ, labhati dhammūpasaṃhitaṃ pāmauj‍jaṃ| pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati; ‘dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai  avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgataumhī’ti labhati atthavaidaṃ, labhati dhammavaidaṃ, labhati dhammūpasaṃhitaṃ pāmauj‍jaṃ; pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| ‘yathaudhi khau pana mai cattaṃ vantaṃ muttaṃ pahīnaṃ paṭinissaṭṭha’nti labhati atthavaidaṃ, labhati dhammavaidaṃ, labhati dhammūpasaṃhitaṃ pāmauj‍jaṃ; pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati|

76. ‘‘sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃsīlau aivaṃdhammau aivaṃpañ‍ñau sālīnaṃ caipi piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jati vicitakāḷakaṃ anaikasūpaṃ anaikabyañ‍janaṃ, naivassa taṃ hauti antarāyāya| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, vatthaṃ saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ malaggahitaṃ acchaudakaṃ āgamma parisuddhaṃ hauti pariyaudātaṃ, uk‍kāmukhaṃ vā panāgamma jātarūpaṃ parisuddhaṃ hauti pariyaudātaṃ, aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃsīlau aivaṃdhammau aivaṃpañ‍ñau sālīnaṃ caipi piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jati vicitakāḷakaṃ anaikasūpaṃ anaikabyañ‍janaṃ, naivassa taṃ hauti antarāyāya|

77. ‘‘sau maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ catutthiṃ (sī॰ pī॰)| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati; karuṇāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… muditāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataina  caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati|

78. ‘‘sau ‘atthi idaṃ, atthi hīnaṃ, atthi paṇītaṃ, atthi imassa sañ‍ñāgatassa uttariṃ nissaraṇa’nti pajānāti| tassa aivaṃ jānatau aivaṃ passatau kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, avij‍jāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai – ‘bhikkhu sinātau antaraina sinānainā’’’ti|

79. taina khau pana samayaina sundarikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau avidūrai nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau sundarikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau  bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘gacchati pana bhavaṃ gautamau bāhukaṃ nadiṃ sināyitu’’nti? ‘‘kiṃ, brāhmaṇa, bāhukāya nadiyā? kiṃ bāhukā nadī karissatī’’ti? ‘‘laukkhasammatā laukhyasammatā (sī॰), maukkhasammatā (pī॰) hi, bhau gautama, bāhukā nadī bahujanassa, puñ‍ñasammatā hi, bhau gautama, bāhukā nadī bahujanassa, bāhukāya pana nadiyā bahujanau pāpakammaṃ kataṃ pavāhaitī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā sundarikabhāradvājaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsi –

‘‘bāhukaṃ adhikak‍kañ‍ca, gayaṃ sundarikaṃ mapi sundarikāmapi (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), sundarikaṃ mahiṃ (itipi)|

sarassatiṃ payāgañ‍ca, athau bāhumatiṃ nadiṃ|

nic‍campi bālau pakkhandau pakkhan‍nau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), kaṇhakammau na sujjhati||

‘‘kiṃ  sundarikā karissati, kiṃ payāgā payāgau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) kiṃ bāhukā nadī|

vairiṃ katakibbisaṃ naraṃ, na hi naṃ saudhayai pāpakamminaṃ||

‘‘suddhassa  vai sadā phaggu, suddhassupausathau sadā|

suddhassa sucikammassa, sadā sampaj‍jatai vataṃ|

idhaiva sināhi brāhmaṇa, sabbabhūtaisu karauhi khaimataṃ||

‘‘sacai musā na bhaṇasi, sacai pāṇaṃ na hiṃsasi|

sacai adin‍naṃ nādiyasi, saddahānau amaccharī|

kiṃ kāhasi gayaṃ gantvā, udapānaupi tai gayā’’ti||

80. aivaṃ  vuttai, sundarikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama! saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti; aivamaivaṃ bhautā gautamaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| labhaiyyāhaṃ bhautau gautamassa santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, labhaiyyaṃ upasampada’’nti| alattha khau sundarikabhāradvājau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ, alattha upasampadaṃ| acirūpasampan‍nau  khau panāyasmā bhāradvājau aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaivadhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau khau panāyasmā bhāradvājau arahataṃ ahausīti|

vatthasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. sal‍laikhasuttaṃ

81. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau āyasmā mahācundau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau  āyasmā mahācundau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yā imā, bhantai, anaikavihitā diṭṭhiyau laukai uppaj‍janti – attavādapaṭisaṃyuttā vā laukavādapaṭisaṃyuttā vā – ādimaiva nu khau, bhantai, bhikkhunau manasikarautau aivamaitāsaṃ diṭṭhīnaṃ pahānaṃ hauti, aivamaitāsaṃ diṭṭhīnaṃ paṭinissaggau hautī’’ti?

82. ‘‘yā imā, cunda, anaikavihitā diṭṭhiyau laukai uppaj‍janti – attavādapaṭisaṃyuttā vā laukavādapaṭisaṃyuttā vā – yattha caitā diṭṭhiyau uppaj‍janti yattha ca anusainti yattha ca samudācaranti taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na mai sau attā’ti – aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñā passatau aivamaitāsaṃ diṭṭhīnaṃ pahānaṃ hauti, aivamaitāsaṃ diṭṭhīnaṃ paṭinissaggau hauti|

‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, cunda, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau bhikkhu vivic‍caiva  kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya| tassa aivamassa – ‘sal‍laikhaina viharāmī’ti| na khau panaitai, cunda, ariyassa vinayai sal‍laikhā vuc‍canti| diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārā aitai  ariyassa vinayai vuc‍canti|

‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, cunda, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya| tassa aivamassa – ‘sal‍laikhaina viharāmī’ti| na khau panaitai, cunda, ariyassa vinayai sal‍laikhā vuc‍canti| diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārā aitai ariyassa vinayai vuc‍canti|

‘‘ṭhānaṃ  khau panaitaṃ, cunda, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharaiyya, satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiyya, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya| tassa aivamassa – ‘sal‍laikhaina viharāmī’ti| na khau panaitai, cunda, ariyassa vinayai sal‍laikhā  vuc‍canti| diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārā aitai ariyassa vinayai vuc‍canti|

‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, cunda, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ  catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya| tassa aivamassa – ‘sal‍laikhaina viharāmī’ti| na khau panaitai, cunda, ariyassa vinayai sal‍laikhā vuc‍canti| diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārā aitai ariyassa vinayai vuc‍canti|

‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, cunda, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau bhikkhu sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā, paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā, nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā, ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya| tassa aivamassa – ‘sal‍laikhaina viharāmī’ti| na khau panaitai, cunda, ariyassa vinayai sal‍laikhā vuc‍canti| santā aitai vihārā ariyassa vinayai vuc‍canti|

‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, cunda, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau bhikkhu sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya| tassa aivamassa – ‘sal‍laikhaina viharāmī’ti| na khau panaitai, cunda, ariyassa vinayai sal‍laikhā vuc‍canti| santā aitai vihārā ariyassa vinayai vuc‍canti|

‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, cunda, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau bhikkhu sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya| tassa aivamassa – ‘sal‍laikhaina viharāmī’ti| na khau panaitai, cunda, ariyassa vinayai sal‍laikhā  vuc‍canti| santā aitai vihārā ariyassa vinayai vuc‍canti|

‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, cunda, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau bhikkhu sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya| tassa aivamassa – ‘sal‍laikhaina viharāmī’ti| na  khau panaitai, cunda, ariyassa vinayai sal‍laikhā vuc‍canti| santā aitai vihārā ariyassa vinayai vuc‍canti|

83. ‘‘idha khau pana vau, cunda, sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai vihiṃsakā bhavissanti, mayamaittha avihiṃsakā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai pāṇātipātī bhavissanti, mayamaittha pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai adin‍nādāyī bhavissanti, mayamaittha adin‍nādānā paṭiviratā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai  abrahmacārī bhavissanti, mayamaittha brahmacārī bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai musāvādī bhavissanti, mayamaittha musāvādā paṭiviratā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai pisuṇavācā pisuṇā vācā (sī॰ pī॰) bhavissanti, mayamaittha pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai pharusavācā pharusā vācā (sī॰ pī॰) bhavissanti, mayamaittha pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai samphappalāpī bhavissanti, mayamaittha samphappalāpā paṭiviratā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai abhijjhālū bhavissanti, mayamaittha anabhijjhālū bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai byāpan‍nacittā bhavissanti, mayamaittha abyāpan‍nacittā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai micchādiṭṭhī bhavissanti, mayamaittha sammādiṭṭhī bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai micchāsaṅkappā  bhavissanti, mayamaittha sammāsaṅkappā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai micchāvācā bhavissanti, mayamaittha sammāvācā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai micchākammantā bhavissanti, mayamaittha sammākammantā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai micchāājīvā bhavissanti, mayamaittha sammāājīvā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai micchāvāyāmā bhavissanti, mayamaittha sammāvāyāmā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai micchāsatī bhavissanti, mayamaittha sammāsatī bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai micchāsamādhi bhavissanti, mayamaittha sammāsamādhī bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai micchāñāṇī bhavissanti, mayamaittha sammāñāṇī bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai micchāvimuttī bhavissanti, mayamaittha sammāvimuttī bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau|

‘‘‘parai thīnamiddhapariyuṭṭhitā bhavissanti, mayamaittha vigatathīnamiddhā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai uddhatā bhavissanti, mayamaittha anuddhatā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai vicikicchī vaicikicchī (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) bhavissanti, mayamaittha tiṇṇavicikicchā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai kaudhanā bhavissanti, mayamaittha ak‍kaudhanā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai upanāhī bhavissanti, mayamaittha anupanāhī bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai  makkhī bhavissanti, mayamaittha amakkhī bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai  paḷāsī bhavissanti, mayamaittha apaḷāsī bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai issukī bhavissanti, mayamaittha anissukī bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai maccharī bhavissanti, mayamaittha amaccharī bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai saṭhā bhavissanti, mayamaittha asaṭhā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai māyāvī bhavissanti, mayamaittha amāyāvī bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai thaddhā bhavissanti, mayamaittha atthaddhā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai atimānī bhavissanti, mayamaittha anatimānī bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai dubbacā bhavissanti, mayamaittha suvacā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai pāpamittā bhavissanti, mayamaittha kalyāṇamittā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai pamattā bhavissanti, mayamaittha appamattā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai assaddhā bhavissanti, mayamaittha saddhā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai ahirikā bhavissanti, mayamaittha hirimanā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai anauttāpī anauttappī (ka॰) bhavissanti, mayamaittha auttāpī bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai appassutā bhavissanti, mayamaittha bahussutā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai kusītā bhavissanti, mayamaittha āraddhavīriyā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai muṭṭhassatī bhavissanti, mayamaittha upaṭṭhitassatī bhavissāmā’ti  sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai duppañ‍ñā bhavissanti, mayamaittha pañ‍ñāsampan‍nā bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau| ‘parai sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī ādhānaggāhī duppaṭinissaggī bhavissanti, mayamaittha asandiṭṭhiparāmāsī anādhānaggāhī suppaṭinissaggī bhavissāmā’ti sal‍laikhau karaṇīyau|

84. ‘‘cittuppādampi khau ahaṃ, cunda, kusalaisu dhammaisu bahukāraṃ bahūpakāraṃ (ka॰) vadāmi, kau pana vādau kāyaina vācāya anuvidhīyanāsu! tasmātiha, cunda, ‘parai vihiṃsakā bhavissanti, mayamaittha avihiṃsakā bhavissāmā’ti cittaṃ uppādaitabbaṃ| ‘parai pāṇātipātī bhavissanti, mayamaittha pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā  bhavissāmā’ti cittaṃ uppādaitabbaṃ…‘parai sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī  ādhānaggāhī duppaṭinissaggī bhavissanti, mayamaittha asandiṭṭhiparāmāsī anādhānaggāhī suppaṭinissaggī bhavissāmā’ti cittaṃ uppādaitabbaṃ|

85. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, cunda, visamau maggau assa, tassa maggau tassāssa (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) añ‍ñau samau maggau parik‍kamanāya; saiyyathā vā pana, cunda, visamaṃ titthaṃ assa, tassa añ‍ñaṃ samaṃ titthaṃ parik‍kamanāya; aivamaiva  khau, cunda, vihiṃsakassa purisapuggalassa avihiṃsā hauti parik‍kamanāya, pāṇātipātissa purisapuggalassa pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī hauti parik‍kamanāya, adin‍nādāyissa purisapuggalassa adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī hauti parik‍kamanāya, abrahmacārissa purisapuggalassa abrahmacariyā vairamaṇī hauti parik‍kamanāya, musāvādissa purisapuggalassa musāvādā vairamaṇī hauti parik‍kamanāya, pisuṇavācassa purisapuggalassa pisuṇāya vācāya vairamaṇī hauti parik‍kamanāya, pharusavācassa purisapuggalassa pharusāya vācāya vairamaṇī hauti parik‍kamanāya, samphappalāpissa purisapuggalassa samphappalāpā vairamaṇī hauti parik‍kamanāya, abhijjhālussa purisapuggalassa anabhijjhā hauti parik‍kamanāya, byāpan‍nacittassa purisapuggalassa abyāpādau hauti parik‍kamanāya, micchādiṭṭhissa purisapuggalassa sammādiṭṭhi hauti parik‍kamanāya, micchāsaṅkappassa purisapuggalassa sammāsaṅkappau hauti parik‍kamanāya, micchāvācassa purisapuggalassa sammāvācā hauti parik‍kamanāya, micchākammantassa purisapuggalassa sammākammantau hauti parik‍kamanāya, micchāājīvassa purisapuggalassa sammāājīvau hauti parik‍kamanāya, micchāvāyāmassa purisapuggalassa sammāvāyāmau hauti parik‍kamanāya, micchāsatissa purisapuggalassa sammāsati hauti parik‍kamanāya, micchāsamādhissa purisapuggalassa sammāsamādhi hauti parik‍kamanāya, micchāñāṇissa purisapuggalassa sammāñāṇaṃ hauti parik‍kamanāya, micchāvimuttissa purisapuggalassa sammāvimutti hauti parik‍kamanāya|

‘‘thīnamiddhapariyuṭṭhitassa purisapuggalassa vigatathinamiddhatā hauti parik‍kamanāya, uddhatassa purisapuggalassa anuddhac‍caṃ  hauti parik‍kamanāya, vicikicchissa purisapuggalassa tiṇṇavicikicchatā hauti parik‍kamanāya, kaudhanassa purisapuggalassa  ak‍kaudhau hauti parik‍kamanāya, upanāhissa purisapuggalassa anupanāhau hauti parik‍kamanāya, makkhissa purisapuggalassa amakkhau hauti parik‍kamanāya, paḷāsissa purisapuggalassa apaḷāsau hauti parik‍kamanāya, issukissa purisapuggalassa anissukitā hauti parik‍kamanāya, maccharissa purisapuggalassa amacchariyaṃ hauti parik‍kamanāya, saṭhassa purisapuggalassa asāṭhaiyyaṃ hauti parik‍kamanāya, māyāvissa purisapuggalassa amāyā amāyāvitā (ka॰) hauti parik‍kamanāya, thaddhassa purisapuggalassa atthaddhiyaṃ hauti parik‍kamanāya, atimānissa purisapuggalassa anatimānau hauti parik‍kamanāya, dubbacassa purisapuggalassa sauvacassatā hauti parik‍kamanāya, pāpamittassa purisapuggalassa kalyāṇamittatā hauti parik‍kamanāya, pamattassa purisapuggalassa appamādau hauti parik‍kamanāya, assaddhassa purisapuggalassa saddhā hauti parik‍kamanāya, ahirikassa purisapuggalassa hirī hauti parik‍kamanāya, anauttāpissa purisapuggalassa auttappaṃ hauti parik‍kamanāya, appassutassa purisapuggalassa bāhusac‍caṃ hauti parik‍kamanāya, kusītassa purisapuggalassa vīriyārambhau hauti parik‍kamanāya, muṭṭhassatissa purisapuggalassa upaṭṭhitassatitā hauti parik‍kamanāya, duppañ‍ñassa purisapuggalassa pañ‍ñāsampadā hauti parik‍kamanāya, sandiṭṭhiparāmāsi-ādhānaggāhi-duppaṭinissaggissa purisapuggalassa asandiṭṭhiparāmāsianādhānaggāhi-suppaṭinissaggitā hauti parik‍kamanāya|

86. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, cunda, yai kaici akusalā dhammā sabbai tai adhaubhāgaṅgamanīyā adhaubhāvaṅgamanīyā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), yai kaici kusalā dhammā sabbai tai uparibhāgaṅgamanīyā uparibhāvaṅgamanīyā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), aivamaiva khau, cunda, vihiṃsakassa purisapuggalassa avihiṃsā hauti uparibhāgāya uparibhāvāya (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰), pāṇātipātissa purisapuggalassa pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī hauti uparibhāgāya…pai॰… sandiṭṭhiparāmāsi-ādhānaggāhi-duppaṭinissaggissa  purisapuggalassa asandiṭṭhiparāmāsi-anādhānaggāhi-suppaṭinissaggitā hauti uparibhāgāya|

87. ‘‘sau vata, cunda, attanā palipapalipan‍nau paraṃ palipapalipan‍naṃ uddharissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| sau vata, cunda, attanā apalipapalipan‍nau paraṃ  palipapalipan‍naṃ uddharissatīti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| sau vata, cunda, attanā adantau avinītau aparinibbutau paraṃ damaissati vinaissati parinibbāpaissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| sau vata, cunda, attanā dantau vinītau parinibbutau paraṃ damaissati vinaissati parinibbāpaissatīti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| aivamaiva khau, cunda, vihiṃsakassa purisapuggalassa avihiṃsā hauti parinibbānāya, pāṇātipātissa purisapuggalassa pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī hauti parinibbānāya| adin‍nādāyissa purisapuggalassa adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī  hauti parinibbānāya| abrahmacārissa purisapuggalassa abrahmacariyā vairamaṇī hauti parinibbānāya| musāvādissa purisapuggalassa musāvādā vairamaṇī hauti parinibbānāya| pisuṇavācassa purisapuggalassa pisuṇāya vācāya vairamaṇī hauti parinibbānāya| pharusavācassa purisapuggalassa pharusāya vācāya vairamaṇī hauti parinibbānāya| samphappalāpissa purisapuggalassa samphappalāpā vairamaṇī hauti parinibbānāya| abhijjhālussa purisapuggalassa anabhijjhā hauti parinibbānāya| byāpan‍nacittassa purisapuggalassa abyāpādau hauti parinibbānāya| micchādiṭṭhissa purisapuggalassa sammādiṭṭhi hauti parinibbānāya| micchāsaṅkappassa purisapuggalassa sammāsaṅkappau hauti parinibbānāya| micchāvācassa purisapuggalassa sammāvācā hauti parinibbānāya| micchākammantassa purisapuggalassa sammākammantau hauti parinibbānāya| micchāājīvassa purisapuggalassa sammāājīvau hauti parinibbānāya| micchāvāyāmassa purisapuggalassa sammāvāyāmau hauti parinibbānāya| micchāsatissa purisapuggalassa sammāsati hauti parinibbānāya| micchāsamādhissa purisapuggalassa sammāsamādhi hauti parinibbānāya| micchāñāṇissa purisapuggalassa sammāñāṇaṃ hauti parinibbānāya| micchāvimuttissa purisapuggalassa sammāvimutti hauti parinibbānāya|

‘‘thīnamiddhapariyuṭṭhitassa  purisapuggalassa vigatathinamiddhatā hauti parinibbānāya| uddhatassa purisapuggalassa anuddhac‍caṃ hauti parinibbānāya| vicikicchissa purisapuggalassa tiṇṇavicikicchatā hauti parinibbānāya| kaudhanassa purisapuggalassa ak‍kaudhau hauti parinibbānāya| upanāhissa purisapuggalassa anupanāhau hauti parinibbānāya| makkhissa purisapuggalassa amakkhau hauti parinibbānāya| paḷāsissa purisapuggalassa apaḷāsau  hauti parinibbānāya| issukissa purisapuggalassa anissukitā hauti parinibbānāya| maccharissa purisapuggalassa amacchariyaṃ hauti parinibbānāya| saṭhassa purisapuggalassa asāṭhaiyyaṃ hauti parinibbānāya| māyāvissa purisapuggalassa amāyā hauti parinibbānāya| thaddhassa purisapuggalassa atthaddhiyaṃ hauti parinibbānāya| atimānissa purisapuggalassa anatimānau hauti parinibbānāya| dubbacassa purisapuggalassa sauvacassatā hauti parinibbānāya| pāpamittassa purisapuggalassa kalyāṇamittatā hauti parinibbānāya| pamattassa purisapuggalassa appamādau  hauti parinibbānāya| assaddhassa purisapuggalassa saddhā hauti parinibbānāya| ahirikassa purisapuggalassa hirī hauti parinibbānāya| anauttāpissa purisapuggalassa auttappaṃ hauti parinibbānāya| appassutassa purisapuggalassa bāhusac‍caṃ hauti parinibbānāya| kusītassa purisapuggalassa vīriyārambhau hauti parinibbānāya| muṭṭhassatissa purisapuggalassa  upaṭṭhitassatitā hauti parinibbānāya| duppañ‍ñassa purisapuggalassa  pañ‍ñāsampadā hauti parinibbānāya| sandiṭṭhiparāmāsi-ādhānaggāhi-duppaṭinissaggissa purisapuggalassa asandiṭṭhiparāmāsi-anādhānaggāhi-suppaṭinissaggitā hauti parinibbānāya|

88. ‘‘iti khau, cunda, daisitau mayā sal‍laikhapariyāyau, daisitau cittuppādapariyāyau, daisitau parik‍kamanapariyāyau, daisitau uparibhāgapariyāyau, daisitau parinibbānapariyāyau| yaṃ khau, cunda, satthārā karaṇīyaṃ sāvakānaṃ hitaisinā anukampakaina anukampaṃ upādāya, kataṃ vau taṃ mayā| ‘aitāni, cunda, rukkhamūlāni, aitāni suñ‍ñāgārāni, jhāyatha, cunda, mā pamādattha, mā pacchāvippaṭisārinau ahuvattha’ – ayaṃ khau amhākaṃ anusāsanī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā mahācundau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

catuttālīsapadā vuttā, sandhayau pañ‍ca daisitā|

sal‍laikhau nāma suttantau, gambhīrau sāgarūpamauti||

sal‍laikhasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sammādiṭṭhisuttaṃ

89. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau bhikkhavai’’ti| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā sāriputtau aitadavauca –

‘‘‘sammādiṭṭhi sammādiṭṭhī (sī॰ syā॰) sammādiṭṭhī’ti, āvusau, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhamma’’nti?

‘‘dūrataupi khau mayaṃ, āvusau, āgacchaiyyāma āyasmatau sāriputtassa santikai aitassa bhāsitassa atthamañ‍ñātuṃ| sādhu vatāyasmantaṃyaiva sāriputtaṃ paṭibhātu aitassa bhāsitassa atthau| āyasmatau sāriputtassa sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, āvusau, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā sāriputtau aitadavauca –

‘‘yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau akusalañ‍ca pajānāti, akusalamūlañ‍ca pajānāti, kusalañ‍ca pajānāti, kusalamūlañ‍ca pajānāti  – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina  saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhammaṃ| katamaṃ panāvusau, akusalaṃ, katamaṃ akusalamūlaṃ, katamaṃ kusalaṃ, katamaṃ kusalamūlaṃ? pāṇātipātau khau, āvusau, akusalaṃ, adin‍nādānaṃ akusalaṃ, kāmaisumicchācārau akusalaṃ, musāvādau akusalaṃ, pisuṇā vācā pisuṇavācā (ka॰) akusalaṃ, pharusā vācā pharusavācā (ka॰) akusalaṃ, samphappalāpau akusalaṃ, abhijjhā akusalaṃ, byāpādau akusalaṃ, micchādiṭṭhi akusalaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍catāvusau akusalaṃ| katamañ‍cāvusau, akusalamūlaṃ? laubhau akusalamūlaṃ, dausau akusalamūlaṃ, mauhau akusalamūlaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, akusalamūlaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍cāvusau, kusalaṃ? pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī kusalaṃ, adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī kusalaṃ, kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇī kusalaṃ, musāvādā vairamaṇī kusalaṃ, pisuṇāya vācāya vairamaṇī kusalaṃ, pharusāya vācāya vairamaṇī kusalaṃ, samphappalāpā vairamaṇī kusalaṃ, anabhijjhā kusalaṃ, abyāpādau kusalaṃ, sammādiṭṭhi kusalaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, kusalaṃ| katamañ‍cāvusau, kusalamūlaṃ? alaubhau kusalamūlaṃ, adausau kusalamūlaṃ, amauhau kusalamūlaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, kusalamūlaṃ|

‘‘yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ akusalaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ akusalamūlaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ kusalaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ kusalamūlaṃ pajānāti, sau sabbasau rāgānusayaṃ pahāya, paṭighānusayaṃ paṭivinaudaitvā, ‘asmī’ti diṭṭhimānānusayaṃ samūhanitvā, avij‍jaṃ pahāya vij‍jaṃ uppādaitvā, diṭṭhaivadhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina  saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhamma’’nti|

90. ‘‘sādhāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ uttari uttariṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) pañhaṃ apucchuṃ apucchiṃsu (syā॰) – ‘‘siyā panāvusau, añ‍ñaupi pariyāyau yathā ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhamma’’nti?

‘‘siyā, āvusau| yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau āhārañ‍ca pajānāti, āhārasamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, āhāraniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, āhāraniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadañ‍ca pajānāti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ  saddhammaṃ| katamau panāvusau, āhārau, katamau āhārasamudayau, katamau āhāraniraudhau, katamā āhāraniraudhagāminī paṭipadā? cattāraumai, āvusau, āhārā bhūtānaṃ vā sattānaṃ ṭhitiyā, sambhavaisīnaṃ vā anuggahāya| katamai cattārau? kabaḷīkārau āhārau auḷārikau  vā sukhumau vā, phassau dutiyau, manausañ‍caitanā tatiyā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ catutthaṃ| taṇhāsamudayā āhārasamudayau, taṇhāniraudhā āhāraniraudhau, ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau āhāraniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi  sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau, sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi’|

‘‘yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ āhāraṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ āhārasamudayaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ āhāraniraudhaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ āhāraniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti, sau sabbasau rāgānusayaṃ pahāya, paṭighānusayaṃ paṭivinaudaitvā, ‘asmī’ti diṭṭhimānānusayaṃ samūhanitvā, avij‍jaṃ pahāya vij‍jaṃ uppādaitvā, diṭṭhaivadhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhamma’’nti|

91. ‘‘sādhāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ uttari pañhaṃ apucchuṃ – ‘‘siyā panāvusau, añ‍ñaupi pariyāyau yathā ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhamma’’nti?

‘‘siyā, āvusau| yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau dukkhañ‍ca pajānāti, dukkhasamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, dukkhaniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadañ‍ca pajānāti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhammaṃ| katamaṃ panāvusau, dukkhaṃ, katamau dukkhasamudayau, katamau dukkhaniraudhau, katamā dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā? jātipi dukkhā, jarāpi dukkhā, maraṇampi dukkhaṃ, saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsāpi dukkhā, appiyaihi sampayaugaupi dukkhau, piyaihi vippayaugaupi dukkhau, yampicchaṃ na  labhati tampi dukkhaṃ, saṃkhittaina pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā pañ‍cupādānakkhandhāpi (ka॰) dukkhā – idaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, dukkhaṃ| katamau cāvusau, dukkhasamudayau? yāyaṃ taṇhā  paunaubbhavikā nandīrāgasahagatā paunaubhavikā (sī॰ pī॰) tatratatrābhinandinī nandirāgasahagatā (sī॰ pī॰), saiyyathidaṃ, kāmataṇhā bhavataṇhā  vibhavataṇhā – ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, dukkhasamudayau| katamau cāvusau, dukkhaniraudhau? yau tassāyaiva taṇhāya asaisavirāganiraudhau cāgau paṭinissaggau mutti anālayau – ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, dukkhaniraudhau| katamā cāvusau, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā? ayamaiva  ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ, sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi – ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā|

‘‘yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ dukkhaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ dukkhasamudayaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ dukkhaniraudhaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti, sau sabbasau rāgānusayaṃ pahāya, paṭighānusayaṃ paṭivinaudaitvā, ‘asmī’ti diṭṭhimānānusayaṃ samūhanitvā, avij‍jaṃ pahāya vij‍jaṃ uppādaitvā, diṭṭhaivadhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhamma’’nti|

92. ‘‘sādhāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ uttari pañhaṃ apucchuṃ – ‘‘siyā panāvusau, añ‍ñaupi pariyāyau yathā ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina  saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhamma’’nti?

‘‘siyā, āvusau| yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau jarāmaraṇañ‍ca pajānāti, jarāmaraṇasamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadañ‍ca pajānāti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhammaṃ| katamaṃ panāvusau, jarāmaraṇaṃ, katamau jarāmaraṇasamudayau, katamau jarāmaraṇaniraudhau, katamā jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā? yā taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ tamhi tamhi sattanikāyai jarā jīraṇatā khaṇḍic‍caṃ pālic‍caṃ valittacatā āyunau saṃhāni indriyānaṃ paripākau – ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, jarā| katamañ‍cāvusau, maraṇaṃ? yā yaṃ (pī॰ ka॰), satipaṭṭhānasuttaipi taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ  tamhā tamhā sattanikāyā cuti cavanatā bhaidau antaradhānaṃ mac‍cu maraṇaṃ kālaṃkiriyā khandhānaṃ bhaidau, kaḷaivarassa nikkhaipau, jīvitindriyassupacchaidau – idaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, maraṇaṃ| iti ayañ‍ca jarā idañ‍ca maraṇaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, jarāmaraṇaṃ| jātisamudayā jarāmaraṇasamudayau, jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaniraudhau, ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yatau  khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ jarāmaraṇaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ jarāmaraṇasamudayaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ jarāmaraṇaniraudhaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ  jarāmaraṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti, sau sabbasau rāgānusayaṃ pahāya…pai॰… dukkhassantakarau hauti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhamma’’nti|

93. ‘‘sādhāvusau’’ti khau…pai॰… apucchuṃ – siyā panāvusau…pai॰…  ‘‘siyā, āvusau| yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau jātiñ‍ca pajānāti, jātisamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, jātiniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, jātiniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadañ‍ca pajānāti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhammaṃ| katamā panāvusau, jāti, katamau jātisamudayau, katamau jātiniraudhau, katamā jātiniraudhagāminī paṭipadā? yā taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ tamhi tamhi sattanikāyai jāti sañ‍jāti auk‍kanti abhinibbatti khandhānaṃ pātubhāvau, āyatanānaṃ paṭilābhau – ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, jāti| bhavasamudayā jātisamudayau, bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau, ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau jātiniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ jātiṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ jātisamudayaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ jātiniraudhaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ jātiniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti, sau sabbasau rāgānusayaṃ pahāya…pai॰… dukkhassantakarau hauti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi  hauti, ujugatāssa  diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhamma’’nti|

94. ‘‘sādhāvusau’’ti khau…pai॰… apucchuṃ – siyā panāvusau…pai॰… ‘‘siyā, āvusau| yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau bhavañ‍ca pajānāti, bhavasamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, bhavaniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, bhavaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadañ‍ca pajānāti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ  saddhammaṃ| katamau panāvusau, bhavau, katamau bhavasamudayau, katamau bhavaniraudhau, katamā bhavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā? tayaumai, āvusau, bhavā – kāmabhavau, rūpabhavau, arūpabhavau| upādānasamudayā bhavasamudayau, upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau, ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ bhavaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ bhavasamudayaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ bhavaniraudhaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ bhavaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti, sau sabbasau rāgānusayaṃ pahāya…pai॰… dukkhassantakarau hauti| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhamma’’nti|

95. ‘‘sādhāvusau’’ti khau…pai॰… apucchuṃ – siyā panāvusau…pai॰… ‘‘siyā, āvusau| yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau upādānañ‍ca pajānāti, upādānasamudayañ‍ca  pajānāti, upādānaniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, upādānaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadañ‍ca pajānāti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhammaṃ| katamaṃ panāvusau, upādānaṃ, katamau upādānasamudayau, katamau upādānaniraudhau, katamā upādānaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā? cattārimāni, āvusau, upādānāni  – kāmupādānaṃ, diṭṭhupādānaṃ, sīlabbatupādānaṃ, attavādupādānaṃ| taṇhāsamudayā upādānasamudayau, taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau, ayamaiva ariyau  aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau upādānaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ upādānaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ upādānasamudayaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ upādānaniraudhaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ upādānaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti, sau sabbasau rāgānusayaṃ pahāya…pai॰… dukkhassantakarau hauti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhamma’’nti|

96. ‘‘sādhāvusau’’ti  khau…pai॰… apucchuṃ – siyā panāvusau…pai॰… ‘‘siyā, āvusau| yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau taṇhañ‍ca pajānāti, taṇhāsamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, taṇhāniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, taṇhāniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadañ‍ca pajānāti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina  saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhammaṃ| katamā panāvusau, taṇhā, katamau taṇhāsamudayau, katamau taṇhāniraudhau, katamā taṇhāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā? chayimai, āvusau, taṇhākāyā – rūpataṇhā, saddataṇhā, gandhataṇhā, rasataṇhā, phauṭṭhabbataṇhā, dhammataṇhā| vaidanāsamudayā taṇhāsamudayau, vaidanāniraudhā taṇhāniraudhau, ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau taṇhāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ taṇhaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ taṇhāsamudayaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ taṇhāniraudhaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ taṇhāniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti, sau sabbasau rāgānusayaṃ pahāya…pai॰… dukkhassantakarau hauti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhamma’’nti|

97. ‘‘sādhāvusau’’ti khau…pai॰… apucchuṃ – siyā panāvusau…pai॰… ‘‘siyā, āvusau| yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau vaidanañ‍ca pajānāti, vaidanāsamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, vaidanāniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, vaidanāniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadañ‍ca pajānāti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau  sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhammaṃ| katamā panāvusau, vaidanā, katamau vaidanāsamudayau, katamau vaidanāniraudhau, katamā vaidanāniraudhagāminī  paṭipadā? chayimai, āvusau, vaidanākāyā – cakkhusamphassajā vaidanā, sautasamphassajā vaidanā, ghānasamphassajā vaidanā, jivhāsamphassajā vaidanā, kāyasamphassajā vaidanā, manausamphassajā vaidanā| phassasamudayā vaidanāsamudayau, phassaniraudhā vaidanāniraudhau, ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau vaidanāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yatau  khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ vaidanaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ vaidanāsamudayaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ vaidanāniraudhaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ vaidanāniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti, sau sabbasau rāgānusayaṃ pahāya…pai॰… dukkhassantakarau hauti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhamma’’nti|

98. ‘‘sādhāvusau’’ti khau…pai॰… apucchuṃ – siyā panāvusau…pai॰… ‘‘siyā, āvusau| yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau phassañ‍ca pajānāti, phassasamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, phassaniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, phassaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadañ‍ca pajānāti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhammaṃ| katamau panāvusau, phassau, katamau phassasamudayau, katamau phassaniraudhau, katamā phassaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā? chayimai, āvusau, phassakāyā – cakkhusamphassau, sautasamphassau, ghānasamphassau, jivhāsamphassau, kāyasamphassau, manausamphassau| saḷāyatanasamudayā phassasamudayau, saḷāyatananiraudhā phassaniraudhau, ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau phassaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ phassaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ phassasamudayaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ phassaniraudhaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ phassaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti, sau sabbasau rāgānusayaṃ pahāya…pai॰… dukkhassantakarau  hauti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhamma’’nti|

99. ‘‘sādhāvusau’’ti khau…pai॰… apucchuṃ – siyā panāvusau…pai॰… ‘‘siyā, āvusau| yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau saḷāyatanañ‍ca pajānāti, saḷāyatanasamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, saḷāyatananiraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, saḷāyatananiraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadañ‍ca pajānāti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina  saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhammaṃ| katamaṃ panāvusau, saḷāyatanaṃ, katamau saḷāyatanasamudayau, katamau saḷāyatananiraudhau, katamā saḷāyatananiraudhagāminī paṭipadā? chayimāni, āvusau, āyatanāni – cakkhāyatanaṃ, sautāyatanaṃ, ghānāyatanaṃ, jivhāyatanaṃ, kāyāyatanaṃ, manāyatanaṃ| nāmarūpasamudayā saḷāyatanasamudayau, nāmarūpaniraudhā saḷāyatananiraudhau, ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau saḷāyatananiraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ saḷāyatanaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ saḷāyatanasamudayaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ saḷāyatananiraudhaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ saḷāyatananiraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti, sau sabbasau rāgānusayaṃ pahāya…pai… dukkhassantakarau hauti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhamma’’nti|

100. ‘‘sādhāvusau’’ti khau…pai॰… apucchuṃ – siyā panāvusau…pai॰… ‘‘siyā, āvusau| yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau nāmarūpañ‍ca pajānāti, nāmarūpasamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, nāmarūpaniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, nāmarūpaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadañ‍ca pajānāti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhammaṃ| katamaṃ panāvusau, nāmarūpaṃ, katamau nāmarūpasamudayau, katamau nāmarūpaniraudhau, katamā nāmarūpaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā? vaidanā, sañ‍ñā, caitanā, phassau, manasikārau  – idaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, nāmaṃ; cattāri ca mahābhūtāni, catun‍nañ‍ca mahābhūtānaṃ upādāyarūpaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, rūpaṃ| iti idañ‍ca nāmaṃ idañ‍ca rūpaṃ – idaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, nāmarūpaṃ| viñ‍ñāṇasamudayā nāmarūpasamudayau, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhau, ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau nāmarūpaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ  nāmarūpaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ nāmarūpasamudayaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ nāmarūpaniraudhaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ nāmarūpaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti, sau sabbasau  rāgānusayaṃ pahāya…pai॰… dukkhassantakarau hauti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhamma’’nti|

101. ‘‘sādhāvusau’’ti khau…pai॰… apucchuṃ – siyā panāvusau…pai॰… ‘‘siyā, āvusau| yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca pajānāti, viñ‍ñāṇasamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadañ‍ca pajānāti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhammaṃ| katamaṃ panāvusau, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, katamau viñ‍ñāṇasamudayau, katamau viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau, katamā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā? chayimai, āvusau, viñ‍ñāṇakāyā – cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| saṅkhārasamudayā viñ‍ñāṇasamudayau, saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau, ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasamudayaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti, sau sabbasau  rāgānusayaṃ pahāya…pai॰… dukkhassantakarau hauti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhamma’’nti|

102. ‘‘sādhāvusau’’ti  khau…pai॰… apucchuṃ – siyā panāvusau…pai॰… ‘‘siyā, āvusau| yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau saṅkhārai ca pajānāti, saṅkhārasamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, saṅkhāraniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadañ‍ca pajānāti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhammaṃ| katamai panāvusau, saṅkhārā, katamau saṅkhārasamudayau, katamau  saṅkhāraniraudhau, katamā saṅkhāraniraudhagāminī paṭipadā? tayaumai, āvusau, saṅkhārā – kāyasaṅkhārau, vacīsaṅkhārau, cittasaṅkhārau| avij‍jāsamudayā saṅkhārasamudayau, avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau, ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau saṅkhāraniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ saṅkhārai pajānāti, aivaṃ saṅkhārasamudayaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ saṅkhāraniraudhaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti, sau sabbasau rāgānusayaṃ pahāya, paṭighānusayaṃ paṭivinaudaitvā, ‘asmī’ti diṭṭhimānānusayaṃ samūhanitvā, avij‍jaṃ pahāya vij‍jaṃ uppādaitvā, diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa  diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhamma’’nti|

103. ‘‘sādhāvusau’’ti khau…pai॰… apucchuṃ – siyā panāvusau…pai॰… ‘‘siyā, āvusau| yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau avij‍jañ‍ca pajānāti, avij‍jāsamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, avij‍jāniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, avij‍jāniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadañ‍ca pajānāti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhammaṃ| katamā panāvusau, avij‍jā, katamau avij‍jāsamudayau, katamau avij‍jāniraudhau, katamā avij‍jāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā? yaṃ khau, āvusau, dukkhai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, dukkhasamudayai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya añ‍ñāṇaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, avij‍jā| āsavasamudayā avij‍jāsamudayau, āsavaniraudhā avij‍jāniraudhau, ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau avij‍jāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yatau  khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ avij‍jaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ avij‍jāsamudayaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ avij‍jāniraudhaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ avij‍jāniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti, sau sabbasau rāgānusayaṃ pahāya, paṭighānusayaṃ paṭivinaudaitvā, ‘asmī’ti diṭṭhimānānusayaṃ samūhanitvā, avij‍jaṃ pahāya vij‍jaṃ uppādaitvā, diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau  sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhamma’’nti|

104. ‘‘sādhāvusau’’ti  khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ uttari pañhaṃ apucchuṃ – ‘‘siyā panāvusau, añ‍ñaupi pariyāyau  yathā ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhamma’’nti?

‘‘siyā, āvusau| yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau āsavañ‍ca pajānāti, āsavasamudayañ‍ca pajānāti, āsavaniraudhañ‍ca pajānāti, āsavaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadañ‍ca pajānāti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhammaṃ| katamau panāvusau, āsavau, katamau āsavasamudayau, katamau āsavaniraudhau, katamā āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadāti? tayaumai, āvusau, āsavā – kāmāsavau, bhavāsavau, avij‍jāsavau| avij‍jāsamudayā āsavasamudayau, avij‍jāniraudhā āsavaniraudhau, ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yatau khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau aivaṃ āsavaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ āsavasamudayaṃ pajānāti, aivaṃ āsavaniraudhaṃ  pajānāti, aivaṃ āsavaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti, sau sabbasau rāgānusayaṃ pahāya, paṭighānusayaṃ paṭivinaudaitvā, ‘asmī’ti diṭṭhimānānusayaṃ samūhanitvā, avij‍jaṃ pahāya vij‍jaṃ uppādaitvā, diṭṭhaiva dhammai dukkhassantakarau hauti – aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau sammādiṭṭhi hauti, ujugatāssa diṭṭhi, dhammai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau, āgatau imaṃ saddhamma’’nti|

idamavaucāyasmā sāriputtau| attamanā tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

sammādiṭṭhisuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ| itau paraṃ kaisuci pautthakaisu imāpi gāthāyau§aivaṃ dissanti –§dukkhaṃ jarāmaraṇaṃ upādānaṃ, saḷāyatanaṃ nāmarūpaṃ| viñ‍ñāṇaṃ yā sā parai, katamā panāvusau padānaṃ||§kiṃ jāti taṇhā ca vaidanā, avij‍jāya catuk‍kanayau| cattāri parai katamā, panāvusau padānaṃ kaivalaṃ||§āhārau ca bhavau phassau, saṅkhārau āsavapañ‍camau| yāva pañ‍ca parai katamā, panāvusau padānaṃ kiṃ||§katamanti chabbidhā vuttaṃ, katamāni catubbidhāni| katamau pañ‍cavidhau vuttau, sabbaisaṃ aikasaṅkhānaṃ pañ‍canayapadāni cāti|| 

10. mahāsatipaṭṭhānasuttaṃ

105. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kurūsu viharati kammāsadhammaṃ nāma kurūnaṃ nigamau| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

uddaisau

106. ‘‘aikāyanau ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, maggau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā, saukaparidaivānaṃ  pariddavānaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) samatik‍kamāya, dukkhadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya, ñāyassa adhigamāya, nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya, yadidaṃ cattārau satipaṭṭhānā|

‘‘katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; cittai cittānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā, vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ|

uddaisau niṭṭhitau|

kāyānupassanā ānāpānapabbaṃ

107. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā nisīdati, pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā, ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya, parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau satauva assasati, satauva satau (sī॰ syā॰) passasati| dīghaṃ  vā assasantau ‘dīghaṃ assasāmī’ti pajānāti, dīghaṃ vā passasantau ‘dīghaṃ passasāmī’ti pajānāti, rassaṃ vā assasantau ‘rassaṃ assasāmī’ti pajānāti, rassaṃ vā passasantau  ‘rassaṃ passasāmī’ti pajānāti, ‘sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, dakkhau bhamakārau vā bhamakārantaivāsī vā dīghaṃ vā añchantau ‘dīghaṃ añchāmī’ti pajānāti, rassaṃ vā añchantau ‘rassaṃ añchāmī’ti pajānāti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dīghaṃ vā assasantau ‘dīghaṃ assasāmī’ti pajānāti, dīghaṃ vā passasantau ‘dīghaṃ passasāmī’ti pajānāti, rassaṃ vā assasantau ‘rassaṃ assasāmī’ti pajānāti, rassaṃ vā passasantau ‘rassaṃ passasāmī’ti pajānāti; ‘sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘sabbakāyapaṭisaṃvaidī passasissāmī’ti sikkhati; ‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ assasissāmī’ti sikkhati, ‘passambhayaṃ kāyasaṅkhāraṃ passasissāmī’ti sikkhati| iti ajjhattaṃ vā kāyai kāyānupassī viharati, bahiddhā vā kāyai kāyānupassī viharati, ajjhattabahiddhā vā kāyai kāyānupassī viharati; samudayadhammānupassī vā kāyasmiṃ viharati, vayadhammānupassī vā kāyasmiṃ viharati, samudayavayadhammānupassī vā kāyasmiṃ viharati| ‘atthi kāyau’ti vā panassa sati pac‍cupaṭṭhitā hauti| yāvadaiva  ñāṇamattāya paṭissatimattāya anissitau ca viharati, na ca kiñ‍ci laukai upādiyati| aivampi khau aivampi (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati|

ānāpānapabbaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

kāyānupassanā iriyāpathapabbaṃ

108. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu gacchantau vā ‘gacchāmī’ti pajānāti, ṭhitau vā ‘ṭhitaumhī’ti pajānāti, nisin‍nau vā ‘nisin‍naumhī’ti  pajānāti, sayānau vā ‘sayānaumhī’ti pajānāti| yathā yathā vā panassa kāyau paṇihitau hauti tathā tathā naṃ pajānāti| iti ajjhattaṃ vā kāyai kāyānupassī viharati, bahiddhā vā kāyai kāyānupassī viharati, ajjhattabahiddhā vā kāyai kāyānupassī viharati; samudayadhammānupassī vā kāyasmiṃ viharati, vayadhammānupassī vā kāyasmiṃ viharati, samudayavayadhammānupassī vā kāyasmiṃ viharati| ‘atthi  kāyau’ti vā panassa sati pac‍cupaṭṭhitā hauti| yāvadaiva ñāṇamattāya paṭissatimattāya anissitau ca viharati, na ca kiñ‍ci laukai upādiyati| aivampi khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati|

iriyāpathapabbaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

kāyānupassanā sampajānapabbaṃ

109. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu abhik‍kantai paṭik‍kantai sampajānakārī hauti, ālaukitai vilaukitai sampajānakārī hauti, samiñ‍jitai pasāritai sampajānakārī hauti, saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇai sampajānakārī hauti, asitai pītai khāyitai sāyitai sampajānakārī hauti, uc‍cārapassāvakammai sampajānakārī hauti, gatai ṭhitai nisin‍nai suttai jāgaritai bhāsitai tuṇhībhāvai sampajānakārī hauti| iti ajjhattaṃ vā kāyai kāyānupassī viharati…pai॰… aivampi khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati|

sampajānapabbaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

kāyānupassanā paṭikūlamanasikārapabbaṃ

110. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ uddhaṃ pādatalā, adhau kaisamatthakā, tacapariyantaṃ pūraṃ nānappakārassa asucinau pac‍cavaikkhati – ‘atthi imasmiṃ kāyai kaisā laumā nakhā dantā tacau maṃsaṃ  nhāru nahāru (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) aṭṭhi aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ vak‍kaṃ hadayaṃ yakanaṃ kilaumakaṃ pihakaṃ papphāsaṃ antaṃ antaguṇaṃ udariyaṃ karīsaṃ pittaṃ saimhaṃ pubbau lauhitaṃ saidau maidau assu vasā khaiḷau siṅghāṇikā lasikā mutta’nti muttaṃ matthaluṅganti (ka॰)|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ubhataumukhā putauḷi mūtauḷī (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) pūrā nānāvihitassa dhañ‍ñassa, saiyyathidaṃ – sālīnaṃ  vīhīnaṃ muggānaṃ māsānaṃ tilānaṃ taṇḍulānaṃ| tamainaṃ cakkhumā purisau muñ‍citvā pac‍cavaikkhaiyya – ‘imai sālī imai vīhī imai  muggā imai māsā imai tilā imai taṇḍulā’ti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ uddhaṃ pādatalā, adhau kaisamatthakā, tacapariyantaṃ pūraṃ nānappakārassa asucinau pac‍cavaikkhati – ‘atthi imasmiṃ kāyai kaisā laumā…pai॰… mutta’nti|

‘‘iti ajjhattaṃ vā kāyai kāyānupassī viharati…pai॰… aivampi khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati|

paṭikūlamanasikārapabbaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

kāyānupassanā dhātumanasikārapabbaṃ

111. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ yathāṭhitaṃ yathāpaṇihitaṃ dhātusau pac‍cavaikkhati – ‘atthi imasmiṃ kāyai pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātū’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, dakkhau gaughātakau vā gaughātakantaivāsī vā gāviṃ vadhitvā catumahāpathai cātummahāpathai (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) bilasau vibhajitvā nisin‍nau assa| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ yathāṭhitaṃ yathāpaṇihitaṃ dhātusau pac‍cavaikkhati – ‘atthi imasmiṃ kāyai pathavīdhātu āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātū’ti| iti ajjhattaṃ vā kāyai kāyānupassī viharati…pai॰…  aivampi khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati|

dhātumanasikārapabbaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

kāyānupassanā navasivathikapabbaṃ

112. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saiyyathāpi passaiyya sarīraṃ sivathikāya chaḍḍitaṃ aikāhamataṃ vā dvīhamataṃ vā tīhamataṃ vā uddhumātakaṃ vinīlakaṃ vipubbakajātaṃ| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ upasaṃharati – ‘ayampi khau kāyau aivaṃdhammau aivaṃbhāvī aivaṃanatītau’ti aitaṃ anatītauti (sī॰ pī॰)| iti ajjhattaṃ vā kāyai kāyānupassī viharati…pai॰… aivampi khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saiyyathāpi passaiyya sarīraṃ sivathikāya chaḍḍitaṃ kākaihi vā khaj‍jamānaṃ kulalaihi vā khaj‍jamānaṃ gijjhaihi vā khaj‍jamānaṃ kaṅkaihi vā khaj‍jamānaṃ sunakhaihi vā khaj‍jamānaṃ byagghaihi vā khaj‍jamānaṃ dīpīhi vā khaj‍jamānaṃ siṅgālaihi vā gijjhaihi vā khaj‍jamānaṃ, suvānaihi vā khaj‍jamānaṃ, sigālaihi vā (syā॰ pī॰) khaj‍jamānaṃ vividhaihi vā pāṇakajātaihi khaj‍jamānaṃ| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ upasaṃharati – ‘ayampi khau kāyau aivaṃdhammau aivaṃbhāvī aivaṃanatītau’ti| iti ajjhattaṃ vā kāyai kāyānupassī viharati…pai॰… aivampi khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saiyyathāpi passaiyya sarīraṃ sivathikāya chaḍḍitaṃ aṭṭhikasaṅkhalikaṃ samaṃsalauhitaṃ nhārusambandhaṃ…pai॰… aṭṭhikasaṅkhalikaṃ nimaṃsalauhitamakkhitaṃ nhārusambandhaṃ…pai॰… aṭṭhikasaṅkhalikaṃ apagatamaṃsalauhitaṃ nhārusambandhaṃ…pai॰… aṭṭhikāni apagatasambandhāni  apagatanhārusambandhāni (syā॰) disā vidisā vikkhittāni, añ‍ñaina hatthaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina pādaṭṭhikaṃ  añ‍ñaina gaupphakaṭṭhikaṃ ‘‘añ‍ñaina gaupphakaṭṭhika’’nti idaṃ sī॰ syā॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu natthi añ‍ñaina jaṅghaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina ūruṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina kaṭiṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina kaṭaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina piṭṭhaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina kaṇṭakaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina phāsukaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina uraṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina aṃsaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina bāhuṭṭhikaṃ (syā॰) añ‍ñaina phāsukaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina piṭṭhiṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina khandhaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina kaṭaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina piṭṭhaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina kaṇṭakaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina phāsukaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina uraṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina aṃsaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina bāhuṭṭhikaṃ (syā॰) añ‍ñaina gīvaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina hanukaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina dantaṭṭhikaṃ añ‍ñaina sīsakaṭāhaṃ| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ upasaṃharati – ‘ayampi khau kāyau aivaṃdhammau aivaṃbhāvī aivaṃanatītau’ti| iti ajjhattaṃ vā kāyai kāyānupassī viharati…pai॰… aivampi khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saiyyathāpi passaiyya sarīraṃ sivathikāya chaḍḍitaṃ, aṭṭhikāni saitāni saṅkhavaṇṇapaṭibhāgāni saṅkhavaṇṇūpanibhāni (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) …pai॰… aṭṭhikāni puñ‍jakitāni tairauvassikāni…pai॰… aṭṭhikāni pūtīni cuṇṇakajātāni| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ upasaṃharati – ‘ayampi khau kāyau aivaṃdhammau aivaṃbhāvī aivaṃanatītau’ti| iti ajjhattaṃ vā kāyai kāyānupassī  viharati, bahiddhā vā kāyai kāyānupassī viharati, ajjhattabahiddhā vā kāyai kāyānupassī viharati; samudayadhammānupassī vā kāyasmiṃ viharati, vayadhammānupassī vā kāyasmiṃ viharati, samudayavayadhammānupassī vā kāyasmiṃ viharati| ‘atthi kāyau’ti vā panassa sati pac‍cupaṭṭhitā hauti| yāvadaiva ñāṇamattāya paṭissatimattāya anissitau ca viharati, na ca kiñ‍ci laukai upādiyati| aivampi khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati|

navasivathikapabbaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

cuddasakāyānupassanā niṭṭhitā|

vaidanānupassanā

113. ‘‘kathañ‍ca  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sukhaṃ vā sukhaṃ, dukkhaṃ, adukkhamasukhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti; dukkhaṃ vā sukhaṃ, dukkhaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti; adukkhamasukhaṃ vā vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti; sāmisaṃ vā sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘sāmisaṃ sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti; nirāmisaṃ vā sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘nirāmisaṃ sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti; sāmisaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘sāmisaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti; nirāmisaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘nirāmisaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti; sāmisaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘sāmisaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti; nirāmisaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘nirāmisaṃ adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti; iti ajjhattaṃ vā vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati, bahiddhā vā vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati, ajjhattabahiddhā vā vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati; samudayadhammānupassī vā vaidanāsu viharati, vayadhammānupassī vā vaidanāsu viharati, samudayavayadhammānupassī vā vaidanāsu viharati| ‘atthi vaidanā’ti vā panassa sati pac‍cupaṭṭhitā hauti| yāvadaiva ñāṇamattāya paṭissatimattāya anissitau ca viharati, na ca kiñ‍ci laukai upādiyati| aivampi khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharati|

vaidanānupassanā niṭṭhitā|

cittānupassanā

114. ‘‘kathañ‍ca  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cittai cittānupassī viharati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘sarāgaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vītarāgaṃ citta’nti pajānāti; sadausaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘sadausaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, vītadausaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vītadausaṃ citta’nti pajānāti; samauhaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘samauhaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, vītamauhaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vītamauhaṃ citta’nti pajānāti; saṃkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘saṃkhittaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vikkhittaṃ citta’nti pajānāti; mahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ ‘mahaggataṃ citta’nti pajānāti, amahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ ‘amahaggataṃ citta’nti pajānāti; sauttaraṃ  vā cittaṃ ‘sauttaraṃ citta’nti pajānāti, anuttaraṃ vā cittaṃ ‘anuttaraṃ citta’nti pajānāti; samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘samāhitaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘asamāhitaṃ citta’nti pajānāti; vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vimuttaṃ citta’nti pajānāti, avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘avimuttaṃ citta’nti pajānāti| iti ajjhattaṃ vā cittai cittānupassī viharati, bahiddhā vā cittai cittānupassī viharati, ajjhattabahiddhā vā cittai cittānupassī viharati; samudayadhammānupassī  vā cittasmiṃ viharati, vayadhammānupassī vā cittasmiṃ viharati, samudayavayadhammānupassī vā cittasmiṃ viharati| ‘atthi citta’nti vā panassa sati pac‍cupaṭṭhitā hauti| yāvadaiva ñāṇamattāya paṭissatimattāya anissitau ca viharati, na ca kiñ‍ci laukai upādiyati| aivampi khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cittai cittānupassī viharati|

cittānupassanā niṭṭhitā|

dhammānupassanā nīvaraṇapabbaṃ

115. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati pañ‍casu nīvaraṇaisu| kathañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati pañ‍casu nīvaraṇaisu?

‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ kāmacchandaṃ ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ kāmacchandau’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ kāmacchandaṃ ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ kāmacchandau’ti pajānāti; yathā ca anuppan‍nassa kāmacchandassa uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nassa kāmacchandassa pahānaṃ hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca pahīnassa kāmacchandassa āyatiṃ anuppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti|

‘‘santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ byāpādaṃ ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ byāpādau’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ byāpādaṃ ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ byāpādau’ti pajānāti; yathā ca anuppan‍nassa byāpādassa  uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nassa byāpādassa pahānaṃ hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca pahīnassa byāpādassa āyatiṃ anuppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti|

‘‘santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ thīnamiddhaṃ ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ thīnamiddha’nti pajānāti, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ thīnamiddhaṃ ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ thīnamiddha’nti pajānāti, yathā ca anuppan‍nassa thīnamiddhassa uppādau hauti tañ‍ca  pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nassa thīnamiddhassa pahānaṃ hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca pahīnassa thīnamiddhassa āyatiṃ anuppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti|

‘‘santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍ca’nti pajānāti, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍ca’nti pajānāti; yathā ca anuppan‍nassa uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nassa uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa pahānaṃ hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca pahīnassa uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa āyatiṃ anuppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti|

‘‘santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ vicikicchaṃ ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ vicikicchā’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ vicikicchaṃ ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ vicikicchā’ti pajānāti; yathā ca anuppan‍nāya vicikicchāya uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nāya vicikicchāya pahānaṃ hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca pahīnāya vicikicchāya āyatiṃ anuppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti|

‘‘iti ajjhattaṃ vā dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati, bahiddhā vā dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati, ajjhattabahiddhā vā dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati; samudayadhammānupassī vā dhammaisu viharati, vayadhammānupassī vā dhammaisu viharati, samudayavayadhammānupassī vā dhammaisu viharati| ‘atthi dhammā’ti vā panassa sati pac‍cupaṭṭhitā hauti| yāvadaiva ñāṇamattāya paṭissatimattāya  anissitau ca viharati, na ca kiñ‍ci laukai upādiyati| aivampi khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati pañ‍casu nīvaraṇaisu|

nīvaraṇapabbaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammānupassanā khandhapabbaṃ

116. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaisu dhammānupassī  viharati pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu| kathañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu – ‘iti rūpaṃ, iti rūpassa samudayau, iti rūpassa atthaṅgamau; iti vaidanā, iti vaidanāya samudayau, iti vaidanāya atthaṅgamau; iti sañ‍ñā, iti sañ‍ñāya samudayau, iti sañ‍ñāya atthaṅgamau; iti saṅkhārā, iti saṅkhārānaṃ samudayau, iti saṅkhārānaṃ atthaṅgamau; iti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayau, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa atthaṅgamau’ti; iti ajjhattaṃ vā dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati, bahiddhā vā dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati, ajjhattabahiddhā vā dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati; samudayadhammānupassī vā dhammaisu viharati, vayadhammānupassī vā dhammaisu viharati, samudayavayadhammānupassī vā dhammaisu viharati| ‘atthi dhammā’ti vā panassa sati pac‍cupaṭṭhitā hauti| yāvadaiva ñāṇamattāya paṭissatimattāya anissitau ca viharati, na ca kiñ‍ci laukai upādiyati| aivampi khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu|

khandhapabbaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammānupassanā āyatanapabbaṃ

117. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati chasu ajjhattikabāhiraisu āyatanaisu| kathañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati chasu  ajjhattikabāhiraisu āyatanaisu?

‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhuñ‍ca pajānāti, rūpai ca pajānāti, yañ‍ca tadubhayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati saṃyaujanaṃ tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca anuppan‍nassa saṃyaujanassa uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nassa saṃyaujanassa pahānaṃ hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca pahīnassa saṃyaujanassa āyatiṃ anuppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti|

‘‘sautañ‍ca  pajānāti, saddai ca pajānāti, yañ‍ca tadubhayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati saṃyaujanaṃ tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca anuppan‍nassa saṃyaujanassa uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nassa saṃyaujanassa pahānaṃ hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca pahīnassa saṃyaujanassa āyatiṃ anuppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti|

‘‘ghānañ‍ca pajānāti, gandhai ca pajānāti, yañ‍ca tadubhayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati saṃyaujanaṃ tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca anuppan‍nassa saṃyaujanassa uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nassa saṃyaujanassa pahānaṃ hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca pahīnassa saṃyaujanassa āyatiṃ anuppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti|

‘‘jivhañ‍ca pajānāti, rasai ca pajānāti, yañ‍ca tadubhayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati saṃyaujanaṃ tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca anuppan‍nassa saṃyaujanassa uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nassa saṃyaujanassa pahānaṃ hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca pahīnassa saṃyaujanassa āyatiṃ anuppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti|

‘‘kāyañ‍ca pajānāti, phauṭṭhabbai ca pajānāti, yañ‍ca tadubhayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati saṃyaujanaṃ tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca anuppan‍nassa saṃyaujanassa uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nassa saṃyaujanassa pahānaṃ hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca pahīnassa saṃyaujanassa āyatiṃ anuppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti|

‘‘manañ‍ca pajānāti, dhammai ca pajānāti, yañ‍ca tadubhayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati saṃyaujanaṃ tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca anuppan‍nassa saṃyaujanassa uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nassa saṃyaujanassa pahānaṃ hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca pahīnassa saṃyaujanassa āyatiṃ anuppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti|

‘‘iti  ajjhattaṃ vā dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati, bahiddhā vā dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati, ajjhattabahiddhā vā dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati; samudayadhammānupassī vā dhammaisu viharati, vayadhammānupassī vā dhammaisu viharati, samudayavayadhammānupassī vā dhammaisu viharati| ‘atthi dhammā’ti vā panassa sati pac‍cupaṭṭhitā hauti| yāvadaiva ñāṇamattāya  paṭissatimattāya anissitau  ca viharati na ca kiñ‍ci laukai upādiyati| aivampi khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati chasu ajjhattikabāhiraisu āyatanaisu|

āyatanapabbaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

dhammānupassanā baujjhaṅgapabbaṃ

118. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati sattasu baujjhaṅgaisu| kathañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati sattasu baujjhaṅgaisu? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ satisambaujjhaṅgau’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ satisambaujjhaṅgaṃ ‘natthi mai  ajjhattaṃ satisambaujjhaṅgau’ti pajānāti, yathā ca anuppan‍nassa satisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nassa satisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūrī hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti|

‘‘santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaṃ ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau’ti pajānāti, yathā ca anuppan‍nassa dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgassa uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nassa dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūrī hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti|

‘‘santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ vīriyasambaujjhaṅgaṃ ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ vīriyasambaujjhaṅgaṃ ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau’ti pajānāti, yathā ca anuppan‍nassa vīriyasambaujjhaṅgassa uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nassa vīriyasambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūrī hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti|

‘‘santaṃ  vā ajjhattaṃ pītisambaujjhaṅgaṃ ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ pītisambaujjhaṅgau’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ pītisambaujjhaṅgaṃ ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ pītisambaujjhaṅgau’ti pajānāti, yathā ca anuppan‍nassa pītisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nassa pītisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūrī hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti|

‘‘santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ passaddhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ passaddhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau’ti pajānāti, yathā ca anuppan‍nassa passaddhisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nassa passaddhisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūrī hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti|

‘‘santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ samādhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ samādhisambaujjhaṅgau’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ samādhisambaujjhaṅgaṃ ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ samādhisambaujjhaṅgau’ti pajānāti, yathā ca anuppan‍nassa samādhisambaujjhaṅgassa uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nassa samādhisambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūrī hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti|

‘‘santaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ ‘atthi mai ajjhattaṃ upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau’ti pajānāti, asantaṃ vā ajjhattaṃ upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgaṃ ‘natthi mai ajjhattaṃ upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau’ti pajānāti, yathā ca anuppan‍nassa upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa uppādau hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti, yathā ca uppan‍nassa upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgassa bhāvanāya pāripūrī hauti tañ‍ca pajānāti|

‘‘iti ajjhattaṃ vā dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati, bahiddhā vā dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati, ajjhattabahiddhā vā dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati; samudayadhammānupassī vā dhammaisu viharati, vayadhammānupassī vā dhammaisu viharati, samudayavayadhammānupassī vā dhammaisu viharati| ‘atthi dhammā’ti vā panassa sati pac‍cupaṭṭhitā hauti| yāvadaiva ñāṇamattāya paṭissatimattāya anissitau ca viharati, na ca kiñ‍ci laukai upādiyati| aivampi khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati sattasu baujjhaṅgaisu|

baujjhaṅgapabbaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ baujjhaṅgapabbaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ| paṭhamabhāṇavāraṃ (syā॰)|

dhammānupassanā sac‍capabbaṃ

119. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati catūsu ariyasac‍caisu| kathañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati catūsu ariyasac‍caisu? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti|

paṭhamabhāṇavārau niṭṭhitau|

dukkhasac‍caniddaisau

120. ‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ? jātipi dukkhā, jarāpi dukkhā, maraṇampi dukkhaṃ, saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsāpi dukkhā, appiyaihi sampayaugaupi dukkhau, piyaihi vippayaugaupi dukkhau ‘‘appiyaihi…pai॰… vippayaugaupi dukkhau’’ti pāṭhau caiva taṃniddaisau ca sī॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu na dissati, sumaṅgalavilāsiniyaṃpi taṃsaṃvaṇṇanā natthi, yampicchaṃ na labhati tampi dukkhaṃ, saṃkhittaina pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā pañ‍cupādānakkhandhāpi (ka॰) dukkhā|

121. ‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, jāti? yā taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ tamhi tamhi sattanikāyai jāti sañ‍jāti auk‍kanti abhinibbatti khandhānaṃ pātubhāvau āyatanānaṃ paṭilābhau, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, jāti|

122. ‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, jarā? yā taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ tamhi tamhi sattanikāyai jarā jīraṇatā khaṇḍic‍caṃ pālic‍caṃ valittacatā āyunau saṃhāni indriyānaṃ paripākau, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, jarā|

123. ‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, maraṇaṃ? yaṃ sumaṅgalavilāsinī aulaukaitabbā taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ tamhā tamhā sattanikāyā cuti cavanatā bhaidau antaradhānaṃ mac‍cu maraṇaṃ kālaṅkiriyā khandhānaṃ bhaidau kaḷaivarassa nikkhaipau jīvitindriyassupacchaidau, idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, maraṇaṃ|

124. ‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, saukau? yau khau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina byasanaina  saman‍nāgatassa añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina  dukkhadhammaina phuṭṭhassa saukau saucanā saucitattaṃ antausaukau antauparisaukau, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, saukau|

125. ‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, paridaivau? yau khau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina byasanaina saman‍nāgatassa añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina dukkhadhammaina phuṭṭhassa ādaivau paridaivau ādaivanā paridaivanā ādaivitattaṃ paridaivitattaṃ, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, paridaivau|

126. ‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ? yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ kāyikaṃ asātaṃ kāyasamphassajaṃ dukkhaṃ asātaṃ vaidayitaṃ, idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ|

127. ‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, daumanassaṃ? yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ caitasikaṃ asātaṃ manausamphassajaṃ dukkhaṃ asātaṃ vaidayitaṃ, idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, daumanassaṃ|

128. ‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, upāyāsau? yau khau, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina byasanaina saman‍nāgatassa añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraina dukkhadhammaina phuṭṭhassa āyāsau upāyāsau āyāsitattaṃ upāyāsitattaṃ, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, upāyāsau|

129. ‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, appiyaihi sampayaugau dukkhau? idha yassa tai haunti aniṭṭhā akantā amanāpā rūpā saddā gandhā rasā phauṭṭhabbā dhammā, yai vā panassa tai haunti anatthakāmā ahitakāmā aphāsukakāmā ayaugakkhaimakāmā, yā taihi saddhiṃ saṅgati samāgamau samaudhānaṃ missībhāvau, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, appiyaihi sampayaugau dukkhau|

130. ‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, piyaihi vippayaugau dukkhau? idha yassa tai haunti iṭṭhā kantā manāpā rūpā saddā gandhā  rasā phauṭṭhabbā dhammā, yai vā panassa tai haunti atthakāmā hitakāmā phāsukakāmā yaugakkhaimakāmā mātā vā pitā vā bhātā vā bhaginī vā mittā vā amac‍cā vā ñātisālauhitā vā, yā taihi saddhiṃ asaṅgati asamāgamau  asamaudhānaṃ amissībhāvau, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, piyaihi vippayaugau dukkhau|

131. ‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, yampicchaṃ na labhati tampi dukkhaṃ? jātidhammānaṃ, bhikkhavai, sattānaṃ aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jati – ‘ahau vata mayaṃ na jātidhammā assāma, na ca vata nau jāti āgacchaiyyā’ti| na khau panaitaṃ icchāya pattabbaṃ, idampi yampicchaṃ na labhati tampi dukkhaṃ| jarādhammānaṃ, bhikkhavai, sattānaṃ aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jati – ‘ahau vata mayaṃ na jarādhammā assāma, na ca vata nau jarā āgacchaiyyā’ti| na khau panaitaṃ icchāya pattabbaṃ, idampi yampicchaṃ na labhati tampi dukkhaṃ| byādhidhammānaṃ, bhikkhavai, sattānaṃ aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jati – ‘ahau vata mayaṃ na byādhidhammā assāma, na ca vata nau byādhi āgacchaiyyā’ti| na khau panaitaṃ icchāya pattabbaṃ, idampi yampicchaṃ na labhati tampi dukkhaṃ| maraṇadhammānaṃ, bhikkhavai, sattānaṃ aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jati – ‘ahau vata mayaṃ na maraṇadhammā assāma, na ca vata nau maraṇaṃ āgacchaiyyā’ti| na khau panaitaṃ icchāya pattabbaṃ, idampi yampicchaṃ na labhati tampi dukkhaṃ| saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsadhammānaṃ, bhikkhavai, sattānaṃ aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jati – ‘ahau vata mayaṃ na saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsadhammā assāma, na ca vata nau saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsadhammā āgacchaiyyu’nti| na khau panaitaṃ icchāya pattabbaṃ, idampi yampicchaṃ na labhati tampi dukkhaṃ|

132. ‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, saṃkhittaina pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā dukkhā? saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau, vaidanupādānakkhandhau, sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhau, saṅkhārupādānakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, saṃkhittaina pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā dukkhā| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ|

samudayasac‍caniddaisau

133. ‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhasamudayaṃ dukkhasamudayau (syā॰) ariyasac‍caṃ? yāyaṃ taṇhā  paunaubbhavikā paunaubhavikā (sī॰ pī॰) nandīrāgasahagatā nandirāgasahagatā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) tatratatrābhinandinī| saiyyathidaṃ – kāmataṇhā bhavataṇhā vibhavataṇhā|

‘‘sā  khau panaisā, bhikkhavai, taṇhā kattha uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati, kattha nivisamānā nivisati? yaṃ laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ, aitthaisā taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati, aittha nivisamānā nivisati|

‘‘kiñ‍ca laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ? cakkhu laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ, aitthaisā taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati, aittha nivisamānā nivisati| sautaṃ laukai…pai॰… ghānaṃ laukai… jivhā  laukai… kāyau laukai… manau laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ, aitthaisā taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati, aittha nivisamānā nivisati|

‘‘rūpā laukai… saddā laukai… gandhā laukai… rasā laukai… phauṭṭhabbā laukai… dhammā laukai piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ, aitthaisā taṇhā uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍jati, aittha nivisamānā nivisati|
