175. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| atha khau mahānāmau sak‍kau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau mahānāmau sak‍kau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘dīgharattāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi – ‘laubhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau, dausau cittassa upak‍kilaisau, mauhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti| aivañ‍cāhaṃ aivaṃpāhaṃ (ka॰), bhantai, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi – ‘laubhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau, dausau cittassa upak‍kilaisau, mauhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau’ti| atha ca pana mai aikadā laubhadhammāpi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti, dausadhammāpi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti, mauhadhammāpi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘kausu nāma mai dhammau ajjhattaṃ appahīnau yaina mai aikadā laubhadhammāpi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti, dausadhammāpi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti, mauhadhammāpi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhantī’’’ti|

176. ‘‘sau aiva khau tai, mahānāma, dhammau ajjhattaṃ appahīnau yaina tai aikadā laubhadhammāpi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti, dausadhammāpi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti, mauhadhammāpi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti| sau ca hi tai, mahānāma, dhammau ajjhattaṃ pahīnau abhavissa, na tvaṃ agāraṃ ajjhāvasaiyyāsi, na kāmai paribhuñ‍jaiyyāsi| yasmā ca khau tai, mahānāma, sau aiva dhammau ajjhattaṃ appahīnau tasmā tvaṃ agāraṃ ajjhāvasasi, kāmai paribhuñ‍jasi|

177. ‘‘‘appassādā  kāmā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau bahūpāyāsā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) aittha bhiyyau’ti – iti caipi, mahānāma, ariyasāvakassa yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhaṃ hauti, sau ca sauva (ka॰) añ‍ñatraiva kāmaihi añ‍ñatra akusalaihi dhammaihi pītisukhaṃ nādhigacchati, añ‍ñaṃ vā tatau santataraṃ; atha khau sau naiva tāva anāvaṭṭī kāmaisu hauti| yatau ca khau, mahānāma, ariyasāvakassa ‘appassādā kāmā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau  aittha bhiyyau’ti – aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya  sudiṭṭhaṃ hauti, sau ca añ‍ñatraiva kāmaihi añ‍ñatra akusalaihi dhammaihi pītisukhaṃ adhigacchati añ‍ñaṃ vā tatau santataraṃ; atha khau sau anāvaṭṭī kāmaisu hauti|

‘‘mayhampi khau, mahānāma, pubbaiva sambaudhā, anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau, ‘appassādā kāmā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau’ti – aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhaṃ hauti, sau ca añ‍ñatraiva kāmaihi añ‍ñatra akusalaihi dhammaihi pītisukhaṃ nājjhagamaṃ, añ‍ñaṃ vā tatau santataraṃ; atha khvāhaṃ naiva tāva anāvaṭṭī kāmaisu pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ| yatau ca khau mai, mahānāma, ‘appassādā kāmā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau’ti – aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭhaṃ ahausi, sau ca sauva (ka॰) añ‍ñatraiva kāmaihi añ‍ñatra akusalaihi dhammaihi pītisukhaṃ ajjhagamaṃ, añ‍ñaṃ vā tatau santataraṃ; athāhaṃ anāvaṭṭī kāmaisu pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ|

178. ‘‘kau ca, mahānāma, kāmānaṃ assādau? pañ‍cimai, mahānāma, kāmaguṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā; sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā – imai khau, mahānāma, pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā| yaṃ khau, mahānāma, imai pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ – ayaṃ kāmānaṃ assādau|

‘‘kau ca, mahānāma, kāmānaṃ ādīnavau? idha, mahānāma, kulaputtau yaina sippaṭṭhānaina jīvikaṃ kappaiti – yadi muddāya yadi gaṇanāya yadi saṅkhānaina yadi kasiyā yadi vaṇij‍jāya yadi gaurakkhaina yadi issatthaina yadi rājapaurisaina yadi sippañ‍ñataraina, sītassa purakkhatau uṇhassa purakkhatau ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīṃsapasamphassaihi rissamānau khuppipāsāya mīyamānau; ayampi, mahānāma, kāmānaṃ ādīnavau sandiṭṭhikau dukkhakkhandhau kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ  kāmādhikaraṇaṃ kāmānamaiva haitu|

‘‘tassa  cai mahānāma kulaputtassa aivaṃ uṭṭhahatau ghaṭatau vāyamatau tai bhaugā nābhinipphaj‍janti, sau saucati kilamati paridaivati urattāḷiṃ kandati sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati ‘maughaṃ  vata mai uṭṭhānaṃ, aphalau vata mai vāyāmau’ti| ayampi, mahānāma, kāmānaṃ ādīnavau sandiṭṭhikau dukkhakkhandhau kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ kāmādhikaraṇaṃ kāmānamaiva haitu|

‘‘tassa cai, mahānāma, kulaputtassa aivaṃ uṭṭhahatau ghaṭatau vāyamatau tai bhaugā abhinipphaj‍janti| sau taisaṃ bhaugānaṃ ārakkhādhikaraṇaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti – ‘kinti mai bhaugai naiva rājānau haraiyyuṃ, na caurā haraiyyuṃ, na aggi dahaiyya, na udakaṃ vahaiyya, na appiyā vā dāyādā haraiyyu’nti| tassa aivaṃ ārakkhatau gaupayatau tai bhaugai rājānau vā haranti, caurā vā haranti, aggi vā dahati, udakaṃ vā vahati, appiyā vā dāyādā haranti| sau saucati kilamati paridaivati urattāḷiṃ kandati sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati – ‘yampi mai ahausi tampi nau natthī’ti| ayampi, mahānāma, kāmānaṃ ādīnavau sandiṭṭhikau dukkhakkhandhau kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ kāmādhikaraṇaṃ kāmānamaiva haitu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, mahānāma, kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ kāmādhikaraṇaṃ kāmānamaiva haitu rājānaupi rājūhi vivadanti, khattiyāpi khattiyaihi vivadanti, brāhmaṇāpi brāhmaṇaihi vivadanti, gahapatīpi gahapatīhi vivadanti, mātāpi puttaina vivadati, puttaupi mātarā vivadati, pitāpi puttaina vivadati, puttaupi pitarā vivadati, bhātāpi bhātarā vivadati, bhātāpi bhaginiyā vivadati, bhaginīpi bhātarā vivadati, sahāyaupi sahāyaina vivadati| tai tattha kalahaviggahavivādāpan‍nā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ pāṇīhipi upak‍kamanti, laiḍḍūhipi upak‍kamanti, daṇḍaihipi upak‍kamanti, satthaihipi upak‍kamanti| tai tattha maraṇampi nigacchanti, maraṇamattampi dukkhaṃ| ayampi, mahānāma, kāmānaṃ ādīnavau sandiṭṭhikau dukkhakkhandhau kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ kāmādhikaraṇaṃ kāmānamaiva haitu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, mahānāma, kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ kāmādhikaraṇaṃ kāmānamaiva haitu asicammaṃ gahaitvā, dhanukalāpaṃ san‍nayhitvā, ubhataubyūḷhaṃ saṅgāmaṃ pakkhandanti usūsupi khippamānaisu, sattīsupi khippamānāsu, asīsupi vij‍jautalantaisu| tai tattha usūhipi vijjhanti, sattiyāpi vijjhanti, asināpi sīsaṃ  chindanti| tai tattha maraṇampi nigacchanti, maraṇamattampi dukkhaṃ| ayampi, mahānāma, kāmānaṃ ādīnavau sandiṭṭhikau dukkhakkhandhau kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ kāmādhikaraṇaṃ kāmānamaiva haitu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, mahānāma, kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ kāmādhikaraṇaṃ kāmānamaiva haitu asicammaṃ  gahaitvā, dhanukalāpaṃ san‍nayhitvā, addāvalaipanā upakāriyau pakkhandanti usūsupi khippamānaisu, sattīsupi khippamānāsu, asīsupi vij‍jautalantaisu| tai tattha usūhipi vijjhanti, sattiyāpi vijjhanti, chakaṇakāyapi ausiñ‍canti, abhivaggainapi aumaddanti, asināpi sīsaṃ chindanti| tai tattha maraṇampi nigacchanti, maraṇamattampi dukkhaṃ| ayampi, mahānāma, kāmānaṃ ādīnavau sandiṭṭhikau dukkhakkhandhau kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ kāmādhikaraṇaṃ kāmānamaiva haitu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, mahānāma, kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ kāmādhikaraṇaṃ kāmānamaiva haitu sandhimpi chindanti, nil‍laupampi haranti, aikāgārikampi  karaunti, paripanthaipi tiṭṭhanti, paradārampi gacchanti| tamainaṃ rājānau gahaitvā vividhā kammakāraṇā kārainti – kasāhipi tāḷainti, vaittaihipi tāḷainti, aḍḍhadaṇḍakaihipi tāḷainti; hatthampi chindanti, pādampi chindanti, hatthapādampi chindanti, kaṇṇampi chindanti, nāsampi chindanti, kaṇṇanāsampi chindanti; bilaṅgathālikampi karaunti, saṅkhamuṇḍikampi karaunti, rāhumukhampi karaunti, jautimālikampi karaunti, hatthapaj‍jautikampi karaunti, airakavattikampi karaunti, cīrakavāsikampi karaunti, aiṇaiyyakampi karaunti, baḷisamaṃsikampi karaunti, kahāpaṇikampi karaunti, khārāpatacchikampi karaunti, palighaparivattikampi karaunti, palālapīṭhakampi karaunti, tattainapi tailaina ausiñ‍canti, sunakhaihipi khādāpainti, jīvantampi sūlai uttāsainti, asināpi sīsaṃ chindanti| tai tattha maraṇampi nigacchanti, maraṇamattampi dukkhaṃ| ayampi, mahānāma, kāmānaṃ ādīnavau sandiṭṭhikau dukkhakkhandhau kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ kāmādhikaraṇaṃ kāmānamaiva haitu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, mahānāma, kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ kāmādhikaraṇaṃ kāmānamaiva haitu kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caranti, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caranti, manasā duc‍caritaṃ caranti| tai kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caritvā, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caritvā, manasā duc‍caritaṃ caritvā, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā, apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍janti| ayampi, mahānāma, kāmānaṃ ādīnavau samparāyikau, dukkhakkhandhau kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ kāmādhikaraṇaṃ kāmānamaiva haitu|

179. ‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, mahānāma, samayaṃ rājagahai viharāmi gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā nigaṇṭhā niganthā (syā॰ ka॰) isigilipassai kāḷasilāyaṃ  ubbhaṭṭhakā haunti āsanapaṭikkhittā, aupak‍kamikā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidayanti| atha khvāhaṃ, mahānāma, sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina isigilipassai kāḷasilā yaina tai nigaṇṭhā  tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā tai nigaṇṭhai aitadavaucaṃ – ‘kin‍nu tumhai, āvusau, nigaṇṭhā ubbhaṭṭhakā āsanapaṭikkhittā, aupak‍kamikā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidayathā’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, mahānāma, tai nigaṇṭhā maṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘nigaṇṭhau, āvusau, nāṭaputtau nāthaputtau (sī॰ pī॰) sabbañ‍ñū sabbadassāvī aparisaisaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ paṭijānāti – ‘‘caratau ca mai tiṭṭhatau ca suttassa ca jāgarassa  ca satataṃ samitaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhita’’nti| sau aivamāha – ‘‘atthi khau vau atthi khau bhau (syā॰ ka॰), nigaṇṭhā, pubbai pāpakammaṃ kataṃ, taṃ imāya kaṭukāya duk‍karakārikāya nij‍jīraitha nij‍jaraitha (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰); yaṃ panaittha mayaṃ panaittha (ka॰) aitarahi kāyaina saṃvutā vācāya saṃvutā manasā saṃvutā taṃ āyatiṃ pāpassa kammassa akaraṇaṃ; iti purāṇānaṃ kammānaṃ tapasā byantibhāvā, navānaṃ kammānaṃ akaraṇā, āyatiṃ anavassavau; āyatiṃ anavassavā kammakkhayau, kammakkhayā dukkhakkhayau, dukkhakkhayā vaidanākkhayau, vaidanākkhayā sabbaṃ dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ bhavissatī’’ti| tañ‍ca panamhākaṃ ruc‍cati caiva khamati ca, taina camha attamanā’ti|

180. ‘‘aivaṃ  vuttai, ahaṃ, mahānāma, tai nigaṇṭhai aitadavaucaṃ – ‘kiṃ pana tumhai, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, jānātha – ahuvamhaiva mayaṃ pubbai na nāhuvamhā’ti? ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’| ‘kiṃ pana tumhai, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, jānātha – akaramhaiva mayaṃ pubbai pāpakammaṃ na nākaramhā’ti? ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’| ‘kiṃ pana tumhai, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, jānātha – aivarūpaṃ vā aivarūpaṃ vā pāpakammaṃ akaramhā’ti? ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’| ‘kiṃ pana tumhai, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, jānātha – aittakaṃ vā dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ, aittakaṃ vā dukkhaṃ nij‍jīraitabbaṃ, aittakamhi vā dukkhai nij‍jiṇṇai sabbaṃ dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ bhavissatī’ti? ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’| ‘kiṃ pana tumhai, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, jānātha – diṭṭhaiva dhammai akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānaṃ, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampada’nti? ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’|

‘‘‘iti kira tumhai, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, na jānātha – ahuvamhaiva mayaṃ pubbai na nāhuvamhāti, na jānātha – akaramhaiva mayaṃ pubbai pāpakammaṃ na nākaramhāti, na jānātha – aivarūpaṃ vā aivarūpaṃ vā pāpakammaṃ akaramhāti, na jānātha – aittakaṃ vā dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ, aittakaṃ vā dukkhaṃ nij‍jīraitabbaṃ, aittakamhi vā dukkhai nij‍jiṇṇai sabbaṃ dukkhaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ bhavissatīti| na jānātha – diṭṭhaiva dhammai akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānaṃ, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadaṃ| aivaṃ santai, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, yai laukai luddā lauhitapāṇinau kurūrakammantā manussaisu  pac‍cājātā tai nigaṇṭhaisu pabbajantī’ti? ‘na  khau, āvusau gautama, sukhaina sukhaṃ adhigantabbaṃ, dukkhaina khau sukhaṃ adhigantabbaṃ; sukhaina cāvusau  gautama, sukhaṃ adhigantabbaṃ abhavissa, rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau sukhaṃ adhigacchaiyya, rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau sukhavihāritarau āyasmatā gautamainā’ti|

‘‘‘addhāyasmantaihi nigaṇṭhaihi sahasā appaṭisaṅkhā vācā bhāsitā – na khau, āvusau gautama, sukhaina sukhaṃ adhigantabbaṃ, dukkhaina khau sukhaṃ adhigantabbaṃ; sukhaina cāvusau gautama, sukhaṃ adhigantabbaṃ abhavissa, rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau sukhaṃ adhigacchaiyya, rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau sukhavihāritarau āyasmatā gautamainā’’ti| api ca ahamaiva tattha paṭipucchitabbau – kau nu khau āyasmantānaṃ sukhavihāritarau rājā vā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau āyasmā vā gautamau’ti? addhāvusau gautama, amhaihi sahasā appaṭisaṅkhā vācā bhāsitā, na khau, āvusau gautama, sukhaina sukhaṃ adhigantabbaṃ, dukkhaina khau sukhaṃ adhigantabbaṃ; sukhaina cāvusau gautama, sukhaṃ adhigantabbaṃ abhavissa, rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau sukhaṃ adhigacchaiyya, rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau sukhavihāritarau āyasmatā gautamaināti| api ca tiṭṭhataitaṃ, idānipi mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ gautamaṃ pucchāma – kau nu khau āyasmantānaṃ sukhavihāritarau rājā vā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau āyasmā vā gautamau’ti?

‘‘‘taina  hāvusau nigaṇṭhā, tumhaiva tattha paṭipucchissāmi, yathā vau khamaiyya tathā naṃ byākaraiyyātha| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñathāvusau nigaṇṭhā, pahauti rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau, aniñ‍jamānau  kāyaina, abhāsamānau vācaṃ, satta rattindivāni aikantasukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidī viharitu’nti? ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’|

‘‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñathāvusau nigaṇṭhā, pahauti rājā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau, aniñ‍jamānau kāyaina, abhāsamānau vācaṃ, cha rattindivāni…pai॰… pañ‍ca rattindivāni… cattāri rattindivāni… tīṇi rattindivāni… dvai rattindivāni… aikaṃ rattindivaṃ aikantasukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidī viharitu’nti? ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’|

‘‘‘ahaṃ  khau, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, pahaumi aniñ‍jamānau kāyaina, abhāsamānau vācaṃ, aikaṃ rattindivaṃ aikantasukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidī viharituṃ| ahaṃ khau, āvusau nigaṇṭhā, pahaumi aniñ‍jamānau kāyaina, abhāsamānau vācaṃ, dvai rattindivāni… tīṇi rattindivāni… cattāri rattindivāni… pañ‍ca rattindivāni… cha rattindivāni… satta rattindivāni aikantasukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidī viharituṃ| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñathāvusau nigaṇṭhā, aivaṃ santai kau sukhavihāritarau rājā vā māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau ahaṃ vā’ti? ‘aivaṃ santai āyasmāva gautamau  sukhavihāritarau rañ‍ñā māgadhaina sainiyaina bimbisārainā’’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau mahānāmau sak‍kau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

cūḷadukkhakkhandhasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. anumānasuttaṃ

181. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau bhaggaisu viharati susumāragirai suṃsumāragirai (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) bhaisakaḷāvanai migadāyai| tatra khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau, bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau aitadavauca –

‘‘pavāraiti  caipi, āvusau, bhikkhu – ‘vadantu maṃ āyasmantau, vacanīyaumhi āyasmantaihī’ti, sau ca hauti dubbacau, dauvacassakaraṇaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau, akkhamau appadakkhiṇaggāhī anusāsaniṃ, atha khau naṃ sabrahmacārī na caiva vattabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti, na ca anusāsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti, na ca tasmiṃ puggalai vissāsaṃ āpaj‍jitabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti|

‘‘katamai cāvusau, dauvacassakaraṇā dhammā? idhāvusau, bhikkhu pāpicchau hauti, pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatau| yampāvusau, bhikkhu pāpicchau hauti, pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatau – ayampi dhammau dauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu attuk‍kaṃsakau hauti paravambhī| yampāvusau, bhikkhu attuk‍kaṃsakau hauti paravambhī – ayampi dhammau dauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu kaudhanau hauti kaudhābhibhūtau| yampāvusau, bhikkhu kaudhanau hauti kaudhābhibhūtau – ayampi dhammau dauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu kaudhanau hauti kaudhahaitu upanāhī| yampāvusau, bhikkhu kaudhanau hauti kaudhahaitu upanāhī – ayampi dhammau dauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu kaudhanau hauti kaudhahaitu abhisaṅgī| yampāvusau, bhikkhu kaudhanau hauti kaudhahaitu abhisaṅgī – ayampi dhammau dauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu kaudhanau hauti kaudhasāmantā kaudhasāmantaṃ (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) vācaṃ nicchāraitā| yampāvusau, bhikkhu kaudhanau hauti kaudhasāmantā vācaṃ nicchāraitā – ayampi dhammau dauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu cauditau cuditau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) caudakaina caudakaṃ paṭippharati| yampāvusau, bhikkhu cauditau caudakaina caudakaṃ paṭippharati – ayampi dhammau dauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu cauditau caudakaina caudakaṃ apasādaiti| yampāvusau, bhikkhu cauditau caudakaina caudakaṃ apasādaiti – ayampi dhammau dauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu cauditau caudakaina caudakassa pac‍cāraupaiti| yampāvusau, bhikkhu cauditau caudakaina caudakassa pac‍cāraupaiti – ayampi dhammau dauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu cauditau caudakaina añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati, bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmaiti, kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti| yampāvusau, bhikkhu cauditau caudakaina añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati, bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmaiti, kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti – ayampi dhammau dauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu cauditau caudakaina apadānai  na sampāyati| yampāvusau, bhikkhu cauditau caudakaina apadānai na sampāyati – ayampi dhammau dauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu makkhī hauti paḷāsī| yampāvusau, bhikkhu makkhī hauti paḷāsī – ayampi dhammau dauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu issukī hauti maccharī| yampāvusau, bhikkhu issukī hauti maccharī – ayampi dhammau dauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu saṭhau hauti māyāvī| yampāvusau, bhikkhu saṭhau hauti māyāvī – ayampi dhammau dauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu thaddhau hauti atimānī| yampāvusau, bhikkhu thaddhau hauti atimānī – ayampi dhammau dauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī hauti ādhānaggāhī duppaṭinissaggī| yampāvusau, bhikkhu sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī hauti ādhānaggāhī duppaṭinissaggī – ayampi dhammau dauvacassakaraṇau| imai vuc‍cantāvusau, dauvacassakaraṇā dhammā|

182. ‘‘nau  caipi, āvusau, bhikkhu pavāraiti – ‘vadantu maṃ āyasmantau, vacanīyaumhi āyasmantaihī’ti, sau ca hauti suvacau, sauvacassakaraṇaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau, khamau padakkhiṇaggāhī anusāsaniṃ, atha khau naṃ sabrahmacārī vattabbañ‍caiva mañ‍ñanti, anusāsitabbañ‍ca mañ‍ñanti, tasmiñ‍ca puggalai vissāsaṃ āpaj‍jitabbaṃ mañ‍ñanti|

‘‘katamai cāvusau, sauvacassakaraṇā dhammā? idhāvusau, bhikkhu na pāpicchau hauti, na pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatau| yampāvusau, bhikkhu na pāpicchau hauti na  pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatau – ayampi dhammau sauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu anattuk‍kaṃsakau hauti aparavambhī| yampāvusau, bhikkhu anattuk‍kaṃsakau hauti aparavambhī – ayampi dhammau sauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu na kaudhanau hauti na kaudhābhibhūtau| yampāvusau, bhikkhu na kaudhanau hauti na kaudhābhibhūtau – ayampi dhammau sauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu na kaudhanau hauti na kaudhahaitu upanāhī| yampāvusau, bhikkhu na kaudhanau hauti na kaudhahaitu upanāhī – ayampi dhammau sauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu na kaudhanau hauti na kaudhahaitu abhisaṅgī| yampāvusau, bhikkhu na kaudhanau hauti na kaudhahaitu abhisaṅgī – ayampi dhammau sauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu na kaudhanau hauti na kaudhasāmantā vācaṃ nicchāraitā| yampāvusau, bhikkhu na kaudhanau hauti na kaudhasāmantā vācaṃ nicchāraitā – ayampi dhammau sauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu cauditau caudakaina caudakaṃ nappaṭippharati| yampāvusau, bhikkhu cauditau caudakaina caudakaṃ nappaṭippharati – ayampi dhammau sauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu cauditau caudakaina caudakaṃ na apasādaiti| yampāvusau, bhikkhu cauditau caudakaina caudakaṃ na apasādaiti – ayampi dhammau  sauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu cauditau caudakaina caudakassa na pac‍cāraupaiti| yampāvusau, bhikkhu cauditau caudakaina caudakassa na pac‍cāraupaiti – ayampi dhammau sauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu cauditau caudakaina na añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati, na bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmaiti, na kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti| yampāvusau, bhikkhu cauditau caudakaina na añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati, na bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmaiti, na kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti – ayampi dhammau sauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu cauditau caudakaina apadānai sampāyati| yampāvusau, bhikkhu cauditau caudakaina apadānai sampāyati – ayampi dhammau sauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu amakkhī hauti apaḷāsī| yampāvusau, bhikkhu amakkhī hauti apaḷāsī – ayampi dhammau sauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu anissukī hauti amaccharī| yampāvusau, bhikkhu anissukī hauti amaccharī – ayampi dhammau sauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu asaṭhau hauti amāyāvī| yampāvusau, bhikkhu asaṭhau hauti amāyāvī – ayampi dhammau sauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu atthaddhau hauti anatimānī| yampāvusau, bhikkhu atthaddhau hauti anatimānī – ayampi dhammau sauvacassakaraṇau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu asandiṭṭhiparāmāsī hauti anādhānaggāhī suppaṭinissaggī| yampāvusau, bhikkhu asandiṭṭhiparāmāsī hauti, anādhānaggāhī suppaṭinissaggī  – ayampi dhammau sauvacassakaraṇau| imai vuc‍cantāvusau, sauvacassakaraṇā dhammā|

183. ‘‘tatrāvusau, bhikkhunā attanāva attānaṃ aivaṃ anuminitabbaṃ anumānitabbaṃ (sī॰) – ‘yau khvāyaṃ puggalau pāpicchau, pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatau, ayaṃ mai puggalau appiyau amanāpau; ahañ‍caiva khau panassaṃ pāpicchau pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatau, ahaṃpāssaṃ paraisaṃ appiyau amanāpau’ti| aivaṃ jānantaināvusau, bhikkhunā ‘na pāpicchau bhavissāmi, na pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatau’ti cittaṃ uppādaitabbaṃ|

‘‘‘yau khvāyaṃ puggalau attuk‍kaṃsakau paravambhī, ayaṃ mai puggalau appiyau amanāpau; ahañ‍caiva khau panassaṃ attuk‍kaṃsakau paravambhī, ahaṃpāssaṃ paraisaṃ appiyau amanāpau’ti| aivaṃ jānantaināvusau, bhikkhunā ‘anattuk‍kaṃsakau bhavissāmi aparavambhī’ti cittaṃ uppādaitabbaṃ|

‘‘‘yau khvāyaṃ puggalau kaudhanau kaudhābhibhūtau, ayaṃ mai puggalau appiyau amanāpau| ahañ‍caiva khau panassaṃ kaudhanau kaudhābhibhūtau, ahaṃpāssaṃ paraisaṃ appiyau amanāpau’ti| aivaṃ jānantaināvusau, bhikkhunā ‘na kaudhanau bhavissāmi na kaudhābhibhūtau’ti cittaṃ uppādaitabbaṃ|

‘‘‘yau khvāyaṃ puggalau kaudhanau kaudhahaitu upanāhī, ayaṃ mai puggalau appiyau amanāpau; ahañ‍caiva khau panassaṃ kaudhanau kaudhahaitu upanāhī, ahaṃpāssaṃ paraisaṃ appiyau amanāpau’ti| aivaṃ jānantaināvusau, bhikkhunā ‘na kaudhanau bhavissāmi na kaudhahaitu upanāhī’ti cittaṃ uppādaitabbaṃ|

‘‘‘yau  khvāyaṃ puggalau kaudhanau kaudhahaitu abhisaṅgī, ayaṃ mai puggalau appiyau amanāpau; ahañ‍caiva  khau panassaṃ kaudhanau kaudhahaitu abhisaṅgī, ahaṃpāssaṃ paraisaṃ appiyau amanāpau’ti| aivaṃ jānantaināvusau, bhikkhunā ‘na kaudhanau bhavissāmi na kaudhahaitu abhisaṅgī’ti cittaṃ uppādaitabbaṃ|

‘‘‘yau  khvāyaṃ puggalau kaudhanau kaudhasāmantā vācaṃ nicchāraitā, ayaṃ mai puggalau appiyau amanāpau; ahañ‍caiva khau panassaṃ kaudhanau kaudhasāmantā vācaṃ nicchāraitā, ahaṃpāssaṃ paraisaṃ appiyau amanāpau’ti| aivaṃ jānantaināvusau, bhikkhunā ‘na kaudhanau bhavissāmi na kaudhasāmantā vācaṃ nicchāraissāmī’ti cittaṃ uppādaitabbaṃ|

‘‘‘yau khvāyaṃ puggalau cauditau caudakaina caudakaṃ paṭippharati, ayaṃ mai puggalau appiyau amanāpau; ahañ‍caiva khau pana cauditau caudakaina caudakaṃ paṭippharaiyyaṃ, ahaṃpāssaṃ paraisaṃ appiyau amanāpau’ti| aivaṃ jānantaināvusau, bhikkhunā ‘cauditau caudakaina caudakaṃ nappaṭippharissāmī’ti cittaṃ uppādaitabbaṃ|

‘‘‘yau khvāyaṃ puggalau cauditau caudakaina caudakaṃ apasādaiti, ayaṃ mai puggalau appiyau amanāpau; ahañ‍caiva khau pana cauditau caudakaina caudakaṃ apasādaiyyaṃ, ahaṃpāssaṃ paraisaṃ appiyau amanāpau’ti| aivaṃ jānantaināvusau, bhikkhunā ‘cauditau caudakaina caudakaṃ na apasādaissāmī’ti cittaṃ uppādaitabbaṃ|

‘‘‘yau khvāyaṃ puggalau cauditau caudakaina caudakassa pac‍cāraupaiti, ayaṃ mai puggalau appiyau amanāpau; ahañ‍caiva khau pana cauditau caudakaina caudakassa pac‍cāraupaiyyaṃ, ahaṃpāssaṃ paraisaṃ appiyau amanāpau’ti| aivaṃ  jānantaināvusau, bhikkhunā ‘cauditau caudakaina caudakassa na pac‍cāraupaissāmī’ti cittaṃ uppādaitabbaṃ|

‘‘‘yau khvāyaṃ puggalau cauditau caudakaina añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati, bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmaiti, kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarauti, ayaṃ mai puggalau appiyau amanāpau; ahañ‍caiva khau pana cauditau caudakaina añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicaraiyyaṃ, bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmaiyyaṃ, kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukaraiyyaṃ, ahaṃpāssaṃ paraisaṃ appiyau amanāpau’ti| aivaṃ jānantaināvusau, bhikkhunā ‘cauditau caudakaina na añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarissāmi, na bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmaissāmi, na kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukarissāmī’ti cittaṃ uppādaitabbaṃ|

‘‘‘yau  khvāyaṃ puggalau cauditau caudakaina apadānai na sampāyati, ayaṃ mai puggalau appiyau amanāpau; ahañ‍caiva khau pana cauditau caudakaina apadānai na sampāyaiyyaṃ, ahaṃpāssaṃ paraisaṃ appiyau amanāpau’ti| aivaṃ jānantaināvusau, bhikkhunā ‘cauditau caudakaina apadānai sampāyissāmī’ti cittaṃ uppādaitabbaṃ|

‘‘‘yau khvāyaṃ puggalau makkhī paḷāsī, ayaṃ mai puggalau appiyau amanāpau; ahañ‍caiva khau panassaṃ makkhī paḷāsī, ahaṃpāssaṃ paraisaṃ appiyau amanāpau’ti| aivaṃ jānantaināvusau, bhikkhunā ‘amakkhī bhavissāmi apaḷāsī’ti cittaṃ uppādaitabbaṃ|

‘‘‘yau khvāyaṃ puggalau issukī maccharī, ayaṃ mai puggalau appiyau amanāpau; ahañ‍caiva khau panassaṃ  issukī maccharī, ahaṃpāssaṃ paraisaṃ appiyau amanāpau’ti| aivaṃ jānantaināvusau, bhikkhunā ‘anissukī bhavissāmi amaccharī’ti cittaṃ uppādaitabbaṃ|

‘‘‘yau khvāyaṃ puggalau saṭhau māyāvī, ayaṃ mai puggalau appiyau amanāpau; ahañ‍caiva khau panassaṃ saṭhau māyāvī, ahaṃpāssaṃ paraisaṃ appiyau amanāpau’ti| aivaṃ jānantaināvusau, bhikkhunā ‘asaṭhau bhavissāmi amāyāvī’ti cittaṃ uppādaitabbaṃ|

‘‘‘yau khvāyaṃ puggalau thaddhau atimānī, ayaṃ mai puggalau appiyau amanāpau; ahañ‍caiva khau panassaṃ thaddhau atimānī, ahaṃpāssaṃ paraisaṃ appiyau amanāpau’ti| aivaṃ jānantaināvusau, bhikkhunā ‘atthaddhau bhavissāmi anatimānī’ti cittaṃ uppādaitabbaṃ|

‘‘‘yau khvāyaṃ puggalau sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī ādhānaggāhī duppaṭinissaggī, ayaṃ mai puggalau appiyau amanāpau; ahañ‍caiva khau panassaṃ sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī ādhānaggāhī duppaṭinissaggī, ahaṃpāssaṃ paraisaṃ appiyau amanāpau’ti| aivaṃ jānantaināvusau, bhikkhunā ‘asandiṭṭhiparāmāsī bhavissāmi anādhānaggāhī suppaṭinissaggī’ti cittaṃ uppādaitabbaṃ|

184. ‘‘tatrāvusau, bhikkhunā attanāva attānaṃ aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘kiṃ nu khaumhi pāpicchau, pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatau’ti? sacai, āvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘pāpicchau khaumhi, pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatau’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai panāvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau  aivaṃ jānāti – ‘na khaumhi pāpicchau, na pāpikānaṃ icchānaṃ vasaṃ gatau’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā attanāva attānaṃ aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘kiṃ nu khaumhi attuk‍kaṃsakau paravambhī’ti? sacai, āvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘attuk‍kaṃsakau khaumhi paravambhī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai panāvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘anattuk‍kaṃsakau  khaumhi aparavambhī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā attanāva attānaṃ aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘kiṃ nu khaumhi kaudhanau kaudhābhibhūtau’ti? sacai, āvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘kaudhanau khaumhi kaudhābhibhūtau’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai panāvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘na khaumhi kaudhanau kaudhābhibhūtau’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā attanāva attānaṃ aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘kiṃ nu khaumhi kaudhanau kaudhahaitu upanāhī’ti? sacai, āvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti ‘kaudhanau khaumhi kaudhahaitu upanāhī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva  pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai panāvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti ‘na khaumhi kaudhanau kaudhahaitu upanāhī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā attanāva attānaṃ aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘kiṃ nu khaumhi kaudhanau kaudhahaitu abhisaṅgī’ti? sacai, āvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘kaudhanau khaumhi kaudhahaitu abhisaṅgī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai panāvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘na khaumhi kaudhanau kaudhahaitu abhisaṅgī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā attanāva attānaṃ aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘kiṃ nu khaumhi kaudhanau kaudhasāmantā vācaṃ nicchāraitā’ti? sacai, āvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘kaudhanau khaumhi kaudhasāmantā vācaṃ nicchāraitā’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai panāvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘na khaumhi kaudhanau kaudhasāmantā vācaṃ nicchāraitā’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā attanāva attānaṃ aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘kiṃ nu khaumhi cauditau caudakaina caudakaṃ  paṭippharāmī’ti? sacai, āvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti ‘cauditau khaumhi caudakaina caudakaṃ paṭippharāmī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai panāvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘cauditau khaumhi caudakaina caudakaṃ nappaṭippharāmī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā attanāva attānaṃ aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘kiṃ nu khaumhi cauditau caudakaina caudakaṃ apasādaimī’ti? sacai, āvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti ‘cauditau khaumhi caudakaina caudakaṃ apasādaimī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai panāvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘cauditau khaumhi caudakaina caudakaṃ  na apasādaimī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā attanāva attānaṃ aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘kiṃ nu khaumhi cauditau caudakaina caudakassa pac‍cāraupaimī’ti? sacai, āvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau  aivaṃ jānāti – ‘cauditau khaumhi caudakaina caudakassa pac‍cāraupaimī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai panāvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘cauditau khaumhi caudakaina caudakassa na pac‍cāraupaimī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina  vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā attanāva attānaṃ aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘kiṃ nu khaumhi cauditau caudakaina añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarāmi, bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmaimi, kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukaraumī’ti? sacai, āvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘cauditau khaumhi caudakaina añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarāmi, bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmaimi, kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukaraumī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai panāvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘cauditau khaumhi caudakaina na añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarāmi, na bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmaimi, na kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātukaraumī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā attanāva attānaṃ aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘kiṃ nu khaumhi cauditau caudakaina apadānai na sampāyāmī’ti? sacai, āvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘cauditau khaumhi caudakaina apadānai na sampāyāmī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai panāvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘cauditau khaumhi caudakaina apadānai sampāyāmī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā attanāva attānaṃ aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘kiṃ nu khaumhi makkhī paḷāsī’ti? sacai, āvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘makkhī khaumhi paḷāsī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai panāvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘amakkhī khaumhi apaḷāsī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā attanāva attānaṃ aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘kiṃ nu khaumhi issukī maccharī’ti? sacai, āvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘issukī khaumhi maccharī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai panāvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘anissukī khaumhi amaccharī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā attanāva attānaṃ aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘kiṃ nu khaumhi saṭhau māyāvī’ti? sacai, āvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘saṭhau khaumhi māyāvī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai panāvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘asaṭhau khaumhi amāyāvī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā attanāva attānaṃ aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ  – ‘kiṃ nu khaumhi thaddhau atimānī’ti? sacai, āvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘thaddhau khaumhi atimānī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai panāvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘atthaddhau khaumhi anatimānī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhunā attanāva attānaṃ aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘kiṃ nu khaumhi sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī ādhānaggāhī duppaṭinissaggī’ti? sacai, āvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī khaumhi ādhānaggāhī duppaṭinissaggī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā  taisaṃyaiva pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai panāvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘asandiṭṭhiparāmāsī khaumhi anādhānaggāhī suppaṭinissaggī’ti, taināvusau, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

‘‘sacai, āvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau sabbaipimai pāpakai akusalai dhammai appahīnai attani samanupassati, taināvusau, bhikkhunā sabbaisaṃyaiva imaisaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai panāvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau sabbaipimai pāpakai akusalai dhammai pahīnai attani samanupassati, taināvusau, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ, ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisu|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, itthī  vā purisau vā, daharau yuvā maṇḍanajātikau, ādāsai vā parisuddhai pariyaudātai, acchai vā udakapattai, sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau, sacai tattha passati rajaṃ vā aṅgaṇaṃ vā, tassaiva rajassa vā aṅgaṇassa vā pahānāya vāyamati; nau cai tattha passati rajaṃ vā aṅgaṇaṃ vā, tainaiva attamanau hauti – ‘lābhā vata mai, parisuddhaṃ vata mai’ti| aivamaiva khau, āvusau, sacai bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau sabbaipimai pāpakai akusalai dhammai appahīnai attani samanupassati, taināvusau, bhikkhunā sabbaisaṃyaiva imaisaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya vāyamitabbaṃ| sacai panāvusau, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau sabbaipimai pāpakai akusalai dhammai pahīnai attani samanupassati, taināvusau, bhikkhunā tainaiva pītipāmauj‍jaina vihātabbaṃ, ahaurattānusikkhinā kusalaisu dhammaisū’’ti|

idamavaucāyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau| attamanā tai bhikkhū āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

anumānasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. caitaukhilasuttaṃ

185. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘yassa kassaci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pañ‍ca caitaukhilā appahīnā, pañ‍ca caitasauvinibandhā caitasauvinibaddhā (sī॰), caitauvinibaddhā (sāratthadīpanīṭīkā) asamucchin‍nā, sau vatimasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘katamāssa pañ‍ca caitaukhilā appahīnā haunti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satthari kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satthari kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, aivamassāyaṃ paṭhamau caitaukhilau appahīnau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammai kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati…pai॰… aivamassāyaṃ dutiyau caitaukhilau appahīnau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṅghai kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati…pai॰… aivamassāyaṃ tatiyau caitaukhilau appahīnau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sikkhāya kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sikkhāya kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati, tassa cittaṃ na namati  ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ  na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, aivamassāyaṃ catutthau caitaukhilau appahīnau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabrahmacārīsu kupitau hauti anattamanau āhatacittau khilajātau| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabrahmacārīsu kupitau hauti anattamanau āhatacittau khilajātau, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ na namati  ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, aivamassāyaṃ pañ‍camau caitaukhilau appahīnau hauti| imāssa pañ‍ca caitaukhilā appahīnā haunti|

186. ‘‘katamāssa pañ‍ca caitasauvinibandhā asamucchin‍nā haunti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāmai avītarāgau avigatarāgau (katthaci) hauti avigatacchandau avigatapaimau avigatapipāsau avigatapariḷāhau avigatataṇhau| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāmai avītarāgau hauti avigatacchandau avigatapaimau avigatapipāsau avigatapariḷāhau avigatataṇhau, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, aivamassāyaṃ paṭhamau caitasauvinibandhau asamucchin‍nau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai avītarāgau hauti…pai॰… aivamassāyaṃ dutiyau caitasauvinibandhau asamucchin‍nau hauti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu rūpai avītarāgau hauti…pai॰… aivamassāyaṃ tatiyau caitasauvinibandhau asamucchin‍nau  hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yāvadatthaṃ udarāvadaihakaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā saiyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ anuyuttau viharati| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yāvadatthaṃ udarāvadaihakaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā saiyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ anuyuttau viharati, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, aivamassāyaṃ catutthau caitasauvinibandhau asamucchin‍nau hauti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ daivanikāyaṃ paṇidhāya brahmacariyaṃ carati – ‘imināhaṃ sīlaina vā vataina vā tapaina vā brahmacariyaina vā daivau vā bhavissāmi daivañ‍ñatarau vā’ti| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ daivanikāyaṃ paṇidhāya brahmacariyaṃ carati – ‘imināhaṃ sīlaina vā vataina vā tapaina vā brahmacariyaina vā daivau vā bhavissāmi daivañ‍ñatarau vā’ti, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, aivamassāyaṃ pañ‍camau caitasauvinibandhau asamucchin‍nau hauti| imāssa pañ‍ca caitasauvinibandhā asamucchin‍nā haunti|

‘‘yassa kassaci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau imai pañ‍ca caitaukhilā appahīnā, imai pañ‍ca caitasauvinibandhā asamucchin‍nā, sau vatimasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

187. ‘‘yassa  kassaci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pañ‍ca caitaukhilā pahīnā, pañ‍ca caitasauvinibandhā susamucchin‍nā, sau vatimasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘katamāssa pañ‍ca caitaukhilā pahīnā haunti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satthari na kaṅkhati na vicikicchati adhimuc‍cati sampasīdati| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satthari na kaṅkhati na vicikicchati adhimuc‍cati sampasīdati, tassa cittaṃ namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, aivamassāyaṃ paṭhamau caitaukhilau pahīnau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammai na kaṅkhati na vicikicchati adhimuc‍cati sampasīdati…pai॰… aivamassāyaṃ dutiyau caitaukhilau pahīnau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṅghai na kaṅkhati na vicikicchati adhimuc‍cati sampasīdati…pai॰… aivamassāyaṃ tatiyau caitaukhilau pahīnau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sikkhāya na kaṅkhati na vicikicchati adhimuc‍cati sampasīdati…pai॰… aivamassāyaṃ catutthau caitaukhilau pahīnau hauti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabrahmacārīsu na kupitau hauti na anattamanau attamanau (sī॰ pī॰) anāhatacittau akhilajātau| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabrahmacārīsu na kupitau hauti na anattamanau anāhatacittau akhilajātau, tassa cittaṃ namati ātappāya  anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, aivamassāyaṃ  pañ‍camau caitaukhilau pahīnau hauti| imāssa pañ‍ca caitaukhilā pahīnā haunti|

188. ‘‘katamāssa  pañ‍ca caitasauvinibandhā susamucchin‍nā haunti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāmai vītarāgau hauti vigatacchandau vigatapaimau vigatapipāsau vigatapariḷāhau vigatataṇhau| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāmai vītarāgau hauti vigatacchandau vigatapaimau vigatapipāsau vigatapariḷāhau vigatataṇhau, tassa cittaṃ namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, aivamassāyaṃ paṭhamau caitasauvinibandhau susamucchin‍nau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai vītarāgau hauti…pai॰… rūpai vītarāgau hauti…pai॰… na yāvadatthaṃ udarāvadaihakaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā saiyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ anuyuttau viharati| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na yāvadatthaṃ udarāvadaihakaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā saiyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ anuyuttau viharati, tassa cittaṃ namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, aivamassāyaṃ catutthau caitasauvinibandhau susamucchin‍nau hauti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na añ‍ñataraṃ daivanikāyaṃ paṇidhāya brahmacariyaṃ carati – ‘imināhaṃ sīlaina vā vataina vā tapaina vā brahmacariyaina vā daivau vā bhavissāmi daivañ‍ñatarau  vā’ti| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na añ‍ñataraṃ daivanikāyaṃ paṇidhāya brahmacariyaṃ carati – ‘imināhaṃ sīlaina vā vataina vā tapaina vā brahmacariyaina vā daivau vā bhavissāmi daivañ‍ñatarau vā’ti, tassa cittaṃ namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, aivamassāyaṃ pañ‍camau caitasauvinibandhau susamucchin‍nau hauti| imāssa pañ‍ca caitasauvinibandhā susamucchin‍nā haunti|

‘‘yassa  kassaci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau imai pañ‍ca caitaukhilā pahīnā, imai pañ‍ca caitasauvinibandhā susamucchin‍nā, sau vatimasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

189. ‘‘sau chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, cittasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ  iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, ussauḷhīyaiva pañ‍camī| sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ ussauḷhīpan‍narasaṅgasaman‍nāgatau  bhikkhu bhabbau abhinibbidāya, bhabbau sambaudhāya, bhabbau anuttarassa yaugakkhaimassa adhigamāya| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kuk‍kuṭiyā aṇḍāni aṭṭha vā dasa vā dvādasa vā| tānassu kuk‍kuṭiyā sammā adhisayitāni sammā parisaiditāni sammā paribhāvitāni| kiñ‍cāpi tassā kuk‍kuṭiyā na aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘ahau vatimai kuk‍kuṭapautakā pādanakhasikhāya vā mukhatuṇḍakaina vā aṇḍakausaṃ  padālaitvā sautthinā abhinibbhij‍jaiyyu’nti| atha khau bhabbāva tai kuk‍kuṭapautakā pādanakhasikhāya vā mukhatuṇḍakaina vā aṇḍakausaṃ padālaitvā sautthinā abhinibbhij‍jituṃ| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ ussauḷhipan‍narasaṅgasaman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bhabbau abhinibbidāya, bhabbau sambaudhāya, bhabbau anuttarassa yaugakkhaimassa adhigamāyā’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

caitaukhilasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. vanapatthasuttaṃ

190. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘vanapatthapariyāyaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasikarautha, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

191. ‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ vanapatthaṃ upanissāya viharati| tassa taṃ vanapatthaṃ upanissāya viharatau anupaṭṭhitā caiva sati na upaṭṭhāti, asamāhitañ‍ca  cittaṃ na samādhiyati, aparikkhīṇā ca āsavā na parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattañ‍ca anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nānupāpuṇāti| yai ca khau imai pabbajitaina jīvitaparikkhārā samudānaitabbā – cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā – tai kasiraina samudāgacchanti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ  – ‘ahaṃ khau imaṃ vanapatthaṃ upanissāya viharāmi, tassa mai imaṃ vanapatthaṃ upanissāya viharatau anupaṭṭhitā caiva sati na upaṭṭhāti, asamāhitañ‍ca cittaṃ na samādhiyati, aparikkhīṇā ca āsavā na parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattañ‍ca anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nānupāpuṇāmi| yai ca khau imai pabbajitaina jīvitaparikkhārā  samudānaitabbā – cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā – tai kasiraina samudāgacchantī’ti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā rattibhāgaṃ vā divasabhāgaṃ vā tamhā vanapatthā pak‍kamitabbaṃ, na vatthabbaṃ|

192. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ vanapatthaṃ upanissāya viharati| tassa taṃ vanapatthaṃ upanissāya viharatau anupaṭṭhitā caiva sati na upaṭṭhāti, asamāhitañ‍ca cittaṃ na samādhiyati, aparikkhīṇā ca āsavā na parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattañ‍ca anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nānupāpuṇāti| yai ca khau imai pabbajitaina jīvitaparikkhārā samudānaitabbā – cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā – tai appakasiraina samudāgacchanti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ – ‘ahaṃ khau imaṃ vanapatthaṃ upanissāya viharāmi| tassa mai imaṃ vanapatthaṃ upanissāya viharatau anupaṭṭhitā caiva sati na upaṭṭhāti asamāhitañ‍ca cittaṃ na samādhiyati, aparikkhīṇā ca āsavā na parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattañ‍ca anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nānupāpuṇāmi| yai ca khau imai pabbajitaina jīvitaparikkhārā samudānaitabbā – cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā – tai appakasiraina samudāgacchanti| na khau panāhaṃ cīvarahaitu agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau na piṇḍapātahaitu…pai॰… na saināsanahaitu…pai॰… na gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārahaitu agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau| atha ca pana mai imaṃ  vanapatthaṃ upanissāya viharatau anupaṭṭhitā caiva sati na upaṭṭhāti, asamāhitañ‍ca cittaṃ na samādhiyati, aparikkhīṇā ca āsavā na parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattañ‍ca anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nānupāpuṇāmī’ti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā saṅkhāpi tamhā vanapatthā pak‍kamitabbaṃ, na vatthabbaṃ|

193. ‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ vanapatthaṃ upanissāya viharati| tassa taṃ vanapatthaṃ upanissāya viharatau anupaṭṭhitā caiva sati upaṭṭhāti, asamāhitañ‍ca cittaṃ samādhiyati, aparikkhīṇā ca āsavā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattañ‍ca anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇāti| yai ca khau imai pabbajitaina jīvitaparikkhārā samudānaitabbā – cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā, tai kasiraina samudāgacchanti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ  – ‘ahaṃ khau imaṃ vanapatthaṃ upanissāya viharāmi| tassa mai imaṃ vanapatthaṃ upanissāya viharatau anupaṭṭhitā caiva sati upaṭṭhāti asamāhitañ‍ca cittaṃ samādhiyati, aparikkhīṇā ca āsavā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattañ‍ca anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇāmi| yai ca khau imai pabbajitaina jīvitaparikkhārā samudānaitabbā – cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā – tai kasiraina samudāgacchanti| na khau panāhaṃ cīvarahaitu agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau, na piṇḍapātahaitu…pai॰… na saināsanahaitu…pai॰… na gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārahaitu agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau| atha ca pana mai imaṃ vanapatthaṃ upanissāya viharatau anupaṭṭhitā caiva sati upaṭṭhāti, asamāhitañ‍ca cittaṃ samādhiyati, aparikkhīṇā ca āsavā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattañ‍ca anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇāmī’ti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā saṅkhāpi tasmiṃ vanapatthai vatthabbaṃ, na pak‍kamitabbaṃ|

194. ‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ vanapatthaṃ upanissāya viharati| tassa taṃ vanapatthaṃ upanissāya viharatau anupaṭṭhitā caiva sati upaṭṭhāti, asamāhitañ‍ca cittaṃ samādhiyati, aparikkhīṇā ca āsavā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattañ‍ca anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇāti| yai ca khau imai pabbajitaina jīvitaparikkhārā samudānaitabbā – cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā – tai appakasiraina samudāgacchanti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ – ‘ahaṃ khau imaṃ vanapatthaṃ upanissāya viharāmi| tassa mai imaṃ vanapatthaṃ upanissāya viharatau anupaṭṭhitā caiva sati upaṭṭhāti asamāhitañ‍ca cittaṃ samādhiyati, aparikkhīṇā ca āsavā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattañ‍ca anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇāmi| yai ca khau imai pabbajitaina jīvitaparikkhārā samudānaitabbā – cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā – tai appakasiraina samudāgacchantī’ti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā yāvajīvampi tasmiṃ vanapatthai vatthabbaṃ, na pak‍kamitabbaṃ|

195. ‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ gāmaṃ upanissāya viharati  …pai॰… añ‍ñataraṃ nigamaṃ upanissāya viharati…pai॰… añ‍ñataraṃ nagaraṃ upanissāya viharati…pai॰… añ‍ñataraṃ janapadaṃ upanissāya viharati…pai॰… añ‍ñataraṃ puggalaṃ upanissāya viharati| tassa taṃ puggalaṃ upanissāya viharatau anupaṭṭhitā caiva sati na upaṭṭhāti, asamāhitañ‍ca cittaṃ na samādhiyati, aparikkhīṇā ca āsavā na parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattañ‍ca anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nānupāpuṇāti| yai ca khau imai pabbajitaina jīvitaparikkhārā samudānaitabbā – cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā – tai kasiraina samudāgacchanti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ – ‘ahaṃ khau imaṃ puggalaṃ upanissāya viharāmi| tassa mai imaṃ puggalaṃ upanissāya viharatau anupaṭṭhitā caiva sati na upaṭṭhāti, asamāhitañ‍ca cittaṃ na samādhiyati, aparikkhīṇā ca āsavā na parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattañ‍ca anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nānupāpuṇāmi| yai ca khau imai pabbajitaina jīvitaparikkhārā samudānaitabbā – cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā – tai kasiraina samudāgacchantī’ti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā rattibhāgaṃ vā divasabhāgaṃ vā sau puggalau anāpucchā pak‍kamitabbaṃ, nānubandhitabbau|

196. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ puggalaṃ upanissāya viharati| tassa taṃ  puggalaṃ upanissāya viharatau anupaṭṭhitā  caiva sati na upaṭṭhāti, asamāhitañ‍ca cittaṃ na samādhiyati, aparikkhīṇā ca  āsavā na parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattañ‍ca anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nānupāpuṇāti| yai ca khau imai pabbajitaina jīvitaparikkhārā samudānaitabbā – cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā, tai appakasiraina samudāgacchanti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ – ‘ahaṃ khau imaṃ puggalaṃ upanissāya viharāmi| tassa mai imaṃ puggalaṃ upanissāya viharatau anupaṭṭhitā caiva sati na upaṭṭhāti, asamāhitañ‍ca cittaṃ na samādhiyati, aparikkhīṇā ca āsavā na parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattañ‍ca anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nānupāpuṇāmi| yai ca khau imai pabbajitaina jīvitaparikkhārā samudānaitabbā – cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā  – tai appakasiraina samudāgacchanti| na khau panāhaṃ cīvarahaitu agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau, na piṇḍapātahaitu…pai॰… na saināsanahaitu…pai॰… na gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārahaitu agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau| atha ca pana mai imaṃ puggalaṃ upanissāya viharatau anupaṭṭhitā caiva sati na upaṭṭhāti, asamāhitañ‍ca cittaṃ na samādhiyati, aparikkhīṇā ca āsavā na parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattañ‍ca anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nānupāpuṇāmī’ti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā saṅkhāpi sau puggalau āpucchā pak‍kamitabbaṃ, nānubandhitabbau|

197. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ puggalaṃ upanissāya viharati| tassa taṃ puggalaṃ upanissāya viharatau anupaṭṭhitā caiva sati upaṭṭhāti, asamāhitañ‍ca  cittaṃ samādhiyati, aparikkhīṇā ca āsavā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattañ‍ca anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇāti| yai ca khau imai pabbajitaina jīvitaparikkhārā samudānaitabbā – cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā – tai kasiraina samudāgacchanti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ – ‘ahaṃ khau imaṃ puggalaṃ upanissāya viharāmi| tassa mai imaṃ puggalaṃ upanissāya viharatau anupaṭṭhitā caiva sati upaṭṭhāti, asamāhitañ‍ca cittaṃ samādhiyati, aparikkhīṇā ca āsavā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattañ‍ca anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇāmi| yai ca khau imai pabbajitaina jīvitaparikkhārā samudānaitabbā – cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā – tai kasiraina samudāgacchanti| na khau panāhaṃ cīvarahaitu agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau, na piṇḍapātahaitu…pai॰… na saināsanahaitu…pai॰… na gilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārahaitu agārasmā  anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau| atha ca pana mai imaṃ puggalaṃ upanissāya viharatau anupaṭṭhitā caiva sati upaṭṭhāti, asamāhitañ‍ca cittaṃ samādhiyati, aparikkhīṇā ca āsavā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattañ‍ca anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇāmī’ti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā saṅkhāpi sau puggalau anubandhitabbau, na pak‍kamitabbaṃ|

198. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ puggalaṃ upanissāya viharati| tassa taṃ puggalaṃ upanissāya viharatau anupaṭṭhitā caiva sati  upaṭṭhāti, asamāhitañ‍ca cittaṃ samādhiyati, aparikkhīṇā ca āsavā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattañ‍ca anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇāti| yai ca khau imai pabbajitaina jīvitaparikkhārā samudānaitabbā – cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā – tai appakasiraina samudāgacchanti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhitabbaṃ – ‘ahaṃ khau imaṃ puggalaṃ upanissāya viharāmi| tassa mai imaṃ puggalaṃ upanissāya viharatau anupaṭṭhitā caiva sati upaṭṭhāti, asamāhitañ‍ca cittaṃ samādhiyati, aparikkhīṇā ca āsavā parikkhayaṃ gacchanti, ananuppattañ‍ca anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ anupāpuṇāmi| yai ca khau imai pabbajitaina jīvitaparikkhārā samudānaitabbā – cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā – tai appakasiraina samudāgacchantī’ti| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā yāvajīvampi sau puggalau anubandhitabbau, na pak‍kamitabbaṃ, api panuj‍jamānainapī’’ti api paṇuj‍jamānaināti (?)|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

vanapatthasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. madhupiṇḍikasuttaṃ

199. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya kapilavatthuṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| kapilavatthusmiṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yaina mahāvanaṃ tainupasaṅkami divāvihārāya| mahāvanaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā bailuvalaṭṭhikāya mūlai divāvihāraṃ nisīdi| daṇḍapāṇipi khau sak‍kau jaṅghāvihāraṃ jaṅghavihāraṃ (ka॰) anucaṅkamamānau anuvicaramānau yaina mahāvanaṃ tainupasaṅkami| mahāvanaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā yaina bailuvalaṭṭhikā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā daṇḍamaulubbha aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau daṇḍapāṇi sak‍kau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃvādī samaṇau kimakkhāyī’’ti? ‘‘yathāvādī khau, āvusau, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya  sadaivamanussāya na kainaci laukai viggayha tiṭṭhati, yathā ca pana kāmaihi visaṃyuttaṃ viharantaṃ taṃ brāhmaṇaṃ akathaṃkathiṃ chin‍nakuk‍kuc‍caṃ bhavābhavai vītataṇhaṃ sañ‍ñā nānusainti – aivaṃvādī khau ahaṃ, āvusau, aivamakkhāyī’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ vuttai daṇḍapāṇi sak‍kau sīsaṃ aukampaitvā, jivhaṃ nil‍lāḷaitvā, tivisākhaṃ nalāṭikaṃ  nalāṭai vuṭṭhāpaitvā daṇḍamaulubbha pak‍kāmi|

200. atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina nigraudhārāmau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya kapilavatthuṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ| kapilavatthusmiṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yaina mahāvanaṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃ divāvihārāya| mahāvanaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā bailuvalaṭṭhikāya mūlai divāvihāraṃ nisīdiṃ| daṇḍapāṇipi khau, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau jaṅghāvihāraṃ anucaṅkamamānau anuvicaramānau yaina mahāvanaṃ tainupasaṅkami| mahāvanaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā yaina bailuvalaṭṭhikā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā mayā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā daṇḍamaulubbha aikamantaṃ  aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau, bhikkhavai, daṇḍapāṇi sak‍kau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘kiṃvādī samaṇau kimakkhāyī’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ vuttai ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, daṇḍapāṇiṃ sak‍kaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – yathāvādī khau, āvusau, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya na kainaci laukai viggayha tiṭṭhati, yathā ca pana kāmaihi visaṃyuttaṃ viharantaṃ taṃ brāhmaṇaṃ akathaṃkathiṃ chin‍nakuk‍kuc‍caṃ bhavābhavai vītataṇhaṃ sañ‍ñā nānusainti – aivaṃvādī khau ahaṃ, āvusau, aivamakkhāyī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ vuttai bhikkhavai, daṇḍapāṇi sak‍kau sīsaṃ  aukampaitvā, jivhaṃ nil‍lāḷaitvā, tivisākhaṃ nalāṭikaṃ nalāṭai vuṭṭhāpaitvā daṇḍamaulubbha pak‍kāmī’’ti|

201. aivaṃ vuttai añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃvādī pana, bhantai, bhagavā sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya na kainaci laukai viggayha tiṭṭhati? kathañ‍ca pana, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ kāmaihi visaṃyuttaṃ viharantaṃ taṃ  brāhmaṇaṃ akathaṃkathiṃ chin‍nakuk‍kuc‍caṃ bhavābhavai vītataṇhaṃ sañ‍ñā nānusaintī’’ti? ‘‘yataunidānaṃ, bhikkhu, purisaṃ papañ‍casañ‍ñāsaṅkhā samudācaranti| aittha cai natthi abhinanditabbaṃ abhivaditabbaṃ ajjhausitabbaṃ| aisaivantau rāgānusayānaṃ, aisaivantau paṭighānusayānaṃ, aisaivantau diṭṭhānusayānaṃ, aisaivantau vicikicchānusayānaṃ, aisaivantau mānānusayānaṃ, aisaivantau bhavarāgānusayānaṃ, aisaivantau avij‍jānusayānaṃ, aisaivantau daṇḍādāna-satthādāna-kalaha-viggaha-vivāda-tuvaṃtuvaṃ-paisuñ‍ña-musāvādānaṃ| aitthaitai pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhantī’ti| idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ pāvisi|

202. atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā, vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā, uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘yataunidānaṃ, bhikkhu, purisaṃ papañ‍casañ‍ñāsaṅkhā samudācaranti| aittha cai natthi abhinanditabbaṃ  abhivaditabbaṃ ajjhausitabbaṃ| aisaivantau rāgānusayānaṃ…pai॰… aitthaitai pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhantī’ti| kau nu khau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajaiyyā’’ti? atha khau taisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā mahākac‍cānau satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ| pahauti cāyasmā mahākac‍cānau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ| yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāma; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyāmā’’ti|

atha khau tai bhikkhū yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā mahākac‍cānaina saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau kac‍cāna, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘yataunidānaṃ, bhikkhu, purisaṃ papañ‍casañ‍ñāsaṅkhā samudācaranti| aittha cai natthi abhinanditabbaṃ abhivaditabbaṃ ajjhausitabbaṃ| aisaivantau  rāgānusayānaṃ…pai॰… aitthaitai pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhantī’ti| taisaṃ nau, āvusau kac‍cāna, amhākaṃ acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘‘yataunidānaṃ, bhikkhu, purisaṃ papañ‍casañ‍ñāsaṅkhā samudācaranti| aittha  cai natthi abhinanditabbaṃ abhivaditabbaṃ ajjhausitabbaṃ| aisaivantau rāgānusayānaṃ…pai॰… aitthaitai pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhantī’’ti| kau nu khau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajaiyyāti? taisaṃ nau, āvusau kac‍cāna, amhākaṃ aitadahausi – ‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā mahākac‍cānau satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ, pahauti  cāyasmā mahākac‍cānau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina  uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ| yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāma; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyāmā’ti| vibhajatāyasmā mahākac‍cānau’’ti|

203. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau atik‍kammaiva mūlaṃ, atik‍kamma khandhaṃ, sākhāpalāsai sāraṃ pariyaisitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyya; aivaṃsampadamidaṃ āyasmantānaṃ satthari sammukhībhūtai, taṃ bhagavantaṃ atisitvā, amhai aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchitabbaṃ mañ‍ñatha| sau hāvusau, bhagavā jānaṃ jānāti, passaṃ passati, cakkhubhūtau ñāṇabhūtau dhammabhūtau brahmabhūtau, vattā pavattā, atthassa nin‍naitā, amatassa dātā, dhammassāmī tathāgatau| sau caiva panaitassa kālau ahausi, yaṃ bhagavantaṃyaiva aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyātha| yathā vau bhagavā byākaraiyya tathā naṃ dhāraiyyāthā’’ti| ‘‘addhāvusau kac‍cāna, bhagavā jānaṃ jānāti, passaṃ passati, cakkhubhūtau ñāṇabhūtau dhammabhūtau brahmabhūtau, vattā pavattā, atthassa nin‍naitā, amatassa dātā, dhammassāmī tathāgatau| sau caiva panaitassa kālau ahausi, yaṃ bhagavantaṃyaiva aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyāma| yathā nau bhagavā byākaraiyya tathā naṃ dhāraiyyāma| api cāyasmā mahākac‍cānau satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ, pahauti  cāyasmā mahākac‍cānau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ| vibhajatāyasmā mahākac‍cānau agaruṃ katvā’’ti agarukatvā (sī॰), agarukaritvā (syā॰ pī॰)| ‘‘taina hāvusau, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasikarautha, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau mahākac‍cānassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā mahākac‍cānau aitadavauca –

204. ‘‘yaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘yataunidānaṃ, bhikkhu, purisaṃ papañ‍casañ‍ñāsaṅkhā samudācaranti| aittha cai natthi abhinanditabbaṃ abhivaditabbaṃ ajjhausitabbaṃ, aisaivantau rāgānusayānaṃ…pai॰… aitthaitai pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhantī’ti, imassa khau ahaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmi –

‘‘cakkhuñ‍cāvusau, paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau, phassapac‍cayā  vaidanā, yaṃ vaidaiti taṃ sañ‍jānāti, yaṃ sañ‍jānāti taṃ vitak‍kaiti, yaṃ vitak‍kaiti taṃ papañ‍caiti, yaṃ papañ‍caiti tataunidānaṃ purisaṃ papañ‍casañ‍ñāsaṅkhā samudācaranti atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naisu cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rūpaisu| sautañ‍cāvusau, paṭic‍ca saddai ca uppaj‍jati sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… ghānañ‍cāvusau, paṭic‍ca gandhai ca uppaj‍jati ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… jivhañ‍cāvusau, paṭic‍ca rasai ca uppaj‍jati jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… kāyañ‍cāvusau, paṭic‍ca phauṭṭhabbai ca uppaj‍jati kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… manañ‍cāvusau, paṭic‍ca dhammai ca uppaj‍jati manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, tiṇṇaṃ saṅgati phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, yaṃ vaidaiti taṃ sañ‍jānāti, yaṃ sañ‍jānāti taṃ vitak‍kaiti, yaṃ vitak‍kaiti taṃ papañ‍caiti, yaṃ papañ‍caiti tataunidānaṃ purisaṃ papañ‍casañ‍ñāsaṅkhā samudācaranti atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naisu manauviñ‍ñaiyyaisu dhammaisu|

‘‘sau vatāvusau, cakkhusmiṃ sati rūpai sati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇai sati phassapañ‍ñattiṃ pañ‍ñāpaissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| phassapañ‍ñattiyā sati vaidanāpañ‍ñattiṃ pañ‍ñāpaissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| vaidanāpañ‍ñattiyā sati sañ‍ñāpañ‍ñattiṃ pañ‍ñāpaissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| sañ‍ñāpañ‍ñattiyā sati  vitak‍kapañ‍ñattiṃ pañ‍ñāpaissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| vitak‍kapañ‍ñattiyā sati papañ‍casañ‍ñāsaṅkhāsamudācaraṇapañ‍ñattiṃ pañ‍ñāpaissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| sau vatāvusau, sautasmiṃ sati saddai sati…pai॰…  ghānasmiṃ sati gandhai sati…pai॰… jivhāya sati rasai sati…pai॰… kāyasmiṃ sati phauṭṭhabbai sati…pai॰… manasmiṃ sati dhammai sati manauviñ‍ñāṇai sati phassapañ‍ñattiṃ pañ‍ñāpaissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| phassapañ‍ñattiyā sati vaidanāpañ‍ñattiṃ pañ‍ñāpaissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| vaidanāpañ‍ñattiyā sati sañ‍ñāpañ‍ñattiṃ pañ‍ñāpaissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| sañ‍ñāpañ‍ñattiyā sati vitak‍kapañ‍ñattiṃ pañ‍ñāpaissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| vitak‍kapañ‍ñattiyā sati papañ‍casañ‍ñāsaṅkhāsamudācaraṇapañ‍ñattiṃ pañ‍ñāpaissatīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘sau vatāvusau, cakkhusmiṃ asati rūpai asati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇai asati phassapañ‍ñattiṃ pañ‍ñāpaissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| phassapañ‍ñattiyā asati vaidanāpañ‍ñattiṃ pañ‍ñāpaissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| vaidanāpañ‍ñattiyā asati sañ‍ñāpañ‍ñattiṃ pañ‍ñāpaissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| sañ‍ñāpañ‍ñattiyā asati vitak‍kapañ‍ñattiṃ pañ‍ñāpaissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| vitak‍kapañ‍ñattiyā asati papañ‍casañ‍ñāsaṅkhāsamudācaraṇapañ‍ñattiṃ pañ‍ñāpaissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| sau vatāvusau, sautasmiṃ  asati saddai asati…pai॰… ghānasmiṃ asati gandhai asati…pai॰… jivhāya asati rasai asati…pai॰… kāyasmiṃ asati phauṭṭhabbai asati…pai॰… manasmiṃ asati dhammai asati manauviñ‍ñāṇai  asati phassapañ‍ñattiṃ pañ‍ñāpaissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| phassapañ‍ñattiyā asati vaidanāpañ‍ñattiṃ pañ‍ñāpaissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| vaidanāpañ‍ñattiyā asati sañ‍ñāpañ‍ñattiṃ pañ‍ñāpaissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| sañ‍ñāpañ‍ñattiyā asati vitak‍kapañ‍ñattiṃ pañ‍ñāpaissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| vitak‍kapañ‍ñattiyā asati papañ‍casañ‍ñāsaṅkhāsamudācaraṇapañ‍ñattiṃ pañ‍ñāpaissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘yaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina  uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘yataunidānaṃ, bhikkhu, purisaṃ papañ‍casañ‍ñāsaṅkhā samudācaranti aittha cai natthi abhinanditabbaṃ abhivaditabbaṃ ajjhausitabbaṃ aisaivantau rāgānusayānaṃ…pai॰… aitthaitai pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhantī’ti, imassa khau ahaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā  saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmi| ākaṅkhamānā ca pana tumhai āyasmantau bhagavantaṃyaiva upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyātha| yathā nau bhagavā byākarauti tathā naṃ dhāraiyyāthā’’ti|

205. atha khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau mahākac‍cānassa bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘yaṃ khau nau, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘yataunidānaṃ, bhikkhu, purisaṃ papañ‍casañ‍ñāsaṅkhā samudācaranti| aittha cai natthi abhinanditabbaṃ abhivaditabbaṃ ajjhausitabbaṃ| aisaivantau rāgānusayānaṃ…pai॰… aitthaitai pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhantī’ti| taisaṃ nau, bhantai, amhākaṃ acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau aitadahausi – ‘idaṃ khau nau, āvusau, bhagavā saṃkhittaina uddaisaṃ uddisitvā vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhajitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘‘yataunidānaṃ, bhikkhu, purisaṃ papañ‍casañ‍ñāsaṅkhā samudācaranti| aittha cai natthi abhinanditabbaṃ abhivaditabbaṃ ajjhausitabbaṃ| aisaivantau rāgānusayānaṃ, aisaivantau paṭighānusayānaṃ, aisaivantau diṭṭhānusayānaṃ, aisaivantau vicikicchānusayānaṃ, aisaivantau mānānusayānaṃ, aisaivantau bhavarāgānusayānaṃ, aisaivantau avij‍jānusayānaṃ, aisaivantau daṇḍādāna-satthādāna-kalaha-viggaha-vivādatuvaṃtuvaṃ-paisuñ‍ña-musāvādānaṃ| aitthaitai pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhantī’’ti| kau nu khau imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajaiyyā’ti? taisaṃ nau, bhantai, amhākaṃ aitadahausi – ‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā mahākac‍cānau satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ, pahauti cāyasmā mahākac‍cānau  imassa bhagavatā saṃkhittaina uddaisassa uddiṭṭhassa vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhajituṃ, yaṃnūna mayaṃ yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau tainupasaṅkamaiyyāma; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyāmā’ti| atha khau mayaṃ, bhantai, yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau tainupasaṅkamimha; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ  mahākac‍cānaṃ  aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchimha| taisaṃ nau, bhantai, āyasmatā mahākac‍cānaina imaihi ākāraihi imaihi padaihi imaihi byañ‍janaihi atthau vibhattau’’ti| ‘‘paṇḍitau, bhikkhavai, mahākac‍cānau; mahāpañ‍ñau, bhikkhavai, mahākac‍cānau| maṃ caipi tumhai, bhikkhavai, aitamatthaṃ paṭipucchaiyyātha, ahampi taṃ aivamaivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ yathā taṃ mahākac‍cānaina byākataṃ| aisau caivaitassa atthau| aivañ‍ca aivaimaiva ca (ka॰) naṃ dhāraithā’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai, purisau jighacchādubbalyaparaitau madhupiṇḍikaṃ adhigacchaiyya, sau yatau yatau sāyaiyya, labhaithaiva sādurasaṃ asaicanakaṃ| aivamaiva khau, bhantai, caitasau bhikkhu dabbajātikau, yatau yatau imassa dhammapariyāyassa pañ‍ñāya atthaṃ upaparikkhaiyya, labhaithaiva attamanataṃ, labhaithaiva caitasau pasādaṃ| kau nāmau ayaṃ kau nāmāyaṃ (syā॰), bhantai, dhammapariyāyau’’ti? ‘‘tasmātiha tvaṃ, ānanda, imaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ madhupiṇḍikapariyāyau tvaiva naṃ dhāraihī’’ti|

idamavauca  bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

madhupiṇḍikasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dvaidhāvitak‍kasuttaṃ

206. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘pubbaiva mai, bhikkhavai, sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau aitadahausi – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ dvidhā katvā dvidhā katvā vitak‍kai viharaiyya’nti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yau cāyaṃ kāmavitak‍kau yau ca byāpādavitak‍kau yau ca vihiṃsāvitak‍kau – imaṃ aikaṃ bhāgamakāsiṃ; yau cāyaṃ naikkhammavitak‍kau yau ca abyāpādavitak‍kau yau ca avihiṃsāvitak‍kau – imaṃ dutiyaṃ bhāgamakāsiṃ|

207. ‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau uppaj‍jati  kāmavitak‍kau| sau aivaṃ pajānāmi – ‘uppan‍nau khau mai ayaṃ kāmavitak‍kau| sau ca khau attabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, parabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, pañ‍ñāniraudhikau vighātapakkhikau anibbānasaṃvattanikau’ anibbānasaṃvattanikau’’ti (?)| ‘attabyābādhāya saṃvattatī’tipi mai, bhikkhavai, paṭisañ‍cikkhatau abbhatthaṃ gacchati; ‘parabyābādhāya saṃvattatī’tipi mai, bhikkhavai, paṭisañ‍cikkhatau abbhatthaṃ gacchati; ‘ubhayabyābādhāya saṃvattatī’tipi mai, bhikkhavai, paṭisañ‍cikkhatau abbhatthaṃ gacchati;  ‘pañ‍ñāniraudhikau vighātapakkhikau anibbānasaṃvattanikau’tipi mai, bhikkhavai, paṭisañ‍cikkhatau abbhatthaṃ gacchati| sau khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, uppan‍nuppan‍naṃ kāmavitak‍kaṃ pajahamaiva atītakālikakiriyāpadāniyaiva vinaudamaiva atītakālikakiriyāpadāniyaiva byantamaiva byantaiva (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) naṃ akāsiṃ|

208. ‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau uppaj‍jati byāpādavitak‍kau…pai॰… uppaj‍jati vihiṃsāvitak‍kau| sau aivaṃ pajānāmi – ‘uppan‍nau khau mai ayaṃ vihiṃsāvitak‍kau| sau ca khau attabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, parabyābādhāyapi  saṃvattati, ubhayabyābādhāyapi saṃvattati, pañ‍ñāniraudhikau vighātapakkhikau anibbānasaṃvattanikau’| ‘attabyābādhāya saṃvattatī’tipi mai, bhikkhavai, paṭisañ‍cikkhatau abbhatthaṃ gacchati; ‘parabyābādhāya saṃvattatī’tipi mai, bhikkhavai, paṭisañ‍cikkhatau abbhatthaṃ gacchati; ‘ubhayabyābādhāya saṃvattatī’tipi mai, bhikkhavai, paṭisañ‍cikkhatau abbhatthaṃ gacchati; ‘pañ‍ñāniraudhikau vighātapakkhikau anibbānasaṃvattanikau’tipi mai, bhikkhavai, paṭisañ‍cikkhatau abbhatthaṃ gacchati| sau khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, uppan‍nuppan‍naṃ vihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ pajahamaiva vinaudamaiva byantamaiva naṃ akāsiṃ|

‘‘yañ‍ñadaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bahulamanuvitak‍kaiti anuvicāraiti, tathā tathā nati hauti caitasau| kāmavitak‍kaṃ cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bahulamanuvitak‍kaiti  anuvicāraiti, pahāsi naikkhammavitak‍kaṃ, kāmavitak‍kaṃ bahulamakāsi, tassa taṃ  kāmavitak‍kāya cittaṃ namati| byāpādavitak‍kaṃ cai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… vihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bahulamanuvitak‍kaiti anuvicāraiti, pahāsi avihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ, vihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ bahulamakāsi, tassa taṃ vihiṃsāvitak‍kāya cittaṃ namati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, vassānaṃ pacchimai māsai saradasamayai kiṭṭhasambādhai gaupālakau gāvau rakkhaiyya| sau tā gāvau tatau tatau daṇḍaina ākauṭaiyya paṭikauṭaiyya san‍nirundhaiyya san‍nivāraiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? passati hi sau, bhikkhavai, gaupālakau tataunidānaṃ vadhaṃ vā bandhanaṃ vā jāniṃ vā garahaṃ vā| aivamaiva khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, addasaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ādīnavaṃ aukāraṃ saṃkilaisaṃ, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ vaudānapakkhaṃ|

209. ‘‘tassa  mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau uppaj‍jati naikkhammavitak‍kau| sau aivaṃ pajānāmi – ‘uppan‍nau khau mai ayaṃ naikkhammavitak‍kau| sau ca khau naivattabyābādhāya saṃvattati, na parabyābādhāya saṃvattati, na ubhayabyābādhāya saṃvattati, pañ‍ñāvuddhikau avighātapakkhikau nibbānasaṃvattanikau’| rattiṃ caipi naṃ, bhikkhavai, anuvitak‍kaiyyaṃ anuvicāraiyyaṃ, naiva tataunidānaṃ bhayaṃ samanupassāmi| divasaṃ caipi naṃ, bhikkhavai, anuvitak‍kaiyyaṃ anuvicāraiyyaṃ, naiva tataunidānaṃ bhayaṃ samanupassāmi| rattindivaṃ caipi naṃ, bhikkhavai, anuvitak‍kaiyyaṃ anuvicāraiyyaṃ, naiva tataunidānaṃ bhayaṃ samanupassāmi| api ca khau mai aticiraṃ anuvitak‍kayatau anuvicārayatau kāyau kilamaiyya| kāyai kilantai kilamantai (ka॰) cittaṃ  ūhañ‍ñaiyya| ūhatai cittai ārā cittaṃ samādhimhāti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ saṇṭhapaimi san‍nisādaimi aikaudiṃ karaumi aikaudi karaumi (pī॰) samādahāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? ‘mā mai cittaṃ ūhañ‍ñī’ti ugghāṭīti (syā॰ ka॰), ūhanīti (pī॰)|

210. ‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau uppaj‍jati abyāpādavitak‍kau…pai॰… uppaj‍jati avihiṃsāvitak‍kau| sau aivaṃ pajānāmi – ‘uppan‍nau khau mai ayaṃ avihiṃsāvitak‍kau| sau ca khau naivattabyābādhāya saṃvattati, na parabyābādhāya saṃvattati, na ubhayabyābādhāya  saṃvattati, pañ‍ñāvuddhikau avighātapakkhikau nibbānasaṃvattanikau’| rattiṃ caipi naṃ, bhikkhavai, anuvitak‍kaiyyaṃ anuvicāraiyyaṃ, naiva tataunidānaṃ bhayaṃ samanupassāmi| divasaṃ caipi naṃ, bhikkhavai, anuvitak‍kaiyyaṃ anuvicāraiyyaṃ, naiva tataunidānaṃ bhayaṃ samanupassāmi| rattindivaṃ caipi naṃ, bhikkhavai, anuvitak‍kaiyyaṃ anuvicāraiyyaṃ, naiva tataunidānaṃ bhayaṃ samanupassāmi| api ca khau mai aticiraṃ anuvitak‍kayatau anuvicārayatau kāyau kilamaiyya| kāyai kilantai cittaṃ ūhañ‍ñaiyya| ūhatai cittai ārā cittaṃ samādhimhāti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ saṇṭhapaimi, san‍nisādaimi, aikaudiṃ karaumi samādahāmi| taṃ kissa haitu? ‘mā mai cittaṃ ūhañ‍ñī’ti|

‘‘yañ‍ñadaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bahulamanuvitak‍kaiti anuvicāraiti, tathā tathā nati hauti caitasau| naikkhammavitak‍kañ‍cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bahulamanuvitak‍kaiti anuvicāraiti, pahāsi  kāmavitak‍kaṃ, naikkhammavitak‍kaṃ bahulamakāsi, tassaṃ taṃ naikkhammavitak‍kāya cittaṃ namati| abyāpādavitak‍kañ‍cai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… avihiṃsāvitak‍kañ‍cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bahulamanuvitak‍kaiti anuvicāraiti, pahāsi vihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ, avihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ bahulamakāsi, tassa taṃ avihiṃsāvitak‍kāya cittaṃ namati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gimhānaṃ pacchimai māsai sabbasassaisu gāmantasambhataisu gaupālakau gāvau rakkhaiyya, tassa rukkhamūlagatassa vā abbhaukāsagatassa vā satikaraṇīyamaiva hauti – ‘aitā aitai (ka॰) gāvau’ti| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, satikaraṇīyamaiva ahausi – ‘aitai dhammā’ti|

211. ‘‘āraddhaṃ khau pana mai, bhikkhavai, vīriyaṃ ahausi asal‍līnaṃ, upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā, passaddhau kāyau asāraddhau, samāhitaṃ cittaṃ aikaggaṃ| sau khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ| vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ| pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca vihāsiṃ satau ca sampajānau, sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaisiṃ, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti ‘upaikkhakau satimā  sukhavihārī’ti, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ| sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ|

212. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaisiṃ| sau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarāmi| saiyyathidaṃ, aikampi jātiṃ…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarāmi| ayaṃ khau  mai, bhikkhavai, rattiyā paṭhamai yāmai paṭhamā vij‍jā adhigatā; avij‍jā vihatā vij‍jā uppan‍nā; tamau vihatau ālaukau uppan‍nau; yathā taṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau|

213. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai sattānaṃ cutūpapātañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaisiṃ| sau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passāmi cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai…pai॰… imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā…pai॰… iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passāmi cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai, yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāmi| ayaṃ khau mai, bhikkhavai, rattiyā majjhimai yāmai dutiyā vij‍jā adhigatā; avij‍jā vihatā vij‍jā uppan‍nā; tamau vihatau ālaukau uppan‍nau; yathā taṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau|

214. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaisiṃ| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ| ‘imai āsavā’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ āsavasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ  āsavaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ  āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ| tassa mai aivaṃ jānatau aivaṃ passatau kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cittha, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cittha, avij‍jāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cittha, vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ ahausi – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ| ayaṃ khau mai, bhikkhavai, rattiyā pacchimai yāmai tatiyā vij‍jā adhigatā; avij‍jā vihatā vij‍jā uppan‍nā; tamau vihatau ālaukau uppan‍nau; yathā taṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau|
