215. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, arañ‍ñai pavanai mahantaṃ nin‍naṃ pal‍lalaṃ| tamainaṃ mahāmigasaṅghau upanissāya viharaiyya| tassa kaucidaiva purisau uppaj‍jaiyya anatthakāmau ahitakāmau ayaugakkhaimakāmau| sau yvāssa maggau khaimau sauvatthikau pītigamanīyau taṃ maggaṃ pidahaiyya, vivaraiyya kummaggaṃ, audahaiyya aukacaraṃ, ṭhapaiyya aukacārikaṃ| aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, mahāmigasaṅghau aparaina samayaina  anayabyasanaṃ anayabyasanaṃ tanuttaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) āpaj‍jaiyya| tassaiva khau pana, bhikkhavai, mahatau migasaṅghassa kaucidaiva purisau uppaj‍jaiyya atthakāmau hitakāmau yaugakkhaimakāmau| sau yvāssa maggau khaimau sauvatthikau pītigamanīyau taṃ maggaṃ vivaraiyya, pidahaiyya kummaggaṃ, ūhanaiyya aukacaraṃ, nāsaiyya aukacārikaṃ| aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, mahāmigasaṅghau aparaina samayaina vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jaiyya|

‘‘upamā khau mai ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, katā atthassa viñ‍ñāpanāya| ayaṃ caivaittha atthau – mahantaṃ nin‍naṃ pal‍lalanti khau, bhikkhavai, kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| mahāmigasaṅghauti khau, bhikkhavai, sattānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| purisau anatthakāmau ahitakāmau ayaugakkhaimakāmauti khau, bhikkhavai, mārassaitaṃ pāpimatau adhivacanaṃ| kummaggauti khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭhaṅgikassaitaṃ micchāmaggassa adhivacanaṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – micchādiṭṭhiyā micchāsaṅkappassa micchāvācāya micchākammantassa micchāājīvassa micchāvāyāmassa micchāsatiyā micchāsamādhissa| aukacarauti khau, bhikkhavai, nandīrāgassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| aukacārikāti  khau, bhikkhavai, avij‍jāyaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| purisau atthakāmau hitakāmau yaugakkhaimakāmauti khau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| khaimau maggau sauvatthikau pītigamanīyauti khau, bhikkhavai, ariyassaitaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa adhivacanaṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhiyā sammāsaṅkappassa sammāvācāya sammākammantassa sammāājīvassa sammāvāyāmassa sammāsatiyā sammāsamādhissa|

‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, vivaṭau mayā khaimau maggau sauvatthikau pītigamanīyau, pihitau kummaggau, ūhatau aukacarau, nāsitā aukacārikā| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, satthārā karaṇīyaṃ sāvakānaṃ hitaisinā anukampakaina anukampaṃ upādāya, kataṃ vau taṃ mayā| aitāni, bhikkhavai, rukkhamūlāni, aitāni suñ‍ñāgārāni; jhāyatha, bhikkhavai, mā pamādattha; mā pacchā vippaṭisārinau ahuvattha| ayaṃ vau amhākaṃ anusāsanī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

dvaidhāvitak‍kasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|

10. vitak‍kasaṇṭhānasuttaṃ

216. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti  tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘adhicittamanuyuttaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā pañ‍ca nimittāni kālaina kālaṃ manasi kātabbāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau yaṃ nimittaṃ āgamma yaṃ nimittaṃ manasikarautau uppaj‍janti pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā chandūpasaṃhitāpi dausūpasaṃhitāpi mauhūpasaṃhitāpi, taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā tamhā nimittā añ‍ñaṃ nimittaṃ manasi kātabbaṃ kusalūpasaṃhitaṃ| tassa tamhā nimittā añ‍ñaṃ nimittaṃ manasikarautau kusalūpasaṃhitaṃ yai pāpakā akusalā  vitak‍kā chandūpasaṃhitāpi dausūpasaṃhitāpi mauhūpasaṃhitāpi tai pahīyanti tai abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| taisaṃ pahānā ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti aikaudibhauti (syā॰ ka॰) samādhiyati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, dakkhau palagaṇḍau vā palagaṇḍantaivāsī vā sukhumāya āṇiyā auḷārikaṃ āṇiṃ abhinihanaiyya abhinīharaiyya abhinivattaiyya abhinivaj‍jaiyya (sī॰ pī॰); aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau yaṃ nimittaṃ āgamma yaṃ nimittaṃ manasikarautau uppaj‍janti pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā chandūpasaṃhitāpi dausūpasaṃhitāpi  mauhūpasaṃhitāpi, taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā tamhā nimittā añ‍ñaṃ nimittaṃ manasi kātabbaṃ kusalūpasaṃhitaṃ| tassa tamhā nimittā añ‍ñaṃ nimittaṃ manasikarautau kusalūpasaṃhitaṃ yai pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā chandūpasaṃhitāpi dausūpasaṃhitāpi mauhūpasaṃhitāpi tai pahīyanti tai abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| taisaṃ pahānā ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti samādhiyati|

217. ‘‘tassa cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau tamhā nimittā añ‍ñaṃ nimittaṃ manasikarautau kusalūpasaṃhitaṃ uppaj‍jantaiva pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā chandūpasaṃhitāpi dausūpasaṃhitāpi mauhūpasaṃhitāpi, taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā taisaṃ vitak‍kānaṃ ādīnavau upaparikkhitabbau – ‘itipimai vitak‍kā akusalā, itipimai vitak‍kā sāvaj‍jā, itipimai vitak‍kā  dukkhavipākā’ti| tassa taisaṃ vitak‍kānaṃ ādīnavaṃ upaparikkhatau yai pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā chandūpasaṃhitāpi dausūpasaṃhitāpi mauhūpasaṃhitāpi tai pahīyanti tai abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| taisaṃ pahānā ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti samādhiyati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, itthī vā purisau vā daharau yuvā maṇḍanakajātikau ahikuṇapaina vā kuk‍kurakuṇapaina vā manussakuṇapaina  vā kaṇṭhai āsattaina aṭṭiyaiyya harāyaiyya jigucchaiyya; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, tassa cai bhikkhunau tamhāpi nimittā añ‍ñaṃ nimittaṃ  manasikarautau kusalūpasaṃhitaṃ uppaj‍jantaiva pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā chandūpasaṃhitāpi dausūpasaṃhitāpi mauhūpasaṃhitāpi, taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā taisaṃ vitak‍kānaṃ ādīnavau upaparikkhitabbau – ‘itipimai vitak‍kā akusalā, itipimai  vitak‍kā sāvaj‍jā, itipimai vitak‍kā dukkhavipākā’ti| tassa taisaṃ vitak‍kānaṃ ādīnavaṃ upaparikkhatau yai pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā chandūpasaṃhitāpi dausūpasaṃhitāpi mauhūpasaṃhitāpi tai pahīyanti tai abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| taisaṃ pahānā ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti samādhiyati|

218. ‘‘tassa cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau taisampi vitak‍kānaṃ ādīnavaṃ upaparikkhatau uppaj‍jantaiva pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā chandūpasaṃhitāpi dausūpasaṃhitāpi mauhūpasaṃhitāpi, taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā taisaṃ vitak‍kānaṃ asatiamanasikārau āpaj‍jitabbau| tassa taisaṃ vitak‍kānaṃ asatiamanasikāraṃ āpaj‍jatau yai pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā chandūpasaṃhitāpi dausūpasaṃhitāpi mauhūpasaṃhitāpi tai pahīyanti tai abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| taisaṃ pahānā ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti samādhiyati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, cakkhumā purisau āpāthagatānaṃ rūpānaṃ adassanakāmau assa; sau nimīlaiyya vā añ‍ñaina vā apalaukaiyya| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, tassa cai bhikkhunau taisampi vitak‍kānaṃ ādīnavaṃ upaparikkhatau uppaj‍jantaiva pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā chandūpasaṃhitāpi dausūpasaṃhitāpi mauhūpasaṃhitāpi, tai pahīyanti tai abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| taisaṃ pahānā ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti samādhiyati|

219. ‘‘tassa  cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau taisampi vitak‍kānaṃ asatiamanasikāraṃ āpaj‍jatau  uppaj‍jantaiva pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā chandūpasaṃhitāpi dausūpasaṃhitāpi mauhūpasaṃhitāpi, taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā taisaṃ vitak‍kānaṃ vitak‍kasaṅkhārasaṇṭhānaṃ manasikātabbaṃ| tassa taisaṃ vitak‍kānaṃ vitak‍kasaṅkhārasaṇṭhānaṃ manasikarautau yai pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā chandūpasaṃhitāpi dausūpasaṃhitāpi mauhūpasaṃhitāpi tai pahīyanti tai abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| taisaṃ pahānā ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti samādhiyati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau sīghaṃ gacchaiyya| tassa aivamassa – ‘kiṃ nu khau ahaṃ sīghaṃ gacchāmi? yaṃnūnāhaṃ saṇikaṃ gacchaiyya’nti| sau saṇikaṃ gacchaiyya| tassa aivamassa – ‘kiṃ nu khau ahaṃ saṇikaṃ gacchāmi? yaṃnūnāhaṃ tiṭṭhaiyya’nti| sau tiṭṭhaiyya| tassa aivamassa – ‘kiṃ nu khau ahaṃ ṭhitau? yaṃnūnāhaṃ nisīdaiyya’nti| sau nisīdaiyya| tassa aivamassa – ‘kiṃ nu khau ahaṃ nisin‍nau? yaṃnūnāhaṃ nipaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau nipaj‍jaiyya| aivañhi sau, bhikkhavai, purisau auḷārikaṃ auḷārikaṃ iriyāpathaṃ abhinivaj‍jaitvā abhinissaj‍jaitvā (syā॰) sukhumaṃ sukhumaṃ iriyāpathaṃ kappaiyya| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, tassa cai bhikkhunau taisampi vitak‍kānaṃ asatiamanasikāraṃ āpaj‍jatau uppaj‍jantaiva pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā chandūpasaṃhitāpi dausūpasaṃhitāpi mauhūpasaṃhitāpi tai pahīyanti tai abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| taisaṃ pahānā ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti samādhiyati|

220. ‘‘tassa  cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau taisampi vitak‍kānaṃ vitak‍kasaṅkhārasaṇṭhānaṃ manasikarautau uppaj‍jantaiva pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā chandūpasaṃhitāpi dausūpasaṃhitāpi mauhūpasaṃhitāpi| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā dantaibhidantamādhāya dantai + abhidantaṃ + ādhāyāti ṭīkāyaṃ padacchaidau, dantaibhīti panaittha karaṇatthau yuttau viya dissati jivhāya tāluṃ āhac‍ca caitasā cittaṃ abhiniggaṇhitabbaṃ abhinippīḷaitabbaṃ abhisantāpaitabbaṃ| tassa dantaibhidantamādhāya jivhāya tāluṃ āhac‍ca caitasā cittaṃ abhiniggaṇhatau abhinippīḷayatau abhisantāpayatau yai pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā chandūpasaṃhitāpi dausūpasaṃhitāpi mauhūpasaṃhitāpi tai pahīyanti tai abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| taisaṃ pahānā ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti samādhiyati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, balavā purisau dubbalataraṃ purisaṃ sīsai vā galai vā khandhai vā gahaitvā abhiniggaṇhaiyya abhinippīḷaiyya abhisantāpaiyya; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, tassa cai bhikkhunau taisampi vitak‍kānaṃ vitak‍kasaṅkhārasaṇṭhānaṃ manasikarautau uppaj‍jantaiva pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā chandūpasaṃhitāpi dausūpasaṃhitāpi mauhūpasaṃhitāpi| taina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā dantaibhidantamādhāya  jivhāya tāluṃ āhac‍ca caitasā cittaṃ abhiniggaṇhitabbaṃ abhinippīḷaitabbaṃ abhisantāpaitabbaṃ| tassa dantaibhidantamādhāya jivhāya tāluṃ āhac‍ca caitasā cittaṃ abhiniggaṇhatau abhinippīḷayatau abhisantāpayatau yai pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā chandūpasaṃhitāpi dausūpasaṃhitāpi  mauhūpasaṃhitāpi  tai pahīyanti tai abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| taisaṃ pahānā ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti samādhiyati|

221. ‘‘yatau khau yatau ca khau (syā॰ ka॰), bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau yaṃ nimittaṃ āgamma yaṃ nimittaṃ manasikarautau uppaj‍janti pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā chandūpasaṃhitāpi dausūpasaṃhitāpi mauhūpasaṃhitāpi, tassa tamhā nimittā añ‍ñaṃ nimittaṃ manasikarautau kusalūpasaṃhitaṃ yai pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā chandūpasaṃhitāpi dausūpasaṃhitāpi mauhūpasaṃhitāpi tai pahīyanti tai abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| taisaṃ pahānā ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti samādhiyati| taisampi vitak‍kānaṃ ādīnavaṃ upaparikkhatau yai pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā chandūpasaṃhitāpi dausūpasaṃhitāpi mauhūpasaṃhitāpi tai pahīyanti tai abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| taisaṃ pahānā ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti samādhiyati| taisampi vitak‍kānaṃ asatiamanasikāraṃ āpaj‍jatau yai pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā chandūpasaṃhitāpi dausūpasaṃhitāpi mauhūpasaṃhitāpi tai pahīyanti tai abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| taisaṃ pahānā ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti samādhiyati| taisampi vitak‍kānaṃ vitak‍kasaṅkhārasaṇṭhānaṃ manasikarautau yai pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā chandūpasaṃhitāpi dausūpasaṃhitāpi mauhūpasaṃhitāpi  tai pahīyanti tai abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| taisaṃ pahānā ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti samādhiyati| dantaibhidantamādhāya jivhāya tāluṃ āhac‍ca caitasā cittaṃ abhiniggaṇhatau abhinippīḷayatau abhisantāpayatau yai pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā chandūpasaṃhitāpi dausūpasaṃhitāpi mauhūpasaṃhitāpi tai pahīyanti tai abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| taisaṃ pahānā ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ santiṭṭhati san‍nisīdati aikaudi hauti  samādhiyati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vasī vitak‍kapariyāyapathaisu| yaṃ vitak‍kaṃ ākaṅkhissati taṃ vitak‍kaṃ vitak‍kaissati, yaṃ vitak‍kaṃ nākaṅkhissati na taṃ vitak‍kaṃ vitak‍kaissati| acchaicchi taṇhaṃ, vivattayi vāvattayi (sī॰ pī॰) saṃyaujanaṃ, sammā mānābhisamayā antamakāsi dukkhassā’’ti|

idamavauca  bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

vitak‍kasaṇṭhānasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|

sīhanādavaggau niṭṭhitau dutiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

cūḷasīhanādalaumahaṃsavarau, mahācūḷadukkhakkhandhaanumānikasuttaṃ|

khilapatthamadhupiṇḍikadvidhāvitak‍ka, pañ‍canimittakathā puna vaggau||


3. aupammavaggau

1. kakacūpamasuttaṃ

222. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā mauḷiyaphaggunau bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ ativailaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau viharati| aivaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau āyasmā mauḷiyaphaggunau bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ viharati – sacai kauci bhikkhu āyasmatau mauḷiyaphaggunassa sammukhā tāsaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, taināyasmā mauḷiyaphaggunau kupitau anattamanau adhikaraṇampi karauti| sacai pana kauci bhikkhu tāsaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ sammukhā āyasmatau mauḷiyaphaggunassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, taina tā bhikkhuniyau kupitā anattamanā adhikaraṇampi karaunti| aivaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau āyasmā mauḷiyaphaggunau bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ viharati| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āyasmā, bhantai, mauḷiyaphaggunau bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ ativailaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau viharati| aivaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau, bhantai, āyasmā mauḷiyaphaggunau bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ viharati – sacai kauci bhikkhu āyasmatau mauḷiyaphaggunassa sammukhā tāsaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, taināyasmā mauḷiyaphaggunau kupitau anattamanau  adhikaraṇampi karauti| sacai pana kauci bhikkhu tāsaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ sammukhā āyasmatau mauḷiyaphaggunassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, taina tā bhikkhuniyau kupitā anattamanā adhikaraṇampi karaunti| aivaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau, bhantai, āyasmā mauḷiyaphaggunau bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ viharatī’’ti|

223. atha khau bhagavā añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mama vacanaina mauḷiyaphaggunaṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaihi – ‘satthā taṃ, āvusau phagguna, āmantaitī’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau paṭissutvā yaināyasmā mauḷiyaphaggunau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mauḷiyaphaggunaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘satthā taṃ, āvusau phagguna, āmantaitī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā mauḷiyaphaggunau tassa  bhikkhunau paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ mauḷiyaphaggunaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, phagguna, bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ ativailaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau viharasi? aivaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau kira tvaṃ, phagguna, bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ viharasi – sacai kauci bhikkhu tuyhaṃ sammukhā tāsaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, taina tvaṃ kupitau anattamanau adhikaraṇampi karausi| sacai pana kauci bhikkhu tāsaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ sammukhā tuyhaṃ avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, taina tā bhikkhuniyau kupitā anattamanā adhikaraṇampi karaunti| aivaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau kira tvaṃ, phagguna, bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ viharasī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘nanu tvaṃ, phagguna, kulaputtau saddhā  agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti|

224. ‘‘na khau tai aitaṃ, phagguna, patirūpaṃ kulaputtassa saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitassa, yaṃ tvaṃ bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ ativailaṃ saṃsaṭṭhau viharaiyyāsi| tasmātiha, phagguna, tava caipi kauci sammukhā tāsaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ avaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyya, tatrāpi tvaṃ, phagguna, yai gaihasitā gaihassitā (?) chandā yai gaihasitā vitak‍kā tai pajahaiyyāsi| tatrāpi tai, phagguna, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na caiva mai cittaṃ vipariṇataṃ bhavissati, na ca pāpikaṃ vācaṃ nicchāraissāmi, hitānukampī ca viharissāmi maittacittau, na dausantarau’ti| aivañhi tai, phagguna, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘tasmātiha, phagguna, tava caipi kauci sammukhā tāsaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ pāṇinā pahāraṃ dadaiyya, laiḍḍunā pahāraṃ dadaiyya, daṇḍaina pahāraṃ dadaiyya, satthaina pahāraṃ dadaiyya| tatrāpi tvaṃ, phagguna, yai gaihasitā chandā yai gaihasitā vitak‍kā tai pajahaiyyāsi| tatrāpi tai, phagguna, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ ‘na caiva mai cittaṃ vipariṇataṃ bhavissati, na ca pāpikaṃ vācaṃ nicchāraissāmi, hitānukampī ca viharissāmi maittacittau, na dausantarau’ti| aivañhi tai, phagguna, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘tasmātiha, phagguna, tava caipi kauci sammukhā avaṇṇaṃ bhāsaiyya, tatrāpi tvaṃ, phagguna, yai gaihasitā chandā yai gaihasitā vitak‍kā tai  pajahaiyyāsi| tatrāpi tai, phagguna, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ ‘na caiva mai cittaṃ vipariṇataṃ bhavissati, na ca pāpikaṃ vācaṃ nicchāraissāmi, hitānukampī ca viharissāmi maittacittau, na dausantarau’ti| aivañhi  tai, phagguna, sikkhitabbaṃ|

‘‘tasmātiha, phagguna, tava caipi kauci pāṇinā pahāraṃ dadaiyya, laiḍḍunā pahāraṃ dadaiyya, daṇḍaina pahāraṃ dadaiyya, satthaina pahāraṃ  dadaiyya, tatrāpi tvaṃ, phagguna, yai gaihasitā chandā yai gaihasitā vitak‍kā tai pajahaiyyāsi| tatrāpi tai, phagguna, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ ‘na caiva mai cittaṃ vipariṇataṃ bhavissati, na ca pāpikaṃ vācaṃ nicchāraissāmi, hitānukampī ca viharissāmi maittacittau, na dausantarau’ti| aivañhi tai, phagguna, sikkhitabba’’nti|

225. atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘ārādhayiṃsu vata mai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū aikaṃ samayaṃ cittaṃ| idhāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū āmantaisiṃ – ahaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, aikāsanabhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jāmi| aikāsanabhaujanaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhuñ‍jamānau appābādhatañ‍ca sañ‍jānāmi appātaṅkatañ‍ca lahuṭṭhānañ‍ca balañ‍ca phāsuvihārañ‍ca| aitha tumhaipi, bhikkhavai, aikāsanabhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jatha| aikāsanabhaujanaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tumhaipi bhuñ‍jamānā appābādhatañ‍ca sañ‍jānissatha appātaṅkatañ‍ca lahuṭṭhānañ‍ca balañ‍ca phāsuvihārañ‍cāti| na mai, bhikkhavai, taisu bhikkhūsu anusāsanī karaṇīyā ahausi; satuppādakaraṇīyamaiva mai, bhikkhavai, taisu bhikkhūsu ahausi|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, subhūmiyaṃ catumahāpathai ājañ‍ñarathau yuttau assa ṭhitau audhastapataudau| tamainaṃ dakkhau yauggācariyau assadammasārathi abhiruhitvā, vāmaina hatthaina rasmiyau gahaitvā, dakkhiṇaina hatthaina pataudaṃ gahaitvā, yainicchakaṃ yadicchakaṃ  sāraiyyapi pac‍cāsāraiyyapi| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, na mai taisu bhikkhūsu anusāsanī karaṇīyā ahausi, satuppādakaraṇīyamaiva mai, bhikkhavai, taisu bhikkhūsu ahausi| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, tumhaipi akusalaṃ pajahatha, kusalaisu dhammaisu āyaugaṃ karautha| aivañhi tumhaipi imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatha|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gāmassa vā nigamassa vā avidūrai mahantaṃ sālavanaṃ| tañ‍cassa aiḷaṇḍaihi sañchan‍naṃ| tassa kaucidaiva purisau uppaj‍jaiyya atthakāmau hitakāmau yaugakkhaimakāmau| sau yā tā sālalaṭṭhiyau kuṭilā aujāpaharaṇiyau aujaharaṇiyau (ka॰) tā chaitvā tacchaitvā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) bahiddhā nīharaiyya, antauvanaṃ suvisaudhitaṃ visaudhaiyya| yā pana tā sālalaṭṭhiyau ujukā sujātā tā sammā pariharaiyya| aivañhaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, sālavanaṃ aparaina samayaina vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jaiyya| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, tumhaipi akusalaṃ pajahatha, kusalaisu dhammaisu āyaugaṃ karautha| aivañhi  tumhaipi imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jissatha|

226. ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, imissāyaiva sāvatthiyā vaidaihikā nāma gahapatānī ahausi| vaidaihikāya, bhikkhavai, gahapatāniyā aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘sauratā vaidaihikā gahapatānī, nivātā vaidaihikā gahapatānī, upasantā vaidaihikā gahapatānī’ti| vaidaihikāya khau pana, bhikkhavai, gahapatāniyā kāḷī nāma dāsī ahausi dakkhā analasā  susaṃvihitakammantā|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, kāḷiyā dāsiyā aitadahausi – ‘mayhaṃ khau ayyāya aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘‘sauratā vaidaihikā gahapatānī, nivātā vaidaihikā gahapatānī, upasantā vaidaihikā gahapatānī’’ti| kiṃ nu khau mai ayyā santaṃyaiva nu khau ajjhattaṃ kaupaṃ na pātukarauti udāhu asantaṃ udāhu mayhamaivaitai mayhaivaitai (sī॰ pī॰) kammantā susaṃvihitā yaina mai ayyā santaṃyaiva ajjhattaṃ kaupaṃ na pātukarauti, nau asantaṃ? yaṃnūnāhaṃ ayyaṃ vīmaṃsaiyya’nti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, kāḷī dāsī divā uṭṭhāsi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, vaidaihikā gahapatānī kāḷiṃ dāsiṃ aitadavauca – ‘hai jai kāḷī’ti| ‘kiṃ, ayyai’ti? ‘kiṃ, jai, divā uṭṭhāsī’ti? ‘na khvayyai na khau ayyai (sī॰ pī॰), kiñ‍cī’ti| ‘nau vata rai kiñ‍ci, pāpi dāsi pāpadāsi (syā॰ ka॰), divā uṭṭhāsī’ti kupitā anattamanā bhākuṭiṃ bhūkuṭiṃ (sī॰ pī॰), bhakuṭiṃ (syā॰) akāsi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, kāḷiyā dāsiyā  aitadahausi – ‘santaṃyaiva khau mai ayyā ajjhattaṃ kaupaṃ na pātukarauti, nau asantaṃ; mayhamaivaitai kammantā susaṃvihitā, yaina mai ayyā santaṃyaiva ajjhattaṃ kaupaṃ na pātukarauti, nau asantaṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ bhiyyausaumattāya ayyaṃ vīmaṃsaiyya’’’nti|

‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, kāḷī dāsī divātaraṃyaiva uṭṭhāsi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, vaidaihikā gahapatānī kāḷiṃ dāsiṃ aitadavauca – ‘hai jai, kāḷī’ti| ‘kiṃ, ayyai’ti? ‘kiṃ, jai, divātaraṃ uṭṭhāsī’ti? ‘na khvayyai, kiñ‍cī’ti| ‘nau vata rai kiñ‍ci, pāpi dāsi, divātaraṃ  uṭṭhāsī’ti kupitā anattamanā anattamanavācaṃ nicchāraisi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, kāḷiyā dāsiyā aitadahausi – ‘santaṃyaiva khau mai ayyā ajjhattaṃ kaupaṃ na pātukarauti, nau asantaṃ| mayhamaivaitai kammantā susaṃvihitā, yaina mai ayyā santaṃyaiva ajjhattaṃ kaupaṃ na pātukarauti, nau asantaṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ bhiyyausaumattāya ayyaṃ vīmaṃsaiyya’nti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, kāḷī dāsī divātaraṃyaiva uṭṭhāsi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, vaidaihikā gahapatānī kāḷiṃ dāsiṃ aitadavauca  – ‘hai jai, kāḷī’ti| ‘kiṃ, ayyai’ti? ‘kiṃ, jai, divā uṭṭhāsī’ti? ‘na khvayyai, kiñ‍cī’ti| ‘nau vata rai kiñ‍ci, pāpi dāsi, divā uṭṭhāsī’ti kupitā anattamanā aggaḷasūciṃ gahaitvā sīsai pahāraṃ adāsi, sīsaṃ vaubhindi vi + ava + bhindi = vaubhindi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, kāḷī dāsī bhin‍naina sīsaina lauhitaina galantaina paṭivissakānaṃ ujjhāpaisi – ‘passathayyai, sauratāya kammaṃ; passathayyai, nivātāya kammaṃ, passathayyai, upasantāya kammaṃ! kathañhi nāma aikadāsikāya divā uṭṭhāsīti kupitā anattamanā aggaḷasūciṃ gahaitvā sīsai pahāraṃ dassati, sīsaṃ vaubhindissatī’ti|

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, vaidaihikāya gahapatāniyā aparaina samayaina aivaṃ pāpakau kittisaddau abbhuggacchi – ‘caṇḍī vaidaihikā gahapatānī, anivātā vaidaihikā gahapatānī, anupasantā vaidaihikā gahapatānī’ti|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau bhikkhu tāvadaiva sauratasauratau hauti nivātanivātau  hauti upasantūpasantau hauti yāva na amanāpā vacanapathā phusanti| yatau ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuṃ  amanāpā vacanapathā phusanti, atha bhikkhu ‘sauratau’ti vaiditabbau, ‘nivātau’ti vaiditabbau, ‘upasantau’ti vaiditabbau| nāhaṃ taṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuṃ ‘suvacau’ti vadāmi yau cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārahaitu suvacau hauti, sauvacassataṃ āpaj‍jati| taṃ kissa haitu? tañhi sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ  alabhamānau na suvacau hauti, na sauvacassataṃ āpaj‍jati| yau ca khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammaṃyaiva sak‍karauntau, dhammaṃ garuṃ karauntau, dhammaṃ mānaintau, dhammaṃ pūjaintau, dhammaṃ apacāyamānau dhammaṃ yaiva sak‍karauntau dhammaṃ garukarauntau dhammaṃ apacāyamānau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) suvacau hauti, sauvacassataṃ āpaj‍jati, tamahaṃ ‘suvacau’ti vadāmi| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, ‘dhammaṃyaiva sak‍karauntā, dhammaṃ garuṃ karauntā, dhammaṃ mānaintā, dhammaṃ pūjaintā, dhammaṃ apacāyamānā suvacā bhavissāma, sauvacassataṃ āpaj‍jissāmā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ|

227. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, vacanapathā yaihi vau parai vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ – kālaina vā akālaina vā; bhūtaina vā abhūtaina vā; saṇhaina vā pharusaina vā; atthasaṃhitaina vā anatthasaṃhitaina vā; maittacittā vā dausantarā vā| kālaina vā, bhikkhavai, parai vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ akālaina vā; bhūtaina vā, bhikkhavai, parai vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ abhūtaina vā; saṇhaina vā, bhikkhavai, parai vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ pharusaina vā; atthasaṃhitaina vā, bhikkhavai, parai vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ anatthasaṃhitaina  vā; maittacittā vā, bhikkhavai, parai vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ dausantarā vā| tatrāpi vau, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na caiva nau cittaṃ vipariṇataṃ bhavissati, na ca pāpikaṃ vācaṃ nicchāraissāma, hitānukampī ca viharissāma maittacittā, na dausantarā| tañ‍ca puggalaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā pharitvā viharissāma, tadārammaṇañ‍ca sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina cittaina vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyābajjhaina abyāpajjhaina (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), abyāpaj‍jaina (ka॰) aṅguttaratikanipātaṭīkā aulaukaitabbā pharitvā viharissāmā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ|

228. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau āgacchaiyya kudālapiṭakaṃ kuddālapiṭakaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) ādāya| sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ imaṃ mahāpathaviṃ apathaviṃ karissāmī’ti| sau tatra tatra vikhaṇaiyya khaṇaiyya (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), tatra tatra vikiraiyya, tatra tatra auṭṭhubhaiyya, tatra tatra aumuttaiyya – ‘apathavī bhavasi, apathavī bhavasī’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu sau purisau imaṃ mahāpathaviṃ apathaviṃ karaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘ayañhi, bhantai, mahāpathavī gambhīrā appamaiyyā| sā na sukarā apathavī kātuṃ; yāvadaiva ca pana sau purisau kilamathassa vighātassa bhāgī assā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cimai vacanapathā yaihi vau parai vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ – kālaina vā akālaina vā; bhūtaina vā abhūtaina vā; saṇhaina vā pharusaina vā; atthasaṃhitaina vā anatthasaṃhitaina vā; maittacittā vā dausantarā vā| kālaina vā, bhikkhavai, parai vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ akālaina  vā; bhūtaina vā bhikkhavai, parai vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ abhūtaina vā; saṇhaina vā, bhikkhavai, parai vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ pharusaina vā; atthasaṃhitaina vā, bhikkhavai, parai vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ anatthasaṃhitaina vā; maittacittā vā, bhikkhavai, parai vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ dausantarā vā| tatrāpi vau, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na caiva nau cittaṃ vipariṇataṃ bhavissati, na ca pāpikaṃ vācaṃ nicchāraissāma, hitānukampī ca viharissāma maittacittā na dausantarā| tañ‍ca puggalaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā pharitvā viharissāma, tadārammaṇañ‍ca sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ pathavisamaina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyābajjhaina pharitvā viharissāmā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ|

229. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau āgacchaiyya lākhaṃ vā haliddiṃ vā nīlaṃ vā mañ‍jiṭṭhaṃ vā ādāya| sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ imasmiṃ ākāsai rūpaṃ likhissāmi, rūpapātubhāvaṃ karissāmī’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu sau purisau imasmiṃ ākāsai rūpaṃ likhaiyya, rūpapātubhāvaṃ karaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘ayañhi, bhantai, ākāsau arūpī anidassanau| tattha na sukaraṃ rūpaṃ likhituṃ, rūpapātubhāvaṃ kātuṃ; yāvadaiva ca pana  sau purisau kilamathassa vighātassa bhāgī assā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cimai vacanapathā yaihi vau parai vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ kālaina vā akālaina vā  …pai॰… ‘na caiva… tadārammaṇañ‍ca  sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ ākāsasamaina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyābajjhaina pharitvā viharissāmā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ|

230. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau āgacchaiyya ādittaṃ tiṇuk‍kaṃ ādāya| sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ imāya ādittāya tiṇuk‍kāya gaṅgaṃ nadiṃ santāpaissāmi saṃparitāpaissāmī’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu sau purisau ādittāya tiṇuk‍kāya gaṅgaṃ nadiṃ santāpaiyya saṃparitāpaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘gaṅgā hi, bhantai, nadī gambhīrā appamaiyyā| sā na sukarā ādittāya tiṇuk‍kāya santāpaituṃ saṃparitāpaituṃ; yāvadaiva ca pana sau purisau kilamathassa vighātassa bhāgī assā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cimai vacanapathā yaihi vau parai vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ kālaina vā akālaina  vā…pai॰… ‘na caiva… tadārammaṇañ‍ca sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ gaṅgāsamaina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyābajjhaina pharitvā viharissāmā’’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ|

231. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, biḷārabhastā madditā sumadditā suparimadditā, mudukā tūlinī chin‍nasassarā chin‍nabhabbharā| atha purisau āgacchaiyya kaṭṭhaṃ vā kathalaṃ kaṭhalaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) vā ādāya| sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ imaṃ biḷārabhastaṃ madditaṃ sumadditaṃ suparimadditaṃ, mudukaṃ tūliniṃ, chin‍nasassaraṃ chin‍nabhabbharaṃ kaṭṭhaina vā kathalaina vā sarasaraṃ karissāmi bharabharaṃ karissāmī’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu sau purisau amuṃ biḷārabhastaṃ madditaṃ sumadditaṃ suparimadditaṃ, mudukaṃ tūliniṃ, chin‍nasassaraṃ chin‍nabhabbharaṃ kaṭṭhaina vā kathalaina vā sarasaraṃ karaiyya, bharabharaṃ karaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘amu hi, bhantai, biḷārabhastā madditā sumadditā suparimadditā, mudukā tūlinī, chin‍nasassarā chin‍nabhabbharā| sā na sukarā kaṭṭhaina vā kathalaina vā sarasaraṃ kātuṃ bharabharaṃ kātuṃ; yāvadaiva ca pana sau purisau kilamathassa vighātassa bhāgī assā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cimai vacanapathā yaihi vau parai vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ kālaina vā  akālaina vā; bhūtaina vā abhūtaina vā; saṇhaina  vā pharusaina vā; atthasaṃhitaina vā anatthasaṃhitaina vā; maittacittā vā dausantarā vā| kālaina vā bhikkhavai parai vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ akālaina vā; bhūtaina vā, bhikkhavai, parai vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ abhūtaina vā; saṇhaina vā, bhikkhavai, parai vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ pharusaina vā; atthasaṃhitaina vā, bhikkhavai, parai vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ anatthasaṃhitaina vā; maittacittā vā, bhikkhavai, parai vadamānā vadaiyyuṃ dausantarā vā| tatrāpi vau, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na caiva nau cittaṃ vipariṇataṃ bhavissati, na ca pāpikaṃ vācaṃ nicchāraissāma hitānukampī ca viharissāma maittacittā na dausantarā| tañ‍ca puggalaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā pharitvā viharissāma, tadārammaṇañ‍ca sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ biḷārabhastāsamaina caitasā  vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyābajjhaina pharitvā viharissāmā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ|

232. ‘‘ubhataudaṇḍakaina caipi, bhikkhavai, kakacaina caurā aucarakā aṅgamaṅgāni aukantaiyyuṃ, tatrāpi yau manau padūsaiyya, na mai sau taina sāsanakarau| tatrāpi vau, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘na caiva nau cittaṃ vipariṇataṃ bhavissati, na ca pāpikaṃ vācaṃ nicchāraissāma, hitānukampī ca viharissāma maittacittā na dausantarā| tañ‍ca puggalaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā pharitvā viharissāma tadārammaṇañ‍ca sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyābajjhaina pharitvā viharissāmā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ|

233. ‘‘imañ‍ca imañ‍cai (?) tumhai, bhikkhavai, kakacūpamaṃ auvādaṃ abhikkhaṇaṃ manasi karaiyyātha| passatha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, taṃ vacanapathaṃ, aṇuṃ vā thūlaṃ vā, yaṃ tumhai nādhivāsaiyyāthā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, imaṃ kakacūpamaṃ auvādaṃ abhikkhaṇaṃ manasikarautha| taṃ vau bhavissati dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

kakacūpamasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. alagaddūpamasuttaṃ

234. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina ariṭṭhassa nāma bhikkhunau gaddhabādhipubbassa gandhabādhipubbassa (ka॰) aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti – ‘‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’ti| assausuṃ khau sambahulā bhikkhū – ‘‘ariṭṭhassa kira nāma bhikkhunau gaddhabādhipubbassa aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – ‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’’ti| atha khau tai bhikkhū yaina ariṭṭhau bhikkhu gaddhabādhipubbau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā ariṭṭhaṃ bhikkhuṃ gaddhabādhipubbaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tai, āvusau ariṭṭha, aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – ‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃbyākhau aivaṃ khau (?) bhagavatau sammukhāyaivassa ‘‘aivaṃbyākhau’’ti ahaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’ti|

atha khau taipi bhikkhū ariṭṭhaṃ bhikkhuṃ gaddhabādhipubbaṃ aitasmā  pāpakā diṭṭhigatā vivaicaitukāmā samanuyuñ‍janti samanugāhanti samanuggāhanti (syā॰) samanubhāsanti – ‘‘mā haivaṃ, āvusau ariṭṭha, avaca, mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi; na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ abbhācikkhanaṃ (ka॰), na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya| anaikapariyāyaināvusau ariṭṭha, antarāyikā dhammā antarāyikā vuttā bhagavatā, alañ‍ca pana tai paṭisaivatau antarāyāya| appassādā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā,ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| aṭṭhikaṅkalūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā…pai॰… maṃsapaisūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā… tiṇuk‍kūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā… aṅgārakāsūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā… supinakūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā… yācitakūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā… rukkhaphalūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā… asisūnūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā… sattisūlūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā… sappasirūpamā kāmā vuttā  bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau’’ti| aivampi khau ariṭṭhau bhikkhu gaddhabādhipubbau  taihi bhikkhūhi samanuyuñ‍jiyamānau samanugāhiyamānau samanuggāhiyamānau (syā॰ vinayaipi) samanubhāsiyamānau tadaiva tathaiva taṃ (vinayai) pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ thāmasā parāmāsā abhinivissa vauharati – ‘‘aivaṃbyākhau ahaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’ti|

235. yatau khau tai bhikkhū nāsakkhiṃsu ariṭṭhaṃ bhikkhuṃ gaddhabādhipubbaṃ  aitasmā pāpakā diṭṭhigatā vivaicaituṃ, atha khau tai bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘ariṭṭhassa nāma, bhantai, bhikkhunau gaddhabādhipubbassa aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – ‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’ti| assumha khau mayaṃ, bhantai – ‘ariṭṭhassa kira nāma bhikkhunau gaddhabādhipubbassa aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’ti| atha khau mayaṃ, bhantai, yaina ariṭṭhau bhikkhu gaddhabādhipubbau tainupasaṅkamimha; upasaṅkamitvā ariṭṭhaṃ bhikkhuṃ gaddhabādhipubbaṃ aitadavaucumha – ‘sac‍caṃ kira tai, āvusau ariṭṭha, aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ vuttai, bhantai, ariṭṭhau bhikkhu gaddhabādhipubbau amhai aitadavauca – ‘aivaṃbyākhau ahaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’ti| atha khau mayaṃ, bhantai, ariṭṭhaṃ bhikkhuṃ gaddhabādhipubbaṃ aitasmā pāpakā diṭṭhigatā vivaicaitukāmā samanuyuñ‍jimha samanugāhimha samanubhāsimha  – ‘mā haivaṃ, āvusau ariṭṭha, avaca, mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi; na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya| anaikapariyāyaināvusau ariṭṭha, antarāyikā dhammā antarāyikā vuttā bhagavatā, alañ‍ca pana tai paṭisaivatau antarāyāya| appassādā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| aṭṭhikaṅkalūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā…pai॰… sappasirūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau’ti| aivampi khau, bhantai, ariṭṭhau bhikkhu gaddhabādhipubbau amhaihi samanuyuñ‍jiyamānau samanugāhiyamānau  samanubhāsiyamānau tadaiva pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ thāmasā parāmāsā abhinivissa vauharati – ‘aivaṃbyākhau ahaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’ti| yatau khau mayaṃ, bhantai, nāsakkhimha ariṭṭhaṃ bhikkhuṃ gaddhabādhipubbaṃ aitasmā pāpakā diṭṭhigatā vivaicaituṃ, atha mayaṃ aitamatthaṃ bhagavatau āraucaimā’’ti|

236. atha khau bhagavā añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mama vacanaina ariṭṭhaṃ bhikkhuṃ gaddhabādhipubbaṃ āmantaihi – ‘satthā taṃ, āvusau ariṭṭha, āmantaitī’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau paṭissutvā, yaina ariṭṭhau bhikkhu gaddhabādhipubbau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā ariṭṭhaṃ bhikkhuṃ gaddhabādhipubbaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘satthā  taṃ, āvusau ariṭṭha, āmantaitī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau ariṭṭhau bhikkhu gaddhabādhipubbau tassa bhikkhunau paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau ariṭṭhaṃ bhikkhuṃ gaddhabādhipubbaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tai, ariṭṭha, aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – ‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃbyākhau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi – ‘yathā yaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’’ti| ‘‘kassa khau nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, mayā aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāsi? nanu mayā, maughapurisa, anaikapariyāyaina antarāyikā dhammā antarāyikā vuttā? alañ‍ca pana tai paṭisaivatau antarāyāya| appassādā kāmā vuttā mayā, bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| aṭṭhikaṅkalūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā… maṃsapaisūpamā  kāmā vuttā mayā… tiṇuk‍kūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā… aṅgārakāsūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā… supinakūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā… yācitakūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā… rukkhaphalūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā… asisūnūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā… sattisūlūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā… sappasirūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā, bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| atha ca pana tvaṃ, maughapurisa, attanā duggahitaina  amhai caiva abbhācikkhasi, attānañ‍ca khanasi, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavasi| tañhi tai, maughapurisa, bhavissati dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāyā’’ti|

atha  khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nāyaṃ ariṭṭhau bhikkhu gaddhabādhipubbau usmīkataupi imasmiṃ dhammavinayai’’ti? ‘‘kiñhi kiṃti (ka॰) siyā, bhantai; nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, ariṭṭhau bhikkhu gaddhabādhipubbau tuṇhībhūtau maṅkubhūtau pattakkhandhau adhaumukhau pajjhāyantau appaṭibhānau nisīdi| atha khau bhagavā ariṭṭhaṃ bhikkhuṃ gaddhabādhipubbaṃ tuṇhībhūtaṃ maṅkubhūtaṃ pattakkhandhaṃ adhaumukhaṃ pajjhāyantaṃ appaṭibhānaṃ viditvā ariṭṭhaṃ bhikkhuṃ gaddhabādhipubbaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pañ‍ñāyissasi khau tvaṃ, maughapurisa, aitaina sakaina pāpakaina diṭṭhigataina| idhāhaṃ bhikkhū paṭipucchissāmī’’ti|

237. atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘tumhaipi mai, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānātha yathāyaṃ ariṭṭhau bhikkhu gaddhabādhipubbau attanā duggahitaina amhai caiva abbhācikkhati, attānañ‍ca khanati, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavatī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai| anaikapariyāyaina hi nau, bhantai, antarāyikā dhammā antarāyikā vuttā bhagavatā; alañ‍ca pana tai paṭisaivatau antarāyāya| appassādā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| aṭṭhikaṅkalūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā…pai॰… sappasirūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau’’ti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhavai, sādhu, khau mai tumhai, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānātha| anaikapariyāyaina hi khau, bhikkhavai, antarāyikā dhammā vuttā mayā, alañ‍ca pana tai paṭisaivatau antarāyāya| appassādā kāmā vuttā mayā, bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| aṭṭhikaṅkalūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā…pai॰… sappasirūpamā kāmā vuttā mayā, bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| atha ca panāyaṃ ariṭṭhau bhikkhu gaddhabādhipubbau attanā duggahitaina amhai caiva abbhācikkhati, attānañ‍ca khanati, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| tañhi tassa maughapurisassa bhavissati dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya| sau vata, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatraiva kāmaihi añ‍ñatra kāmasañ‍ñāya añ‍ñatra kāmavitak‍kaihi kāmai paṭisaivissatīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati’’|

238. ‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cai maughapurisā dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇanti – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthaṃ, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍laṃ| tai taṃ dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇitvā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ pañ‍ñāya atthaṃ na upaparikkhanti| taisaṃ tai dhammā pañ‍ñāya atthaṃ anupaparikkhataṃ  na nijjhānaṃ khamanti| tai upārambhānisaṃsā caiva dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇanti itivādappamaukkhānisaṃsā ca| yassa catthāya dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇanti tañ‍cassa atthaṃ  nānubhaunti| taisaṃ tai dhammā duggahitā dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya saṃvattanti| taṃ kissa haitu? duggahitattā, bhikkhavai, dhammānaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau alagaddatthikau alagaddagavaisī alagaddapariyaisanaṃ caramānau| sau passaiyya mahantaṃ alagaddaṃ| tamainaṃ bhaugai vā naṅguṭṭhai vā gaṇhaiyya| tassa sau alagaddau paṭiparivattitvā paṭinivattitvā (syā॰ ka॰) hatthai vā bāhāya vā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vā aṅgapac‍caṅgai ḍaṃsaiyya ḍasaiyya (sī॰ pī॰)| sau tataunidānaṃ  maraṇaṃ vā nigacchaiyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? duggahitattā, bhikkhavai, alagaddassa| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cai maughapurisā dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇanti – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthaṃ, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍laṃ| tai taṃ dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇitvā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ pañ‍ñāya atthaṃ na upaparikkhanti| taisaṃ tai dhammā pañ‍ñāya atthaṃ anupaparikkhataṃ na nijjhānaṃ khamanti| tai upārambhānisaṃsā caiva dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇanti itivādappamaukkhānisaṃsā ca| yassa catthāya dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇanti tañ‍cassa atthaṃ nānubhaunti| taisaṃ tai dhammā duggahitā dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya saṃvattanti| taṃ kissa haitu? duggahitattā bhikkhavai dhammānaṃ|

239. ‘‘idha  pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cai kulaputtā dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇanti – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthaṃ, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍laṃ| tai taṃ dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇitvā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ pañ‍ñāya atthaṃ upaparikkhanti| taisaṃ tai dhammā pañ‍ñāya atthaṃ upaparikkhataṃ nijjhānaṃ  khamanti| tai na caiva upārambhānisaṃsā dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇanti na itivādappamaukkhānisaṃsā ca na ca itivādappamaukkhānisaṃsā (?)| yassa catthāya dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇanti tañ‍cassa atthaṃ anubhaunti| taisaṃ tai dhammā suggahitā dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya saṃvattanti| taṃ kissa haitu? suggahitattā bhikkhavai dhammānaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau alagaddatthikau alagaddagavaisī alagaddapariyaisanaṃ caramānau| sau passaiyya mahantaṃ alagaddaṃ| tamainaṃ ajapadaina daṇḍaina suniggahitaṃ niggaṇhaiyya| ajapadaina daṇḍaina suniggahitaṃ niggahitvā, gīvāya suggahitaṃ gaṇhaiyya| kiñ‍cāpi sau, bhikkhavai, alagaddau tassa purisassa hatthaṃ vā bāhaṃ vā añ‍ñataraṃ vā aṅgapac‍caṅgaṃ bhaugaihi palivaiṭhaiyya, atha khau sau naiva tataunidānaṃ maraṇaṃ vā nigacchaiyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? suggahitattā, bhikkhavai, alagaddassa| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cai kulaputtā dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇanti – suttaṃ, gaiyyaṃ, vaiyyākaraṇaṃ, gāthaṃ, udānaṃ, itivuttakaṃ, jātakaṃ, abbhutadhammaṃ, vaidal‍laṃ| tai taṃ dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇitvā taisaṃ dhammānaṃ pañ‍ñāya atthaṃ upaparikkhanti| taisaṃ tai dhammā pañ‍ñāya atthaṃ upaparikkhataṃ nijjhānaṃ khamanti| tai na caiva upārambhānisaṃsā dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇanti, na itivādappamaukkhānisaṃsā ca| yassa catthāya dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇanti, tañ‍cassa atthaṃ anubhaunti| taisaṃ tai dhammā suggahitā dīgharattaṃ atthāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattanti| taṃ kissa haitu? suggahitattā, bhikkhavai, dhammānaṃ| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, yassa  mai bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānaiyyātha, tathā naṃ dhāraiyyātha| yassa ca pana mai bhāsitassa atthaṃ na ājānaiyyātha, ahaṃ vau tattha paṭipucchitabbau, yai vā panāssu viyattā bhikkhū|

240. ‘‘kul‍lūpamaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisaissāmi nittharaṇatthāya, nau gahaṇatthāya| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasikarautha, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau| sau passaiyya mahantaṃ udakaṇṇavaṃ, aurimaṃ tīraṃ sāsaṅkaṃ sappaṭibhayaṃ, pārimaṃ tīraṃ khaimaṃ appaṭibhayaṃ; na cassa nāvā santāraṇī uttarasaitu vā apārā pāraṃ gamanāya| tassa aivamassa  – ‘ayaṃ khau mahāudakaṇṇavau, aurimaṃ tīraṃ sāsaṅkaṃ sappaṭibhayaṃ, pārimaṃ tīraṃ khaimaṃ appaṭibhayaṃ; natthi ca nāvā santāraṇī uttarasaitu vā apārā pāraṃ gamanāya| yaṃnūnāhaṃ tiṇakaṭṭhasākhāpalāsaṃ saṃkaḍḍhitvā, kul‍laṃ bandhitvā, taṃ kul‍laṃ nissāya hatthaihi ca pādaihi ca vāyamamānau sautthinā pāraṃ uttaraiyya’nti| atha khau sau, bhikkhavai, purisau tiṇakaṭṭhasākhāpalāsaṃ saṃkaḍḍhitvā, kul‍laṃ bandhitvā taṃ kul‍laṃ nissāya hatthaihi ca pādaihi ca vāyamamānau sautthinā pāraṃ uttaraiyya| tassa purisassa uttiṇṇassa tiṇṇassa (pī॰ ka॰) pāraṅgatassa aivamassa – ‘bahukārau khau mai ayaṃ kul‍lau; imāhaṃ kul‍laṃ nissāya hatthaihi ca pādaihi ca vāyamamānau sautthinā pāraṃ uttiṇṇau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ imaṃ kul‍laṃ sīsai vā āraupaitvā khandhai  vā uc‍cāraitvā uc‍caupaitvā (ka॰) yaina kāmaṃ pak‍kamaiyya’nti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nu sau purisau aivaṃkārī tasmiṃ kul‍lai kic‍cakārī assā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘kathaṃkārī ca sau, bhikkhavai, purisau tasmiṃ kul‍lai kic‍cakārī assa? idha, bhikkhavai, tassa purisassa uttiṇṇassa pāraṅgatassa aivamassa – ‘bahukārau khau mai ayaṃ kul‍lau; imāhaṃ kul‍laṃ nissāya  hatthaihi ca pādaihi ca vāyamamānau sautthinā pāraṃ uttiṇṇau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ imaṃ kul‍laṃ thalai vā ussādaitvā ussāraitvā (ka॰) udakai vā aupilāpaitvā yaina kāmaṃ pak‍kamaiyya’nti| aivaṃkārī khau sau, bhikkhavai, purisau tasmiṃ kul‍lai kic‍cakārī assa| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, kul‍lūpamau mayā dhammau daisitau nittharaṇatthāya, nau gahaṇatthāya| kul‍lūpamaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, dhammaṃ daisitaṃ, ājānantaihi dhammāpi vau pahātabbā pagaiva adhammā|

241. ‘‘chayimāni, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhiṭṭhānāni| katamāni cha? idha, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau ariyānaṃ adassāvī ariyadhammassa akauvidau ariyadhammai avinītau, sappurisānaṃ adassāvī sappurisadhammassa akauvidau sappurisadhammai avinītau, rūpaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti  samanupassati; vaidanaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassati; sañ‍ñaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassati; saṅkhārai ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau  mai attā’ti samanupassati; yampi taṃ diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ, anuvicaritaṃ manasā tampi ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassati; yampi taṃ diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ – sau laukau sau attā, sau paic‍ca bhavissāmi nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau avipariṇāmadhammau, sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassāmīti – tampi ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassati| sutavā ca khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau ariyānaṃ dassāvī ariyadhammassa kauvidau ariyadhammai suvinītau, sappurisānaṃ dassāvī sappurisadhammassa kauvidau sappurisadhammai suvinītau, rūpaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati; vaidanaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati; sañ‍ñaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati; saṅkhārai ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati; yampi taṃ diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ, anuvicaritaṃ manasā, tampi ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati; yampi taṃ diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ – sau laukau sau attā, sau paic‍ca bhavissāmi nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau avipariṇāmadhammau, sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassāmīti – tampi ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati| sau aivaṃ samanupassantau asati na paritassatī’’ti|

242. aivaṃ vuttai, añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘siyā nu khau, bhantai, bahiddhā asati paritassanā’’ti? ‘‘siyā, bhikkhū’’ti – bhagavā  avauca| ‘‘idha bhikkhu aikac‍cassa  aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahu vata mai, taṃ vata mai natthi; siyā vata mai, taṃ vatāhaṃ na labhāmī’ti| sau saucati kilamati paridaivati urattāḷiṃ kandati sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, bahiddhā asati paritassanā hautī’’ti|

‘‘siyā pana, bhantai, bahiddhā asati aparitassanā’’ti? ‘‘siyā, bhikkhū’’ti – bhagavā avauca| ‘‘idha bhikkhu aikac‍cassa na aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahu vata mai, taṃ vata mai natthi; siyā vata mai, taṃ vatāhaṃ na labhāmī’ti| sau na saucati  na kilamati na paridaivati na urattāḷiṃ kandati na sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, bahiddhā asati aparitassanā hautī’’ti|

‘‘siyā nu khau, bhantai, ajjhattaṃ asati paritassanā’’ti? ‘‘siyā, bhikkhū’’ti – bhagavā avauca| ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, aikac‍cassa aivaṃ diṭṭhi hauti – ‘sau laukau sau attā, sau paic‍ca bhavissāmi nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau avipariṇāmadhammau, sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassāmī’ti| sau suṇāti tathāgatassa vā tathāgatasāvakassa vā sabbaisaṃ diṭṭhiṭṭhānādhiṭṭhānapariyuṭṭhānābhinivaisānusayānaṃ samugghātāya sabbasaṅkhārasamathāya sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggāya taṇhākkhayāya virāgāya niraudhāya nibbānāya dhammaṃ daisaintassa| tassa aivaṃ hauti  – ‘ucchij‍jissāmi nāmassu, vinassissāmi nāmassu, nassu nāma bhavissāmī’ti| sau saucati kilamati paridaivati urattāḷiṃ kandati sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, ajjhattaṃ asati paritassanā hautī’’ti|

‘‘siyā pana, bhantai, ajjhattaṃ asati aparitassanā’’ti? ‘‘siyā, bhikkhū’’ti  bhagavā avauca| ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, aikac‍cassa na aivaṃ diṭṭhi hauti – ‘sau laukau sau attā, sau paic‍ca bhavissāmi nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau avipariṇāmadhammau, sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassāmī’ti| sau suṇāti tathāgatassa vā tathāgatasāvakassa vā sabbaisaṃ diṭṭhiṭṭhānādhiṭṭhānapariyuṭṭhānābhinivaisānusayānaṃ samugghātāya sabbasaṅkhārasamathāya sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggāya taṇhākkhayāya virāgāya niraudhāya nibbānāya dhammaṃ daisaintassa| tassa na aivaṃ hauti – ‘ucchij‍jissāmi nāmassu, vinassissāmi nāmassu, nassu nāma bhavissāmī’ti| sau na saucati  na kilamati na paridaivati na urattāḷiṃ kandati na sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, ajjhattaṃ asati aparitassanā hauti’’|

243. ‘‘taṃ tañ‍ca (ka॰), bhikkhavai, pariggahaṃ pariggaṇhaiyyātha, yvāssa yvāssu (ka॰) pariggahau nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau avipariṇāmadhammau, sassatisamaṃ tathaiva tiṭṭhaiyya| passatha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, taṃ pariggahaṃ yvāssa pariggahau nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau avipariṇāmadhammau, sassatisamaṃ tathaiva tiṭṭhaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai| ahampi khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, pariggahaṃ na samanupassāmi  yvāssa pariggahau nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau avipariṇāmadhammau sassatisamaṃ tathaiva tiṭṭhaiyya|

‘‘taṃ, bhikkhavai, attavādupādānaṃ upādiyaitha, yaṃsa yassa (syā॰ ka॰) attavādupādānaṃ upādiyatau na uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā| passatha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, taṃ attavādupādānaṃ  yaṃsa attavādupādānaṃ upādiyatau na uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai| ahampi khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, attavādupādānaṃ na samanupassāmi yaṃsa attavādupādānaṃ upādiyatau na uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā|

‘‘taṃ, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhinissayaṃ nissayaitha yaṃsa diṭṭhinissayaṃ nissayatau na uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā| passatha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, taṃ diṭṭhinissayaṃ yaṃsa diṭṭhinissayaṃ nissayatau na uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai| ahampi khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhinissayaṃ na samanupassāmi yaṃsa diṭṭhinissayaṃ nissayatau  na uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā’’|

244. ‘‘attani vā, bhikkhavai, sati attaniyaṃ mai ti assā’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘attaniyai vā, bhikkhavai, sati attā mai ti assā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘attani  ca, bhikkhavai, attaniyai ca sac‍catau thaitatau anupalabbhamānai, yampi taṃ diṭṭhiṭṭhānaṃ – ‘sau laukau sau attā, sau paic‍ca bhavissāmi nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau avipariṇāmadhammau, sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassāmī’ti – nanāyaṃ na ca khauyaṃ (ka॰), bhikkhavai, kaivalau paripūrau bāladhammau’’’ti?

‘‘kiñhi nau siyā, bhantai, kaivalau hi, bhantai, paripūrau kaivalau paripūrau (sī॰ pī॰) bāladhammau’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ  vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti?

‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yaṃ  panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, yaṃ kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ, ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā, auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā, hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā, yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ rūpaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti – aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| yā kāci vaidanā…pai॰… yā kāci sañ‍ñā… yai kaici saṅkhārā… yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ, ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā, auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā, hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā, yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti – aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ’’|

245. ‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpasmiṃ nibbindati, vaidanāya nibbindati, sañ‍ñāya nibbindati, saṅkhāraisu nibbindati, viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ nibbindati, nibbidā viraj‍jati nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), virāgā vimuc‍cati, vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ukkhittapalighau itipi, saṃkiṇṇaparikkhau itipi, abbūḷhaisikau itipi, niraggaḷau itipi, ariyau pan‍naddhajau pan‍nabhārau visaṃyuttau itipi|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ukkhittapalighau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau avij‍jā pahīnā hauti, ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā, āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ukkhittapalighau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṃkiṇṇaparikkhau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau paunaubbhavikau  jātisaṃsārau pahīnau hauti, ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau, āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṃkiṇṇaparikkhau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu abbūḷhaisikau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau taṇhā pahīnā hauti, ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā, āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu abbūḷhaisikau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu niraggaḷau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pañ‍ca aurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni haunti, ucchin‍namūlāni tālāvatthukatāni anabhāvaṃkatāni, āyatiṃ anuppādadhammāni| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu niraggaḷau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyau pan‍naddhajau pan‍nabhārau visaṃyuttau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau asmimānau pahīnau hauti, ucchin‍namūlau tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau, āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyau pan‍naddhajau pan‍nabhārau visaṃyuttau hauti|

246. ‘‘aivaṃ vimuttacittaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuṃ saindā daivā sabrahmakā sapajāpatikā anvaisaṃ nādhigacchanti – ‘idaṃ nissitaṃ tathāgatassa viñ‍ñāṇa’nti| taṃ kissa haitu? diṭṭhaivāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammai tathāgataṃ ananuvij‍jauti vadāmi| aivaṃvādiṃ khau maṃ, bhikkhavai, aivamakkhāyiṃ aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā asatā tucchā musā abhūtaina abbhācikkhanti – ‘vainayikau samaṇau gautamau, satau sattassa ucchaidaṃ vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ pañ‍ñāpaitī’ti| yathā cāhaṃ na, bhikkhavai bhikkhavai na (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), yathā cāhaṃ na vadāmi, tathā maṃ tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā asatā tucchā musā abhūtaina abbhācikkhanti – ‘vainayikau samaṇau gautamau, satau sattassa ucchaidaṃ vināsaṃ vibhavaṃ pañ‍ñāpaitī’ti| pubbai cāhaṃ bhikkhavai, aitarahi ca dukkhañ‍caiva pañ‍ñāpaimi, dukkhassa ca niraudhaṃ| tatra cai, bhikkhavai, parai tathāgataṃ ak‍kausanti paribhāsanti rausainti vihaisainti, tatra, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa na hauti āghātau na appac‍cayau na caitasau anabhiraddhi|

‘‘tatra cai, bhikkhavai, parai tathāgataṃ sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjainti, tatra, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa na hauti ānandau na saumanassaṃ na caitasau uppilāvitattaṃ| tatra cai, bhikkhavai, parai  vā tathāgataṃ sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjainti, tatra, bhikkhavai, tathāgatassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yaṃ khau idaṃ pubbai pariñ‍ñātaṃ tattha mai aivarūpā kārā sak‍kārā (ka॰) karīyantī’ti| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, tumhai caipi parai ak‍kausaiyyuṃ paribhāsaiyyuṃ rausaiyyuṃ vihaisaiyyuṃ, tatra tumhai hi na āghātau na appac‍cayau na caitasau anabhiraddhi karaṇīyā| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, tumhai caipi parai sak‍karaiyyuṃ garuṃ karaiyyuṃ mānaiyyuṃ pūjaiyyuṃ, tatra tumhaihi na ānandau na saumanassaṃ na caitasau uppilāvitattaṃ karaṇīyaṃ| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, tumhai caipi parai sak‍karaiyyuṃ garuṃ  karaiyyuṃ mānaiyyuṃ pūjaiyyuṃ, tatra tumhākaṃ aivamassa – ‘yaṃ khau idaṃ pubbai pariñ‍ñātaṃ, tattha mai tattha nau (ka॰) tattha + imaiti padacchaidau aivarūpā kārā karīyantī’ti|

247. ‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, yaṃ na tumhākaṃ taṃ pajahatha; taṃ vau pahīnaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ? rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ, taṃ pajahatha; taṃ vau pahīnaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| vaidanā, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ, taṃ  pajahatha; sā vau pahīnā dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| sañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ, taṃ pajahatha; sā vau pahīnā dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| saṅkhārā, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ, tai pajahatha; tai vau pahīnā dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissanti| viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ, taṃ pajahatha; taṃ vau pahīnaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, yaṃ  imasmiṃ jaitavanai tiṇakaṭṭhasākhāpalāsaṃ, taṃ janau haraiyya vā dahaiyya vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā karaiyya| api nu tumhākaṃ aivamassa – ‘amhai janau harati vā dahati vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā karautī’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘na hi nau aitaṃ, bhantai, attā vā attaniyaṃ vā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yaṃ na tumhākaṃ taṃ pajahatha; taṃ vau pahīnaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ? rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ, taṃ pajahatha; taṃ vau pahīnaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| vaidanā, bhikkhavai…pai॰… sañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai… saṅkhārā, bhikkhavai…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ, taṃ pajahatha; taṃ vau pahīnaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati|

248. ‘‘aivaṃ  svākkhātau, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammau uttānau vivaṭau pakāsitau chin‍napilautikau| aivaṃ svākkhātai, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammai uttānai vivaṭai pakāsitai chin‍napilautikai yai tai bhikkhū arahantau khīṇāsavā vusitavantau katakaraṇīyā auhitabhārā anuppattasadatthā parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanā sammadañ‍ñā vimuttā, vaṭṭaṃ taisaṃ natthi pañ‍ñāpanāya| aivaṃ svākkhātau, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammau uttānau vivaṭau pakāsitau chin‍napilautikau| aivaṃ svākkhātai, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammai uttānai vivaṭai pakāsitai chin‍napilautikai yaisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni  pahīnāni, sabbai tai aupapātikā, tattha  parinibbāyinau, anāvattidhammā tasmā laukā| aivaṃ svākkhātau, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammau uttānau vivaṭau pakāsitau chin‍napilautikau| aivaṃ svākkhātai, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammai uttānai vivaṭai pakāsitai chin‍napilautikai yaisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ tīṇi saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni, rāgadausamauhā tanubhūtā, sabbai tai sakadāgāminau, sakidaiva imaṃ laukaṃ āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karissanti| aivaṃ svākkhātau, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammau uttānau vivaṭau pakāsitau chin‍napilautikau| aivaṃ svākkhātai, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammai uttānai vivaṭai pakāsitai chin‍napilautikai yaisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ tīṇi saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni, sabbai tai sautāpan‍nā, avinipātadhammā, niyatā sambaudhiparāyanā| aivaṃ svākkhātau, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammau uttānau vivaṭau pakāsitau chin‍napilautikau| aivaṃ svākkhātai, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammai uttānai vivaṭai pakāsitai chin‍napilautikai yai tai bhikkhū dhammānusārinau saddhānusārinau sabbai tai sambaudhiparāyanā| aivaṃ svākkhātau, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammau uttānau vivaṭau pakāsitau chin‍napilautikau| aivaṃ svākkhātai, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammai uttānai vivaṭai pakāsitai chin‍napilautikai yaisaṃ mayi saddhāmattaṃ paimamattaṃ sabbai tai saggaparāyanā’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

alagaddūpamasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. vammikasuttaṃ

249. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā kumārakassapau andhavanai viharati| atha khau añ‍ñatarā daivatā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ andhavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaināyasmā kumārakassapau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau sā daivatā āyasmantaṃ kumārakassapaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘bhikkhu  bhikkhu, ayaṃ vammikau vammīkau (katthaci) sak‍katānurūpaṃ rattiṃ dhūmāyati, divā paj‍jalati| brāhmaṇau aivamāha – ‘abhikkhaṇa, sumaidha, satthaṃ ādāyā’ti| abhikkhaṇantau sumaidhau satthaṃ ādāya addasa laṅgiṃ ‘laṅgī, bhadantai’ti| brāhmaṇau aivamāha – ‘ukkhipa laṅgiṃ; abhikkhaṇa, sumaidha, satthaṃ ādāyā’ti| abhikkhaṇantau sumaidhau satthaṃ ādāya addasa uddhumāyikaṃ| ‘uddhumāyikā, bhadantai’ti| brāhmaṇau aivamāha – ‘ukkhipa uddhumāyikaṃ; abhikkhaṇa, sumaidha, satthaṃ ādāyā’ti| abhikkhaṇantau sumaidhau satthaṃ ādāya addasa dvidhāpathaṃ| ‘dvidhāpathau, bhadantai’ti| brāhmaṇau aivamāha – ‘ukkhipa dvidhāpathaṃ; abhikkhaṇa, sumaidha, satthaṃ ādāyā’ti| abhikkhaṇantau sumaidhau satthaṃ ādāya addasa caṅgavāraṃ paṅkavāraṃ (syā॰), caṅkavāraṃ (ka॰)| ‘caṅgavārau, bhadantai’ti| brāhmaṇau aivamāha – ‘ukkhipa  caṅgavāraṃ; abhikkhaṇa, sumaidha, satthaṃ ādāyā’ti| abhikkhaṇantau sumaidhau satthaṃ ādāya addasa kummaṃ| ‘kummau, bhadantai’ti| brāhmaṇau aivamāha – ‘ukkhipa kummaṃ; abhikkhaṇa, sumaidha, satthaṃ ādāyā’ti| abhikkhaṇantau sumaidhau satthaṃ ādāya addasa asisūnaṃ| ‘asisūnā, bhadantai’ti| brāhmaṇau aivamāha – ‘ukkhipa asisūnaṃ; abhikkhaṇa, sumaidha, satthaṃ ādāyā’ti| abhikkhaṇantau sumaidhau satthaṃ ādāya addasa maṃsapaisiṃ| ‘maṃsapaisi, bhadantai’ti| brāhmaṇau aivamāha – ‘ukkhipa maṃsapaisiṃ; abhikkhaṇa, sumaidha, satthaṃ ādāyā’ti| abhikkhaṇantau sumaidhau satthaṃ ādāya addasa nāgaṃ| ‘nāgau, bhadantai’ti| brāhmaṇau aivamāha – ‘tiṭṭhatu nāgau, mā nāgaṃ ghaṭṭaisi; namau karauhi nāgassā’’’ti|

‘‘imai  khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, pañhai bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā pucchaiyyāsi, yathā ca tai bhagavā byākarauti tathā naṃ dhāraiyyāsi| nāhaṃ taṃ, bhikkhu, passāmi sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya, yau imaisaṃ pañhānaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaina cittaṃ ārādhaiyya añ‍ñatra tathāgataina vā, tathāgatasāvakaina vā, itau vā pana sutvā’’ti – idamavauca sā daivatā| idaṃ vatvā tatthaivantaradhāyi|

250. atha khau āyasmā kumārakassapau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā kumārakassapau bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘imaṃ, bhantai, rattiṃ añ‍ñatarā daivatā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ andhavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā  yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau, bhantai, sā daivatā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘bhikkhu bhikkhu, ayaṃ vammikau rattiṃ dhūmāyati, divā paj‍jalati’| brāhmaṇau aivamāha – ‘abhikkhaṇa, sumaidha, satthaṃ ādāyā’ti| abhikkhaṇantau sumaidhau satthaṃ ādāya…pai॰… itau vā pana sutvāti| idamavauca, bhantai, sā daivatā| idaṃ vatvā tatthaivantaradhāyi| ‘kau nu khau, bhantai, vammikau, kā rattiṃ dhūmāyanā, kā divā paj‍jalanā, kau brāhmaṇau, kau sumaidhau, kiṃ satthaṃ, kiṃ abhikkhaṇaṃ, kā laṅgī, kā uddhumāyikā, kau dvidhāpathau, kiṃ caṅgavāraṃ, kau kummau, kā asisūnā, kā maṃsapaisi, kau nāgau’’’ti?

251. ‘‘‘vammikau’ti  khau, bhikkhu, imassaitaṃ cātumahābhūtikassa cātummahābhūtikassa (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) kāyassa adhivacanaṃ, mātāpaittikasambhavassa audanakummāsūpacayassa anic‍cucchādana-parimaddanabhaidana-viddhaṃsana-dhammassa|

‘‘yaṃ khau, bhikkhu, divā kammantai kammantaṃ (ka॰) ārabbha rattiṃ anuvitak‍kaiti anuvicāraiti – ayaṃ rattiṃ dhūmāyanā| yaṃ khau, bhikkhu, rattiṃ anuvitak‍kaitvā anuvicāraitvā divā kammantai payaujaiti kāyaina vācāya ‘manasā’ ( ) natthi (sī॰ syā॰) – ayaṃ divā paj‍jalanā|

‘‘‘brāhmaṇau’ti  khau, bhikkhu, tathāgatassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| ‘sumaidhau’ti khau bhikkhu saikkhassaitaṃ bhikkhunau adhivacanaṃ|

‘‘‘sattha’nti khau, bhikkhu, ariyāyaitaṃ pañ‍ñāya adhivacanaṃ| ‘abhikkhaṇa’nti khau, bhikkhu, vīriyārambhassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ|

‘‘‘laṅgī’ti khau, bhikkhu, avij‍jāyaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ukkhipa laṅgiṃ, pajaha avij‍jaṃ; abhikkhaṇa, sumaidha, satthaṃ ādāyāti ayamaitassa atthau|

‘‘‘uddhumāyikā’ti khau, bhikkhu, kaudhūpāyāsassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ukkhipa uddhumāyikaṃ, pajaha kaudhūpāyāsaṃ; abhikkhaṇa, sumaidha, satthaṃ ādāyāti ayamaitassa atthau|

‘‘‘dvidhāpathau’ti  khau, bhikkhu, vicikicchāyaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ukkhipa dvidhāpathaṃ, pajaha vicikicchaṃ; abhikkhaṇa, sumaidha, satthaṃ ādāyāti ayamaitassa atthau|

‘‘‘caṅgavāra’nti khau, bhikkhu, pañ‍can‍naitaṃ nīvaraṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – kāmacchandanīvaraṇassa, byāpādanīvaraṇassa, thīnamiddhanīvaraṇassa, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍canīvaraṇassa, vicikicchānīvaraṇassa| ukkhipa caṅgavāraṃ, pajaha pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai; abhikkhaṇa, sumaidha, satthaṃ ādāyāti ayamaitassa atthau|

‘‘‘kummau’ti khau, bhikkhu, pañ‍can‍naitaṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ adhivacanaṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhassa, vaidanupādānakkhandhassa, sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhassa, saṅkhārupādānakkhandhassa, viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhassa| ukkhipa kummaṃ, pajaha pañ‍cupādānakkhandhai; abhikkhaṇa, sumaidha, satthaṃ ādāyāti ayamaitassa atthau|

‘‘‘asisūnā’ti khau, bhikkhu, pañ‍can‍naitaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ – cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyānaṃ rūpānaṃ iṭṭhānaṃ kantānaṃ manāpānaṃ piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ rajanīyānaṃ, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyānaṃ saddānaṃ…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyānaṃ gandhānaṃ…pai॰… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyānaṃ rasānaṃ…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyānaṃ  phauṭṭhabbānaṃ iṭṭhānaṃ kantānaṃ manāpānaṃ piyarūpānaṃ kāmūpasaṃhitānaṃ  rajanīyānaṃ| ukkhipa asisūnaṃ, pajaha pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai; abhikkhaṇa, sumaidha, satthaṃ ādāyāti ayamaitassa atthau|

‘‘‘maṃsapaisī’ti khau, bhikkhu, nandīrāgassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ukkhipa maṃsapaisiṃ, pajaha nandīrāgaṃ; abhikkhaṇa, sumaidha, satthaṃ ādāyāti ayamaitassa atthau|

‘‘‘nāgau’ti khau, bhikkhu, khīṇāsavassaitaṃ bhikkhunau adhivacanaṃ| tiṭṭhatu nāgau, mā nāgaṃ ghaṭṭaisi; namau karauhi nāgassāti ayamaitassa atthau’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā kumārakassapau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

vammikasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. rathavinītasuttaṃ

252. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| atha khau sambahulā jātibhūmakā bhikkhū jātibhūmiyaṃ vassaṃvuṭṭhā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nai khau tai bhikkhū bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘kau nu khau, bhikkhavai, jātibhūmiyaṃ jātibhūmakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ aivaṃ sambhāvitau – ‘attanā ca appicchau appicchakathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā, attanā ca santuṭṭhau santuṭṭhikathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā, attanā ca pavivittau pavivaikakathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā, attanā ca asaṃsaṭṭhau asaṃsaggakathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā, attanā ca āraddhavīriyau vīriyārambhakathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā, attanā ca sīlasampan‍nau sīlasampadākathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā, attanā ca samādhisampan‍nau samādhisampadākathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā, attanā ca pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau pañ‍ñāsampadākathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā, attanā ca vimuttisampan‍nau vimuttisampadākathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā, attanā ca vimuttiñāṇadassanasampan‍nau vimuttiñāṇadassanasampadākathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā, auvādakau viñ‍ñāpakau sandassakau samādapakau samuttaijakau  sampahaṃsakau sabrahmacārīna’’’nti? ‘‘puṇṇau nāma, bhantai, āyasmā mantāṇiputtau  jātibhūmiyaṃ jātibhūmakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ aivaṃ sambhāvitau – ‘attanā ca appicchau appicchakathañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ kattā, attanā ca santuṭṭhau…pai॰… auvādakau viñ‍ñāpakau sandassakau samādapakau samuttaijakau sampahaṃsakau sabrahmacārīna’’’nti|

253. taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavatau avidūrai nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau āyasmatau sāriputtassa aitadahausi – ‘‘lābhā āyasmatau puṇṇassa mantāṇiputtassa, suladdhalābhā āyasmatau puṇṇassa mantāṇiputtassa, yassa viñ‍ñū sabrahmacārī satthu sammukhā anumassa anumassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanti, tañ‍ca satthā abbhanumaudati| appaiva nāma mayampi kadāci  karahaci āyasmatā puṇṇaina mantāṇiputtaina saddhiṃ samāgacchaiyyāma samāgamaṃ gacchaiyya (ka॰), appaiva nāma siyā kaucidaiva kathāsal‍lāpau’’ti|

254. atha  khau bhagavā rājagahai yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina sāvatthi taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina sāvatthi tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa  ārāmai| assausi khau āyasmā puṇṇau mantāṇiputtau – ‘‘bhagavā kira sāvatthiṃ anuppattau; sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai’’ti|

255. atha khau āyasmā puṇṇau mantāṇiputtau saināsanaṃ saṃsāmaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina sāvatthi taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi| anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina sāvatthi jaitavanaṃ anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ puṇṇaṃ mantāṇiputtaṃ bhagavā dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| atha khau āyasmā puṇṇau mantāṇiputtau bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samuttaijitau sampahaṃsitau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā yaina andhavanaṃ tainupasaṅkami divāvihārāya|

256. atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yassa khau tvaṃ, āvusau sāriputta, puṇṇassa nāma bhikkhunau mantāṇiputtassa abhiṇhaṃ kittayamānau ahausi, sau bhagavatā  dhammiyā kathāya sandassitau samādapitau samuttaijitau sampahaṃsitau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā yaina andhavanaṃ taina pak‍kantau divāvihārāyā’’ti|

atha  khau āyasmā sāriputtau taramānarūpau nisīdanaṃ ādāya āyasmantaṃ puṇṇaṃ mantāṇiputtaṃ piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhi sīsānulaukī| atha khau āyasmā puṇṇau mantāṇiputtau andhavanaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai divāvihāraṃ nisīdi| āyasmāpi  khau sāriputtau andhavanaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai divāvihāraṃ nisīdi|

atha  khau āyasmā sāriputtau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā puṇṇau mantāṇiputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā puṇṇaina mantāṇiputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ puṇṇaṃ mantāṇiputtaṃ aitadavauca –

257. ‘‘bhagavati nau, āvusau, brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti?

‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau, sīlavisuddhatthaṃ bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti?

‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘kiṃ panāvusau, cittavisuddhatthaṃ bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti?

‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau, diṭṭhivisuddhatthaṃ bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti?

‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘kiṃ panāvusau, kaṅkhāvitaraṇavisuddhatthaṃ bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti?

‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau, maggāmaggañāṇadassanavisuddhatthaṃ bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti?

‘‘nau  hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘kiṃ panāvusau, paṭipadāñāṇadassanavisuddhatthaṃ bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti?

‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau, ñāṇadassanavisuddhatthaṃ  bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti?

‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau, sīlavisuddhatthaṃ bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ panāvusau, cittavisuddhatthaṃ bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau, diṭṭhivisuddhatthaṃ…pai॰… kaṅkhāvitaraṇavisuddhatthaṃ…pai॰… maggāmaggañāṇadassanavisuddhatthaṃ…pai॰… paṭipadāñāṇadassanavisuddhatthaṃ…pai॰… kiṃ nu khau, āvusau, ñāṇadassanavisuddhatthaṃ bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘nau hidaṃ āvusau’ti vadaisi| kimatthaṃ carahāvusau, bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ  vussatī’’ti? ‘‘anupādāparinibbānatthaṃ khau, āvusau, bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau, sīlavisuddhi anupādāparinibbāna’’nti?

‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘kiṃ panāvusau, cittavisuddhi anupādāparinibbāna’’nti?

‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau, diṭṭhivisuddhi anupādāparinibbāna’’nti?

‘‘nau  hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘kiṃ panāvusau kaṅkhāvitaraṇavisuddhi anupādāparinibbāna’’nti?

‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau, maggāmaggañāṇadassanavisuddhi anupādāparinibbāna’’nti?

‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘kiṃ panāvusau, paṭipadāñāṇadassanavisuddhi anupādāparinibbāna’’nti?

‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau, ñāṇadassanavisuddhi anupādāparinibbāna’’nti?

‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘kiṃ panāvusau, añ‍ñatra imaihi dhammaihi anupādāparinibbāna’’nti?

‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau, sīlavisuddhi anupādāparinibbāna’nti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ panāvusau, cittavisuddhi anupādāparinibbāna’nti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau, diṭṭhivisuddhi anupādāparinibbāna’nti…pai॰… kaṅkhāvitaraṇavisuddhi… maggāmaggañāṇadassanavisuddhi… paṭipadāñāṇadassanavisuddhi… ‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau, ñāṇadassanavisuddhi anupādāparinibbāna’nti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi| ‘kiṃ panāvusau, añ‍ñatra imaihi dhammaihi anupādāparinibbāna’nti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi| yathākathaṃ panāvusau, imassa bhāsitassa atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti?

258. ‘‘sīlavisuddhiṃ  cai, āvusau, bhagavā anupādāparinibbānaṃ pañ‍ñapaiyya, saupādānaṃyaiva samānaṃ anupādāparinibbānaṃ pañ‍ñapaiyya pañ‍ñāpaissa (sī॰ syā॰) aivamañ‍ñatthapi| cittavisuddhiṃ cai, āvusau, bhagavā anupādāparinibbānaṃ pañ‍ñapaiyya, saupādānaṃyaiva samānaṃ anupādāparinibbānaṃ pañ‍ñapaiyya| diṭṭhivisuddhiṃ cai, āvusau, bhagavā anupādāparinibbānaṃ pañ‍ñapaiyya, saupādānaṃyaiva samānaṃ anupādāparinibbānaṃ pañ‍ñapaiyya| kaṅkhāvitaraṇavisuddhiṃ cai, āvusau, bhagavā anupādāparinibbānaṃ pañ‍ñapaiyya, saupādānaṃyaiva samānaṃ anupādāparinibbānaṃ pañ‍ñapaiyya| maggāmaggañāṇadassanavisuddhiṃ cai, āvusau, bhagavā anupādāparinibbānaṃ pañ‍ñapaiyya, saupādānaṃyaiva samānaṃ anupādāparinibbānaṃ pañ‍ñapaiyya| paṭipadāñāṇadassanavisuddhiṃ cai, āvusau, bhagavā anupādāparinibbānaṃ pañ‍ñapaiyya, saupādānaṃyaiva samānaṃ anupādāparinibbānaṃ pañ‍ñapaiyya| ñāṇadassanavisuddhiṃ cai, āvusau, bhagavā anupādāparinibbānaṃ pañ‍ñapaiyya, saupādānaṃyaiva  samānaṃ anupādāparinibbānaṃ pañ‍ñapaiyya| añ‍ñatra cai, āvusau, imaihi dhammaihi anupādāparinibbānaṃ abhavissa, puthuj‍janau parinibbāyaiyya| puthuj‍janau hi, āvusau, añ‍ñatra imaihi dhammaihi| taina hāvusau, upamaṃ tai karissāmi; upamāyapidhaikac‍cai viñ‍ñū purisā bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānanti|

259. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa sāvatthiyaṃ paṭivasantassa  sākaitai kiñ‍cidaiva ac‍cāyikaṃ karaṇīyaṃ uppaj‍jaiyya| tassa antarā ca sāvatthiṃ antarā ca sākaitaṃ satta rathavinītāni upaṭṭhapaiyyuṃ| atha khau, āvusau, rājā pasainadi kausalau sāvatthiyā nikkhamitvā antaipuradvārā paṭhamaṃ rathavinītaṃ abhiruhaiyya, paṭhamaina rathavinītaina dutiyaṃ rathavinītaṃ pāpuṇaiyya, paṭhamaṃ rathavinītaṃ vissaj‍jaiyya dutiyaṃ rathavinītaṃ abhiruhaiyya| dutiyaina rathavinītaina tatiyaṃ rathavinītaṃ pāpuṇaiyya, dutiyaṃ rathavinītaṃ vissaj‍jaiyya, tatiyaṃ rathavinītaṃ  abhiruhaiyya| tatiyaina rathavinītaina catutthaṃ rathavinītaṃ pāpuṇaiyya, tatiyaṃ rathavinītaṃ vissaj‍jaiyya, catutthaṃ rathavinītaṃ abhiruhaiyya| catutthaina rathavinītaina pañ‍camaṃ rathavinītaṃ pāpuṇaiyya, catutthaṃ rathavinītaṃ vissaj‍jaiyya, pañ‍camaṃ rathavinītaṃ abhiruhaiyya| pañ‍camaina rathavinītaina chaṭṭhaṃ rathavinītaṃ pāpuṇaiyya, pañ‍camaṃ rathavinītaṃ vissaj‍jaiyya, chaṭṭhaṃ rathavinītaṃ abhiruhaiyya| chaṭṭhaina rathavinītaina sattamaṃ rathavinītaṃ pāpuṇaiyya, chaṭṭhaṃ rathavinītaṃ vissaj‍jaiyya, sattamaṃ rathavinītaṃ abhiruhaiyya| sattamaina rathavinītaina sākaitaṃ anupāpuṇaiyya antaipuradvāraṃ| tamainaṃ antaipuradvāragataṃ samānaṃ mittāmac‍cā ñātisālauhitā aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘iminā tvaṃ, mahārāja, rathavinītaina sāvatthiyā sākaitaṃ anuppattau antaipuradvāra’nti? kathaṃ byākaramānau nu khau, āvusau, rājā pasainadi kausalau sammā byākaramānau byākaraiyyā’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ byākaramānau khau, āvusau, rājā pasainadi kausalau sammā byākaramānau byākaraiyya – ‘idha mai sāvatthiyaṃ paṭivasantassa sākaitai kiñ‍cidaiva ac‍cāyikaṃ karaṇīyaṃ uppaj‍ji uppaj‍jati (ka॰)| tassa mai antarā ca sāvatthiṃ antarā ca sākaitaṃ satta rathavinītāni upaṭṭhapaisuṃ| atha khvāhaṃ sāvatthiyā  nikkhamitvā antaipuradvārā paṭhamaṃ rathavinītaṃ abhiruhiṃ| paṭhamaina rathavinītaina dutiyaṃ rathavinītaṃ pāpuṇiṃ, paṭhamaṃ rathavinītaṃ vissaj‍jiṃ dutiyaṃ rathavinītaṃ abhiruhiṃ| dutiyaina rathavinītaina tatiyaṃ rathavinītaṃ pāpuṇiṃ, dutiyaṃ rathavinītaṃ vissaj‍jiṃ, tatiyaṃ rathavinītaṃ abhiruhiṃ| tatiyaina rathavinītaina catutthaṃ rathavinītaṃ pāpuṇiṃ, tatiyaṃ rathavinītaṃ vissaj‍jiṃ, catutthaṃ rathavinītaṃ abhiruhiṃ| catutthaina rathavinītaina pañ‍camaṃ rathavinītaṃ pāpuṇiṃ, catutthaṃ rathavinītaṃ vissaj‍jiṃ, pañ‍camaṃ  rathavinītaṃ abhiruhiṃ| pañ‍camaina rathavinītaina chaṭṭhaṃ rathavinītaṃ pāpuṇiṃ, pañ‍camaṃ rathavinītaṃ vissaj‍jiṃ, chaṭṭhaṃ rathavinītaṃ abhiruhiṃ| chaṭṭhaina rathavinītaina sattamaṃ rathavinītaṃ pāpuṇiṃ, chaṭṭhaṃ rathavinītaṃ vissaj‍jiṃ, sattamaṃ rathavinītaṃ abhiruhiṃ| sattamaina rathavinītaina sākaitaṃ anuppattau antaipuradvāra’nti| aivaṃ byākaramānau khau, āvusau, rājā pasainadi kausalau sammā byākaramānau byākaraiyyā’’ti|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, āvusau, sīlavisuddhi yāvadaiva cittavisuddhatthā, cittavisuddhi yāvadaiva diṭṭhivisuddhatthā, diṭṭhivisuddhi yāvadaiva kaṅkhāvitaraṇavisuddhatthā, kaṅkhāvitaraṇavisuddhi  yāvadaiva maggāmaggañāṇadassanavisuddhatthā, maggāmaggañāṇadassanavisuddhi yāvadaiva paṭipadāñāṇadassanavisuddhatthā, paṭipadāñāṇadassanavisuddhi yāvadaiva ñāṇadassanavisuddhatthā, ñāṇadassanavisuddhi yāvadaiva anupādāparinibbānatthā| anupādāparinibbānatthaṃ khau, āvusau, bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti|

260. aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ puṇṇaṃ mantāṇiputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kaunāmau āyasmā, kathañ‍ca panāyasmantaṃ sabrahmacārī jānantī’’ti? ‘‘puṇṇauti khau mai, āvusau, nāmaṃ; mantāṇiputtauti ca pana maṃ sabrahmacārī jānantī’’ti| ‘‘acchariyaṃ, āvusau, abbhutaṃ, āvusau! yathā taṃ sutavatā sāvakaina sammadaiva satthusāsanaṃ ājānantaina, aivamaiva āyasmatā  puṇṇaina mantāṇiputtaina gambhīrā  gambhīrapañhā anumassa anumassa byākatā| lābhā sabrahmacārīnaṃ, suladdhalābhā sabrahmacārīnaṃ, yai āyasmantaṃ puṇṇaṃ mantāṇiputtaṃ labhanti dassanāya, labhanti payirūpāsanāya| cailaṇḍukaina cailaṇḍakaina (ka॰), cailaṇḍupaikaina (?) caipi sabrahmacārī āyasmantaṃ puṇṇaṃ mantāṇiputtaṃ  muddhanā pariharantā labhaiyyuṃ dassanāya, labhaiyyuṃ payirūpāsanāya, taisampi lābhā taisampi suladdhaṃ, amhākampi lābhā amhākampi suladdhaṃ, yai mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ puṇṇaṃ mantāṇiputtaṃ labhāma dassanāya, labhāma payirūpāsanāyā’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā puṇṇau mantāṇiputtau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nāmau āyasmā, kathañ‍ca panāyasmantaṃ sabrahmacārī jānantī’’ti? ‘‘upatissauti khau mai, āvusau, nāmaṃ; sāriputtauti ca pana maṃ sabrahmacārī jānantī’’ti| ‘‘satthukappaina vata kira, bhau khau (ka॰), sāvakaina saddhiṃ mantayamānā na jānimha – ‘āyasmā sāriputtau’ti| sacai hi mayaṃ jānaiyyāma ‘āyasmā sāriputtau’ti, aittakampi nau nappaṭibhāsaiyya nappaṭibhaiyya (?)| acchariyaṃ, āvusau, abbhutaṃ, āvusau! yathā taṃ sutavatā sāvakaina sammadaiva satthusāsanaṃ ājānantaina, aivamaiva āyasmatā sāriputtaina gambhīrā gambhīrapañhā anumassa anumassa pucchitā| lābhā sabrahmacārīnaṃ suladdhalābhā sabrahmacārīnaṃ, yai āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ labhanti dassanāya, labhanti payirūpāsanāya| cailaṇḍukaina caipi sabrahmacārī āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ muddhanā pariharantā  labhaiyyuṃ dassanāya, labhaiyyuṃ payirūpāsanāya, taisampi  lābhā taisampi suladdhaṃ, amhākampi lābhā amhākampi suladdhaṃ, yai mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ labhāma dassanāya, labhāma payirūpāsanāyā’’ti|

itiha tai ubhaupi mahānāgā añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa subhāsitaṃ samanumaudiṃsūti|

rathavinītasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. nivāpasuttaṃ

261. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘na, bhikkhavai, naivāpikau nivāpaṃ nivapati migajātānaṃ – ‘imaṃ mai nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ migajātā paribhuñ‍jantā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ yāpaintū’ti| aivañ‍ca khau, bhikkhavai, naivāpikau nivāpaṃ nivapati migajātānaṃ – ‘imaṃ mai nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ migajātā anupakhaj‍ja mucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jissanti, anupakhaj‍ja mucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jamānā madaṃ āpaj‍jissanti, mattā samānā pamādaṃ āpaj‍jissanti, pamattā samānā yathākāmakaraṇīyā bhavissanti imasmiṃ nivāpai’ti|

262. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, paṭhamā migajātā amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ naivāpikassa anupakhaj‍ja mucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tai tattha anupakhaj‍ja mucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jamānā madaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, mattā samānā pamādaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, pamattā samānā yathākāmakaraṇīyā ahaisuṃ naivāpikassa amusmiṃ nivāpai| aivañhi tai, bhikkhavai, paṭhamā migajātā na parimuc‍ciṃsu naivāpikassa iddhānubhāvā|
