263. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, dutiyā migajātā aivaṃ samacintaisuṃ – ‘yai khau  tai paṭhamā migajātā amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ naivāpikassa anupakhaj‍ja mucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jiṃsu| tai tattha anupakhaj‍ja mucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jamānā madaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, mattā samānā pamādaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, pamattā samānā yathākāmakaraṇīyā  ahaisuṃ naivāpikassa amusmiṃ nivāpai| aivañhi tai paṭhamā migajātā na parimuc‍ciṃsu naivāpikassa iddhānubhāvā| yaṃnūna mayaṃ sabbasau nivāpabhaujanā paṭiviramaiyyāma, bhayabhaugā paṭiviratā arañ‍ñāyatanāni ajjhaugāhaitvā viharaiyyāmā’ti| tai sabbasau nivāpabhaujanā paṭiviramiṃsu, bhayabhaugā paṭiviratā arañ‍ñāyatanāni  ajjhaugāhaitvā vihariṃsu| taisaṃ gimhānaṃ pacchimai māsai, tiṇaudakasaṅkhayai, adhimattakasimānaṃ pattau kāyau hauti| taisaṃ adhimattakasimānaṃ pattakāyānaṃ balavīriyaṃ parihāyi| balavīriyai parihīnai tamaiva nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ naivāpikassa pac‍cāgamiṃsu| tai tattha anupakhaj‍ja mucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jiṃsu| tai tattha anupakhaj‍ja mucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jamānā madaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, mattā samānā pamādaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, pamattā samānā yathākāmakaraṇīyā ahaisuṃ naivāpikassa amusmiṃ nivāpai| aivañhi tai, bhikkhavai, dutiyāpi migajātā na parimuc‍ciṃsu naivāpikassa iddhānubhāvā|

264. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, tatiyā migajātā aivaṃ samacintaisuṃ – ‘yai khau tai paṭhamā migajātā amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ naivāpikassa…pai॰… aivañhi tai paṭhamā migajātā na parimuc‍ciṃsu naivāpikassa iddhānubhāvā| yaipi tai dutiyā migajātā aivaṃ samacintaisuṃ – yai khau tai paṭhamā migajātā  amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ naivāpikassa…pai॰… aivañhi tai paṭhamā migajātā na parimuc‍ciṃsu naivāpikassa iddhānubhāvā| yaṃnūna mayaṃ sabbasau nivāpabhaujanā paṭiviramaiyyāma, bhayabhaugā paṭiviratā arañ‍ñāyatanāni ajjhaugāhaitvā viharaiyyāmāti| tai sabbasau nivāpabhaujanā paṭiviramiṃsu, bhayabhaugā paṭiviratā arañ‍ñāyatanāni ajjhaugāhaitvā vihariṃsu| taisaṃ gimhānaṃ pacchimai māsai tiṇaudakasaṅkhayai adhimattakasimānaṃ pattau kāyau hauti| taisaṃ adhimattakasimānaṃ pattakāyānaṃ balavīriyaṃ parihāyi| balavīriyai parihīnai tamaiva nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ naivāpikassa pac‍cāgamiṃsu| tai tattha anupakhaj‍ja mucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jiṃsu| tai tattha anupakhaj‍ja mucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jamānā madaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, mattā samānā pamādaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, pamattā samānā yathākāmakaraṇīyā ahaisuṃ naivāpikassa amusmiṃ nivāpai| aivañhi tai dutiyāpi migajātā na parimuc‍ciṃsu naivāpikassa iddhānubhāvā| yaṃnūna mayaṃ amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ naivāpikassa upanissāya  āsayaṃ kappaiyyāma| tatrāsayaṃ kappaitvā amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ naivāpikassa ananupakhaj‍ja amucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jissāma, ananupakhaj‍ja amucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jamānā na madaṃ āpaj‍jissāma, amattā samānā na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jissāma, appamattā samānā na yathākāmakaraṇīyā bhavissāma naivāpikassa amusmiṃ nivāpai’ti| tai amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ naivāpikassa upanissāya āsayaṃ kappayiṃsu| tatrāsayaṃ kappaitvā amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ naivāpikassa ananupakhaj‍ja  amucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tai tattha ananupakhaj‍ja amucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jamānā na madaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, amattā samānā  na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, appamattā samānā na yathākāmakaraṇīyā ahaisuṃ naivāpikassa amusmiṃ nivāpai|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, naivāpikassa ca naivāpikaparisāya ca aitadahausi – ‘saṭhāssunāmimai tatiyā migajātā kaitabinau, iddhimantāssunāmimai tatiyā migajātā parajanā; imañ‍ca nāma nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ paribhuñ‍janti, na ca naisaṃ  jānāma āgatiṃ vā gatiṃ vā| yaṃnūna mayaṃ imaṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ mahatīhi daṇḍavākarāhi daṇḍavāgurāhi (syā॰) samantā sappadaisaṃ anuparivāraiyyāma – appaiva nāma tatiyānaṃ migajātānaṃ āsayaṃ passaiyyāma, yattha tai gāhaṃ gacchaiyyu’nti| tai amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ mahatīhi daṇḍavākarāhi samantā sappadaisaṃ anuparivāraisuṃ| addasaṃsu khau, bhikkhavai, naivāpikau ca naivāpikaparisā ca tatiyānaṃ migajātānaṃ āsayaṃ, yattha tai gāhaṃ agamaṃsu| aivañhi tai, bhikkhavai, tatiyāpi migajātā na parimuc‍ciṃsu naivāpikassa iddhānubhāvā|

265. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, catutthā migajātā aivaṃ samacintaisuṃ – ‘yai khau tai paṭhamā migajātā…pai॰… aivañhi tai paṭhamā migajātā na parimuc‍ciṃsu naivāpikassa iddhānubhāvā| yaipi tai dutiyā migajātā aivaṃ samacintaisuṃ ‘yai khau tai paṭhamā migajātā…pai॰… aivañhi tai paṭhamā migajātā na parimuc‍ciṃsu naivāpikassa iddhānubhāvā| yaṃnūna mayaṃ  sabbasau nivāpabhaujanā paṭiviramaiyyāma, bhayabhaugā paṭiviratā arañ‍ñāyatanāni ajjhaugāhaitvā viharaiyyāmā’ti| tai sabbasau nivāpabhaujanā paṭiviramiṃsu…pai॰… aivañhi tai dutiyāpi migajātā na parimuc‍ciṃsu naivāpikassa iddhānubhāvā| yaipi tai tatiyā migajātā aivaṃ samacintaisuṃ ‘yai khau tai paṭhamā  migajātā…pai॰… aivañhi tai paṭhamā migajātā na parimuc‍ciṃsu naivāpikassa iddhānubhāvā| yaipi tai dutiyā migajātā aivaṃ samacintaisuṃ ‘yai khau tai paṭhamā migajātā…pai॰… aivañhi tai paṭhamā migajātā na parimuc‍ciṃsu naivāpikassa iddhānubhāvā| yaṃnūna mayaṃ sabbasau nivāpabhaujanā paṭiviramaiyyāma, bhayabhaugā paṭiviratā arañ‍ñāyatanāni ajjhaugāhaitvā viharaiyyāmā’ti| tai sabbasau nivāpabhaujanā paṭiviramiṃsu…pai॰… aivañhi tai dutiyāpi migajātā na parimuc‍ciṃsu naivāpikassa iddhānubhāvā| yaṃnūna mayaṃ amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ naivāpikassa upanissāya āsayaṃ kappaiyyāma, tatrāsayaṃ kappaitvā amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ naivāpikassa ananupakhaj‍ja amucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jissāma, ananupakhaj‍ja amucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jamānā na madaṃ āpaj‍jissāma, amattā  samānā na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jissāma, appamattā samānā na yathākāmakaraṇīyā bhavissāma naivāpikassa  amusmiṃ nivāpai’ti| tai amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ naivāpikassa upanissāya āsayaṃ kappayiṃsu, tatrāsayaṃ kappaitvā amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ naivāpikassa ananupakhaj‍ja amucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tai tattha ananupakhaj‍ja amucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jamānā na madaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, amattā samānā na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, appamattā samānā na yathākāmakaraṇīyā ahaisuṃ naivāpikassa amusmiṃ nivāpai|

‘‘tatra naivāpikassa ca naivāpikaparisāya ca aitadahausi – ‘saṭhāssunāmimai tatiyā migajātā kaitabinau, iddhimantāssunāmimai tatiyā  migajātā parajanā, imañ‍ca nāma nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ paribhuñ‍janti| na ca naisaṃ jānāma āgatiṃ vā gatiṃ vā| yaṃnūna mayaṃ imaṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ mahatīti  daṇḍavākarāhi samantā sappadaisaṃ anuparivāraiyyāma, appaiva nāma tatiyānaṃ migajātānaṃ āsayaṃ passaiyyāma, yattha tai gāhaṃ gacchaiyyu’nti| tai amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ mahatīti daṇḍavākarāhi samantā sappadaisaṃ anuparivāraisuṃ| addasaṃsu khau naivāpikau ca naivāpikaparisā ca tatiyānaṃ migajātānaṃ āsayaṃ, yattha tai gāhaṃ agamaṃsu| aivañhi tai tatiyāpi migajātā na parimuc‍ciṃsu naivāpikassa iddhānubhāvā| yaṃnūna mayaṃ yattha agati naivāpikassa ca naivāpikaparisāya ca tatrāsayaṃ kappaiyyāma, tatrāsayaṃ kappaitvā amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ naivāpikassa ananupakhaj‍ja amucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jissāma, ananupakhaj‍ja amucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jamānā na madaṃ āpaj‍jissāma, amattā samānā na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jissāma, appamattā samānā na yathākāmakaraṇīyā  bhavissāma naivāpikassa amusmiṃ nivāpai’ti| tai yattha agati naivāpikassa ca naivāpikaparisāya ca tatrāsayaṃ kappayiṃsu| tatrāsayaṃ kappaitvā amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ naivāpikassa ananupakhaj‍ja amucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tai tattha ananupakhaj‍ja amucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jamānā na madaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, amattā samānā na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, appamattā samānā na yathākāmakaraṇīyā ahaisuṃ naivāpikassa amusmiṃ nivāpai|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, naivāpikassa ca naivāpikaparisāya ca aitadahausi – ‘saṭhāssunāmimai catutthā migajātā kaitabinau, iddhimantāssunāmimai catutthā migajātā parajanā| imañ‍ca nāma nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ paribhuñ‍janti, na ca naisaṃ jānāma āgatiṃ vā gatiṃ vā| yaṃnūna mayaṃ imaṃ nivāpaṃ  nivuttaṃ mahatīhi daṇḍavākarāhi samantā sappadaisaṃ anuparivāraiyyāma, appaiva nāma catutthānaṃ migajātānaṃ āsayaṃ passaiyyāma yattha tai gāhaṃ gacchaiyyu’nti| tai  amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ mahatīhi daṇḍavākarāhi samantā sappadaisaṃ anuparivāraisuṃ| naiva khau, bhikkhavai, addasaṃsu naivāpikau ca naivāpikaparisā ca catutthānaṃ migajātānaṃ āsayaṃ, yattha tai gāhaṃ gacchaiyyuṃ| tatra, bhikkhavai, naivāpikassa ca naivāpikaparisāya ca aitadahausi – ‘sacai khau mayaṃ catutthai migajātai ghaṭṭaissāma, tai ghaṭṭitā añ‍ñai ghaṭṭissanti tai ghaṭṭitā añ‍ñai ghaṭṭissanti| aivaṃ imaṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ sabbasau migajātā parimuñ‍cissanti| yaṃnūna mayaṃ catutthai migajātai ajjhupaikkhaiyyāmā’ti| ajjhupaikkhiṃsu khau, bhikkhavai, naivāpikau ca naivāpikaparisā ca catutthai migajātai| aivañhi tai, bhikkhavai, catutthā migajātā parimuc‍ciṃsu naivāpikassa iddhānubhāvā|

266. ‘‘upamā khau mai ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, katā atthassa viñ‍ñāpanāya| ayaṃ caivaittha atthau – nivāpauti khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naitaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| naivāpikauti khau, bhikkhavai, mārassaitaṃ pāpimatau adhivacanaṃ| naivāpikaparisāti khau, bhikkhavai, māraparisāyaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| migajātāti khau, bhikkhavai, samaṇabrāhmaṇānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ|

267. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, paṭhamā samaṇabrāhmaṇā amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ mārassa amūni ca laukāmisāni anupakhaj‍ja mucchitā  bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jiṃsu| tai tattha anupakhaj‍ja mucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jamānā madaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, mattā samānā pamādaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, pamattā samānā yathākāmakaraṇīyā ahaisuṃ mārassa amusmiṃ nivāpai amusmiñ‍ca laukāmisai| aivañhi tai, bhikkhavai, paṭhamā samaṇabrāhmaṇā na parimuc‍ciṃsu mārassa iddhānubhāvā| saiyyathāpi tai, bhikkhavai, paṭhamā migajātā tathūpamai ahaṃ imai paṭhamai samaṇabrāhmaṇai vadāmi|

268. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, dutiyā samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃ samacintaisuṃ – ‘yai khau tai paṭhamā samaṇabrāhmaṇā amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ mārassa amūni ca laukāmisāni anupakhaj‍ja mucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jiṃsu| tai tattha anupakhaj‍ja mucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jamānā madaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, mattā samānā pamādaṃ  āpaj‍jiṃsu, pamattā samānā yathākāmakaraṇīyā ahaisuṃ mārassa amusmiṃ nivāpai amusmiñ‍ca laukāmisai| aivañhi tai paṭhamā samaṇabrāhmaṇā na parimuc‍ciṃsu  mārassa iddhānubhāvā| yaṃnūna mayaṃ sabbasau nivāpabhaujanā laukāmisā paṭiviramaiyyāma, bhayabhaugā paṭiviratā arañ‍ñāyatanāni ajjhaugāhaitvā viharaiyyāmā’ti| tai sabbasau nivāpabhaujanā laukāmisā paṭiviramiṃsu, bhayabhaugā paṭiviratā arañ‍ñāyatanāni ajjhaugāhaitvā viharaiyyāmāti| tai sabbasau nivāpabhaujanā laukāmisā paṭiviramiṃsu, bhayabhaugā paṭiviratā arañ‍ñāyatanāni ajjhaugāhaitvā vihariṃsu| tai tattha sākabhakkhāpi ahaisuṃ, sāmākabhakkhāpi ahaisuṃ, nīvārabhakkhāpi ahaisuṃ, daddulabhakkhāpi ahaisuṃ, haṭabhakkhāpi ahaisuṃ, kaṇabhakkhāpi ahaisuṃ, ācāmabhakkhāpi ahaisuṃ, piñ‍ñākabhakkhāpi ahaisuṃ, tiṇabhakkhāpi ahaisuṃ, gaumayabhakkhāpi ahaisuṃ, vanamūlaphalāhārā yāpaisuṃ pavattaphalabhaujī|

‘‘taisaṃ gimhānaṃ pacchimai māsai, tiṇaudakasaṅkhayai, adhimattakasimānaṃ pattau kāyau hauti| taisaṃ adhimattakasimānaṃ  pattakāyānaṃ balavīriyaṃ parihāyi| balavīriyai parihīnai caitauvimutti parihāyi| caitauvimuttiyā parihīnāya tamaiva nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ mārassa pac‍cāgamiṃsu tāni ca laukāmisāni| tai tattha anupakhaj‍ja mucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jiṃsu| tai tattha anupakhaj‍ja mucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jamānā madaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, mattā samānā pamādaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, pamattā samānā yathākāmakaraṇīyā ahaisuṃ mārassa amusmiṃ nivāpai amusmiñ‍ca laukāmisai| aivañhi tai, bhikkhavai, dutiyāpi samaṇabrāhmaṇā na parimuc‍ciṃsu mārassa  iddhānubhāvā| saiyyathāpi tai, bhikkhavai, dutiyā migajātā tathūpamai ahaṃ imai dutiyai samaṇabrāhmaṇai vadāmi|

269. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, tatiyā samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃ samacintaisuṃ – ‘yai khau tai paṭhamā samaṇabrāhmaṇā amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ mārassa amūni ca laukāmisāni…pai॰…| aivañhi tai paṭhamā samaṇabrāhmaṇā na parimuc‍ciṃsu mārassa iddhānubhāvā| yaipi tai dutiyā samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃ samacintaisuṃ – ‘yai khau tai paṭhamā samaṇabrāhmaṇā amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ mārassa amūni ca laukāmisāni…pai॰…| aivañhi tai paṭhamā samaṇabrāhmaṇā na parimuc‍ciṃsu mārassa iddhānubhāvā| yaṃnūna mayaṃ sabbasau nivāpabhaujanā laukāmisā paṭiviramaiyyāma, bhayabhaugā paṭiviratā arañ‍ñāyatanāni ajjhaugāhaitvā viharaiyyāmā’ti| tai sabbasau nivāpabhaujanā laukāmisā  paṭiviramiṃsu| bhayabhaugā paṭiviratā arañ‍ñāyatanāni ajjhaugāhaitvā vihariṃsu| tai tattha sākabhakkhāpi ahaisuṃ…pai॰… pavattaphalabhaujī| taisaṃ gimhānaṃ  pacchimai māsai tiṇaudakasaṅkhayai adhimattakasimānaṃ  pattau kāyau hauti| taisaṃ adhimattakasimānaṃ pattakāyānaṃ balavīriyaṃ parihāyi, balavīriyai parihīnai caitauvimutti parihāyi, caitauvimuttiyā parihīnāya tamaiva nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ mārassa pac‍cāgamiṃsu tāni ca laukāmisāni| tai tattha anupakhaj‍ja mucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jiṃsu| tai tattha anupakhaj‍ja mucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jamānā madaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, mattā samānā pamādaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, pamattā samānā yathākāmakaraṇīyā ahaisuṃ mārassa amusmiṃ nivāpai amusmiñ‍ca laukāmisai| aivañhi tai dutiyāpi samaṇabrāhmaṇā na parimuc‍ciṃsu mārassa iddhānubhāvā| yaṃnūna mayaṃ amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ mārassa amūni ca laukāmisāni upanissāya āsayaṃ kappaiyyāma, tatrāsayaṃ kappaitvā amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ mārassa amūni ca laukāmisāni ananupakhaj‍ja amucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jissāma, ananupakhaj‍ja amucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jamānā na madaṃ āpaj‍jissāma, amattā samānā na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jissāma, appamattā samānā na yathākāmakaraṇīyā bhavissāma mārassa amusmiṃ nivāpai amusmiñ‍ca laukāmisai’’ti|

‘‘tai amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ mārassa amūni ca laukāmisāni upanissāya āsayaṃ kappayiṃsu| tatrāsayaṃ kappaitvā amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ mārassa amūni ca laukāmisāni ananupakhaj‍ja amucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jiṃsu| tai tattha ananupakhaj‍ja amucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jamānā na madaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, amattā samānā na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, appamattā samānā na yathākāmakaraṇīyā ahaisuṃ mārassa amusmiṃ nivāpai amusmiñ‍ca laukāmisai| api ca khau aivaṃdiṭṭhikā ahaisuṃ – sassatau laukau itipi, asassatau laukau itipi; antavā laukau itipi, anantavā laukau itipi; taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīraṃ itipi, añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīraṃ itipi; hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā itipi, na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā itipi, hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā itipi, naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā itipi| aivañhi tai, bhikkhavai, tatiyāpi samaṇabrāhmaṇā  na parimuc‍ciṃsu mārassa iddhānubhāvā| saiyyathāpi tai, bhikkhavai, tatiyā migajātā tathūpamai ahaṃ imai tatiyai samaṇabrāhmaṇai vadāmi|

270. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, catutthā samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃ samacintaisuṃ – ‘yai khau tai paṭhamā samaṇabrāhmaṇā amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ mārassa…pai॰…| aivañhi tai paṭhamā samaṇabrāhmaṇā na parimuc‍ciṃsu  mārassa iddhānubhāvā| yaipi tai dutiyā samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃ samacintaisuṃ – ‘yai khau tai paṭhamā samaṇabrāhmaṇā…pai॰…| aivañhi tai paṭhamā samaṇabrāhmaṇā na parimuc‍ciṃsu mārassa iddhānubhāvā| yaṃnūna mayaṃ sabbasau nivāpabhaujanā laukāmisā paṭiviramaiyyāma bhayabhaugā paṭiviratā arañ‍ñāyatanāni ajjhaugāhaitvā viharaiyyāmā’ti| tai sabbasau nivāpabhaujanā laukāmisā paṭiviramiṃsu…pai॰…| aivañhi tai dutiyāpi samaṇabrāhmaṇā na parimuc‍ciṃsu mārassa iddhānubhāvā| yaipi tai tatiyā samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃ samacintaisuṃ yai khau tai paṭhamā samaṇabrāhmaṇā  …pai॰…| aivañhi tai paṭhamā samaṇabrāhmaṇā na parimuc‍ciṃsu mārassa iddhānubhāvā| yaipi tai dutiyā samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃ samacintaisuṃ yai khau tai paṭhamā samaṇabrāhmaṇā…pai॰…| aivañhi tai paṭhamā samaṇabrāhmaṇā na parimuc‍ciṃsu mārassa iddhānubhāvā| yaṃnūna mayaṃ sabbasau nivāpabhaujanā laukāmisā paṭiviramaiyyāma, bhayabhaugā paṭiviratā arañ‍ñāyatanāni ajjhaugāhaitvā viharaiyyāmā’ti| tai sabbasau nivāpabhaujanā laukāmisā paṭiviramiṃsu…pai॰…| aivañhi tai dutiyāpi samaṇabrāhmaṇā na parimuc‍ciṃsu mārassa iddhānubhāvā| yaṃnūna mayaṃ amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ mārassa amūni ca laukāmisāni upanissāya āsayaṃ kappaiyyāma| tatrāsayaṃ kappaitvā amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ mārassa amūni ca laukāmisāni ananupakhaj‍ja amucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jissāma, ananupakhaj‍ja amucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jamānā na madaṃ āpaj‍jissāma, amattā samānā na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jissāma, appamattā samānā na yathākāmakaraṇīyā bhavissāma mārassa amusmiṃ nivāpai amusmiñ‍ca laukāmisaiti|

‘‘tai amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ mārassa amūni ca laukāmisāni upanissāya āsayaṃ kappayiṃsu| tatrāsayaṃ kappaitvā amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ mārassa amūni ca laukāmisāni ananupakhaj‍ja amucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jiṃsu| tai tattha ananupakhaj‍ja amucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jamānā na madaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu| amattā samānā  na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu| appamattā samānā na yathākāmakaraṇīyā ahaisuṃ mārassa amusmiṃ nivāpai amusmiñ‍ca laukāmisai| api ca khau aivaṃdiṭṭhikā ahaisuṃ sassatau laukau itipi…pai॰… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā itipi| aivañhi tai tatiyāpi samaṇabrāhmaṇā na parimuc‍ciṃsu mārassa iddhānubhāvā| yaṃnūna mayaṃ yattha agati mārassa ca māraparisāya ca tatrāsayaṃ kappaiyyāma| tatrāsayaṃ kappaitvā amuṃ nivāpaṃ nivuttaṃ mārassa amūni  ca laukāmisāni ananupakhaj‍ja amucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jissāma, ananupakhaj‍ja amucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jamānā na madaṃ āpaj‍jissāma, amattā samānā na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jissāma, appamattā samānā na yathākāmakaraṇīyā bhavissāma mārassa amusmiṃ nivāpai amusmiṃ ca laukāmisaiti|

‘‘tai  yattha agati mārassa ca māraparisāya ca tatrāsayaṃ kappayiṃsu| tatrāsayaṃ kappaitvā amuṃ nivāpaṃ  nivuttaṃ mārassa amūni ca laukāmisāni ananupakhaj‍ja amucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tai tattha ananupakhaj‍ja amucchitā bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jamānā na madaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, amattā samānā na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, appamattā samānā na yathākāmakaraṇīyā ahaisuṃ mārassa amusmiṃ nivāpai amusmiñ‍ca laukāmisai| aivañhi tai, bhikkhavai, catutthā samaṇabrāhmaṇā parimuc‍ciṃsu mārassa iddhānubhāvā| saiyyathāpi tai, bhikkhavai, catutthā migajātā tathūpamai ahaṃ imai catutthai samaṇabrāhmaṇai vadāmi|

271. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, agati mārassa ca māraparisāya ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu andhamakāsi māraṃ, apadaṃ vadhitvā māracakkhuṃ adassanaṃ gatau pāpimatau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai…pai॰… pāpimatau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau, sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti  ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai…pai॰… pāpimatau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā, pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā, adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ  upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai…pai॰… pāpimatau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā  nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai…pai॰… pāpimatau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai…pai॰… pāpimatau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma  ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai…pai॰… pāpimatau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai…pai॰… pāpimatau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā haunti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu andhamakāsi māraṃ, apadaṃ vadhitvā māracakkhuṃ adassanaṃ gatau pāpimatau tiṇṇau laukai visattika’’nti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

nivāpasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. pāsarāsisuttaṃ

272. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘cirassutā nau, āvusau ānanda, bhagavatau sammukhā dhammī kathā| sādhu mayaṃ, āvusau ānanda, labhaiyyāma bhagavatau sammukhā dhammiṃ kathaṃ savanāyā’’ti| ‘‘taina hāyasmantau yaina rammakassa brāhmaṇassa assamau tainupasaṅkamatha; appaiva nāma labhaiyyātha bhagavatau sammukhā dhammiṃ kathaṃ savanāyā’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau ānandassa pac‍cassausuṃ|

atha khau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘āyāmānanda, yaina pubbārāmau migāramātupāsādau tainupasaṅkamissāma divāvihārāyā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| atha  khau bhagavā āyasmatā ānandaina saddhiṃ yaina pubbārāmau migāramātupāsādau tainupasaṅkami divāvihārāya| atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘āyāmānanda, yaina  pubbakauṭṭhakau tainupasaṅkamissāma gattāni parisiñ‍citu’’nti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi|

273. atha khau bhagavā āyasmatā ānandaina saddhiṃ yaina pubbakauṭṭhakau tainupasaṅkami gattāni parisiñ‍cituṃ| pubbakauṭṭhakai gattāni parisiñ‍citvā pac‍cuttaritvā aikacīvarau aṭṭhāsi gattāni pubbāpayamānau| atha khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, rammakassa brāhmaṇassa assamau avidūrai| ramaṇīyau, bhantai, rammakassa brāhmaṇassa assamau; pāsādikau, bhantai, rammakassa brāhmaṇassa assamau| sādhu, bhantai, bhagavā yaina rammakassa brāhmaṇassa assamau tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina|

atha  khau bhagavā yaina rammakassa brāhmaṇassa assamau tainupasaṅkami| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū rammakassa brāhmaṇassa assamai dhammiyā kathāya san‍nisin‍nā haunti| atha khau bhagavā bahidvārakauṭṭhakai aṭṭhāsi kathāpariyausānaṃ āgamayamānau| atha khau bhagavā kathāpariyausānaṃ viditvā uk‍kāsitvā aggaḷaṃ ākauṭaisi| vivariṃsu khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau dvāraṃ| atha khau bhagavā rammakassa brāhmaṇassa assamaṃ pavisitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘kāyanuttha, bhikkhavai, aitarahi kathāya san‍nisin‍nā? kā ca pana vau antarākathā vippakatā’’ti? ‘‘bhagavantamaiva khau nau, bhantai, ārabbha dhammī kathā vippakatā, atha bhagavā anuppattau’’ti| ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai! aitaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tumhākaṃ patirūpaṃ kulaputtānaṃ saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitānaṃ yaṃ tumhai dhammiyā kathāya san‍nisīdaiyyātha| san‍nipatitānaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, dvayaṃ karaṇīyaṃ – dhammī vā kathā, ariyau vā tuṇhībhāvau’’|

274. ‘‘dvaimā, bhikkhavai, pariyaisanā – ariyā ca pariyaisanā, anariyā ca pariyaisanā|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, anariyā pariyaisanā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau attanā jātidhammau samānau jātidhammaṃyaiva pariyaisati, attanā jarādhammau samānau jarādhammaṃyaiva  pariyaisati, attanā byādhidhammau samānau byādhidhammaṃyaiva pariyaisati, attanā maraṇadhammau samānau maraṇadhammaṃyaiva pariyaisati, attanā saukadhammau samānau saukadhammaṃyaiva pariyaisati, attanā saṃkilaisadhammau samānau saṃkilaisadhammaṃyaiva pariyaisati|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, jātidhammaṃ vadaitha? puttabhariyaṃ, bhikkhavai, jātidhammaṃ, dāsidāsaṃ jātidhammaṃ, ajaiḷakaṃ jātidhammaṃ, kuk‍kuṭasūkaraṃ jātidhammaṃ, hatthigavāssavaḷavaṃ jātidhammaṃ, jātarūparajataṃ jātidhammaṃ| jātidhammā haitai, bhikkhavai, upadhayau| aitthāyaṃ gathitau gadhītau (syā॰ ka॰) mucchitau ajjhāpan‍nau attanā jātidhammau samānau jātidhammaṃyaiva pariyaisati|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, jarādhammaṃ vadaitha? puttabhariyaṃ, bhikkhavai, jarādhammaṃ, dāsidāsaṃ jarādhammaṃ, ajaiḷakaṃ jarādhammaṃ, kuk‍kuṭasūkaraṃ  jarādhammaṃ, hatthigavāssavaḷavaṃ jarādhammaṃ, jātarūparajataṃ  jarādhammaṃ| jarādhammā haitai, bhikkhavai, upadhayau| aitthāyaṃ gathitau mucchitau ajjhāpan‍nau attanā jarādhammau samānau jarādhammaṃyaiva pariyaisati|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, byādhidhammaṃ vadaitha? puttabhariyaṃ, bhikkhavai, byādhidhammaṃ, dāsidāsaṃ byādhidhammaṃ, ajaiḷakaṃ byādhidhammaṃ, kuk‍kuṭasūkaraṃ byādhidhammaṃ, hatthigavāssavaḷavaṃ byādhidhammaṃ| byādhidhammā haitai, bhikkhavai, upadhayau| aitthāyaṃ gathitau mucchitau ajjhāpan‍nau attanā byādhidhammau samānau byādhidhammaṃyaiva pariyaisati|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, maraṇadhammaṃ vadaitha? puttabhariyaṃ, bhikkhavai, maraṇadhammaṃ, dāsidāsaṃ maraṇadhammaṃ, ajaiḷakaṃ maraṇadhammaṃ, kuk‍kuṭasūkaraṃ maraṇadhammaṃ, hatthigavāssavaḷavaṃ maraṇadhammaṃ| maraṇadhammā haitai, bhikkhavai, upadhayau| aitthāyaṃ gathitau mucchitau ajjhāpan‍nau attanā maraṇadhammau samānau maraṇadhammaṃyaiva pariyaisati|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saukadhammaṃ vadaitha? puttabhariyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saukadhammaṃ, dāsidāsaṃ saukadhammaṃ, ajaiḷakaṃ saukadhammaṃ, kuk‍kuṭasūkaraṃ saukadhammaṃ, hatthigavāssavaḷavaṃ saukadhammaṃ| saukadhammā haitai, bhikkhavai, upadhayau| aitthāyaṃ gathitau mucchitau ajjhāpan‍nau attanā saukadhammau samānau saukadhammaṃyaiva pariyaisati|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saṃkilaisadhammaṃ vadaitha? puttabhariyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃkilaisadhammaṃ, dāsidāsaṃ saṃkilaisadhammaṃ, ajaiḷakaṃ saṃkilaisadhammaṃ, kuk‍kuṭasūkaraṃ saṃkilaisadhammaṃ, hatthigavāssavaḷavaṃ saṃkilaisadhammaṃ, jātarūparajataṃ saṃkilaisadhammaṃ| saṃkilaisadhammā haitai, bhikkhavai, upadhayau| aitthāyaṃ gathitau mucchitau ajjhāpan‍nau attanā saṃkilaisadhammau samānau saṃkilaisadhammaṃyaiva pariyaisati| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, anariyā pariyaisanā|

275. ‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, ariyā pariyaisanā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau attanā jātidhammau samānau jātidhammai ādīnavaṃ viditvā  ajātaṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyaisati, attanā jarādhammau samānau jarādhammai ādīnavaṃ viditvā ajaraṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyaisati, attanā byādhidhammau samānau byādhidhammai ādīnavaṃ viditvā abyādhiṃ anuttaraṃ  yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyaisati, attanā maraṇadhammau samānau maraṇadhammai  ādīnavaṃ viditvā amataṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyaisati, attanā saukadhammau samānau saukadhammai ādīnavaṃ viditvā asaukaṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyaisati, attanā saṃkilaisadhammau samānau saṃkilaisadhammai ādīnavaṃ viditvā asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyaisati| ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyā pariyaisanā|

276. ‘‘ahampi sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, pubbaiva sambaudhā anabhisambuddhau baudhisattauva samānau attanā jātidhammau samānau jātidhammaṃyaiva pariyaisāmi, attanā jarādhammau samānau jarādhammaṃyaiva pariyaisāmi, attanā  byādhidhammau samānau byādhidhammaṃyaiva pariyaisāmi, attanā maraṇadhammau samānau maraṇadhammaṃyaiva pariyaisāmi, attanā saukadhammau samānau saukadhammaṃyaiva pariyaisāmi, attanā saṃkilaisadhammau samānau saṃkilaisadhammaṃyaiva pariyaisāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘kiṃ nu khau ahaṃ attanā jātidhammau samānau jātidhammaṃyaiva pariyaisāmi, attanā jarādhammau samānau…pai॰… byādhidhammau samānau… maraṇadhammau samānau… saukadhammau samānau… attanā saṃkilaisadhammau samānau saṃkilaisadhammaṃyaiva pariyaisāmi? yaṃnūnāhaṃ attanā jātidhammau samānau jātidhammai ādīnavaṃ viditvā ajātaṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyaisaiyyaṃ, attanā jarādhammau samānau jarādhammai ādīnavaṃ viditvā ajaraṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyaisaiyyaṃ, attanā byādhidhammau samānau byādhidhammai ādīnavaṃ viditvā abyādhiṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyaisaiyyaṃ, attanā maraṇadhammau samānau maraṇadhammai ādīnavaṃ viditvā amataṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyaisaiyyaṃ, attanā saukadhammau samānau saukadhammai ādīnavaṃ viditvā asaukaṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyaisaiyyaṃ, attanā saṃkilaisadhammau samānau saṃkilaisadhammai ādīnavaṃ viditvā asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyaisaiyya’nti|

277. ‘‘sau khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, aparaina samayaina daharauva samānau susukāḷakaisau, bhadraina yaubbanaina saman‍nāgatau paṭhamaina vayasā akāmakānaṃ mātāpitūnaṃ assumukhānaṃ rudantānaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni  vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajiṃ| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau kiṃ kusalagavaisī kiṃkusalaṃgavaisī (ka॰) anuttaraṃ santivarapadaṃ pariyaisamānau yaina āḷārau kālāmau tainupasaṅkamiṃ| upasaṅkamitvā āḷāraṃ kālāmaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘icchāmahaṃ, āvusau kālāma, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai  brahmacariyaṃ caritu’nti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhikkhavai, āḷārau kālāmau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘viharatāyasmā; tādisau ayaṃ dhammau yattha viñ‍ñū purisau  nacirassaiva sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyyā’ti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, nacirassaiva khippamaiva taṃ dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇiṃ| sau khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, tāvatakainaiva auṭṭhapahatamattaina lapitalāpanamattaina ñāṇavādañ‍ca vadāmi thairavādañ‍ca, ‘jānāmi passāmī’ti ca paṭijānāmi ahañ‍caiva añ‍ñai ca| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘na khau āḷārau kālāmau imaṃ dhammaṃ kaivalaṃ saddhāmattakaina sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharāmīti pavaidaiti; addhā āḷārau kālāmau imaṃ dhammaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ viharatī’ti|

‘‘atha khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaina āḷārau kālāmau tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā āḷāraṃ kālāmaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘kittāvatā nau, āvusau kālāma, imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharāmīti pavaidaisī’ti upasampaj‍ja pavaidaisīti (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)? aivaṃ vuttai, bhikkhavai, āḷārau kālāmau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ  pavaidaisi| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘na khau āḷārassaiva kālāmassa atthi saddhā, mayhaṃpatthi saddhā; na khau āḷārassaiva kālāmassa atthi vīriyaṃ, mayhaṃpatthi vīriyaṃ; na khau āḷārassaiva kālāmassa atthi sati, mayhaṃpatthi sati; na khau āḷārassaiva kālāmassa atthi samādhi, mayhaṃpatthi samādhi; na khau āḷārassaiva kālāmassa atthi pañ‍ñā, mayhaṃpatthi pañ‍ñā| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaṃ dhammaṃ āḷārau kālāmau sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharāmīti pavaidaiti, tassa dhammassa sacchikiriyāya padahaiyya’nti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, nacirassaiva khippamaiva taṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ|

‘‘atha khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaina āḷārau kālāmau tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā āḷāraṃ kālāmaṃ aitadavaucaṃ –

‘aittāvatā nau, āvusau kālāma, imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja pavaidaisī’ti?

‘aittāvatā  khau ahaṃ, āvusau, imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja pavaidaimī’ti|

‘ahampi  khau, āvusau, aittāvatā imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharāmī’ti|

‘lābhā nau, āvusau, suladdhaṃ nau, āvusau, yai mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ tādisaṃ sabrahmacāriṃ passāma| iti yāhaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja pavaidaimi taṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharasi| yaṃ tvaṃ  dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharasi tamahaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā  sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja pavaidaimi| iti yāhaṃ dhammaṃ jānāmi taṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ jānāsi, yaṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ jānāsi tamahaṃ dhammaṃ jānāmi| iti yādisau ahaṃ tādisau tuvaṃ, yādisau tuvaṃ tādisau ahaṃ| aihi dāni, āvusau, ubhauva santā imaṃ gaṇaṃ pariharāmā’ti| iti khau, bhikkhavai, āḷārau kālāmau ācariyau mai samānau (attanau) ( ) natthi (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) antaivāsiṃ maṃ samānaṃ attanā attanau (sī॰ pī॰) samasamaṃ ṭhapaisi, uḷārāya ca maṃ pūjāya pūjaisi| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘nāyaṃ dhammau nibbidāya na virāgāya na niraudhāya na upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati, yāvadaiva ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanūpapattiyā’ti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, taṃ dhammaṃ analaṅkaritvā tasmā dhammā nibbij‍ja apak‍kamiṃ|

278. ‘‘sau khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, kiṃ kusalagavaisī anuttaraṃ santivarapadaṃ pariyaisamānau yaina udakau uddakau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) rāmaputtau tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā udakaṃ rāmaputtaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘icchāmahaṃ, āvusau āvusau rāma (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰) mahāsattau rāmaputtamaiva avauca, na rāmaṃ, rāmau hi tattha gaṇācariyau bhavaiyya, tadā ca kālaṅkatau asantau| tainaivaittha rāmāyattāni kriyapadāni atītakālavasaina āgatāni, udakau ca rāmaputtau mahāsattassa sabrahmacārītvaiva vuttau, na ācariyauti| ṭīkāyaṃ ca ‘‘pāḷiyaṃ rāmassaiva samāpattilābhitā āgatā na udakassā’’ti ādi pacchābhāgai pakāsitā, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai brahmacariyaṃ caritu’nti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhikkhavai, udakau rāmaputtau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘viharatāyasmā; tādisau ayaṃ dhammau yattha viñ‍ñū purisau nacirassaiva sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyyā’ti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, nacirassaiva khippamaiva taṃ dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇiṃ| sau khau  ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, tāvatakainaiva auṭṭhapahatamattaina lapitalāpanamattaina ñāṇavādañ‍ca vadāmi  thairavādañ‍ca, ‘jānāmi passāmī’ti ca paṭijānāmi ahañ‍caiva añ‍ñai ca| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘na khau rāmau imaṃ dhammaṃ kaivalaṃ saddhāmattakaina sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharāmīti pavaidaisi; addhā rāmau imaṃ dhammaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ vihāsī’ti|

‘‘atha khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaina udakau rāmaputtau tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā udakaṃ rāmaputtaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘kittāvatā nau, āvusau, rāmau imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja  viharāmīti pavaidaisī’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, bhikkhavai, udakau rāmaputtau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ pavaidaisi| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘na khau rāmassaiva ahausi saddhā, mayhaṃpatthi saddhā; na khau rāmassaiva ahausi vīriyaṃ, mayhaṃpatthi vīriyaṃ; na khau rāmassaiva ahausi sati, mayhaṃpatthi sati; na khau rāmassaiva ahausi samādhi, mayhaṃpatthi samādhi, na khau rāmassaiva ahausi pañ‍ñā, mayhaṃpatthi pañ‍ñā| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaṃ dhammaṃ rāmau sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharāmīti pavaidaisi, tassa dhammassa sacchikiriyāya padahaiyya’nti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, nacirassaiva khippamaiva taṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ|

‘‘atha khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaina udakau rāmaputtau tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā udakaṃ rāmaputtaṃ aitadavaucaṃ –

‘aittāvatā nau, āvusau, rāmau imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja pavaidaisī’ti?

‘aittāvatā khau, āvusau, rāmau imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja pavaidaisī’ti|

‘ahampi khau, āvusau, aittāvatā imaṃ  dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharāmī’ti|

‘lābhā nau, āvusau, suladdhaṃ nau, āvusau, yai mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ tādisaṃ sabrahmacāriṃ passāma| iti yaṃ dhammaṃ rāmau sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja pavaidaisi, taṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharasi| yaṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharasi, taṃ dhammaṃ rāmau sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja pavaidaisi| iti yaṃ dhammaṃ rāmau abhiñ‍ñāsi taṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ jānāsi, yaṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ jānāsi, taṃ dhammaṃ rāmau abhiñ‍ñāsi| iti yādisau rāmau ahausi tādisau tuvaṃ, yādisau tuvaṃ tādisau rāmau ahausi| aihi dāni, āvusau, tuvaṃ imaṃ gaṇaṃ pariharā’ti| iti khau, bhikkhavai, udakau rāmaputtau sabrahmacārī mai samānau ācariyaṭṭhānai maṃ ṭhapaisi, uḷārāya ca maṃ pūjāya pūjaisi| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘nāyaṃ dhammau nibbidāya na virāgāya na niraudhāya na upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati, yāvadaiva naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanūpapattiyā’ti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, taṃ dhammaṃ analaṅkaritvā tasmā dhammā nibbij‍ja apak‍kamiṃ|

279. ‘‘sau khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, kiṃ kusalagavaisī anuttaraṃ santivarapadaṃ pariyaisamānau magadhaisu anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina uruvailā sainānigamau tadavasariṃ| tatthaddasaṃ  ramaṇīyaṃ bhūmibhāgaṃ, pāsādikañ‍ca vanasaṇḍaṃ, nadiñ‍ca sandantiṃ saitakaṃ supatitthaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ, samantā sāmantā (?) ca gaucaragāmaṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘ramaṇīyau vata, bhau, bhūmibhāgau, pāsādikau ca vanasaṇḍau, nadī ca sandati saitakā supatitthā ramaṇīyā, samantā ca gaucaragāmau| alaṃ vatidaṃ kulaputtassa padhānatthikassa padhānāyā’ti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, tatthaiva nisīdiṃ – alamidaṃ padhānāyāti|

280. ‘‘sau khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, attanā jātidhammau samānau jātidhammai ādīnavaṃ viditvā ajātaṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyaisamānau ajātaṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃ, attanā jarādhammau samānau jarādhammai ādīnavaṃ viditvā ajaraṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyaisamānau ajaraṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃ, attanā byādhidhammau samānau byādhidhammai ādīnavaṃ viditvā abyādhiṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyaisamānau abyādhiṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃ, attanā maraṇadhammau samānau maraṇadhammai ādīnavaṃ viditvā amataṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃ, attanā saukadhammau samānau saukadhammai ādīnavaṃ viditvā asaukaṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃ, attanā saṃkilaisadhammau samānau saṃkilaisadhammai ādīnavaṃ viditvā asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyaisamānau asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃ| ñāṇañ‍ca pana mai dassanaṃ udapādi – ‘akuppā mai vimutti, ayamantimā jāti, natthi dāni punabbhavau’ti|

281. ‘‘tassa  mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘adhigatau khau myāyaṃ dhammau gambhīrau duddasau duranubaudhau santau paṇītau atak‍kāvacarau nipuṇau paṇḍitavaidanīyau| ālayarāmā khau panāyaṃ pajā ālayaratā ālayasammuditā| ālayarāmā khau panāyaṃ  pajā ālayaratāya ālayasammuditāya duddasaṃ idaṃ ṭhānaṃ yadidaṃ – idappac‍cayatā paṭic‍casamuppādau| idampi khau ṭhānaṃ duddasaṃ yadidaṃ – sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhākkhayau  virāgau niraudhau nibbānaṃ| ahañ‍caiva  khau pana dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ, parai ca mai na ājānaiyyuṃ, sau mamassa kilamathau, sā mamassa vihaisā’ti| apissu maṃ, bhikkhavai, imā anacchariyā gāthāyau paṭibhaṃsu pubbai assutapubbā –

‘kicchaina mai adhigataṃ, halaṃ dāni pakāsituṃ|

rāgadausaparaitaihi, nāyaṃ dhammau susambudhau||

‘paṭisautagāmiṃ nipuṇaṃ, gambhīraṃ duddasaṃ aṇuṃ|

rāgarattā na dakkhanti, tamaukhandhaina āvuṭā’’’ti āvaṭāti (sī॰), āvutā (syā॰)||

282. ‘‘itiha mai, bhikkhavai, paṭisañ‍cikkhatau appaussuk‍katāya cittaṃ namati, nau dhammadaisanāya| atha khau, bhikkhavai, brahmunau sahampatissa mama caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya aitadahausi – ‘nassati vata bhau laukau, vinassati vata bhau laukau, yatra hi nāma tathāgatassa arahatau sammāsambuddhassa appaussuk‍katāya cittaṃ namati namissati (?), nau dhammadaisanāyā’ti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, brahmā sahampati – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaiva – brahmalaukai antarahitau mama puratau pāturahausi| atha khau, bhikkhavai, brahmā sahampati aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaināhaṃ tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘daisaitu, bhantai, bhagavā dhammaṃ, daisaitu  sugatau dhammaṃ| santi sattā apparajakkhajātikā, assavanatā dhammassa parihāyanti| bhavissanti dhammassa añ‍ñātārau’ti| idamavauca, bhikkhavai, brahmā sahampati| idaṃ vatvā athāparaṃ aitadavauca –

‘pāturahausi  magadhaisu pubbai,

dhammau asuddhau samalaihi cintitau|

apāpuraitaṃ avāpuraitaṃ (sī॰) amatassa dvāraṃ,

suṇantu dhammaṃ vimalainānubuddhaṃ||

‘sailai  yathā pabbatamuddhaniṭṭhitau,

yathāpi passai janataṃ samantatau|

tathūpamaṃ dhammamayaṃ sumaidha,

pāsādamāruyha samantacakkhu|

saukāvatiṇṇaṃ saukāvakiṇṇaṃ (syā॰) janatamapaitasaukau,

avaikkhassu jātijarābhibhūtaṃ||

‘uṭṭhaihi  vīra vijitasaṅgāma,

satthavāha aṇaṇa vicara laukai|

daisassu daisaitu (syā॰ ka॰) bhagavā dhammaṃ,

añ‍ñātārau bhavissantī’’’ti||

283. ‘‘atha khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, brahmunau ca ajjhaisanaṃ viditvā sattaisu ca kāruñ‍ñataṃ paṭic‍ca buddhacakkhunā laukaṃ vaulaukaisiṃ| addasaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, buddhacakkhunā laukaṃ vaulaukaintau sattai apparajakkhai  mahārajakkhai, tikkhindriyai mudindriyai, svākārai dvākārai, suviñ‍ñāpayai duviñ‍ñāpayai, appaikac‍cai paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinai dassāvinau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) viharantai, appaikac‍cai na paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinai dassāvinau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) viharantai| saiyyathāpi nāma uppaliniyaṃ vā paduminiyaṃ vā puṇḍarīkiniyaṃ vā appaikac‍cāni uppalāni vā padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā udakai jātāni udakai saṃvaḍḍhāni udakānuggatāni antaunimuggapausīni, appaikac‍cāni uppalāni vā padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā udakai jātāni udakai saṃvaḍḍhāni udakānuggatāni samaudakaṃ ṭhitāni, appaikac‍cāni uppalāni vā padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā udakai jātāni udakai saṃvaḍḍhāni udakaṃ ac‍cuggamma ṭhitāni tiṭṭhanti (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) anupalittāni udakaina; aivamaiva khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, buddhacakkhunā  laukaṃ vaulaukaintau addasaṃ sattai apparajakkhai mahārajakkhai, tikkhindriyai mudindriyai, svākārai dvākārai, suviñ‍ñāpayai duviñ‍ñāpayai, appaikac‍cai paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinai viharantai, appaikac‍cai na paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinai viharantai| atha khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, brahmānaṃ sahampatiṃ gāthāya pac‍cabhāsiṃ –

‘apārutā  taisaṃ amatassa dvārā,

yai sautavantau pamuñ‍cantu saddhaṃ|

vihiṃsasañ‍ñī paguṇaṃ na bhāsiṃ,

dhammaṃ paṇītaṃ manujaisu brahmai’’’ti||

‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, brahmā sahampati ‘katāvakāsau khaumhi bhagavatā dhammadaisanāyā’ti maṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyi|

284. ‘‘tassa  mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘kassa nu khau ahaṃ paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ; kau imaṃ dhammaṃ khippamaiva ājānissatī’ti? tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘ayaṃ khau āḷārau kālāmau paṇḍitau viyattau maidhāvī dīgharattaṃ apparajakkhajātikau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ āḷārassa kālāmassa  paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ| sau imaṃ dhammaṃ khippamaiva ājānissatī’ti| atha khau maṃ, bhikkhavai, daivatā upasaṅkamitvā aitadavauca – ‘sattāhakālaṅkatau, bhantai, āḷārau kālāmau’ti| ñāṇañ‍ca pana mai dassanaṃ udapādi – ‘sattāhakālaṅkatau āḷārau kālāmau’ti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘mahājāniyau khau āḷārau kālāmau| sacai hi sau imaṃ dhammaṃ suṇaiyya, khippamaiva ājānaiyyā’ti|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘kassa nu khau ahaṃ paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ; kau imaṃ dhammaṃ khippamaiva ājānissatī’ti? tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘ayaṃ khau udakau rāmaputtau paṇḍitau viyattau maidhāvī dīgharattaṃ apparajakkhajātikau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ udakassa rāmaputtassa paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ| sau imaṃ dhammaṃ khippamaiva ājānissatī’ti| atha khau maṃ, bhikkhavai, daivatā upasaṅkamitvā aitadavauca – ‘abhidausakālaṅkatau, bhantai, udakau rāmaputtau’ti| ñāṇañ‍ca pana mai dassanaṃ udapādi – ‘abhidausakālaṅkatau udakau rāmaputtau’ti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘mahājāniyau khau udakau rāmaputtau| sacai hi sau imaṃ dhammaṃ suṇaiyya, khippamaiva ājānaiyyā’ti|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘kassa nu khau ahaṃ paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiyyaṃ; kau imaṃ dhammaṃ khippamaiva ājānissatī’ti? tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘bahukārā  khau mai pañ‍cavaggiyā bhikkhū, yai maṃ padhānapahitattaṃ upaṭṭhahiṃsu| yaṃnūnāhaṃ pañ‍cavaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiyya’nti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘kahaṃ nu khau aitarahi pañ‍cavaggiyā bhikkhū viharantī’ti? addasaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina pañ‍cavaggiyai bhikkhū bārāṇasiyaṃ viharantai isipatanai migadāyai| atha khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, uruvailāyaṃ yathābhirantaṃ viharitvā yaina bārāṇasī taina cārikaṃ pak‍kamiṃ pak‍kāmiṃ (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰)|

285. ‘‘addasā khau maṃ, bhikkhavai, upakau ājīvakau antarā ājīvikau (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) ca gayaṃ antarā ca baudhiṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍naṃ| disvāna maṃ aitadavauca – ‘vippasan‍nāni khau tai, āvusau, indriyāni, parisuddhau chavivaṇṇau pariyaudātau! kaṃsi tvaṃ, āvusau, uddissa pabbajitau, kau vā tai satthā, kassa vā tvaṃ dhammaṃ raucaisī’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, upakaṃ ājīvakaṃ gāthāhi ajjhabhāsiṃ –

‘sabbābhibhū sabbavidūhamasmi, sabbaisu dhammaisu anūpalittau|

sabbañ‍jahau taṇhākkhayai vimuttau, sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya kamuddisaiyyaṃ||

‘na  mai ācariyau atthi, sadisau mai na vij‍jati|

sadaivakasmiṃ laukasmiṃ, natthi mai paṭipuggalau||

‘ahañhi arahā laukai, ahaṃ satthā anuttarau|

aikaumhi sammāsambuddhau, sītibhūtausmi nibbutau||

‘dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattaituṃ, gacchāmi kāsinaṃ puraṃ|

andhībhūtasmiṃ andhabhūtasmiṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) laukasmiṃ, āhañchaṃ amatadundubhi’nti||

‘yathā khau tvaṃ, āvusau, paṭijānāsi, arahasi anantajinau’ti!

‘mādisā  vai jinā haunti, yai pattā āsavakkhayaṃ|

jitā mai pāpakā dhammā, tasmāhamupaka jinau’ti||

‘‘aivaṃ  vuttai, bhikkhavai, upakau ājīvakau ‘hupaiyyapāvusau’ti huvaiyyapāvusau (sī॰ pī॰), huvaiyyāvusau (syā॰) vatvā sīsaṃ aukampaitvā ummaggaṃ gahaitvā pak‍kāmi|

286. ‘‘atha khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina bārāṇasī isipatanaṃ migadāyau yaina pañ‍cavaggiyā bhikkhū tainupasaṅkamiṃ| addasaṃsu khau maṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cavaggiyā bhikkhū dūratau āgacchantaṃ| disvāna añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ saṇṭhapaisuṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ katikaṃ saṇṭhapaisuṃ (vinayapiṭakai mahāvaggai) – ‘ayaṃ khau, āvusau, samaṇau gautamau āgacchati bāhul‍likau bāhulikau (sī॰ pī॰) sāratthadīpanīṭīkāya samaiti padhānavibbhantau āvattau bāhul‍lāya| sau naiva abhivādaitabbau, na pac‍cuṭṭhātabbau; nāssa pattacīvaraṃ paṭiggahaitabbaṃ| api ca khau āsanaṃ ṭhapaitabbaṃ, sacai ākaṅkhissati nisīdissatī’ti| yathā yathā khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, upasaṅkamiṃ tathā  tathā pañ‍cavaggiyā bhikkhū nāsakkhiṃsu sakāya katikāya saṇṭhātuṃ| appaikac‍cai maṃ pac‍cuggantvā pattacīvaraṃ paṭiggahaisuṃ, appaikac‍cai āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaisuṃ, appaikac‍cai pādaudakaṃ upaṭṭhapaisuṃ| api ca khau maṃ nāmaina ca āvusauvādaina ca samudācaranti|

‘‘aivaṃ vuttai, ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cavaggiyai bhikkhū aitadavaucaṃ – ‘mā, bhikkhavai, tathāgataṃ nāmaina ca āvusauvādaina ca samudācaratha samudācarittha (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)| arahaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau sammāsambuddhau| audahatha, bhikkhavai, sautaṃ, amatamadhigataṃ, ahamanusāsāmi, ahaṃ dhammaṃ daisaimi| yathānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipaj‍jamānā nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissathā’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cavaggiyā bhikkhū maṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘tāyapi khau tvaṃ, āvusau gautama, iriyāya tāya paṭipadāya tāya duk‍karakārikāya nājjhagamā uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ, kiṃ pana tvaṃ aitarahi bāhul‍likau padhānavibbhantau āvattau bāhul‍lāya adhigamissasi uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisa’nti? aivaṃ vuttai, ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cavaggiyai bhikkhū aitadavaucaṃ – ‘na, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau bāhul‍likau, na padhānavibbhantau, na āvattau bāhul‍lāya| arahaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau sammāsambuddhau| audahatha, bhikkhavai, sautaṃ, amatamadhigataṃ, ahamanusāsāmi, ahaṃ dhammaṃ daisaimi| yathānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipaj‍jamānā nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā  sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissathā’ti| dutiyampi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cavaggiyā bhikkhū maṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘tāyapi khau tvaṃ, āvusau gautama, iriyāya tāya paṭipadāya tāya duk‍karakārikāya nājjhagamā uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ, kiṃ pana tvaṃ aitarahi bāhul‍likau padhānavibbhantau āvattau bāhul‍lāya adhigamissasi uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisa’nti? dutiyampi khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cavaggiyai bhikkhū aitadavaucaṃ – ‘na, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau bāhul‍likau…pai॰… upasampaj‍ja viharissathā’ti| tatiyampi khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cavaggiyā bhikkhū maṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘tāyapi khau tvaṃ, āvusau gautama, iriyāya tāya paṭipadāya tāya duk‍karakārikāya nājjhagamā uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ, kiṃ pana tvaṃ aitarahi bāhul‍likau padhānavibbhantau āvattau bāhul‍lāya adhigamissasi uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisa’nti?

‘‘aivaṃ vuttai, ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cavaggiyai bhikkhū aitadavaucaṃ – ‘abhijānātha mai nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, itau pubbai aivarūpaṃ pabhāvitamaita’nti bhāsitamaitanti (sī॰ syā॰ vinayaipi)? ‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’| ‘arahaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau sammāsambuddhau| audahatha, bhikkhavai, sautaṃ, amatamadhigataṃ, ahamanusāsāmi, ahaṃ dhammaṃ daisaimi| yathānusiṭṭhaṃ tathā paṭipaj‍jamānā nacirassaiva  – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā  upasampaj‍ja viharissathā’ti|

‘‘asakkhiṃ khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cavaggiyai bhikkhū sañ‍ñāpaituṃ| dvaipi sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū auvadāmi, tayau bhikkhū piṇḍāya caranti| yaṃ tayau bhikkhū piṇḍāya caritvā āharanti taina chabbaggiyā chabbaggā (sī॰ syā॰) yāpaima| tayaupi sudaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhū auvadāmi, dvai bhikkhū piṇḍāya caranti| yaṃ dvai bhikkhū piṇḍāya caritvā āharanti taina chabbaggiyā yāpaima| atha khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cavaggiyā  bhikkhū mayā aivaṃ auvadiyamānā aivaṃ anusāsiyamānā attanā jātidhammā samānā jātidhammai ādīnavaṃ viditvā ajātaṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyaisamānā ajātaṃ anuttaraṃ  yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃsu, attanā jarādhammā samānā jarādhammai ādīnavaṃ viditvā ajaraṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyaisamānā ajaraṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃsu, attanā byādhidhammā samānā…pai॰… attanā maraṇadhammā samānā… attanā saukadhammā samānā… attanā saṃkilaisadhammā samānā saṃkilaisadhammai ādīnavaṃ viditvā asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ pariyaisamānā asaṃkiliṭṭhaṃ anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ nibbānaṃ ajjhagamaṃsu| ñāṇañ‍ca pana naisaṃ dassanaṃ udapādi – ‘akuppā nau  vimutti akuppā naisaṃ vimutti (ka॰), ayamantimā jāti, natthi dāni punabbhavau’ti|

287. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, kāmaguṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā imai pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai gathitā mucchitā ajjhaupan‍nā anādīnavadassāvinau anissaraṇapañ‍ñā paribhuñ‍janti, tai aivamassu vaiditabbā – ‘anayamāpan‍nā byasanamāpan‍nā yathākāmakaraṇīyā pāpimatau’ pāpimatau’’ti (?)| ‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ārañ‍ñakau magau baddhau pāsarāsiṃ adhisayaiyya| sau aivamassa vaiditabbau – anayamāpan‍nau byasanamāpan‍nau yathākāmakaraṇīyau luddassa| āgacchantai ca pana luddai yaina kāmaṃ na pak‍kamissatī’ti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai hi kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā imai pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai gathitā mucchitā ajjhaupan‍nā anādīnavadassāvinau anissaraṇapañ‍ñā paribhuñ‍janti, tai aivamassu vaiditabbā – ‘anayamāpan‍nā byasanamāpan‍nā yathākāmakaraṇīyā pāpimatau’| yai ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā imai pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai agathitā amucchitā anajjhaupan‍nā ādīnavadassāvinau nissaraṇapañ‍ñā  paribhuñ‍janti, tai aivamassu vaiditabbā – ‘na anayamāpan‍nā na byasanamāpan‍nā na  yathākāmakaraṇīyā pāpimatau’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ārañ‍ñakau magau abaddhau pāsarāsiṃ adhisayaiyya| sau aivamassa vaiditabbau – ‘na anayamāpan‍nau na byasanamāpan‍nau na yathākāmakaraṇīyau luddassa| āgacchantai ca pana luddai yaina kāmaṃ pak‍kamissatī’ti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai hi kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā imai pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai agathitā amucchitā anajjhaupan‍nā ādīnavadassāvinau nissaraṇapañ‍ñā paribhuñ‍janti, tai aivamassu vaiditabbā – ‘na anayamāpan‍nā na byasanamāpan‍nā na  yathākāmakaraṇīyā pāpimatau’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ārañ‍ñakau magau arañ‍ñai pavanai caramānau vissatthau gacchati, vissatthau tiṭṭhati, vissatthau nisīdati, vissatthau saiyyaṃ kappaiti| taṃ kissa haitu? anāpāthagatau, bhikkhavai, luddassa| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu andhamakāsi māraṃ apadaṃ, vadhitvā māracakkhuṃ adassanaṃ gatau pāpimatau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai…pai॰… pāpimatau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati, satau ca sampajānau, sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai…pai॰… pāpimatau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai…pai॰… pāpimatau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā  nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai…pai॰… pāpimatau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai…pai॰… pāpimatau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai…pai॰… pāpimatau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma  naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai…pai॰… pāpimatau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā haunti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu andhamakāsi māraṃ apadaṃ, vadhitvā māracakkhuṃ adassanaṃ gatau pāpimatau| tiṇṇau laukai visattikaṃ vissatthau gacchati, vissatthau tiṭṭhati, vissatthau nisīdati, vissatthau saiyyaṃ kappaiti| taṃ kissa haitu? anāpāthagatau, bhikkhavai, pāpimatau’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

pāsarāsisuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. cūḷahatthipadaupamasuttaṃ

288. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina jāṇussauṇi brāhmaṇau sabbasaitaina vaḷavābhirathaina vaḷabhīrathaina (sī॰ pī॰) sāvatthiyā niyyāti divādivassa| addasā khau jāṇussauṇi brāhmaṇau pilautikaṃ paribbājakaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna pilautikaṃ paribbājakaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘handa, kutau nu bhavaṃ vacchāyanau āgacchati divādivassā’’ti?

‘‘itau hi khau ahaṃ, bhau, āgacchāmi samaṇassa gautamassa santikā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñati, bhavaṃ vacchāyanau, samaṇassa gautamassa pañ‍ñāvaiyyattiyaṃ?

‘‘paṇḍitau mañ‍ñai’’ti|

‘‘kau cāhaṃ, bhau, kau ca samaṇassa gautamassa pañ‍ñāvaiyyattiyaṃ jānissāmi! saupi nūnassa tādisauva yau samaṇassa gautamassa pañ‍ñāvaiyyattiyaṃ jānaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘uḷārāya khalu bhavaṃ vacchāyanau samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ pasaṃsāya pasaṃsatī’’ti|

‘‘kau cāhaṃ, bhau, kau ca samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ pasaṃsissāmi?

‘‘pasatthapasatthauva sau bhavaṃ gautamau saiṭṭhau daivamanussāna’’nti|

‘‘kaṃ pana bhavaṃ vacchāyanau atthavasaṃ sampassamānau samaṇai gautamai aivaṃ abhippasan‍nau’’ti abhippasan‍nau hautīti (syā॰)?

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhau, kusalau nāgavanikau nāgavanaṃ pavisaiyya| sau passaiyya nāgavanai  mahantaṃ  hatthipadaṃ, dīghatau ca āyataṃ, tiriyañ‍ca vitthataṃ| sau niṭṭhaṃ gacchaiyya – ‘mahā vata, bhau, nāgau’ti| aivamaiva khau ahaṃ, bhau, yatau addasaṃ samaṇai gautamai cattāri padāni athāhaṃ niṭṭhamagamaṃ – ‘sammāsambuddhau bhagavā, svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau, suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau’ti|

289. ‘‘katamāni cattāri? idhāhaṃ, bhau, passāmi aikac‍cai khattiyapaṇḍitai nipuṇai kataparappavādai vālavaidhirūpai, tai bhindantā vaubhindantā (sī॰ pī॰) vi + ava + bhindantā mañ‍ñai caranti pañ‍ñāgataina diṭṭhigatāni| tai suṇanti – ‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau amukaṃ nāma gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā ausarissatī’ti| tai pañhaṃ abhisaṅkharaunti – ‘imaṃ mayaṃ pañhaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ upasaṅkamitvā pucchissāma| aivaṃ cai nau puṭṭhau aivaṃ byākarissati, aivamassa mayaṃ vādaṃ āraupaissāma| aivaṃ caipi nau puṭṭhau aivaṃ byākarissati, aivampissa mayaṃ vādaṃ āraupaissāmā’ti| tai suṇanti – ‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau amukaṃ nāma gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā ausaṭau’ti| tai yaina samaṇau gautamau tainupasaṅkamanti| tai samaṇau gautamau dhammiyā kathāya sandassaiti samādapaiti samuttaijaiti sampahaṃsaiti| tai samaṇaina gautamaina dhammiyā kathāya sandassitā samādapitā samuttaijitā sampahaṃsitā na caiva samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ pañhaṃ pucchanti, kutaussa kutassa (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) vādaṃ āraupaissanti? añ‍ñadatthu  samaṇassaiva gautamassa sāvakā sampaj‍janti| yadāhaṃ, bhau, samaṇai gautamai imaṃ paṭhamaṃ  padaṃ addasaṃ athāhaṃ niṭṭhamagamaṃ – ‘sammāsambuddhau bhagavā, svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau, suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau’ti|

‘‘puna caparāhaṃ, bhau, passāmi idhaikac‍cai brāhmaṇapaṇḍitai…pai॰… gahapatipaṇḍitai…pai॰… samaṇapaṇḍitai nipuṇai kataparappavādai vālavaidhirūpai tai bhindantā mañ‍ñai caranti pañ‍ñāgataina diṭṭhigatāni| tai suṇanti – ‘samaṇau khalu bhau gautamau amukaṃ nāma  gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā ausarissatī’ti| tai pañhaṃ abhisaṅkharaunti ‘imaṃ mayaṃ pañhaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ upasaṅkamitvā pucchissāma| aivaṃ cai nau puṭṭhau aivaṃ byākarissati, aivamassa mayaṃ vādaṃ āraupaissāma| aivaṃ caipi nau puṭṭhau aivaṃ byākarissati, aivaṃpissa mayaṃ vādaṃ āraupaissāmā’ti| tai suṇanti ‘samaṇau khalu bhau gautamau amukaṃ nāma gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā ausaṭau’ti| tai yaina samaṇau gautamau tainupasaṅkamanti| tai samaṇau gautamau dhammiyā kathāya sandassaiti samādapaiti samuttaijaiti sampahaṃsaiti| tai samaṇaina gautamaina dhammiyā kathāya sandassitā samādapitā samuttaijitā sampahaṃsitā na caiva samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ pañhaṃ pucchanti, kutaussa  vādaṃ āraupaissanti? añ‍ñadatthu samaṇaṃyaiva gautamaṃ aukāsaṃ yācanti agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jāya| tai samaṇau gautamau pabbājaiti pabbājaiti upasampādaiti (sī॰)| tai tattha pabbajitā samānā vūpakaṭṭhā appamattā ātāpinau pahitattā viharantā  nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti| tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘manaṃ vata, bhau, anassāma, manaṃ vata, bhau, panassāma; mayañhi pubbai assamaṇāva samānā samaṇamhāti paṭijānimha, abrāhmaṇāva samānā brāhmaṇamhāti paṭijānimha, anarahantauva samānā arahantamhāti paṭijānimha| idāni khaumha samaṇā, idāni khaumha brāhmaṇā, idāni khaumha arahantau’ti| yadāhaṃ, bhau, samaṇai gautamai imaṃ catutthaṃ padaṃ addasaṃ athāhaṃ niṭṭhamagamaṃ – ‘sammāsambuddhau bhagavā, svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau, suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau’’’ti|

‘‘yatau  khau ahaṃ, bhau, samaṇai gautamai imāni cattāri padāni addasaṃ athāhaṃ niṭṭhamagamaṃ – ‘sammāsambuddhau bhagavā, svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau, suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau’’’ti|

290. aivaṃ vuttai, jāṇussauṇi brāhmaṇau sabbasaitā vaḷavābhirathā aurauhitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā tikkhattuṃ udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘‘namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa; namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa; namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| appaiva nāma mayampi kadāci karahaci  taina bhautā gautamaina saddhiṃ samāgacchaiyyāma, appaiva nāma siyā kaucidaiva kathāsal‍lāpau’’ti! atha khau jāṇussauṇi brāhmaṇau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau jāṇussauṇi brāhmaṇau yāvatakau ahausi pilautikaina paribbājakaina saddhiṃ kathāsal‍lāpau taṃ sabbaṃ bhagavatau āraucaisi| aivaṃ vuttai, bhagavā jāṇussauṇiṃ brāhmaṇaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau, brāhmaṇa, aittāvatā hatthipadaupamau vitthāraina paripūrau hauti| api ca, brāhmaṇa, yathā hatthipadaupamau vitthāraina paripūrau hauti taṃ suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau jāṇussauṇi brāhmaṇau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

291. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, nāgavanikau nāgavanaṃ pavisaiyya| sau passaiyya nāgavanai mahantaṃ  hatthipadaṃ, dīghatau ca āyataṃ, tiriyañ‍ca vitthataṃ| yau hauti kusalau nāgavanikau naiva tāva niṭṭhaṃ gacchati – ‘mahā vata, bhau, nāgau’ti| taṃ kissa haitu? santi hi, brāhmaṇa, nāgavanai vāmanikā nāma hatthiniyau mahāpadā, tāsaṃ paitaṃ padaṃ assāti|

‘‘sau tamanugacchati| tamanugacchantau passati nāgavanai mahantaṃ hatthipadaṃ, dīghatau ca āyataṃ, tiriyañ‍ca vitthataṃ, uc‍cā ca nisaivitaṃ| yau hauti kusalau nāgavanikau naiva tāva niṭṭhaṃ gacchati – ‘mahā vata, bhau, nāgau’ti| taṃ kissa haitu? santi hi, brāhmaṇa, nāgavanai uc‍cā kāḷārikā nāma hatthiniyau mahāpadā, tāsaṃ paitaṃ padaṃ assāti|

‘‘sau  tamanugacchati| tamanugacchantau passati nāgavanai  mahantaṃ hatthipadaṃ, dīghatau ca āyataṃ, tiriyañ‍ca vitthataṃ, uc‍cā ca nisaivitaṃ, uc‍cā ca dantaihi ārañ‍jitāni| yau hauti kusalau nāgavanikau naiva tāva niṭṭhaṃ gacchati – ‘mahā vata, bhau, nāgau’ti| taṃ kissa haitu? santi hi, brāhmaṇa, nāgavanai uc‍cā kaṇairukā nāma hatthiniyau mahāpadā, tāsaṃ paitaṃ padaṃ assāti|

‘‘sau tamanugacchati| tamanugacchantau passati nāgavanai mahantaṃ hatthipadaṃ, dīghatau ca āyataṃ, tiriyañ‍ca vitthataṃ, uc‍cā ca nisaivitaṃ, uc‍cā ca dantaihi ārañ‍jitāni, uc‍cā ca sākhābhaṅgaṃ| tañ‍ca nāgaṃ passati rukkhamūlagataṃ vā abbhaukāsagataṃ vā gacchantaṃ vā tiṭṭhantaṃ vā nisin‍naṃ vā nipan‍naṃ vā| sau niṭṭhaṃ gacchati – ‘ayamaiva sau mahānāgau’ti|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, brāhmaṇa, idha tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ; kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| taṃ dhammaṃ suṇāti gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vā kulai pac‍cājātau| sau taṃ dhammaṃ sutvā tathāgatai saddhaṃ paṭilabhati| sau taina saddhāpaṭilābhaina saman‍nāgatau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘sambādhau gharāvāsau rajaupathau, abbhaukāsau pabbaj‍jā| nayidaṃ sukaraṃ agāraṃ  ajjhāvasatā aikantaparipuṇṇaṃ aikantaparisuddhaṃ saṅkhalikhitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajaiyya’nti| sau aparaina samayaina appaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ pahāya mahantaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ pahāya appaṃ vā ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya mahantaṃ vā ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati|

292. ‘‘sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāsājīvasamāpan‍nau pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, nihitadaṇḍau  nihitasatthau laj‍jī dayāpan‍nau sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharati|

‘‘adin‍nādānaṃ pahāya adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti din‍nādāyī din‍napāṭikaṅkhī| athainaina sucibhūtaina attanā viharati|

‘‘abrahmacariyaṃ pahāya brahmacārī hauti ārācārī viratau maithunā gāmadhammā|

‘‘musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti sac‍cavādī sac‍casandhau thaitau ṭhaitau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) pac‍cayikau avisaṃvādakau laukassa|

‘‘pisuṇaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, itau sutvā na amutra akkhātā imaisaṃ bhaidāya, amutra vā sutvā na imaisaṃ akkhātā amūsaṃ bhaidāya| iti bhin‍nānaṃ vā sandhātā sahitānaṃ vā anuppadātā, samaggārāmau samaggaratau samagganandī samaggakaraṇiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti|

‘‘pharusaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti| yā sā vācā nailā kaṇṇasukhā paimanīyā hadayaṅgamā paurī bahujanakantā bahujanamanāpā  tathārūpiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti|

‘‘samphappalāpaṃ pahāya samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti kālavādī bhūtavādī atthavādī dhammavādī vinayavādī, nidhānavatiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā kālaina sāpadaisaṃ pariyantavatiṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ|

293. ‘‘sau  bījagāmabhūtagāmasamārambhā paṭiviratau hauti, aikabhattikau hauti rattūparatau, viratau vikālabhaujanā, nac‍cagītavāditavisūkadassanā paṭiviratau hauti, mālāgandhavilaipanadhāraṇamaṇḍanavibhūsanaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti, uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanā paṭiviratau hauti, jātarūparajatapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti, āmakadhañ‍ñapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti, āmakamaṃsapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti, itthikumārikapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti, dāsidāsapaṭiggahaṇā  paṭiviratau hauti, ajaiḷakapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti, kuk‍kuṭasūkarapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti, hatthigavāssavaḷavāpaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti, khaittavatthupaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti, dūtaiyyapahiṇagamanānuyaugā paṭiviratau hauti, kayavik‍kayā paṭiviratau hauti, tulākūṭakaṃsakūṭamānakūṭā paṭiviratau hauti, uk‍kauṭanavañ‍cananikatisāciyaugā paṭiviratau hauti, chaidanavadhabandhanaviparāmausaālaupasahasākārā sāhasākārā (ka॰) paṭiviratau hauti imassa anantaraṃ ‘‘sau iminā ariyaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau ajjhattaṃ anavaj‍jasukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaitī’’ti vacanaṃ dīghanikāyai āgataṃ, taṃ idha santausakathāvasānai āgataṃ, sā ca santausakathā tattha satisampajañ‍ñānantaramaiva āgatā|

294. ‘‘sau santuṭṭhau hauti kāyaparihārikaina cīvaraina kucchiparihārikaina piṇḍapātaina| sau yaina yainaiva  pak‍kamati samādāyaiva pak‍kamati| saiyyathāpi nāma pakkhī sakuṇau yaina yainaiva ḍaiti sapattabhārauva ḍaiti, aivamaiva bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti kāyaparihārikaina cīvaraina kucchiparihārikaina piṇḍapātaina| sau yaina yainaiva pak‍kamati samādāyaiva pak‍kamati| sau iminā ariyaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau ajjhattaṃ anavaj‍jasukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

295. ‘‘sau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati  manindriyaṃ, manindriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sau iminā ariyaina indriyasaṃvaraina saman‍nāgatau ajjhattaṃ abyāsaikasukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

‘‘sau abhik‍kantai paṭik‍kantai sampajānakārī hauti, ālaukitai vilaukitai sampajānakārī hauti, samiñ‍jitai pasāritai sampajānakārī hauti, saṃghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇai sampajānakārī hauti, asitai pītai  khāyitai sāyitai sampajānakārī hauti, uc‍cārapassāvakammai sampajānakārī hauti, gatai ṭhitai nisin‍nai suttai jāgaritai bhāsitai tuṇhībhāvai  sampajānakārī hauti|

296. ‘‘sau iminā ca ariyaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau, (imāya ca ariyāya santuṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau) ( ) aitthantarai pāṭhau idha nadissati, catuk‍kaṅguttarai pana imasmiṃ ṭhānai dissati, aṭṭhakathāṭīkāsu ca tadatthau pakāsitau| tasmā sau aittha paṭīpūritau iminā ca ariyaina indriyasaṃvaraina saman‍nāgatau, iminā ca ariyaina satisampajañ‍ñaina saman‍nāgatau vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajati arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ palālapuñ‍jaṃ| sau pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā, ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya, parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau abhijjhaṃ laukai pahāya vigatābhijjhaina caitasā viharati, abhijjhāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| byāpādappadausaṃ pahāya abyāpan‍nacittau viharati, sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī byāpādappadausā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| thinamiddhaṃ pahāya vigatathinamiddhau viharati ālaukasañ‍ñī satau sampajānau, thinamiddhā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ pahāya anuddhatau viharati, ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittau uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| vicikicchaṃ pahāya tiṇṇavicikicchau viharati akathaṃkathī kusalaisu dhammaisu, vicikicchāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti|

297. ‘‘sau imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai, vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idampi vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, tathāgatapadaṃ itipi, tathāgatanisaivitaṃ itipi, tathāgatārañ‍jitaṃ itipi| na tvaiva tāva ariyasāvakau niṭṭhaṃ gacchati – ‘sammāsambuddhau  bhagavā, svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau, suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau’ti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idampi vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa…pai॰…  suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau’ti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau, sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti  tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idampi vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa…pai॰… suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau’ti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā, pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā, adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| idampi vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, tathāgatapadaṃ itipi, tathāgatanisaivitaṃ itipi, tathāgatārañ‍jitaṃ itipi| na tvaiva tāva ariyasāvakau niṭṭhaṃ gacchati – ‘sammāsambuddhau bhagavā, svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau, suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau’ti|

298. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ, dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| idampi vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, tathāgatapadaṃ itipi, tathāgatanisaivitaṃ itipi, tathāgatārañ‍jitaṃ itipi| na tvaiva tāva ariyasāvakau niṭṭhaṃ gacchati – ‘sammāsambuddhau bhagavā, svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau, suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau’ti|

‘‘sau  aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai sattānaṃ cutūpapātañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau dibbaina cakkhunā  visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti| idampi vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, tathāgatapadaṃ itipi, tathāgatanisaivitaṃ itipi, tathāgatārañ‍jitaṃ itipi| na tvaiva tāva ariyasāvakau niṭṭhaṃ gacchati – ‘sammāsambuddhau bhagavā, svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau, suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau’ti|

299. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya  cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ‘imai āsavā’ti  yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| idampi vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, tathāgatapadaṃ itipi, tathāgatanisaivitaṃ itipi, tathāgatārañ‍jitaṃ itipi| na tvaiva tāva ariyasāvakau niṭṭhaṃ gatau hauti, api ca khau niṭṭhaṃ gacchati – ‘sammāsambuddhau bhagavā, svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau, suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau’ti|

‘‘tassa aivaṃ jānatau aivaṃ passatau kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, avij‍jāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti  pajānāti| idampi vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇa, tathāgatapadaṃ itipi, tathāgatanisaivitaṃ itipi, tathāgatārañ‍jitaṃ itipi| aittāvatā khau, brāhmaṇa, ariyasāvakau niṭṭhaṃ gatau hauti – ‘sammāsambuddhau bhagavā, svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau, suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau’ti| aittāvatā khau, brāhmaṇa, hatthipadaupamau vitthāraina paripūrau hautī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, jāṇussauṇi brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama! saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya – cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti; aivamaivaṃ bhautā gautamaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aisāhaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi, dhammañ‍ca, bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

cūḷahatthipadaupamasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. mahāhatthipadaupamasuttaṃ

300. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau bhikkhavai’’ti| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā sāriputtau aitadavauca – ‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, yāni kānici jaṅgalānaṃ pāṇānaṃ padajātāni sabbāni tāni hatthipadai samaudhānaṃ gacchanti, hatthipadaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati yadidaṃ mahantattaina; aivamaiva khau, āvusau, yai kaici kusalā dhammā sabbaitai catūsu ariyasac‍caisu saṅgahaṃ gacchanti| katamaisu catūsu? dukkhai ariyasac‍cai, dukkhasamudayai ariyasac‍cai, dukkhaniraudhai ariyasac‍cai, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ariyasac‍cai’’|

301. ‘‘katamañ‍cāvusau, dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ? jātipi dukkhā, jarāpi dukkhā, maraṇampi dukkhaṃ, saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsāpi dukkhā, yampicchaṃ na labhati tampi dukkhaṃ; saṃkhittaina, pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā dukkhā| katamai cāvusau, pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā? saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau, vaidanupādānakkhandhau, sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhau, saṅkhārupādānakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau|

‘‘katamau cāvusau, rūpupādānakkhandhau? cattāri ca mahābhūtāni, catun‍nañ‍ca mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya rūpaṃ|

‘‘katamā cāvusau, cattārau mahābhūtā? pathavīdhātu, āpaudhātu, taijaudhātu, vāyaudhātu|

302. ‘‘katamā cāvusau, pathavīdhātu? pathavīdhātu siyā ajjhattikā, siyā bāhirā| katamā cāvusau, ajjhattikā pathavīdhātu? yaṃ ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ kakkhaḷaṃ kharigataṃ upādin‍naṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – kaisā laumā nakhā dantā tacau maṃsaṃ nhāru aṭṭhi aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ vak‍kaṃ hadayaṃ yakanaṃ kilaumakaṃ pihakaṃ papphāsaṃ antaṃ antaguṇaṃ udariyaṃ karīsaṃ, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi kiñ‍ci ajjhattaṃ  pac‍cattaṃ kakkhaḷaṃ kharigataṃ upādin‍naṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, ajjhattikā pathavīdhātu| yā caiva khau pana ajjhattikā pathavīdhātu, yā ca bāhirā pathavīdhātu, pathavīdhāturaivaisā| ‘taṃ naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti – aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā pathavīdhātuyā nibbindati, pathavīdhātuyā cittaṃ virājaiti|

‘‘hauti  khau sau, āvusau, samayau yaṃ bāhirā āpaudhātu pakuppati pathavīdhātu pakuppati (ka॰)| antarahitā tasmiṃ samayai bāhirā pathavīdhātu hauti| tassā hi nāma, āvusau, bāhirāya pathavīdhātuyā tāva mahal‍likāya anic‍catā pañ‍ñāyissati, khayadhammatā pañ‍ñāyissati, vayadhammatā pañ‍ñāyissati, vipariṇāmadhammatā pañ‍ñāyissati| kiṃ panimassa mattaṭṭhakassa kāyassa taṇhupādin‍nassa ‘ahanti vā mamanti vā asmī’ti vā? atha khvāssa nautaivaittha hauti|

‘‘tañ‍cai, āvusau, bhikkhuṃ parai ak‍kausanti paribhāsanti rausainti vihaisainti, sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘uppan‍nā khau mai ayaṃ sautasamphassajā dukkhavaidanā| sā ca khau paṭic‍ca, nau apaṭic‍ca| kiṃ paṭic‍ca? phassaṃ  paṭic‍ca’| sau saupikhau (syā॰), saupi (ka॰) phassau anic‍cauti passati, vaidanā anic‍cāti passati, sañ‍ñā anic‍cāti passati, saṅkhārā anic‍cāti passati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍canti passati| tassa dhātārammaṇamaiva cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati adhimuc‍cati|

‘‘tañ‍cai, āvusau, bhikkhuṃ parai aniṭṭhaihi akantaihi amanāpaihi samudācaranti – pāṇisamphassainapi laiḍḍusamphassainapi daṇḍasamphassainapi satthasamphassainapi| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘tathābhūtau khau ayaṃ kāyau yathābhūtasmiṃ kāyai pāṇisamphassāpi kamanti, laiḍḍusamphassāpi kamanti, daṇḍasamphassāpi kamanti, satthasamphassāpi kamanti| vuttaṃ khau panaitaṃ bhagavatā kakacūpamauvādai – ‘‘ubhataudaṇḍakaina caipi, bhikkhavai, kakacaina caurā aucarakā aṅgamaṅgāni aukantaiyyuṃ, tatrāpi yau manau padūsaiyya na mai sau taina sāsanakarau’’ti| āraddhaṃ khau pana mai vīriyaṃ bhavissati asal‍līnaṃ, upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā, passaddhau kāyau asāraddhau, samāhitaṃ cittaṃ aikaggaṃ| kāmaṃ dāni imasmiṃ kāyai pāṇisamphassāpi kamantu, laiḍḍusamphassāpi kamantu, daṇḍasamphassāpi kamantu, satthasamphassāpi kamantu, karīyati hidaṃ buddhānaṃ sāsana’nti|

‘‘tassa cai, āvusau, bhikkhunau aivaṃ buddhaṃ anussaratau aivaṃ dhammaṃ anussaratau aivaṃ saṅghaṃ  anussaratau upaikkhā kusalanissitā na saṇṭhāti| sau taina  saṃvij‍jati saṃvaigaṃ āpaj‍jati – ‘alābhā vata mai, na vata  mai lābhā, dul‍laddhaṃ vata mai, na vata mai suladdhaṃ, yassa mai aivaṃ buddhaṃ anussaratau, aivaṃ dhammaṃ anussaratau, aivaṃ saṅghaṃ anussaratau, upaikkhā kusalanissitā na saṇṭhātī’ti| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, suṇisā sasuraṃ disvā saṃvij‍jati saṃvaigaṃ āpaj‍jati; aivamaiva khau, āvusau, tassa cai bhikkhunau aivaṃ buddhaṃ anussaratau, aivaṃ dhammaṃ anussaratau, aivaṃ saṅghaṃ anussaratau, upaikkhā kusalanissitā na saṇṭhāti, sau taina saṃvij‍jati saṃvaigaṃ āpaj‍jati – ‘alābhā vata mai na vata mai lābhā, dul‍laddhaṃ vata mai, na vata mai suladdhaṃ, yassa mai aivaṃ buddhaṃ anussaratau aivaṃ dhammaṃ anussaratau, aivaṃ saṅghaṃ anussaratau, upaikkhā kusalanissitā na saṇṭhātī’ti| tassa cai, āvusau, bhikkhunau aivaṃ buddhaṃ anussaratau, aivaṃ dhammaṃ anussaratau, aivaṃ saṅghaṃ anussaratau upaikkhā kusalanissitā saṇṭhāti, sau taina attamanau  hauti| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau bahukataṃ hauti|

303. ‘‘katamā cāvusau, āpaudhātu? āpaudhātu siyā ajjhattikā, siyā bāhirā| katamā cāvusau ajjhattikā āpaudhātu? yaṃ ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ āpau āpaugataṃ upādin‍naṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – pittaṃ saimhaṃ pubbau lauhitaṃ saidau maidau assu vasā khaiḷau siṅghāṇikā lasikā muttaṃ, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi kiñ‍ci ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ āpau āpaugataṃ upādin‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, ajjhattikā āpaudhātu| yā  caiva khau pana ajjhattikā āpaudhātu yā ca bāhirā āpaudhātu, āpaudhāturaivaisā| ‘taṃ naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā āpaudhātuyā nibbindati, āpaudhātuyā cittaṃ virājaiti|

‘‘hauti khau sau, āvusau, samayau yaṃ bāhirā āpaudhātu pakuppati| sā gāmampi vahati, nigamampi vahati, nagarampi vahati, janapadampi vahati, janapadapadaisampi vahati| hauti khau sau, āvusau, samayau yaṃ mahāsamuddai yaujanasatikānipi udakāni augacchanti, dviyaujanasatikānipi udakāni augacchanti, tiyaujanasatikānipi udakāni augacchanti, catuyaujanasatikānipi udakāni augacchanti, pañ‍cayaujanasatikānipi udakāni augacchanti, chayaujanasatikānipi udakāni augacchanti, sattayaujanasatikānipi udakāni augacchanti| hauti khau sau, āvusau, samayau yaṃ mahāsamuddai  sattatālampi udakaṃ saṇṭhāti, chattālampi udakaṃ  saṇṭhāti, pañ‍catālampi udakaṃ saṇṭhāti, catuttālampi udakaṃ saṇṭhāti, titālampi udakaṃ saṇṭhāti, dvitālampi udakaṃ saṇṭhāti, tālamattampi tālaṃpi (sī॰) udakaṃ saṇṭhāti| hauti khau sau, āvusau, samayau yaṃ mahāsamuddai sattapaurisampi udakaṃ saṇṭhāti, chappaurisampi udakaṃ saṇṭhāti, pañ‍capaurisampi udakaṃ saṇṭhāti, catuppaurisampi udakaṃ saṇṭhāti, tipaurisampi udakaṃ saṇṭhāti, dvipaurisampi udakaṃ saṇṭhāti, paurisamattampi paurisaṃpi (sī॰) udakaṃ saṇṭhāti| hauti khau sau, āvusau, samayau yaṃ mahāsamuddai  aḍḍhapaurisampi udakaṃ saṇṭhāti, kaṭimattampi udakaṃ saṇṭhāti, jāṇukamattampi udakaṃ saṇṭhāti, gaupphakamattampi udakaṃ saṇṭhāti| hauti khau sau, āvusau, samayau, yaṃ mahāsamuddai aṅgulipabbataimanamattampi udakaṃ na hauti| tassā hi nāma, āvusau, bāhirāya āpaudhātuyā tāva mahal‍likāya anic‍catā  pañ‍ñāyissati, khayadhammatā pañ‍ñāyissati, vayadhammatā pañ‍ñāyissati, vipariṇāmadhammatā pañ‍ñāyissati| kiṃ panimassa mattaṭṭhakassa kāyassa taṇhupādin‍nassa ‘ahanti vā mamanti vā asmīti’ vā? atha khvāssa nautaivaittha hauti…pai॰… tassa cai, āvusau, bhikkhunau aivaṃ buddhaṃ anussaratau, aivaṃ dhammaṃ anussaratau, aivaṃ saṅghaṃ anussaratau upaikkhā kusalanissitā saṇṭhāti| sau taina attamanau hauti| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau bahukataṃ hauti|

304. ‘‘katamā cāvusau, taijaudhātu? taijaudhātu siyā ajjhattikā, siyā bāhirā| katamā cāvusau, ajjhattikā taijaudhātu? yaṃ ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ taijau taijaugataṃ upādin‍naṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – yaina ca santappati, yaina ca jīrīyati, yaina ca pariḍayhati, yaina ca asitapītakhāyitasāyitaṃ sammā pariṇāmaṃ gacchati, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi kiñ‍ci ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ taijau taijaugataṃ upādin‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, ajjhattikā taijaudhātu| yā caiva khau pana ajjhattikā taijaudhātu yā ca bāhirā taijaudhātu, taijaudhāturaivaisā| ‘taṃ naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā taijaudhātuyā nibbindati, taijaudhātuyā cittaṃ virājaiti|
