‘‘hauti  khau sau, āvusau, samayau yaṃ bāhirā taijaudhātu pakuppati| sā gāmampi dahati, nigamampi dahati, nagarampi dahati, janapadampi dahati, janapadapadaisampi dahati| sā haritantaṃ vā panthantaṃ vā sailantaṃ vā udakantaṃ vā ramaṇīyaṃ vā bhūmibhāgaṃ āgamma anāhārā nibbāyati| hauti khau sau, āvusau, samayau yaṃ kuk‍kuṭapattainapi nhārudaddulainapi aggiṃ gavaisanti| tassā hi nāma, āvusau, bāhirāya taijaudhātuyā tāva mahal‍likāya anic‍catā pañ‍ñāyissati, khayadhammatā pañ‍ñāyissati, vayadhammatā pañ‍ñāyissati, vipariṇāmadhammatā pañ‍ñāyissati| kiṃ panimassa mattaṭṭhakassa kāyassa taṇhupādin‍nassa ‘ahanti vā mamanti vā asmī’ti vā? atha khvāssa nautaivaittha hauti…pai॰… tassa cai, āvusau, bhikkhunau aivaṃ buddhaṃ anussaratau aivaṃ dhammaṃ anussaratau aivaṃ saṅghaṃ anussaratau upaikkhā kusalanissitā saṇṭhāti, sau taina attamanau hauti| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau bahukataṃ hauti|

305. ‘‘katamā cāvusau, vāyaudhātu? vāyaudhātu siyā ajjhattikā, siyā bāhirā| katamā cāvusau, ajjhattikā vāyaudhātu? yaṃ ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ vāyau vāyaugataṃ upādin‍naṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – uddhaṅgamā vātā, adhaugamā vātā, kucchisayā vātā, kauṭṭhāsayā kauṭṭhasayā (sī॰ pī॰) vātā, aṅgamaṅgānusārinau  vātā, assāsau passāsau iti, yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi kiñ‍ci ajjhattaṃ pac‍cattaṃ vāyau vāyaugataṃ upādin‍naṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, ajjhattikā vāyaudhātu| yā caiva khau pana ajjhattikā vāyaudhātu, yā ca bāhirā vāyaudhātu, vāyaudhāturaivaisā| ‘taṃ naitaṃ mama naisauhamasmi na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā vāyaudhātuyā nibbindati vāyaudhātuyā cittaṃ virājaiti|

‘‘hauti  khau sau, āvusau, samayau yaṃ bāhirā vāyaudhātu pakuppati| sā gāmampi vahati, nigamampi vahati, nagarampi vahati, janapadampi vahati, janapadapadaisampi vahati| hauti khau sau, āvusau, samayau yaṃ gimhānaṃ pacchimai māsai tālavaṇṭainapi vidhūpanainapi vātaṃ pariyaisanti, aussavanaipi  tiṇāni na icchanti| tassā hi nāma, āvusau, bāhirāya vāyaudhātuyā tāva mahal‍likāya anic‍catā pañ‍ñāyissati, khayadhammatā pañ‍ñāyissati, vayadhammatā pañ‍ñāyissati, vipariṇāmadhammatā pañ‍ñāyissati| kiṃ panimassa mattaṭṭhakassa kāyassa taṇhupādin‍nassa ‘ahanti vā mamanti vā asmī’ti vā? atha khvāssa nautaivaittha hauti|

‘‘tañ‍cai, āvusau, bhikkhuṃ parai ak‍kausanti paribhāsanti rausainti vihaisainti| sau aivaṃ pajānāti, uppan‍nā khau mai ayaṃ sautasamphassajā dukkhā vaidanā| sā ca khau paṭic‍ca, nau apaṭic‍ca| kiṃ paṭic‍ca? phassaṃ paṭic‍ca| saupi phassau anic‍cauti passati, vaidanā anic‍cāti  passati, sañ‍ñā anic‍cāti passati, saṅkhārā anic‍cāti passati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍canti passati| tassa dhātārammaṇamaiva cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati adhimuc‍cati|

‘‘tañ‍cai, āvusau, bhikkhuṃ parai aniṭṭhaihi akantaihi amanāpaihi samudācaranti, pāṇisamphassainapi laiḍḍusamphassainapi daṇḍasamphassainapi satthasamphassainapi| sau aivaṃ pajānāti ‘tathābhūtau khau ayaṃ kāyau yathābhūtasmiṃ kāyai pāṇisamphassāpi kamanti, laiḍḍusamphassāpi kamanti, daṇḍasamphassāpi kamanti, satthasamphassāpi kamanti| vuttaṃ khau panaitaṃ bhagavatā kakacūpamauvādai ‘‘ubhataudaṇḍakaina caipi, bhikkhavai, kakacaina caurā aucarakā aṅgamaṅgāni aukantaiyyuṃ| tatrāpi yau manau padūsaiyya, na mai sau taina sāsanakarau’’ti| āraddhaṃ khau pana mai vīriyaṃ bhavissati asal‍līnaṃ, upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā, passaddhau kāyau asāraddhau, samāhitaṃ cittaṃ aikaggaṃ| kāmaṃ dāni imasmiṃ kāyai pāṇisamphassāpi kamantu, laiḍḍusamphassāpi kamantu, daṇḍasamphassāpi kamantu, satthasamphassāpi kamantu| karīyati hidaṃ buddhānaṃ sāsana’nti|

‘‘tassa cai, āvusau, bhikkhunau aivaṃ buddhaṃ anussaratau, aivaṃ dhammaṃ anussaratau, aivaṃ saṅghaṃ anussaratau upaikkhā kusalanissitā na saṇṭhāti| sau taina saṃvij‍jati saṃvaigaṃ āpaj‍jati – ‘alābhā vata mai, na vata mai lābhā, dul‍laddhaṃ vata mai, na vata mai suladdhaṃ| yassa mai aivaṃ buddhaṃ  anussaratau, aivaṃ dhammaṃ anussaratau, aivaṃ saṅghaṃ anussaratau upaikkhā kusalanissitā  na saṇṭhātī’ti| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, suṇisā sasuraṃ disvā saṃvij‍jati saṃvaigaṃ āpaj‍jati; aivamaiva khau, āvusau, tassa cai  bhikkhunau aivaṃ buddhaṃ anussaratau, aivaṃ dhammaṃ anussaratau, aivaṃ saṅghaṃ anussaratau, upaikkhā kusalanissitā na saṇṭhāti| sau taina saṃvij‍jati saṃvaigaṃ āpaj‍jati – ‘alābhā vata mai, na vata mai lābhā, dul‍laddhaṃ vata mai, na vata mai suladdhaṃ| yassa mai aivaṃ buddhaṃ anussaratau, aivaṃ dhammaṃ anussaratau, aivaṃ saṅghaṃ anussaratau, upaikkhā kusalanissitā na saṇṭhātī’ti| tassa cai, āvusau, bhikkhunau aivaṃ buddhaṃ anussaratau, aivaṃ dhammaṃ anussaratau, aivaṃ saṅghaṃ anussaratau, upaikkhā kusalanissitā saṇṭhāti, sau taina attamanau hauti| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau bahukataṃ hauti|

306. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, kaṭṭhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca val‍liñ‍ca paṭic‍ca tiṇañ‍ca paṭic‍ca mattikañ‍ca paṭic‍ca ākāsau parivāritau agāraṃ tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati; aivamaiva khau, āvusau, aṭṭhiñ‍ca  paṭic‍ca nhāruñ‍ca paṭic‍ca maṃsañ‍ca paṭic‍ca cammañ‍ca paṭic‍ca ākāsau parivāritau rūpaṃ tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| ajjhattikañ‍caiva, āvusau, cakkhuṃ aparibhin‍naṃ hauti, bāhirā ca rūpā na āpāthaṃ āgacchanti, nau ca taj‍jau saman‍nāhārau hauti, naiva tāva taj‍jassa viñ‍ñāṇabhāgassa pātubhāvau hauti| ajjhattikañ‍caiva ajjhattikañ‍cai (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), ajjhattikañ‍caipi (?), āvusau, cakkhuṃ aparibhin‍naṃ hauti bāhirā ca rūpā āpāthaṃ āgacchanti, nau ca taj‍jau saman‍nāhārau hauti, naiva  tāva taj‍jassa viñ‍ñāṇabhāgassa pātubhāvau hauti| yatau ca khau, āvusau, ajjhattikañ‍caiva cakkhuṃ aparibhin‍naṃ hauti, bāhirā ca rūpā āpāthaṃ āgacchanti, taj‍jau ca saman‍nāhārau hauti| aivaṃ taj‍jassa viñ‍ñāṇabhāgassa pātubhāvau hauti| yaṃ tathābhūtassa rūpaṃ taṃ rūpupādānakkhandhai saṅgahaṃ gacchati, yā tathābhūtassa vaidanā sā vaidanupādānakkhandhai saṅgahaṃ gacchati, yā tathābhūtassa sañ‍ñā sā sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhai saṅgahaṃ gacchati, yai tathābhūtassa saṅkhārā tai saṅkhārupādānakkhandhai saṅgahaṃ gacchanti, yaṃ tathābhūtassa viñ‍ñāṇaṃ taṃ viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhai saṅgahaṃ gacchati|

‘‘sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘aivañhi kira imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ saṅgahau san‍nipātau samavāyau hauti| vuttaṃ khau panaitaṃ bhagavatā – ‘yau paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ passati  sau dhammaṃ passati; yau dhammaṃ passati sau paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ passatīti| paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā khau panimai yadidaṃ pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā| yau imaisu pañ‍casu  upādānakkhandhaisu chandau ālayau anunayau ajjhausānaṃ sau dukkhasamudayau| yau imaisu pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ, sau dukkhaniraudhau’ti| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau bahukataṃ hauti|

‘‘ajjhattikañ‍caiva, āvusau, sautaṃ aparibhin‍naṃ hauti…pai॰… ghānaṃ aparibhin‍naṃ hauti… jivhā aparibhin‍nā hauti… kāyau aparibhin‍nau hauti… manau aparibhin‍nau hauti, bāhirā ca dhammā na āpāthaṃ āgacchanti  nau ca taj‍jau saman‍nāhārau hauti, naiva tāva taj‍jassa viñ‍ñāṇabhāgassa pātubhāvau hauti| ajjhattikau caiva, āvusau, manau aparibhin‍nau hauti, bāhirā ca dhammā āpāthaṃ āgacchanti, nau ca taj‍jau saman‍nāhārau hauti, naiva tāva taj‍jassa viñ‍ñāṇabhāgassa pātubhāvau hauti| yatau ca khau, āvusau, ajjhattikau caiva manau aparibhin‍nau hauti, bāhirā ca dhammā āpāthaṃ āgacchanti, taj‍jau ca saman‍nāhārau hauti, aivaṃ taj‍jassa viñ‍ñāṇabhāgassa pātubhāvau hauti| yaṃ tathābhūtassa rūpaṃ taṃ rūpupādānakkhandhai saṅgahaṃ gacchati, yā tathābhūtassa vaidanā sā vaidanupādānakkhandhai saṅgahaṃ gacchati, yā tathābhūtassa sañ‍ñā  sā sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhai saṅgahaṃ gacchati, yai tathābhūtassa saṅkhārā tai saṅkhārupādānakkhandhai saṅgahaṃ gacchanti, yaṃ tathābhūtassa viñ‍ñāṇaṃ taṃ viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhai saṅgahaṃ gacchati| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘aivañhi kira imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ saṅgahau san‍nipātau samavāyau hauti| vuttaṃ khau panaitaṃ bhagavatā – ‘‘yau paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ passati sau dhammaṃ passati; yau dhammaṃ passati sau paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ passatī’’ti| paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā khau panimai yadidaṃ pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā| yau imaisu pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu chandau ālayau anunayau ajjhausānaṃ sau dukkhasamudayau| yau imaisu pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ sau dukkhaniraudhau’ti| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau bahukataṃ hautī’’ti|

idamavauca  āyasmā sāriputtau| attamanā tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

mahāhatthipadaupamasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. mahāsāraupamasuttaṃ

307. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai acirapak‍kantai daivadattai| tatra khau bhagavā daivadattaṃ ārabbha bhikkhū āmantaisi –

‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau kulaputtau saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti – ‘autiṇṇaumhi jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, dukkhautiṇṇau dukkhaparaitau, appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithā’ti| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ abhinibbattaiti| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina attamanau hauti paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina attānuk‍kaṃsaiti paraṃ vambhaiti – ‘ahamasmi lābhasak‍kārasilaukavā lābhī silaukavā (sī॰ pī॰), lābhī sak‍kāra silaukavā (syā॰), imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū appañ‍ñātā appaisakkhā’ti| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina maj‍jati pamaj‍jati pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati, pamattau samānau dukkhaṃ viharati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau atik‍kammaiva sāraṃ atik‍kamma phaigguṃ atik‍kamma tacaṃ atik‍kamma papaṭikaṃ, sākhāpalāsaṃ chaitvā ādāya pak‍kamaiyya ‘sāra’nti mañ‍ñamānau| tamainaṃ cakkhumā purisau disvā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na vatāyaṃ bhavaṃ purisau añ‍ñāsi  sāraṃ, na añ‍ñāsi phaigguṃ, na añ‍ñāsi tacaṃ, na añ‍ñāsi papaṭikaṃ, na añ‍ñāsi sākhāpalāsaṃ| tathā hayaṃ tathāpāyaṃ (ka॰) bhavaṃ purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau atik‍kammaiva sāraṃ atik‍kamma phaigguṃ atik‍kamma tacaṃ atik‍kamma papaṭikaṃ, sākhāpalāsaṃ chaitvā ādāya pak‍kantau ‘sāra’nti mañ‍ñamānau| yañ‍cassa sāraina sārakaraṇīyaṃ tañ‍cassa atthaṃ nānubhavissatī’ti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau kulaputtau saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti – autiṇṇaumhi jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, dukkhautiṇṇau dukkhaparaitau, appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithā’ti| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau  samānau lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ abhinibbattaiti| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina attamanau hauti paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti  ‘ahamasmi lābhasak‍kārasilaukavā, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū appañ‍ñātā appaisakkhā’ti| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina maj‍jati pamaj‍jati pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati, pamattau samānau dukkhaṃ viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sākhāpalāsaṃ aggahaisi brahmacariyassa; taina ca vausānaṃ āpādi|

308. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau kulaputtau saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti – ‘autiṇṇaumhi jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi  paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, dukkhautiṇṇau dukkhaparaitau, appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithā’ti| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ abhinibbattaiti| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na attamanau hauti na paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na maj‍jati nappamaj‍jati na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati| appamattau samānau sīlasampadaṃ ārādhaiti| sau tāya sīlasampadāya attamanau hauti paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau tāya sīlasampadāya attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti – ‘ahamasmi sīlavā kalyāṇadhammau, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū dussīlā pāpadhammā’ti| sau tāya sīlasampadāya maj‍jati pamaj‍jati pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati, pamattau samānau dukkhaṃ viharati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau atik‍kammaiva sāraṃ atik‍kamma phaigguṃ atik‍kamma tacaṃ, papaṭikaṃ chaitvā ādāya pak‍kamaiyya ‘sāra’nti mañ‍ñamānau| tamainaṃ cakkhumā purisau disvā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na vatāyaṃ bhavaṃ purisau añ‍ñāsi sāraṃ, na añ‍ñāsi phaigguṃ, na añ‍ñāsi tacaṃ, na añ‍ñāsi papaṭikaṃ, na añ‍ñāsi sākhāpalāsaṃ| tathā hayaṃ bhavaṃ purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau atik‍kammaiva sāraṃ  atik‍kamma phaigguṃ atik‍kamma  tacaṃ, papaṭikaṃ chaitvā ādāya pak‍kantau ‘sāra’nti mañ‍ñamānau; yañ‍cassa sāraina sārakaraṇīyaṃ tañ‍cassa atthaṃ nānubhavissatī’ti|

‘‘aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau kulaputtau saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti – ‘autiṇṇaumhi jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, dukkhautiṇṇau dukkhaparaitau, appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithā’’ti| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ abhinibbattaiti| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na attamanau hauti na paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na maj‍jati nappamaj‍jati na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati| appamattau samānau sīlasampadaṃ ārādhaiti| sau tāya sīlasampadāya attamanau hauti paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau tāya sīlasampadāya attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti – ‘ahamasmi sīlavā kalyāṇadhammau, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū dussīlā pāpadhammā’ti| sau tāya sīlasampadāya maj‍jati pamaj‍jati pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati, pamattau samānau dukkhaṃ viharati| ayaṃ  vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu papaṭikaṃ aggahaisi brahmacariyassa; taina ca vausānaṃ āpādi|

309. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau kulaputtau saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti – ‘autiṇṇaumhi jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi  paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, dukkhautiṇṇau dukkhaparaitau, appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithā’ti| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ abhinibbattaiti| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na attamanau hauti na paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na maj‍jati nappamaj‍jati na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati, appamattau samānau sīlasampadaṃ ārādhaiti| sau tāya sīlasampadāya attamanau hauti nau ca khau paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau tāya sīlasampadāya na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| sau tāya sīlasampadāya na maj‍jati nappamaj‍jati na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati| appamattau samānau samādhisampadaṃ ārādhaiti| sau tāya samādhisampadāya attamanau hauti paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau tāya samādhisampadāya attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti – ‘ahamasmi samāhitau aikaggacittau, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū asamāhitā vibbhantacittā’ti| sau tāya samādhisampadāya maj‍jati pamaj‍jati pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati, pamattau samānau dukkhaṃ viharati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau atik‍kammaiva sāraṃ atik‍kamma phaigguṃ tacaṃ chaitvā ādāya pak‍kamaiyya ‘sāra’nti mañ‍ñamānau| tamainaṃ cakkhumā purisau disvā aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘na vatāyaṃ bhavaṃ purisau añ‍ñāsi sāraṃ, na añ‍ñāsi phaigguṃ, na añ‍ñāsi tacaṃ, na añ‍ñāsi papaṭikaṃ, na añ‍ñāsi sākhāpalāsaṃ| tathā hayaṃ bhavaṃ purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau atik‍kammaiva sāraṃ atik‍kamma phaigguṃ tacaṃ chaitvā ādāya pak‍kantau ‘sāra’nti mañ‍ñamānau| yañ‍cassa sāraina sārakaraṇīyaṃ tañ‍cassa atthaṃ nānubhavissatī’ti|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau kulaputtau saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti – ‘autiṇṇaumhi jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, dukkhautiṇṇau dukkhaparaitau, appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithā’’ti| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ abhinibbattaiti| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na attamanau hauti na paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na maj‍jati nappamaj‍jati na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati, appamattau samānau sīlasampadaṃ ārādhaiti| sau tāya sīlasampadāya attamanau hauti nau ca khau paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau tāya sīlasampadāya na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| sau tāya sīlasampadāya na maj‍jati nappamaj‍jati na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati, appamattau samānau samādhisampadaṃ ārādhaiti| sau tāya samādhisampadāya attamanau hauti paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau tāya samādhisampadāya attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti – ‘ahamasmi  samāhitau aikaggacittau, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū asamāhitā vibbhantacittā’ti| sau tāya  samādhisampadāya maj‍jati pamaj‍jati pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati, pamattau samānau dukkhaṃ viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tacaṃ aggahaisi brahmacariyassa; taina ca vausānaṃ āpādi|

310. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau kulaputtau saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti – ‘autiṇṇaumhi jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, dukkhautiṇṇau dukkhaparaitau, appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa  antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithā’ti| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ abhinibbattaiti| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na attamanau hauti na paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na maj‍jati nappamaj‍jati na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati| appamattau samānau sīlasampadaṃ ārādhaiti| sau tāya sīlasampadāya attamanau hauti, nau ca khau paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau tāya sīlasampadāya na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| sau tāya sīlasampadāya na maj‍jati nappamaj‍jati na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati, appamattau samānau samādhisampadaṃ ārādhaiti| sau tāya samādhisampadāya attamanau hauti, nau ca khau paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau tāya samādhisampadāya na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| sau tāya samādhisampadāya na maj‍jati nappamaj‍jati  na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati appamattau samānau ñāṇadassanaṃ ārādhaiti| sau taina ñāṇadassanaina attamanau hauti paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau taina ñāṇadassanaina attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti – ‘ahamasmi jānaṃ passaṃ viharāmi| imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū ajānaṃ apassaṃ viharantī’ti| sau taina ñāṇadassanaina maj‍jati pamaj‍jati pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati, pamattau samānau dukkhaṃ viharati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau atik‍kammaiva sāraṃ phaigguṃ chaitvā ādāya pak‍kamaiyya ‘sāra’nti mañ‍ñamānau| tamainaṃ cakkhumā purisau disvā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na vatāyaṃ bhavaṃ purisau añ‍ñāsi sāraṃ na añ‍ñāsi phaigguṃ na añ‍ñāsi tacaṃ na añ‍ñāsi papaṭikaṃ na añ‍ñāsi sākhāpalāsaṃ| tathā hayaṃ bhavaṃ purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa  tiṭṭhatau sāravatau atik‍kammaiva sāraṃ phaigguṃ chaitvā ādāya pak‍kantau ‘sāra’nti mañ‍ñamānau| yañ‍cassa sāraina sārakaraṇīyaṃ tañ‍cassa atthaṃ nānubhavissatī’ti| aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau kulaputtau saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti – ‘autiṇṇaumhi jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, dukkhautiṇṇau dukkhaparaitau, appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithā’ti| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ abhinibbattaiti| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina  na attamanau hauti na paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| sau  taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na maj‍jati nappamaj‍jati na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati, appamattau samānau sīlasampadaṃ ārādhaiti| sau tāya sīlasampadāya attamanau hauti, nau ca khau paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau tāya sīlasampadāya na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| sau tāya sīlasampadāya na maj‍jati nappamaj‍jati na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati, appamattau samānau samādhisampadaṃ ārādhaiti| sau tāya samādhisampadāya attamanau hauti, nau ca khau paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau tāya samādhisampadāya na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| sau tāya samādhisampadāya na maj‍jati nappamaj‍jati na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati, appamattau samānau ñāṇadassanaṃ ārādhaiti| sau taina ñāṇadassanaina attamanau hauti paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau taina ñāṇadassanaina attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti – ‘ahamasmi jānaṃ passaṃ viharāmi, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū ajānaṃ apassaṃ viharantī’ti| sau taina ñāṇadassanaina maj‍jati pamaj‍jati pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati, pamattau samānau dukkhaṃ viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu phaigguṃ aggahaisi brahmacariyassa; taina ca vausānaṃ āpādi|

311. ‘‘idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau kulaputtau saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti – ‘autiṇṇaumhi jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi  paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, dukkhautiṇṇau dukkhaparaitau, appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithā’ti| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ abhinibbattaiti| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na attamanau hauti, na paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na maj‍jati nappamaj‍jati na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati, appamattau samānau sīlasampadaṃ ārādhaiti| sau tāya sīlasampadāya attamanau hauti, nau ca khau paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau tāya sīlasampadāya na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| sau tāya sīlasampadāya na maj‍jati nappamaj‍jati na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati, appamattau samānau samādhisampadaṃ ārādhaiti| sau tāya samādhisampadāya attamanau hauti, nau ca khau paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau tāya samādhisampadāya na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| sau tāya samādhisampadāya na maj‍jati nappamaj‍jati na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati, appamattau samānau ñāṇadassanaṃ ārādhaiti| sau taina ñāṇadassanaina attamanau hauti, nau ca khau paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau taina ñāṇadassanaina na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| sau taina ñāṇadassanaina na maj‍jati nappamaj‍jati na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati, appamattau samānau asamayavimaukkhaṃ  ārādhaiti| aṭṭhānamaitaṃ aṭṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ (ka॰), bhikkhavai, anavakāsau yaṃ sau bhikkhu tāya asamayavimuttiyā parihāyaitha|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau sārañ‍ñaiva chaitvā ādāya pak‍kamaiyya ‘sāra’nti jānamānau| tamainaṃ cakkhumā purisau disvā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘añ‍ñāsi vatāyaṃ bhavaṃ purisau sāraṃ, añ‍ñāsi phaigguṃ, añ‍ñāsi tacaṃ, añ‍ñāsi papaṭikaṃ, añ‍ñāsi sākhāpalāsaṃ| tathā hayaṃ bhavaṃ purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau  rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau sārañ‍ñaiva chaitvā ādāya pak‍kantau ‘sāra’nti jānamānau| yañ‍cassa sāraina sārakaraṇīyaṃ tañ‍cassa atthaṃ anubhavissatī’ti|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau kulaputtau saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti – ‘autiṇṇaumhi jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, dukkhautiṇṇau dukkhaparaitau, appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithā’ti| sau  aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ abhinibbattaiti| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na attamanau hauti, na paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na maj‍jati nappamaj‍jati na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati, appamattau samānau sīlasampadaṃ ārādhaiti| sau tāya sīlasampadāya attamanau hauti, nau ca khau paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau tāya sīlasampadāya na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| sau tāya sīlasampadāya na maj‍jati nappamaj‍jati  na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati, appamattau samānau samādhisampadaṃ ārādhaiti| sau tāya samādhisampadāya attamanau hauti, nau ca khau paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau tāya samādhisampadāya na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| sau tāya samādhisampadāya na maj‍jati nappamaj‍jati na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati, appamattau samānau ñāṇadassanaṃ ārādhaiti| sau taina ñāṇadassanaina attamanau hauti, nau ca khau paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau taina ñāṇadassanaina na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| sau taina ñāṇadassanaina na maj‍jati nappamaj‍jati na pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati, appamattau samānau asamayavimaukkhaṃ  ārādhaiti| aṭṭhānamaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, anavakāsau yaṃ sau bhikkhu tāya asamayavimuttiyā parihāyaitha|

‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, nayidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ lābhasak‍kārasilaukānisaṃsaṃ, na sīlasampadānisaṃsaṃ, na samādhisampadānisaṃsaṃ, na ñāṇadassanānisaṃsaṃ| yā ca khau ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, akuppā caitauvimutti – aitadatthamidaṃ, bhikkhavai, brahmacariyaṃ, aitaṃ sāraṃ aitaṃ pariyausāna’’nti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

mahāsāraupamasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|

10. cūḷasāraupamasuttaṃ

312. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau piṅgalakaucchau brāhmaṇau yaina  bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau piṅgalakaucchau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yaimai, bhau gautama, samaṇabrāhmaṇā saṅghinau gaṇinau gaṇācariyā ñātā yasassinau titthakarā sādhusammatā, bahujanassa, saiyyathidaṃ – pūraṇau kassapau, makkhali gausālau, ajitau kaisakambalau, pakudhau kac‍cāyanau, sañ‍cayau sañ‍jayau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) bailaṭṭhaputtau, nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau, sabbaitai sakāya paṭiñ‍ñāya abbhañ‍ñaṃsu sabbaiva nābbhañ‍ñaṃsu, udāhu aikac‍cai abbhañ‍ñaṃsu aikac‍cai nābbhañ‍ñaṃsū’’ti? ‘‘alaṃ, brāhmaṇa, tiṭṭhataitaṃ – sabbaitai sakāya paṭiñ‍ñāya abbhañ‍ñaṃsu sabbaiva nābbhañ‍ñaṃsu, udāhu aikac‍cai abbhañ‍ñaṃsu aikac‍cai nābbhañ‍ñaṃsūti| dhammaṃ tai, brāhmaṇa, daisaissāmi, taṃ suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau piṅgalakaucchau brāhmaṇau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

313. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇa, purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau atik‍kammaiva sāraṃ atik‍kamma phaigguṃ atik‍kamma tacaṃ  atik‍kamma papaṭikaṃ, sākhāpalāsaṃ chaitvā ādāya pak‍kamaiyya ‘sāra’nti mañ‍ñamānau| tamainaṃ cakkhumā purisau disvā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na vatāyaṃ bhavaṃ purisau añ‍ñāsi sāraṃ, na añ‍ñāsi phaigguṃ, na añ‍ñāsi tacaṃ, na añ‍ñāsi papaṭikaṃ, na añ‍ñāsi sākhāpalāsaṃ| tathā hayaṃ bhavaṃ purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau atik‍kammaiva sāraṃ atik‍kamma phaigguṃ atik‍kamma tacaṃ atik‍kamma papaṭikaṃ, sākhāpalāsaṃ chaitvā ādāya pak‍kantau ‘sāra’nti mañ‍ñamānau| yañ‍cassa sāraina sārakaraṇīyaṃ tañ‍cassa atthaṃ nānubhavissatī’ti|

314. ‘‘saiyyathāpi vā pana, brāhmaṇa, purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau  mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau atik‍kammaiva sāraṃ atik‍kamma phaigguṃ atik‍kamma tacaṃ, papaṭikaṃ chaitvā ādāya  pak‍kamaiyya ‘sāra’nti mañ‍ñamānau| tamainaṃ cakkhumā purisau disvā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na vatāyaṃ bhavaṃ purisau añ‍ñāsi sāraṃ, na añ‍ñāsi phaigguṃ, na añ‍ñāsi tacaṃ, na añ‍ñāsi papaṭikaṃ, na añ‍ñāsi sākhāpalāsaṃ| tathā hayaṃ bhavaṃ purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau atik‍kammaiva sāraṃ atik‍kamma phaigguṃ atik‍kamma tacaṃ papaṭikaṃ chaitvā ādāya pak‍kantau ‘sāra’nti mañ‍ñamānau| yañ‍cassa sāraina sārakaraṇīyaṃ tañ‍cassa atthaṃ nānubhavissatī’ti|

315. ‘‘saiyyathāpi vā pana, brāhmaṇa, purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau  sāravatau atik‍kammaiva sāraṃ atik‍kamma phaigguṃ, tacaṃ chaitvā ādāya pak‍kamaiyya ‘sāra’nti mañ‍ñamānau| tamainaṃ cakkhumā purisau disvā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na vatāyaṃ bhavaṃ purisau añ‍ñāsi sāraṃ, na añ‍ñāsi phaigguṃ, na añ‍ñāsi tacaṃ, na añ‍ñāsi papaṭikaṃ, na añ‍ñāsi sākhāpalāsaṃ| tathā hayaṃ bhavaṃ purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau atik‍kammaiva sāraṃ atik‍kamma phaigguṃ, tacaṃ chaitvā ādāya pak‍kantau ‘sāra’nti mañ‍ñamānau| yañ‍cassa sāraina sārakaraṇīyaṃ tañ‍cassa atthaṃ nānubhavissatī’ti|

316. ‘‘saiyyathāpi vā pana, brāhmaṇa, purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau atik‍kammaiva sāraṃ, phaigguṃ chaitvā ādāya pak‍kamaiyya ‘sāra’nti mañ‍ñamānau| tamainaṃ cakkhumā purisau disvā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘na vatāyaṃ bhavaṃ purisau añ‍ñāsi sāraṃ, na añ‍ñāsi phaigguṃ, na añ‍ñāsi tacaṃ, na añ‍ñāsi papaṭikaṃ, na añ‍ñāsi sākhāpalāsaṃ| tathā hayaṃ bhavaṃ purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau atik‍kammaiva sāraṃ, phaigguṃ chaitvā ādāya pak‍kantau ‘sāra’nti mañ‍ñamānau| yañ‍cassa sāraina sārakaraṇīyaṃ tañ‍cassa atthaṃ nānubhavissatī’ti|

317. ‘‘saiyyathāpi vā pana, brāhmaṇa, purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau  mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau sārañ‍ñaiva  chaitvā ādāya pak‍kamaiyya ‘sāra’nti jānamānau| tamainaṃ cakkhumā purisau disvā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘añ‍ñāsi vatāyaṃ bhavaṃ purisau sāraṃ, añ‍ñāsi phaigguṃ, añ‍ñāsi tacaṃ, añ‍ñāsi papaṭikaṃ, añ‍ñāsi sākhāpalāsaṃ| tathā hayaṃ bhavaṃ purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau sārañ‍ñaiva chaitvā ādāya  pak‍kantau ‘sāra’nti jānamānau| yañ‍cassa sāraina sārakaraṇīyaṃ tañ‍cassa atthaṃ anubhavissatī’ti|

318. ‘‘aivamaiva khau, brāhmaṇa, idhaikac‍cau puggalau saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti – ‘autiṇṇaumhi jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, dukkhautiṇṇau dukkhaparaitau, appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithā’ti| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ abhinibbattaiti| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina attamanau hauti paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti – ‘ahamasmi lābhasak‍kārasilaukavā, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū appañ‍ñātā appaisakkhā’ti| lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina ca yai añ‍ñai dhammā uttaritarā ca paṇītatarā ca taisaṃ dhammānaṃ sacchikiriyāya na chandaṃ janaiti, na vāyamati, aulīnavuttikau ca hauti sāthalikau| saiyyathāpi sau, brāhmaṇa, purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau atik‍kammaiva sāraṃ atik‍kamma phaigguṃ atik‍kamma tacaṃ atik‍kamma papaṭikaṃ, sākhāpalāsaṃ chaitvā  ādāya pak‍kantau ‘sāra’nti mañ‍ñamānau| yañ‍cassa sāraina sārakaraṇīyaṃ tañ‍cassa atthaṃ nānubhavissati| tathūpamāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

319. ‘‘idha pana, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cau puggalau saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti – ‘autiṇṇaumhi jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, dukkhautiṇṇau dukkhaparaitau, appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithā’ti| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ abhinibbattaiti| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na  attamanau hauti na paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina ca yai añ‍ñai dhammā uttaritarā ca paṇītatarā ca taisaṃ dhammānaṃ sacchikiriyāya chandaṃ janaiti, vāyamati, anaulīnavuttikau ca hauti asāthalikau| sau sīlasampadaṃ ārādhaiti| sau tāya sīlasampadāya attamanau hauti, paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau tāya sīlasampadāya attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti – ‘ahamasmi sīlavā kalyāṇadhammau, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū dussīlā pāpadhammā’ti| sīlasampadāya ca yai añ‍ñai dhammā uttaritarā ca paṇītatarā ca taisaṃ dhammānaṃ  sacchikiriyāya na chandaṃ janaiti, na vāyamati, aulīnavuttikau ca hauti sāthalikau| saiyyathāpi sau, brāhmaṇa, purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī  sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau atik‍kammaiva sāraṃ atik‍kamma phaigguṃ atik‍kamma tacaṃ, papaṭikaṃ chaitvā ādāya pak‍kantau ‘sāra’nti mañ‍ñamānau| yañ‍cassa sāraina sārakaraṇīyaṃ, tañ‍cassa atthaṃ nānubhavissati| tathūpamāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

320. ‘‘idha pana, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cau puggalau saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti – ‘autiṇṇaumhi jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, dukkhautiṇṇau dukkhaparaitau, appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithā’ti| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ abhinibbattaiti| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na attamanau hauti, na paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina ca yai añ‍ñai dhammā uttaritarā ca paṇītatarā ca taisaṃ dhammānaṃ sacchikiriyāya chandaṃ janaiti, vāyamati, anaulīnavuttikau ca hauti asāthalikau| sau sīlasampadaṃ ārādhaiti| sau tāya sīlasampadāya attamanau hauti nau ca khau paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau tāya sīlasampadāya na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| sīlasampadāya ca yai añ‍ñai dhammā uttaritarā ca paṇītatarā ca taisaṃ dhammānaṃ sacchikiriyāya chandaṃ janaiti, vāyamati, anaulīnavuttikau ca hauti asāthalikau| sau samādhisampadaṃ ārādhaiti| sau tāya samādhisampadāya attamanau hauti, paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau tāya samādhisampadāya attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti – ‘ahamasmi samāhitau aikaggacittau, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū  asamāhitā vibbhantacittā’ti| samādhisampadāya ca yai añ‍ñai dhammā uttaritarā ca paṇītatarā ca, taisaṃ dhammānaṃ sacchikiriyāya na chandaṃ janaiti, na vāyamati, aulīnavuttikau ca hauti sāthalikau| saiyyathāpi sau, brāhmaṇa, purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau atik‍kammaiva sāraṃ atik‍kamma phaigguṃ, tacaṃ chaitvā ādāya pak‍kantau ‘sāra’nti mañ‍ñamānau| yañ‍cassa  sāraina sārakaraṇīyaṃ tañ‍cassa atthaṃ nānubhavissati| tathūpamāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

321. ‘‘idha pana, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cau puggalau saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti – ‘autiṇṇaumhi jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina…pai॰…  antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithā’ti| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ abhinibbattaiti| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na attamanau hauti na paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina ca yai añ‍ñai dhammā uttaritarā ca paṇītatarā ca taisaṃ dhammānaṃ sacchikiriyāya chandaṃ janaiti, vāyamati, anaulīnavuttikau ca hauti asāthalikau| sau sīlasampadaṃ ārādhaiti| sau tāya sīlasampadāya attamanau hauti, nau ca khau paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau tāya sīlasampadāya na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| sīlasampadāya ca yai añ‍ñai dhammā uttaritarā ca paṇītatarā ca taisaṃ dhammānaṃ sacchikiriyāya chandaṃ janaiti, vāyamati, anaulīnavuttikau ca hauti asāthalikau| sau samādhisampadaṃ ārādhaiti| sau tāya samādhisampadāya attamanau hauti, nau ca khau paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau tāya samādhisampadāya na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| samādhisampadāya ca yai añ‍ñai dhammā uttaritarā ca paṇītatarā ca taisaṃ dhammānaṃ  sacchikiriyāya chandaṃ janaiti, vāyamati, anaulīnavuttikau ca hauti asāthalikau| sau ñāṇadassanaṃ ārādhaiti| sau taina ñāṇadassanaina attamanau hauti, paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau taina ñāṇadassanaina attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, paraṃ vambhaiti – ‘ahamasmi jānaṃ passaṃ viharāmi, imai panañ‍ñai bhikkhū ajānaṃ apassaṃ viharantī’ti| ñāṇadassanaina ca yai añ‍ñai dhammā uttaritarā ca paṇītatarā ca taisaṃ dhammānaṃ sacchikiriyāya na chandaṃ janaiti, na vāyamati, aulīnavuttikau ca hauti sāthalikau| saiyyathāpi sau, brāhmaṇa, purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau atik‍kammaiva sāraṃ, phaigguṃ chaitvā ādāya pak‍kantau ‘sāra’nti mañ‍ñamānau| yañ‍cassa sāraina sārakaraṇīyaṃ tañ‍cassa atthaṃ nānubhavissati| tathūpamāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

322. ‘‘idha pana, brāhmaṇa, aikac‍cau puggalau saddhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti – ‘autiṇṇaumhi jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, dukkhautiṇṇau dukkhaparaitau, appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithā’ti| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau  lābhasak‍kārasilaukaṃ abhinibbattaiti| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na attamanau hauti, na paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau taina lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| lābhasak‍kārasilaukaina ca yai añ‍ñai dhammā uttaritarā ca paṇītatarā ca taisaṃ dhammānaṃ sacchikiriyāya chandaṃ janaiti, vāyamati, anaulīnavuttikau ca hauti asāthalikau| sau sīlasampadaṃ ārādhaiti| sau tāya sīlasampadāya attamanau hauti, nau ca khau paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau tāya sīlasampadāya na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| sīlasampadāya ca yai añ‍ñai dhammā uttaritarā ca paṇītatarā ca taisaṃ dhammānaṃ sacchikiriyāya chandaṃ janaiti, vāyamati, anaulīnavuttikau ca hauti asāthalikau| sau samādhisampadaṃ ārādhaiti| sau tāya samādhisampadāya attamanau hauti, nau ca khau paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau tāya samādhisampadāya na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na paraṃ vambhaiti| samādhisampadāya ca yai añ‍ñai dhammā uttaritarā ca paṇītatarā ca taisaṃ dhammānaṃ sacchikiriyāya chandaṃ janaiti, vāyamati, anaulīnavuttikau ca hauti asāthalikau| sau ñāṇadassanaṃ ārādhaiti| sau taina ñāṇadassanaina attamanau hauti, nau ca khau paripuṇṇasaṅkappau| sau taina ñāṇadassanaina na attānuk‍kaṃsaiti, na  paraṃ vambhaiti| ñāṇadassanaina ca yai añ‍ñai dhammā uttaritarā ca paṇītatarā  ca taisaṃ dhammānaṃ sacchikiriyāya chandaṃ janaiti, vāyamati, anaulīnavuttikau ca hauti asāthalikau|

323. ‘‘katamai ca, brāhmaṇa, dhammā ñāṇadassanaina uttaritarā ca paṇītatarā ca? idha, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayampi khau, brāhmaṇa, dhammau ñāṇadassanaina uttaritarau ca paṇītatarau ca|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayampi khau, brāhmaṇa, dhammau ñāṇadassanaina uttaritarau ca paṇītatarau ca|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati, satau ca sampajānau  sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayampi khau, brāhmaṇa, dhammau ñāṇadassanaina uttaritarau ca paṇītatarau ca|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayampi khau, brāhmaṇa, dhammau ñāṇadassanaina uttaritarau ca paṇītatarau ca|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhu sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayampi khau, brāhmaṇa, dhammau ñāṇadassanaina uttaritarau ca paṇītatarau ca|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhu sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayampi khau, brāhmaṇa, dhammau ñāṇadassanaina uttaritarau ca paṇītatarau ca|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhu sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayampi khau, brāhmaṇa, dhammau ñāṇadassanaina uttaritarau ca paṇītatarau ca|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhu sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayampi khau, brāhmaṇa, dhammau ñāṇadassanaina uttaritarau ca  paṇītatarau ca|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇa, bhikkhu sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā haunti| ayampi khau, brāhmaṇa, dhammau ñāṇadassanaina uttaritarau ca paṇītatarau ca| imai khau, brāhmaṇa, dhammā ñāṇadassanaina uttaritarā ca paṇītatarā ca|

324. ‘‘saiyyathāpi  sau, brāhmaṇa, purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau mahatau rukkhassa tiṭṭhatau sāravatau sāraṃyaiva chaitvā ādāya pak‍kantau ‘sāra’nti jānamānau| yañ‍cassa sāraina sārakaraṇīyaṃ tañ‍cassa atthaṃ anubhavissati| tathūpamāhaṃ, brāhmaṇa, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘iti khau, brāhmaṇa, nayidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ lābhasak‍kārasilaukānisaṃsaṃ, na sīlasampadānisaṃsaṃ, na samādhisampadānisaṃsaṃ, na ñāṇadassanānisaṃsaṃ| yā ca khau ayaṃ, brāhmaṇa, akuppā caitauvimutti – aitadatthamidaṃ, brāhmaṇa, brahmacariyaṃ, aitaṃ sāraṃ aitaṃ pariyausāna’’nti|

aivaṃ vuttai, piṅgalakaucchau brāhmaṇau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama…pai॰… upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitaṃ saraṇaṃ gata’’nti|

cūḷasāraupamasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|

aupammavaggau niṭṭhitau tatiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

mauḷiyaphaggunariṭṭhañ‍ca  nāmau, andhavanai kathipuṇṇaṃ nivāpau|

rāsikaṇairumahāgajanāmau, sārūpamau sāravarau (syā॰), sāravanau (ka॰) puna piṅgalakaucchau||


4. mahāyamakavaggau

1. cūḷagausiṅgasuttaṃ

325. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā nātikai nādikai (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), ñātikai (ka॰) viharati giñ‍jakāvasathai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā ca anuruddhau āyasmā ca nandiyau āyasmā ca kimilau kimbilau (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) gausiṅgasālavanadāyai viharanti| atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina gausiṅgasālavanadāyau tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau dāyapālau bhagavantaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mā, samaṇa, aitaṃ dāyaṃ pāvisi| santaittha tayau kulaputtā attakāmarūpā viharanti| mā taisaṃ aphāsumakāsī’’ti|

assausi khau āyasmā anuruddhau dāyapālassa bhagavatā saddhiṃ mantayamānassa| sutvāna dāyapālaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mā, āvusau dāyapāla, bhagavantaṃ vāraisi| satthā nau bhagavā anuppattau’’ti| atha khau āyasmā anuruddhau yaināyasmā ca nandiyau āyasmā ca kimilau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantañ‍ca nandiyaṃ āyasmantañ‍ca kimilaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kamathāyasmantau, abhik‍kamathāyasmantau, satthā nau bhagavā  anuppattau’’ti| atha  khau āyasmā ca anuruddhau āyasmā ca nandiyau āyasmā ca kimilau bhagavantaṃ pac‍cuggantvā – aikau bhagavatau pattacīvaraṃ paṭiggahaisi, aikau āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaisi, aikau pādaudakaṃ upaṭṭhāpaisi| nisīdi bhagavā pañ‍ñattai āsanai| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā pādai pakkhālaisi| taipi khau āyasmantau bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

326. ‘‘kac‍ci vau, anuruddhā, khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci yāpanīyaṃ, kac‍ci piṇḍakaina na kilamathā’’ti? ‘‘khamanīyaṃ, bhagavā, yāpanīyaṃ, bhagavā; na ca mayaṃ, bhantai, piṇḍakaina kilamāmā’’ti| ‘‘kac‍ci pana vau, anuruddhā, samaggā sammaudamānā avivadamānā khīraudakībhūtā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ piyacakkhūhi sampassantā viharathā’’ti? ‘‘taggha mayaṃ, bhantai, samaggā sammaudamānā avivadamānā khīraudakībhūtā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ piyacakkhūhi sampassantā viharāmā’’ti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tumhai, anuruddhā, samaggā sammaudamānā avivadamānā khīraudakībhūtā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ piyacakkhūhi sampassantā viharathā’’ti? ‘‘idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘lābhā vata mai, suladdhaṃ vata mai, yauhaṃ aivarūpaihi sabrahmacārīhi saddhiṃ viharāmī’ti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, imaisu āyasmantaisu maittaṃ kāyakammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ āvi caiva rahau ca; maittaṃ vacīkammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ āvi caiva rahau ca; maittaṃ manaukammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ āvi caiva rahau ca| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ sakaṃ cittaṃ nikkhipitvā  imaisaṃyaiva āyasmantānaṃ cittassa vasaina vattaiyya’nti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhantai, sakaṃ cittaṃ nikkhipitvā imaisaṃyaiva āyasmantānaṃ cittassa vasaina vattāmi| nānā hi khau nau, bhantai, kāyā aikañ‍ca pana mañ‍ñai citta’’nti|

āyasmāpi khau nandiyau…pai॰… āyasmāpi khau kimilau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘mayhampi, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘lābhā vata mai, suladdhaṃ vata mai, yauhaṃ aivarūpaihi sabrahmacārīhi saddhiṃ viharāmī’ti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, imaisu āyasmantaisu maittaṃ kāyakammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ āvi caiva rahau ca, maittaṃ vacīkammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ āvi caiva rahau ca, maittaṃ manaukammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ āvi caiva rahau ca| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ sakaṃ  cittaṃ nikkhipitvā imaisaṃyaiva āyasmantānaṃ cittassa vasaina vattaiyya’nti| sau khau ahaṃ, bhantai, sakaṃ cittaṃ nikkhipitvā imaisaṃyaiva āyasmantānaṃ cittassa vasaina vattāmi| nānā hi khau nau, bhantai, kāyā aikañ‍ca pana mañ‍ñai cittanti|

‘‘aivaṃ khau mayaṃ, bhantai, samaggā sammaudamānā avivadamānā khīraudakībhūtā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ piyacakkhūhi sampassantā viharāmā’’ti|

327. ‘‘sādhu sādhu, anuruddhā! kac‍ci pana vau, anuruddhā, appamattā ātāpinau pahitattā viharathā’’ti? ‘‘taggha mayaṃ, bhantai, appamattā ātāpinau pahitattā viharāmā’’ti| ‘‘yathā  kathaṃ pana tumhai, anuruddhā, appamattā ātāpinau pahitattā viharathā’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhantai, amhākaṃ yau paṭhamaṃ gāmatau piṇḍāya paṭik‍kamati sau āsanāni  pañ‍ñapaiti, pānīyaṃ paribhaujanīyaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti, avak‍kārapātiṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti| yau pacchā gāmatau  piṇḍāya paṭik‍kamati, sacai hauti bhuttāvasaisau sacai ākaṅkhati bhuñ‍jati, nau cai ākaṅkhati appaharitai vā chaḍḍaiti, appāṇakai vā udakai aupilāpaiti| sau āsanāni paṭisāmaiti, pānīyaṃ paribhaujanīyaṃ paṭisāmaiti, avak‍kārapātiṃ paṭisāmaiti, bhattaggaṃ sammaj‍jati| yau passati pānīyaghaṭaṃ vā paribhaujanīyaghaṭaṃ vā vac‍caghaṭaṃ vā rittaṃ tucchaṃ sau upaṭṭhāpaiti| sacassa hauti avisayhaṃ, hatthavikāraina dutiyaṃ āmantaitvā hatthavilaṅghakaina upaṭṭhāpaima, na tvaiva mayaṃ, bhantai, tappac‍cayā vācaṃ bhindāma| pañ‍cāhikaṃ khau pana mayaṃ, bhantai, sabbarattikaṃ dhammiyā kathāya san‍nisīdāma| aivaṃ khau mayaṃ, bhantai, appamattā ātāpinau pahitattā viharāmā’’ti|

328. ‘‘sādhu sādhu, anuruddhā! atthi pana vau, anuruddhā, aivaṃ appamattānaṃ ātāpīnaṃ pahitattānaṃ viharantānaṃ uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisau adhigatau phāsuvihārau’’ti? ‘‘kiñhi nau siyā, bhantai! idha mayaṃ, bhantai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāma vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāma| ayaṃ khau nau, bhantai, amhākaṃ appamattānaṃ ātāpīnaṃ pahitattānaṃ viharantānaṃ uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisau adhigatau phāsuvihārau’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, anuruddhā! aitassa  pana vau, anuruddhā, vihārassa samatik‍kamāya aitassa vihārassa  paṭippassaddhiyā atthañ‍ñau uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisau adhigatau phāsuvihārau’’ti? ‘‘kiñhi nau siyā, bhantai! idha mayaṃ, bhantai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāma vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāma| aitassa, bhantai, vihārassa samatik‍kamāya aitassa vihārassa paṭippassaddhiyā ayamañ‍ñau uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisau adhigatau phāsuvihārau’’ti|

‘‘sādhu  sādhu, anuruddhā! aitassa pana vau, anuruddhā, vihārassa samatik‍kamāya aitassa vihārassa paṭippassaddhiyā atthañ‍ñau uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisau adhigatau phāsuvihārau’’ti? ‘‘kiñhi  nau siyā, bhantai! idha mayaṃ, bhantai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāma pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakā ca viharāma, satā ca sampajānā, sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaima, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāma| aitassa, bhantai, vihārassa samatik‍kamāya aitassa vihārassa paṭippassaddhiyā ayamañ‍ñau uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisau adhigatau phāsuvihārau’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, anuruddhā! aitassa pana vau, anuruddhā, vihārassa samatik‍kamāya aitassa vihārassa paṭippassaddhiyā atthañ‍ñau uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisau adhigatau phāsuvihārau’’ti? ‘‘kiñhi nau siyā, bhantai! idha mayaṃ, bhantai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāma sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā, pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā, adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāma| aitassa, bhantai, vihārassa samatik‍kamāya aitassa vihārassa paṭippassaddhiyā ayamañ‍ñau uttari manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisau adhigatau phāsuvihārau’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, anuruddhā! aitassa pana vau, anuruddhā, vihārassa samatik‍kamāya aitassa vihārassa paṭippassaddhiyā atthañ‍ñau uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisau adhigatau phāsuvihārau’’ti? ‘‘kiñhi nau siyā, bhantai! idha mayaṃ, bhantai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāma sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ  upasampaj‍ja viharāma| aitassa, bhantai, vihārassa samatik‍kamāya aitassa vihārassa paṭippassaddhiyā ayamañ‍ñau uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisau adhigatau phāsuvihārau’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, anuruddhā! aitassa pana vau, anuruddhā, vihārassa samatik‍kamāya aitassa vihārassa paṭippassaddhiyā atthañ‍ñau uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisau adhigatau phāsuvihārau’’ti? ‘‘kiñhi nau siyā, bhantai! idha mayaṃ, bhantai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāma  sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāma…pai॰… sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ  samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāma…pai॰… sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma  naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāma| aitassa, bhantai, vihārassa samatik‍kamāya aitassa vihārassa paṭippassaddhiyā ayamañ‍ñau uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisau adhigatau phāsuvihārau’’ti|

329. ‘‘sādhu sādhu, anuruddhā! aitassa pana vau, anuruddhā, vihārassa samatik‍kamāya aitassa vihārassa paṭippassaddhiyā atthañ‍ñau uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisau adhigatau phāsuvihārau’’ti? ‘‘kiñhi nau siyā, bhantai! idha mayaṃ, bhantai, yāvadaiva ākaṅkhāma sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāma, pañ‍ñāya ca nau disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā| aitassa, bhantai, vihārassa samatik‍kamāya aitassa vihārassa paṭippassaddhiyā ayamañ‍ñau uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisau adhigatau phāsuvihārau| imamhā ca mayaṃ, bhantai, phāsuvihārā añ‍ñaṃ phāsuvihāraṃ uttaritaraṃ vā paṇītataraṃ vā na samanupassāmā’’ti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, anuruddhā! imamhā phāsuvihārā uttaritarau vā paṇītatarau vā phāsuvihārau natthī’’ti|

330. atha khau bhagavā āyasmantañ‍ca anuruddhaṃ āyasmantañ‍ca nandiyaṃ āyasmantañ‍ca kimilaṃ dhammiyā kathāya sandassaitvā samādapaitvā  samuttaijaitvā sampahaṃsaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi| atha khau āyasmā ca anuruddhau āyasmā ca nandiyau āyasmā ca kimilau bhagavantaṃ anusaṃyāyitvā anusaṃsāvaitvā (sī॰), anusāvaitvā (ṭīkā) tatau paṭinivattitvā āyasmā ca nandiyau  āyasmā ca kimilau āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau mayaṃ āyasmatau anuruddhassa aivamāraucimha – ‘imāsañ‍ca imāsañ‍ca vihārasamāpattīnaṃ mayaṃ lābhinau’ti, yaṃ nau āyasmā anuruddhau bhagavatau sammukhā yāva āsavānaṃ khayā pakāsaitī’’ti? ‘‘na khau mai āyasmantau aivamāraucaisuṃ – ‘imāsañ‍ca imāsañ‍ca vihārasamāpattīnaṃ mayaṃ lābhinau’ti, api ca mai āyasmantānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca viditau – ‘imāsañ‍ca imāsañ‍ca vihārasamāpattīnaṃ imai āyasmantau lābhinau’ti| daivatāpi mai  aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ – ‘imāsañ‍ca imāsañ‍ca vihārasamāpattīnaṃ imai āyasmantau lābhinau’ti| tamainaṃ bhagavatā pañhābhipuṭṭhaina byākata’’nti|

331. atha  khau dīghau parajanau yakkhau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau dīghau parajanau yakkhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘lābhā vata, bhantai, vaj‍jīnaṃ, suladdhalābhā vaj‍jipajāya, yattha tathāgatau viharati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau, imai ca tayau kulaputtā – āyasmā ca anuruddhau, āyasmā ca nandiyau, āyasmā ca kimilau’’ti| dīghassa parajanassa yakkhassa saddaṃ sutvā bhummā daivā saddamanussāvaisuṃ – ‘lābhā  vata, bhau, vaj‍jīnaṃ, suladdhalābhā vaj‍jipajāya, yattha tathāgatau viharati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau, imai ca tayau kulaputtā – āyasmā ca anuruddhau, āyasmā ca nandiyau, āyasmā ca kimilau’ti| bhummānaṃ daivānaṃ saddaṃ sutvā cātumahārājikā daivā…pai॰… tāvatiṃsā daivā…pai॰… yāmā daivā…pai॰… tusitā daivā…pai॰… nimmānaratī daivā…pai॰… paranimmitavasavattī daivā…pai॰… brahmakāyikā daivā saddamanussāvaisuṃ – ‘‘lābhā vata, bhau, vaj‍jīnaṃ, suladdhalābhā vaj‍jipajāya, yattha tathāgatau viharati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau, imai ca tayau kulaputtā – āyasmā ca anuruddhau, āyasmā ca nandiyau, āyasmā ca kimilau’’ti| itiha tai āyasmantau taina khaṇaina (taina layaina) ( ) sī॰ syā॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu natthi taina muhuttaina yāvabrahmalaukā viditā saṃviditā (ka॰) ahaisuṃ|

‘‘aivamaitaṃ, dīgha, aivamaitaṃ, dīgha! yasmāpi, dīgha, kulā aitai tayau kulaputtā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā, tañ‍caipi kulaṃ aitai tayau kulaputtai pasan‍nacittaṃ anussaraiyya, tassapāssa kulassa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya| yasmāpi, dīgha, kulaparivaṭṭā aitai tayau kulaputtā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ  pabbajitā, sau caipi kulaparivaṭṭau aitai tayau kulaputtai pasan‍nacittau anussaraiyya, tassapāssa kulaparivaṭṭassa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya| yasmāpi, dīgha, gāmā aitai tayau kulaputtā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā, sau caipi gāmau aitai tayau kulaputtai  pasan‍nacittau anussaraiyya, tassapāssa gāmassa  dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya| yasmāpi, dīgha, nigamā aitai tayau kulaputtā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā, sau caipi nigamau aitai tayau kulaputtai pasan‍nacittau anussaraiyya, tassapāssa nigamassa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya| yasmāpi, dīgha, nagarā aitai tayau kulaputtā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā, tañ‍caipi nagaraṃ aitai tayau kulaputtai pasan‍nacittaṃ anussaraiyya, tassapāssa nagarassa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya| yasmāpi, dīgha, janapadā aitai tayau kulaputtā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā, sau caipi janapadau aitai tayau kulaputtai pasan‍nacittau anussaraiyya, tassapāssa janapadassa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya| sabbai caipi, dīgha, khattiyā aitai tayau kulaputtai pasan‍nacittā  anussaraiyyuṃ, sabbaisānaṃpāssa khattiyānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya| sabbai caipi, dīgha, brāhmaṇā…pai॰… sabbai caipi, dīgha, vaissā…pai॰… sabbai caipi, dīgha, suddā aitai tayau kulaputtai pasan‍nacittā anussaraiyyuṃ, sabbaisānaṃpāssa suddānaṃ dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya| sadaivakau caipi, dīgha, laukau samārakau sabrahmakau sassamaṇabrāhmaṇī pajā sadaivamanussā aitai tayau kulaputtai pasan‍nacittā anussaraiyya, sadaivakassapāssa laukassa samārakassa sabrahmakassa sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya| passa, dīgha, yāva aitai tayau kulaputtā bahujanahitāya paṭipan‍nā bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya, atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussāna’’nti|

idamavauca  bhagavā| attamanau dīghau parajanau yakkhau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

cūḷagausiṅgasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. mahāgausiṅgasuttaṃ

332. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā gausiṅgasālavanadāyai viharati sambahulaihi abhiñ‍ñātaihi abhiñ‍ñātaihi thairaihi sāvakaihi saddhiṃ – āyasmatā ca sāriputtaina āyasmatā ca mahāmauggal‍lānaina āyasmatā  ca mahākassapaina āyasmatā ca anuruddhaina āyasmatā ca raivataina āyasmatā ca ānandaina, añ‍ñaihi ca abhiñ‍ñātaihi abhiñ‍ñātaihi thairaihi sāvakaihi saddhiṃ| atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā mahākassapau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahākassapaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āyāmāvusau, kassapa, yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkamissāma dhammassavanāyā’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā mahākassapau āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa pac‍cassausi| atha khau āyasmā ca mahāmauggal‍lānau āyasmā ca mahākassapau āyasmā ca anuruddhau yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu dhammassavanāya| addasā khau āyasmā ānandau āyasmantañ‍ca mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ āyasmantañ‍ca mahākassapaṃ āyasmantañ‍ca anuruddhaṃ yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkamantai dhammassavanāya| disvāna yaināyasmā raivatau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ raivataṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘upasaṅkamantā khau amū, āvusau āyasmantāvusau (ka॰) raivata, sappurisā yaināyasmā sāriputtau  taina dhammassavanāya| āyāmāvusau raivata, yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkamissāma dhammassavanāyā’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā raivatau āyasmatau ānandassa pac‍cassausi| atha khau āyasmā ca raivatau āyasmā ca ānandau yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu dhammassavanāya|

333. addasā khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantañ‍ca raivataṃ āyasmantañ‍ca ānandaṃ dūratauva āgacchantai| disvāna āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aitu khau āyasmā ānandau! svāgataṃ āyasmatau ānandassa bhagavatau upaṭṭhākassa bhagavatau santikāvacarassa! ramaṇīyaṃ, āvusau ānanda, gausiṅgasālavanaṃ, dausinā ratti, sabbaphāliphul‍lā sabbapāliphul‍lā (sī॰) sālā, dibbā, mañ‍ñai, gandhā sampavanti; kathaṃrūpaina, āvusau ānanda, bhikkhunā gausiṅgasālavanaṃ saubhaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘idhāvusau sāriputta, bhikkhu bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau| yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā  sātthā sabyañ‍janā; kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ  parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā haunti, dhātā dhatā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), vacasā paricitā, manasānupaikkhitā, diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā| sau catassan‍naṃ parisānaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti parimaṇḍalaihi padabyañ‍janaihi anuppabandhaihi appabaddhaihi (sī॰ pī॰) anusayasamugghātāya| aivarūpaina khau, āvusau sāriputta, bhikkhunā gausiṅgasālavanaṃ saubhaiyyā’’ti|

334. aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ raivataṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘byākataṃ khau, āvusau raivata, āyasmatā ānandaina yathāsakaṃ paṭibhānaṃ| tattha dāni mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ raivataṃ pucchāma – ‘ramaṇīyaṃ, āvusau raivata, gausiṅgasālavanaṃ, dausinā ratti, sabbaphāliphul‍lā sālā, dibbā, mañ‍ñai, gandhā sampavanti; kathaṃrūpaina, āvusau raivata, bhikkhunā gausiṅgasālavanaṃ saubhaiyyā’’’ti? ‘‘idhāvusau sāriputta, bhikkhu paṭisal‍lānārāmau hauti paṭisal‍lānaratau, ajjhattaṃ caitausamathamanuyuttau anirākatajjhānau, vipassanāya saman‍nāgatau, brūhaitā suñ‍ñāgārānaṃ| aivarūpaina khau, āvusau sāriputta, bhikkhunā gausiṅgasālavanaṃ saubhaiyyā’’ti|

335. aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘byākataṃ khau, āvusau anuruddha, āyasmatā raivataina yathāsakaṃ paṭibhānaṃ| tattha dāni mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ pucchāma – ‘ramaṇīyaṃ, āvusau anuruddha, gausiṅgasālavanaṃ, dausinā ratti, sabbaphāliphul‍lā sālā, dibbā, mañ‍ñai, gandhā sampavanti; kathaṃrūpaina, āvusau anuruddha, bhikkhunā gausiṅgasālavanaṃ saubhaiyyā’’’ti? ‘‘idhāvusau sāriputta, bhikkhu dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sahassaṃ laukānaṃ vaulaukaiti| saiyyathāpi, āvusau sāriputta, cakkhumā purisau uparipāsādavaragatau sahassaṃ naimimaṇḍalānaṃ vaulaukaiyya; aivamaiva khau, āvusau sāriputta, bhikkhu dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sahassaṃ laukānaṃ vaulaukaiti| aivarūpaina khau, āvusau sāriputta, bhikkhunā gausiṅgasālavanaṃ saubhaiyyā’’ti|

336. aivaṃ  vuttai, āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ mahākassapaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘byākataṃ khau, āvusau kassapa, āyasmatā anuruddhaina yathāsakaṃ paṭibhānaṃ| tattha dāni mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ mahākassapaṃ pucchāma – ‘ramaṇīyaṃ, āvusau kassapa, gausiṅgasālavanaṃ, dausinā ratti, sabbaphāliphul‍lā sālā, dibbā, mañ‍ñai, gandhā sampavanti; kathaṃrūpaina, āvusau kassapa, bhikkhunā  gausiṅgasālavanaṃ saubhaiyyā’’’ti? ‘‘idhāvusau sāriputta, bhikkhu attanā ca ārañ‍ñikau  hauti ārañ‍ñikattassa ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca piṇḍapātikau hauti piṇḍapātikattassa ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca paṃsukūlikau hauti paṃsukūlikattassa ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca taicīvarikau hauti taicīvarikattassa ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca appicchau hauti appicchatāya ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca santuṭṭhau hauti santuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca pavivittau hauti pavivaikassa ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca asaṃsaṭṭhau hauti asaṃsaggassa ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca āraddhavīriyau hauti vīriyārambhassa ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca sīlasampan‍nau hauti sīlasampadāya ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca samādhisampan‍nau hauti samādhisampadāya ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau hauti pañ‍ñāsampadāya ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca vimuttisampan‍nau hauti vimuttisampadāya ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca vimuttiñāṇadassanasampan‍nau hauti vimuttiñāṇadassanasampadāya ca vaṇṇavādī| aivarūpaina khau, āvusau sāriputta, bhikkhunā gausiṅgasālavanaṃ saubhaiyyā’’ti|

337. aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘byākataṃ khau, āvusau mauggal‍lāna, āyasmatā mahākassapaina yathāsakaṃ paṭibhānaṃ| tattha dāni mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ pucchāma – ‘ramaṇīyaṃ, āvusau mauggal‍lāna, gausiṅgasālavanaṃ, dausinā ratti, sabbaphāliphul‍lā sālā, dibbā, mañ‍ñai, gandhā sampavanti; kathaṃrūpaina, āvusau mauggal‍lāna, bhikkhunā gausiṅgasālavanaṃ saubhaiyyā’’’ti? ‘‘idhāvusau sāriputta, dvai bhikkhū abhidhammakathaṃ kathainti, tai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ pañhaṃ pucchanti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa pañhaṃ puṭṭhā vissaj‍jainti, nau ca saṃsādainti saṃsārainti (ka॰), dhammī ca naisaṃ kathā pavattinī hauti| aivarūpaina khau, āvusau sāriputta, bhikkhunā gausiṅgasālavanaṃ saubhaiyyā’’ti|

338. atha  khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘byākataṃ khau, āvusau sāriputta, amhaihi sabbaihaiva yathāsakaṃ paṭibhānaṃ| tattha dāni mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ pucchāma – ‘ramaṇīyaṃ, āvusau sāriputta, gausiṅgasālavanaṃ, dausinā ratti, sabbaphāliphul‍lā sālā, dibbā, mañ‍ñai, gandhā sampavanti; kathaṃrūpaina, āvusau sāriputta, bhikkhunā gausiṅgasālavanaṃ saubhaiyyā’’’ti? ‘‘idhāvusau mauggal‍lāna, bhikkhu cittaṃ vasaṃ vattaiti, nau ca bhikkhu cittassa vasaina vattati| sau yāya vihārasamāpattiyā ākaṅkhati  pubbaṇhasamayaṃ viharituṃ, tāya vihārasamāpattiyā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ viharati; yāya vihārasamāpattiyā  ākaṅkhati majjhanhikasamayaṃ majjhantikasamayaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) viharituṃ, tāya vihārasamāpattiyā majjhanhikasamayaṃ  viharati; yāya vihārasamāpattiyā ākaṅkhati sāyanhasamayaṃ viharituṃ, tāya vihārasamāpattiyā sāyanhasamayaṃ viharati| saiyyathāpi, āvusau mauggal‍lāna, rañ‍ñau vā rājamahāmattassa vā nānārattānaṃ dussānaṃ dussakaraṇḍakau pūrau assa| sau yañ‍ñadaiva dussayugaṃ ākaṅkhaiyya pubbaṇhasamayaṃ pārupituṃ, taṃ tadaiva dussayugaṃ pubbaṇhasamayaṃ pārupaiyya; yañ‍ñadaiva dussayugaṃ ākaṅkhaiyya majjhanhikasamayaṃ pārupituṃ, taṃ tadaiva dussayugaṃ majjhanhikasamayaṃ pārupaiyya; yañ‍ñadaiva dussayugaṃ ākaṅkhaiyya sāyanhasamayaṃ pārupituṃ, taṃ tadaiva dussayugaṃ sāyanhasamayaṃ pārupaiyya| aivamaiva khau, āvusau mauggal‍lāna, bhikkhu cittaṃ vasaṃ vattaiti, nau ca bhikkhu cittassa vasaina vattati| sau yāya vihārasamāpattiyā ākaṅkhati pubbaṇhasamayaṃ viharituṃ, tāya vihārasamāpattiyā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ viharati; yāya vihārasamāpattiyā ākaṅkhati majjhanhikasamayaṃ viharituṃ, tāya vihārasamāpattiyā majjhanhikasamayaṃ viharati; yāya vihārasamāpattiyā ākaṅkhati sāyanhasamayaṃ viharituṃ, tāya vihārasamāpattiyā sāyanhasamayaṃ viharati| aivarūpaina khau, āvusau mauggal‍lāna, bhikkhunā gausiṅgasālavanaṃ saubhaiyyā’’ti|

339. atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau tai āyasmantai aitadavauca – ‘‘byākataṃ khau, āvusau, amhaihi sabbaihaiva yathāsakaṃ paṭibhānaṃ| āyāmāvusau, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamissāma; upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ  bhagavatau  āraucaissāma| yathā nau bhagavā byākarissati tathā naṃ dhāraissāmā’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai āyasmantau āyasmatau sāriputtassa pac‍cassausuṃ| atha khau tai āyasmantau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘addasaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, āyasmantañ‍ca raivataṃ āyasmantañ‍ca ānandaṃ dūratauva āgacchantai| disvāna āyasmantaṃ  ānandaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘aitu khau āyasmā ānandau! svāgataṃ āyasmatau ānandassa bhagavatau upaṭṭhākassa bhagavatau santikāvacarassa! ramaṇīyaṃ, āvusau ānanda, gausiṅgasālavanaṃ, dausinā ratti, sabbaphāliphul‍lā sālā, dibbā, mañ‍ñai, gandhā sampavanti; kathaṃrūpaina, āvusau ānanda, bhikkhunā gausiṅgasālavanaṃ saubhaiyyā’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, bhantai, āyasmā ānandau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘idhāvusau, sāriputta, bhikkhu bahussutau hauti sutadharau…pai॰… anusayasamugghātāya| aivarūpaina khau, āvusau sāriputta, bhikkhunā gausiṅgasālavanaṃ  saubhaiyyā’’’ti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, sāriputta! yathā taṃ ānandauva sammā byākaramānau byākaraiyya| ānandau hi, sāriputta, bahussutau sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau| yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthā sabyañ‍janā; kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ  brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā haunti, dhātā, vacasā paricitā, manasānupaikkhitā, diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā| sau catassan‍naṃ parisānaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti parimaṇḍalaihi padabyañ‍janaihi anuppabandhaihi anusayasamugghātāyā’’ti|

340. ‘‘aivaṃ vuttai, ahaṃ, bhantai, āyasmantaṃ raivataṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘byākataṃ khau, āvusau raivata āyasmatā ānandaina yathāsakaṃ paṭibhānaṃ| tattha dāni mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ raivataṃ pucchāma – ramaṇīyaṃ, āvusau raivata, gausiṅgasālavanaṃ, dausinā ratti, sabbaphāliphul‍lā sālā, dibbā mañ‍ñai gandhā sampavanti| kathaṃrūpaina, āvusau raivata, bhikkhunā gausiṅgasālavanaṃ saubhaiyyā’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, bhantai, āyasmā raivatau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘idhāvusau sāriputta bhikkhu paṭisal‍lānārāmau hauti paṭisal‍lānaratau, ajjhattaṃ caitausamathamanuyuttau, anirākatajjhānau, vipassanāya saman‍nāgatau, brūhaitā suñ‍ñāgārānaṃ| aivarūpaina khau, āvusau sāriputta, bhikkhunā gausiṅgasālavanaṃ saubhaiyyā’’’ti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, sāriputta! yathā taṃ raivatauva sammā byākaramānau byākaraiyya| raivatau hi, sāriputta, paṭisal‍lānārāmau paṭisal‍lānaratau, ajjhattaṃ caitausamathamanuyuttau anirākatajjhānau, vipassanāya saman‍nāgatau brūhaitā suñ‍ñāgārāna’’nti|

341. ‘‘aivaṃ  vuttai, ahaṃ, bhantai, āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘byākataṃ khau āvusau anuruddha āyasmatā raivataina…pai॰… kathaṃrūpaina, āvusau  anuruddha, bhikkhunā gausiṅgasālavanaṃ saubhaiyyā’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhantai, āyasmā anuruddhau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘idhāvusau sāriputta, bhikkhu dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sahassaṃ laukānaṃ vaulaukaiti| saiyyathāpi, āvusau sāriputta, cakkhumā purisau…pai॰… aivarūpaina khau āvusau sāriputta bhikkhunā gausiṅgasālavanaṃ saubhaiyyā’’’ti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, sāriputta, yathā taṃ anuruddhauva sammā byākaramānau byākaraiyya| anuruddhau hi, sāriputta, dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sahassaṃ laukānaṃ vaulaukaitī’’ti|

342. ‘‘aivaṃ vuttai, ahaṃ, bhantai, āyasmantaṃ mahākassapaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘byākataṃ khau, āvusau  kassapa āyasmatā anuruddhaina yathāsakaṃ paṭibhānaṃ| tattha dāni mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ mahākassapaṃ pucchāma…pai॰… kathaṃ rūpaina khau, āvusau kassapa, bhikkhunā gausiṅgasālavanaṃ saubhaiyyā’ti? aivaṃ vuttai bhantai, āyasmā mahākassapau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘idhāvusau sāriputta, bhikkhu attanā ca ārañ‍ñikau hauti ārañ‍ñikattassa ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca piṇḍapātikau hauti…pai॰… attanā ca paṃsukūlikau hauti…pai॰… attanā ca taicīvarikau hauti…pai॰… attanā ca appicchau hauti…pai॰… attanā ca santuṭṭhau hauti…pai॰… attanā ca pavivittau hauti…pai॰… attanā ca asaṃsaṭṭhau hauti…pai॰… attanā ca āraddhavīriyau hauti…pai॰… attanā ca sīlasampan‍nau hauti…pai॰… attanā ca samādhisampan‍nau hauti…pai॰… attanā ca pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau hauti… attanā ca vimuttisampan‍nau  hauti…  attanā ca vimuttiñāṇadassanasampan‍nau hauti vimuttiñāṇadassanasampadāya ca vaṇṇavādī| aivarūpaina khau, āvusau sāriputta, bhikkhunā gausiṅgasālavanaṃ saubhaiyyā’’’ti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, sāriputta! yathā taṃ kassapauva sammā byākaramānau byākaraiyya| kassapau hi, sāriputta, attanā ca ārañ‍ñikau ārañ‍ñikattassa ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca piṇḍapātikau piṇḍapātikattassa ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca paṃsukūlikau paṃsukūlikattassa ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca taicīvarikau taicīvarikattassa ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca appicchau appicchatāya ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca santuṭṭhau santuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca pavivittau pavivaikassa ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca asaṃsaṭṭhau asaṃsaggassa ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca āraddhavīriyau vīriyārambhassa ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca sīlasampan‍nau sīlasampadāya ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca samādhisampan‍nau samādhisampadāya ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau pañ‍ñāsampadāya ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca vimuttisampan‍nau vimuttisampadāya ca vaṇṇavādī, attanā ca vimuttiñāṇadassanasampan‍nau vimuttiñāṇadassanasampadāya ca vaṇṇavādī’’ti|

343. ‘‘aivaṃ vuttai, ahaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘byākataṃ khau, āvusau mauggal‍lāna, āyasmatā mahākassapaina  yathāsakaṃ paṭibhānaṃ| tattha dāni mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ pucchāma…pai॰… kathaṃrūpaina, āvusau mauggal‍lāna, bhikkhunā gausiṅgasālavanaṃ saubhaiyyā’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, bhantai, āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘idhāvusau sāriputta, dvai bhikkhū abhidhammakathaṃ kathainti| tai añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ pañhaṃ pucchanti, añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa  pañhaṃ puṭṭhā vissaj‍jainti, nau ca saṃsādainti, dhammī ca naisaṃ kathā pavattinī hauti| aivarūpaina khau, āvusau sāriputta, bhikkhunā gausiṅgasālavanaṃ saubhaiyyā’’’ti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, sāriputta, yathā taṃ mauggal‍lānauva sammā byākaramānau byākaraiyya| mauggal‍lānau hi, sāriputta, dhammakathikau’’ti|

344. aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘atha khvāhaṃ, bhantai, āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘byākataṃ khau, āvusau sāriputta, amhaihi sabbaihaiva yathāsakaṃ paṭibhānaṃ| tattha dāni  mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ pucchāma – ramaṇīyaṃ, āvusau sāriputta, gausiṅgasālavanaṃ, dausinā ratti, sabbaphāliphul‍lā sālā, dibbā, mañ‍ñai, gandhā sampavanti| kathaṃrūpaina, āvusau sāriputta, bhikkhunā gausiṅgasālavanaṃ saubhaiyyā’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, bhantai, āyasmā sāriputtau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘idhāvusau, mauggal‍lāna, bhikkhu cittaṃ vasaṃ vattaiti nau ca bhikkhu cittassa vasaina vattati| sau yāya vihārasamāpattiyā ākaṅkhati pubbaṇhasamayaṃ viharituṃ, tāya vihārasamāpattiyā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ  viharati; yāya vihārasamāpattiyā ākaṅkhati majjhanhikasamayaṃ viharituṃ, tāya vihārasamāpattiyā majjhanhikasamayaṃ viharati; yāya vihārasamāpattiyā ākaṅkhati sāyanhasamayaṃ viharituṃ, tāya vihārasamāpattiyā sāyanhasamayaṃ viharati| saiyyathāpi, āvusau mauggal‍lāna, rañ‍ñau vā rājamahāmattassa vā nānārattānaṃ dussānaṃ dussakaraṇḍakau pūrau assa| sau yañ‍ñadaiva dussayugaṃ ākaṅkhaiyya pubbaṇhasamayaṃ pārupituṃ, taṃ tadaiva dussayugaṃ pubbaṇhasamayaṃ pārupaiyya; yañ‍ñadaiva dussayugaṃ ākaṅkhaiyya majjhanhikasamayaṃ pārupituṃ, taṃ tadaiva dussayugaṃ majjhanhikasamayaṃ pārupaiyya; yañ‍ñadaiva dussayugaṃ ākaṅkhaiyya sāyanhasamayaṃ pārupituṃ, taṃ tadaiva dussayugaṃ sāyanhasamayaṃ pārupaiyya| aivamaiva khau, āvusau mauggal‍lāna, bhikkhu cittaṃ vasaṃ vattaiti, nau ca bhikkhu cittassa vasaina vattati| sau yāya vihārasamāpattiyā ākaṅkhati pubbaṇhasamayaṃ viharituṃ, tāya vihārasamāpattiyā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ viharati; yāya vihārasamāpattiyā ākaṅkhati majjhanhikasamayaṃ viharituṃ, tāya vihārasamāpattiyā majjhanhikasamayaṃ viharati; yāya vihārasamāpattiyā ākaṅkhati sāyanhasamayaṃ viharituṃ, tāya vihārasamāpattiyā sāyanhasamayaṃ viharati| aivarūpaina khau, āvusau mauggal‍lāna, bhikkhunā gausiṅgasālavanaṃ saubhaiyyā’’’ti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, mauggal‍lāna! yathā taṃ sāriputtauva sammā byākaramānau byākaraiyya| sāriputtau hi, mauggal‍lāna, cittaṃ vasaṃ vattaiti nau ca sāriputtau cittassa vasaina vattati| sau yāya vihārasamāpattiyā  ākaṅkhati pubbaṇhasamayaṃ viharituṃ, tāya vihārasamāpattiyā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ viharati; yāya vihārasamāpattiyā ākaṅkhati majjhanhikasamayaṃ viharituṃ, tāya vihārasamāpattiyā majjhanhikasamayaṃ viharati; yāya vihārasamāpattiyā ākaṅkhati sāyanhasamayaṃ viharituṃ, tāya vihārasamāpattiyā sāyanhasamayaṃ viharatī’’ti|

345. aivaṃ  vuttai, āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kassa nu khau, bhantai, subhāsita’’nti? ‘‘sabbaisaṃ vau, sāriputta, subhāsitaṃ pariyāyaina| api ca mamapi suṇātha yathārūpaina bhikkhunā gausiṅgasālavanaṃ saubhaiyya| idha, sāriputta, bhikkhu pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā – ‘na tāvāhaṃ imaṃ pal‍laṅkaṃ bhindissāmi yāva mai nānupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍cissatī’ti| aivarūpaina khau, sāriputta, bhikkhunā gausiṅgasālavanaṃ saubhaiyyā’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai āyasmantau tai bhikkhū (ka॰) bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

mahāgausiṅgasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. mahāgaupālakasuttaṃ

346. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘aikādasahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau gaupālakau abhabbau gaugaṇaṃ pariharituṃ phātiṃ kātuṃ phātikattuṃ (sī॰ pī॰), phātikātuṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰)| katamaihi aikādasahi? idha, bhikkhavai, gaupālakau na rūpañ‍ñū hauti, na lakkhaṇakusalau hauti, na āsāṭikaṃ hāraitā sāṭaitā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) hauti, na vaṇaṃ paṭicchādaitā hauti, na dhūmaṃ kattā hauti, na titthaṃ jānāti, na pītaṃ jānāti, na vīthiṃ jānāti, na gaucarakusalau hauti anavasaisadauhī ca hauti| yai tai usabhā gaupitarau gaupariṇāyakā tai na atiraikapūjāya pūjaitā hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aikādasahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau gaupālakau abhabbau gaugaṇaṃ pariharituṃ phātiṃ kātuṃ| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, aikādasahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu abhabbau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai  vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jituṃ| katamaihi aikādasahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na rūpañ‍ñū hauti, na lakkhaṇakusalau hauti, na āsāṭikaṃ hāraitā hauti, na vaṇaṃ paṭicchādaitā hauti, na dhūmaṃ kattā hauti, na titthaṃ jānāti, na pītaṃ jānāti, na vīthiṃ jānāti, na gaucarakusalau hauti, anavasaisadauhī ca hauti| yai tai  bhikkhū thairā rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitā saṅghapitarau saṅghapariṇāyakā tai na atiraikapūjāya pūjaitā hauti|

347. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na rūpañ‍ñū hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaṃ kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ sabbaṃ rūpaṃ ‘cattāri mahābhūtāni, catun‍nañ‍ca mahābhūtānaṃ upādāyarūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na rūpañ‍ñū hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na lakkhaṇakusalau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘kammalakkhaṇau  bālau, kammalakkhaṇau paṇḍitau’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na lakkhaṇakusalau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na āsāṭikaṃ hāraitā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu uppan‍naṃ kāmavitak‍kaṃ adhivāsaiti, nappajahati na vinaudaiti na byantī karauti na anabhāvaṃ gamaiti| uppan‍naṃ byāpādavitak‍kaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍naṃ vihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍nuppan‍nai pāpakai akusalai dhammai adhivāsaiti, nappajahati  na vinaudaiti na byantī karauti na anabhāvaṃ gamaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na āsāṭikaṃ hāraitā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na vaṇaṃ paṭicchādaitā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā nimittaggāhī hauti anubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya na paṭipaj‍jati, na rakkhati  cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai na saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā…pai॰… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā…pai॰… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya nimittaggāhī hauti anubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya na paṭipaj‍jati, na  rakkhati manindriyaṃ, manindriyai na saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na vaṇaṃ paṭicchādaitā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na dhūmaṃ kattā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ na vitthāraina paraisaṃ daisaitā hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na dhūmaṃ kattā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na titthaṃ jānāti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yai tai bhikkhū bahussutā āgatāgamā dhammadharā vinayadharā mātikādharā, tai kālaina kālaṃ upasaṅkamitvā na paripucchati, na paripañhati – ‘idaṃ, bhantai, kathaṃ? imassa kau atthau’ti? tassa tai āyasmantau avivaṭañ‍caiva na vivaranti, anuttānīkatañ‍ca na uttānī karaunti, anaikavihitaisu  ca kaṅkhāṭhānīyaisu dhammaisu kaṅkhaṃ na paṭivinaudainti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na titthaṃ jānāti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na pītaṃ jānāti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai daisiyamānai na labhati atthavaidaṃ, na labhati dhammavaidaṃ, na labhati dhammūpasaṃhitaṃ pāmauj‍jaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na pītaṃ jānāti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na  vīthiṃ jānāti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na vīthiṃ jānāti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na gaucarakusalau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cattārau satipaṭṭhānai yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na gaucarakusalau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anavasaisadauhī hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuṃ saddhā gahapatikā abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavārainti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraihi, tatra bhikkhu mattaṃ na jānāti paṭiggahaṇāya| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anavasaisadauhī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yai tai bhikkhū thairā rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitā saṅghapitarau saṅghapariṇāyakā tai na atiraikapūjāya pūjaitā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yai tai bhikkhū thairā rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitā saṅghapitarau saṅghapariṇāyakā, taisu na maittaṃ kāyakammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhāpaiti āvi caiva rahau ca; na maittaṃ vacīkammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhāpaiti āvi caiva rahau ca; na maittaṃ manaukammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhāpaiti āvi caiva rahau ca| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yai tai bhikkhū thairā rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitā saṅghapitarau saṅghapariṇāyakā tai na atiraikapūjāya pūjaitā hauti|

‘‘imaihi khau bhikkhavai aikādasahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu abhabbau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jituṃ|

348. ‘‘aikādasahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau gaupālakau bhabbau gaugaṇaṃ pariharituṃ phātiṃ kātuṃ| katamaihi aikādasahi? idha, bhikkhavai, gaupālakau rūpañ‍ñū hauti, lakkhaṇakusalau hauti, āsāṭikaṃ hāraitā hauti, vaṇaṃ paṭicchādaitā hauti, dhūmaṃ kattā hauti, titthaṃ jānāti, pītaṃ jānāti, vīthiṃ jānāti, gaucarakusalau hauti, sāvasaisadauhī ca hauti| yai tai usabhā gaupitarau gaupariṇāyakā tai atiraikapūjāya pūjaitā hauti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aikādasahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau gaupālakau bhabbau gaugaṇaṃ pariharituṃ phātiṃ kātuṃ| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, aikādasahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bhabbau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jituṃ| katamaihi aikādasahi? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu rūpañ‍ñū hauti, lakkhaṇakusalau hauti, āsāṭikaṃ hāraitā hauti, vaṇaṃ paṭicchādaitā hauti, dhūmaṃ kattā hauti, titthaṃ jānāti, pītaṃ jānāti, vīthiṃ jānāti, gaucarakusalau hauti, sāvasaisadauhī ca hauti| yai tai bhikkhū thairā rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitā saṅghapitarau saṅghapariṇāyakā tai atiraikapūjāya pūjaitā hauti|

349. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu rūpañ‍ñū hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaṃ kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ sabbaṃ rūpaṃ ‘cattāri mahābhūtāni, catun‍nañ‍ca mahābhūtānaṃ upādāyarūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu rūpañ‍ñū hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu lakkhaṇakusalau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kammalakkhaṇau bālau, kammalakkhaṇau paṇḍitauti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu lakkhaṇakusalau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āsāṭikaṃ hāraitā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu uppan‍naṃ  kāmavitak‍kaṃ nādhivāsaiti, pajahati vinaudaiti byantī karauti anabhāvaṃ gamaiti| uppan‍naṃ byāpādavitak‍kaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍naṃ vihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍nuppan‍nai pāpakai akusalai dhammai nādhivāsaiti, pajahati vinaudaiti byantī karauti anabhāvaṃ gamaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āsāṭikaṃ hāraitā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaṇaṃ paṭicchādaitā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhunā  rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā…pai॰… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā…pai॰… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati manindriyaṃ, manindriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaṇaṃ paṭicchādaitā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhūmaṃ kattā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yathāsutaṃ yathāpariyattaṃ dhammaṃ vitthāraina paraisaṃ daisaitā hauti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhūmaṃ kattā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu titthaṃ jānāti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yai tai bhikkhū bahussutā āgatāgamā dhammadharā vinayadharā mātikādharā tai kālaina kālaṃ  upasaṅkamitvā paripucchati, paripañhati – ‘idaṃ, bhantai, kathaṃ? imassa kau atthau’ti? tassa tai āyasmantau avivaṭañ‍caiva vivaranti, anuttānīkatañ‍ca uttānī karaunti, anaikavihitaisu ca kaṅkhāṭhānīyaisu dhammaisu kaṅkhaṃ paṭivinaudainti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu titthaṃ jānāti|
