‘‘kathañ‍ca bhikkhavai, bhikkhu  pītaṃ jānāti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai daisiyamānai labhati atthavaidaṃ, labhati dhammavaidaṃ, labhati dhammūpasaṃhitaṃ pāmauj‍jaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pītaṃ jānāti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vīthiṃ jānāti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vīthiṃ jānāti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu gaucarakusalau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cattārau satipaṭṭhānai yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu gaucarakusalau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca  bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sāvasaisadauhī hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuṃ saddhā gahapatikā abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavārainti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraihi| tatra bhikkhu mattaṃ jānāti paṭiggahaṇāya| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sāvasaisadauhī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yai tai bhikkhū thairā rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitā saṅghapitarau saṅghapariṇāyakā, tai atiraikapūjāya pūjaitā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yai tai bhikkhū thairā rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitā saṅghapitarau saṅghapariṇāyakā taisu maittaṃ kāyakammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhāpaiti āvi caiva rahau  ca; maittaṃ vacīkammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhāpaiti āvi caiva rahau ca; maittaṃ manaukammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhāpaiti āvi caiva rahau ca| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yai tai bhikkhū thairā rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitā saṅghapitarau saṅghapariṇāyakā tai atiraikapūjāya pūjaitā hauti|

‘‘imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, aikādasahi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bhabbau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jitu’’nti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

mahāgaupālakasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. cūḷagaupālakasuttaṃ

350. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaj‍jīsu viharati uk‍kacailāyaṃ gaṅgāya nadiyā tīrai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, māgadhakau gaupālakau duppañ‍ñajātikau, vassānaṃ pacchimai māsai saradasamayai, asamavaikkhitvā gaṅgāya nadiyā aurimaṃ tīraṃ, asamavaikkhitvā pārimaṃ tīraṃ, atitthainaiva gāvau patāraisi uttaraṃ tīraṃ suvidaihānaṃ| atha khau, bhikkhavai, gāvau majjhaigaṅgāya nadiyā sautai āmaṇḍaliyaṃ karitvā tatthaiva anayabyasanaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi sau, bhikkhavai, māgadhakau gaupālakau duppañ‍ñajātikau, vassānaṃ pacchimai māsai saradasamayai, asamavaikkhitvā gaṅgāya nadiyā aurimaṃ tīraṃ, asamavaikkhitvā pārimaṃ tīraṃ, atitthainaiva gāvau patāraisi uttaraṃ tīraṃ suvidaihānaṃ| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai hi kaici yai kaici (syā॰ kaṃ॰) samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā akusalā imassa laukassa akusalā parassa laukassa, akusalā māradhaiyyassa akusalā amāradhaiyyassa, akusalā mac‍cudhaiyyassa akusalā amac‍cudhaiyyassa, taisaṃ yai sautabbaṃ saddahātabbaṃ mañ‍ñissanti, taisaṃ taṃ bhavissati dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya|

351. ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, māgadhakau gaupālakau sappañ‍ñajātikau, vassānaṃ  pacchimai māsai saradasamayai, samavaikkhitvā gaṅgāya nadiyā aurimaṃ tīraṃ, samavaikkhitvā pārimaṃ tīraṃ, titthainaiva gāvau patāraisi uttaraṃ tīraṃ suvidaihānaṃ| sau paṭhamaṃ patāraisi yai tai usabhā gaupitarau gaupariṇāyakā| tai tiriyaṃ gaṅgāya sautaṃ chaitvā sautthinā pāraṃ agamaṃsu| athāparai patāraisi balavagāvau dammagāvau| taipi tiriyaṃ gaṅgāya sautaṃ chaitvā sautthinā pāraṃ agamaṃsu| athāparai patāraisi vacchatarai vacchatariyau| taipi tiriyaṃ gaṅgāya sautaṃ chaitvā sautthinā pāraṃ agamaṃsu| athāparai patāraisi vacchakai kisābalakai kisabalakai (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)| taipi tiriyaṃ gaṅgāya sautaṃ chaitvā sautthinā pāraṃ agamaṃsu| bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, vacchakau taruṇakau tāvadaiva jātakau mātugauravakaina vuyhamānau, saupi tiriyaṃ gaṅgāya sautaṃ chaitvā sautthinā pāraṃ agamāsi| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā  hi sau, bhikkhavai, māgadhakau gaupālakau sappañ‍ñajātikau, vassānaṃ pacchimai māsai saradasamayai, samavaikkhitvā gaṅgāya nadiyā aurimaṃ  tīraṃ, samavaikkhitvā pārimaṃ tīraṃ, titthainaiva gāvau patāraisi uttaraṃ tīraṃ suvidaihānaṃ| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai hi kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā kusalā imassa laukassa kusalā parassa laukassa, kusalā māradhaiyyassa kusalā amāradhaiyyassa, kusalā mac‍cudhaiyyassa kusalā amac‍cudhaiyyassa, taisaṃ yai sautabbaṃ saddahātabbaṃ mañ‍ñissanti, taisaṃ taṃ bhavissati dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya|

352. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai tai usabhā gaupitarau gaupariṇāyakā tai tiriyaṃ gaṅgāya sautaṃ chaitvā sautthinā pāraṃ agamaṃsu, aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai tai bhikkhū arahantau khīṇāsavā vusitavantau katakaraṇīyā auhitabhārā anuppattasadatthā parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanā sammadañ‍ñā vimuttā, tai tiriyaṃ mārassa sautaṃ chaitvā sautthinā pāraṃ gatā|

‘‘saiyyathāpi tai, bhikkhavai, balavagāvau dammagāvau tiriyaṃ gaṅgāya sautaṃ chaitvā sautthinā pāraṃ agamaṃsu, aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai tai bhikkhū pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikā tattha parinibbāyinau anāvattidhammā tasmā laukā, taipi tiriyaṃ mārassa sautaṃ chaitvā sautthinā pāraṃ gamissanti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi tai, bhikkhavai, vacchatarā vacchatariyau tiriyaṃ gaṅgāya sautaṃ chaitvā sautthinā pāraṃ agamaṃsu, aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai tai bhikkhū tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā rāgadausamauhānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāminau sakiṃdaiva imaṃ laukaṃ āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karissanti, taipi tiriyaṃ mārassa sautaṃ chaitvā sautthinā pāraṃ gamissanti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi tai, bhikkhavai, vacchakā kisābalakā tiriyaṃ gaṅgāya sautaṃ chaitvā sautthinā pāraṃ agamaṃsu, aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai tai bhikkhū tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā sautāpan‍nā avinipātadhammā niyatā sambaudhiparāyanā, taipi tiriyaṃ mārassa sautaṃ chaitvā sautthinā pāraṃ gamissanti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi sau, bhikkhavai, vacchakau taruṇakau tāvadaiva jātakau mātugauravakaina  vuyhamānau tiriyaṃ  gaṅgāya sautaṃ chaitvā sautthinā  pāraṃ agamāsi, aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai tai bhikkhū dhammānusārinau saddhānusārinau, taipi tiriyaṃ mārassa sautaṃ chaitvā sautthinā pāraṃ gamissanti|

‘‘ahaṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, kusalau  imassa laukassa kusalau parassa laukassa, kusalau māradhaiyyassa kusalau amāradhaiyyassa, kusalau mac‍cudhaiyyassa kusalau amac‍cudhaiyyassa| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, yai sautabbaṃ saddahātabbaṃ mañ‍ñissanti, taisaṃ taṃ bhavissati dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvā sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘ayaṃ laukau parau laukau, jānatā suppakāsitau|

yañ‍ca māraina sampattaṃ, appattaṃ yañ‍ca mac‍cunā||

‘‘sabbaṃ laukaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, sambuddhaina pajānatā|

vivaṭaṃ amatadvāraṃ, khaimaṃ nibbānapattiyā||

‘‘chin‍naṃ pāpimatau sautaṃ, viddhastaṃ vinaḷīkataṃ|

pāmauj‍jabahulā hautha, khaimaṃ pattattha patthaitha (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰ aṭṭhakathāyaṃ saṃvaṇṇaitabbapāṭhau) bhikkhavau’’ti||

cūḷagaupālakasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. cūḷasac‍cakasuttaṃ

353. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau vaisāliyaṃ paṭivasati bhassappavādakau paṇḍitavādau sādhusammatau bahujanassa| sau vaisāliyaṃ parisati aivaṃ vācaṃ bhāsati – ‘‘nāhaṃ taṃ passāmi samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā, saṅghiṃ gaṇiṃ gaṇācariyaṃ, api arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ paṭijānamānaṃ, yau mayā vādaina vādaṃ samāraddhau na saṅkampaiyya na sampakampaiyya na sampavaidhaiyya, yassa na kacchaihi saidā muc‍caiyyuṃ| thūṇaṃ caipāhaṃ acaitanaṃ vādaina vādaṃ samārabhaiyyaṃ, sāpi mayā vādaina vādaṃ samāraddhā  saṅkampaiyya sampakampaiyya sampavaidhaiyya| kau pana vādau manussabhūtassā’’ti?

atha khau āyasmā assaji pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya vaisāliṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| addasā khau sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau vaisāliyaṃ jaṅghāvihāraṃ anucaṅkamamānau anuvicaramānau  āyasmantaṃ assajiṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna yaināyasmā assaji tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā assajinā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau āyasmantaṃ assajiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kathaṃ pana, bhau assaji, samaṇau gautamau sāvakai vinaiti, kathaṃbhāgā  ca pana samaṇassa gautamassa sāvakaisu anusāsanī bahulā pavattatī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ khau, aggivaissana, bhagavā sāvakai vinaiti, aivaṃbhāgā ca pana bhagavatau sāvakaisu anusāsanī bahulā pavattati – ‘rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ, vaidanā anic‍cā, sañ‍ñā anic‍cā, saṅkhārā anic‍cā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ| rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anattā, vaidanā anattā, sañ‍ñā anattā, saṅkhārā anattā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā| sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā, sabbai dhammā anattā’ti| aivaṃ khau, aggivaissana, bhagavā sāvakai vinaiti, aivaṃbhāgā ca pana bhagavatau sāvakaisu anusāsanī bahulā pavattatī’’ti| ‘‘dussutaṃ vata, bhau assaji, assumha yai mayaṃ aivaṃvādiṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ assumha| appaiva nāma mayaṃ kadāci karahaci taina bhautā gautamaina saddhiṃ samāgacchaiyyāma, appaiva nāma siyā kaucidaiva kathāsal‍lāpau, appaiva nāma tasmā pāpakā diṭṭhigatā vivaicaiyyāmā’’ti|

354. taina khau pana samayaina pañ‍camattāni licchavisatāni santhāgārai sandhāgārai (ka॰) san‍nipatitāni haunti kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| atha khau sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau yaina tai licchavī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tai licchavī aitadavauca – ‘‘abhik‍kamantu bhauntau licchavī, abhik‍kamantu bhauntau licchavī, aj‍ja mai samaṇaina gautamaina saddhiṃ kathāsal‍lāpau bhavissati| sacai mai samaṇau gautamau tathā patiṭṭhissati yathā ca mai yathāssa mai (sī॰ pī॰) ñātañ‍ñataraina sāvakaina assajinā nāma bhikkhunā patiṭṭhitaṃ, saiyyathāpi  nāma balavā purisau dīghalaumikaṃ aiḷakaṃ laumaisu gahaitvā ākaḍḍhaiyya parikaḍḍhaiyya samparikaḍḍhaiyya, aivamaivāhaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ vādaina vādaṃ ākaḍḍhissāmi parikaḍḍhissāmi samparikaḍḍhissāmi| saiyyathāpi nāma balavā sauṇḍikākammakārau mahantaṃ sauṇḍikākiḷañ‍jaṃ gambhīrai udakarahadai pakkhipitvā kaṇṇai gahaitvā ākaḍḍhaiyya parikaḍḍhaiyya samparikaḍḍhaiyya, aivamaivāhaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ vādaina vādaṃ ākaḍḍhissāmi parikaḍḍhissāmi samparikaḍḍhissāmi| saiyyathāpi nāma balavā sauṇḍikādhuttau vālaṃ  thālaṃ (ka॰) kaṇṇai gahaitvā audhunaiyya niddhunaiyya nipphauṭaiyya nicchādaiyya (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), nicchauṭaiyya (ka॰), nippauṭhaiyya (syā॰ kaṃ॰) aivamaivāhaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ vādaina vādaṃ audhunissāmi niddhunissāmi nipphauṭaissāmi| saiyyathāpi nāma kuñ‍jarau saṭṭhihāyanau gambhīraṃ paukkharaṇiṃ augāhaitvā sāṇadhauvikaṃ nāma kīḷitajātaṃ kīḷati, aivamaivāhaṃ samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ sāṇadhauvikaṃ mañ‍ñai kīḷitajātaṃ kīḷissāmi| abhik‍kamantu bhauntau licchavī, abhik‍kamantu bhauntau licchavī, aj‍ja mai samaṇaina gautamaina saddhiṃ kathāsal‍lāpau bhavissatī’’ti| tatraikac‍cai licchavī aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘kiṃ samaṇau gautamau sac‍cakassa nigaṇṭhaputtassa vādaṃ āraupaissati, atha khau sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau samaṇassa gautamassa vādaṃ āraupaissatī’’ti? aikac‍cai licchavī aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘kiṃ sau bhavamānau sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau yau bhagavatau vādaṃ āraupaissati, atha khau bhagavā sac‍cakassa nigaṇṭhaputtassa vādaṃ āraupaissatī’’ti? atha khau sac‍cakau  nigaṇṭhaputtau pañ‍camattaihi licchavisataihi parivutau yaina mahāvanaṃ kūṭāgārasālā tainupasaṅkami|

355. taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū abbhaukāsai caṅkamanti| atha khau sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘kahaṃ nu khau, bhau, aitarahi sau bhavaṃ gautamau viharati? dassanakāmā hi mayaṃ taṃ bhavantaṃ gautama’’nti| ‘‘aisa, aggivaissana, bhagavā mahāvanaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai divāvihāraṃ nisin‍nau’’ti| atha khau sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau mahatiyā licchaviparisāya saddhiṃ mahāvanaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| taipi khau licchavī appaikac‍cai bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu, appaikac‍cai bhagavatā  saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu, sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| appaikac‍cai yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu, appaikac‍cai bhagavatau santikai nāmagauttaṃ sāvaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu, appaikac‍cai tuṇhībhūtā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu|

356. aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pucchaiyyāhaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ kiñ‍cidaiva daisaṃ, sacai mai bhavaṃ gautamau aukāsaṃ karauti pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇāyā’’ti| ‘‘puccha, aggivaissana, yadākaṅkhasī’’ti| ‘‘kathaṃ pana bhavaṃ gautamau sāvakai vinaiti, kathaṃbhāgā ca pana bhautau gautamassa sāvakaisu anusāsanī bahulā pavattatī’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, sāvakai vinaimi, aivaṃbhāgā ca pana mai sāvakaisu anusāsanī bahulā pavattati – ‘rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ, vaidanā anic‍cā, sañ‍ñā anic‍cā, saṅkhārā anic‍cā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ| rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anattā, vaidanā anattā, sañ‍ñā anattā, saṅkhārā anattā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā| sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā, sabbai dhammā anattā’ti| aivaṃ khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, sāvakai vinaimi, aivaṃbhāgā ca pana mai sāvakaisu anusāsanī bahulā pavattatī’’ti|

‘‘upamā maṃ, bhau gautama, paṭibhātī’’ti| ‘‘paṭibhātu taṃ, aggivaissanā’’ti bhagavā avauca|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, yai kaicimai bījagāmabhūtagāmā vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍janti, sabbai tai pathaviṃ nissāya pathaviyaṃ patiṭṭhāya| aivamaitai bījagāmabhūtagāmā vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍janti| saiyyathāpi vā pana, bhau gautama, yai kaicimai balakaraṇīyā kammantā karīyanti, sabbai tai pathaviṃ nissāya pathaviyaṃ patiṭṭhāya| aivamaitai balakaraṇīyā kammantā karīyanti| aivamaiva khau, bhau gautama, rūpattāyaṃ purisapuggalau rūpai patiṭṭhāya puñ‍ñaṃ vā apuñ‍ñaṃ vā pasavati, vaidanattāyaṃ purisapuggalau vaidanāyaṃ patiṭṭhāya puñ‍ñaṃ vā  apuñ‍ñaṃ vā pasavati, sañ‍ñattāyaṃ purisapuggalau sañ‍ñāyaṃ patiṭṭhāya puñ‍ñaṃ vā apuñ‍ñaṃ vā pasavati, saṅkhārattāyaṃ purisapuggalau saṅkhāraisu  patiṭṭhāya puñ‍ñaṃ vā apuñ‍ñaṃ vā pasavati, viñ‍ñāṇattāyaṃ purisapuggalau viñ‍ñāṇai patiṭṭhāya puñ‍ñaṃ vā apuñ‍ñaṃ vā pasavatī’’ti|

‘‘nanu tvaṃ, aggivaissana, aivaṃ vadaisi – ‘rūpaṃ mai attā, vaidanā mai attā, sañ‍ñā mai attā, saṅkhārā mai attā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘ahañhi, bhau gautama, aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘rūpaṃ mai attā, vaidanā mai attā, sañ‍ñā mai attā, saṅkhārā mai attā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ mai attā’ti, ayañ‍ca mahatī janatā’’ti|

‘‘kiñhi tai, aggivaissana, mahatī janatā karissati? iṅgha tvaṃ, aggivaissana, sakañ‍ñaiva vādaṃ nibbaiṭhaihī’’ti| ‘‘ahañhi, bhau gautama, aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘rūpaṃ mai attā, vaidanā mai attā, sañ‍ñā mai attā, saṅkhārā mai attā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ mai attā’’’ti|

357. ‘‘taina hi, aggivaissana, tañ‍ñaivaittha paṭipucchissāmi, yathā tai khamaiyya tathā naṃ tathā taṃ (ka॰) byākaraiyyāsi| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, aggivaissana, vattaiyya rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa sakasmiṃ vijitai vasau – ghātaitāyaṃ vā ghātaituṃ, jāpaitāyaṃ vā jāpaituṃ, pabbājaitāyaṃ vā pabbājaituṃ, saiyyathāpi rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa, saiyyathāpi vā pana rañ‍ñau māgadhassa ajātasattussa vaidaihiputtassā’’ti? ‘‘vattaiyya, bhau gautama, rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa sakasmiṃ vijitai vasau – ghātaitāyaṃ vā ghātaituṃ, jāpaitāyaṃ vā jāpaituṃ, pabbājaitāyaṃ vā pabbājaituṃ, saiyyathāpi rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa, saiyyathāpi vā pana rañ‍ñau māgadhassa ajātasattussa vaidaihiputtassa| imaisampi hi, bhau gautama, saṅghānaṃ gaṇānaṃ – saiyyathidaṃ, vaj‍jīnaṃ mal‍lānaṃ – vattati sakasmiṃ vijitai vasau – ghātaitāyaṃ vā ghātaituṃ, jāpaitāyaṃ vā jāpaituṃ, pabbājaitāyaṃ vā pabbājaituṃ| kiṃ pana rañ‍ñau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa, saiyyathāpi rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa, saiyyathāpi vā pana rañ‍ñau māgadhassa ajātasattussa vaidaihiputtassa? vattaiyya, bhau gautama, vattituñ‍ca marahatī’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, aggivaissana, yaṃ tvaṃ aivaṃ vadaisi – ‘rūpaṃ mai attā’ti, vattati tai  tasmiṃ rūpai vasau – aivaṃ mai rūpaṃ hautu, aivaṃ mai rūpaṃ mā ahausī’’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau tuṇhī ahausi| dutiyampi khau bhagavā sac‍cakaṃ nigaṇṭhaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, aggivaissana, yaṃ tvaṃ aivaṃ vadaisi – ‘rūpaṃ mai attā’ti, vattati tai tasmiṃ rūpai vasau – aivaṃ mai rūpaṃ hautu, aivaṃ mai rūpaṃ mā ahausī’’ti? dutiyampi  khau sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau tuṇhī ahausi| atha khau bhagavā sac‍cakaṃ nigaṇṭhaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘byākarauhi dāni, aggivaissana, na dāni tai tuṇhībhāvassa kālau| yau kauci, aggivaissana tathāgataina yāvatatiyaṃ sahadhammikaṃ pañhaṃ puṭṭhau na byākarauti, aitthaivassa sattadhā muddhā phalatī’’ti|

taina khau pana samayaina vajirapāṇi yakkhau āyasaṃ vajiraṃ ādāya ādittaṃ sampaj‍jalitaṃ sajautibhūtaṃ sac‍cakassa nigaṇṭhaputtassa uparivaihāsaṃ ṭhitau hauti – ‘sacāyaṃ  sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau bhagavatā yāvatatiyaṃ sahadhammikaṃ pañhaṃ puṭṭhau na byākarissati aitthaivassa sattadhā muddhaṃ phālaissāmī’ti| taṃ khau pana vajirapāṇiṃ yakkhaṃ bhagavā caiva passati sac‍cakau ca nigaṇṭhaputtau| atha khau sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau bhītau saṃviggau laumahaṭṭhajātau bhagavantaṃyaiva  tāṇaṃ gavaisī bhagavantaṃyaiva laiṇaṃ gavaisī bhagavantaṃyaiva saraṇaṃ gavaisī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pucchatu maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau, byākarissāmī’’ti|

358. ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, aggivaissana, yaṃ tvaṃ aivaṃ vadaisi – ‘rūpaṃ mai attā’ti, vattati tai tasmiṃ rūpai vasau – aivaṃ mai rūpaṃ hautu, aivaṃ mai rūpaṃ mā ahausī’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’|

‘‘manasi karauhi, aggivaissana; manasi karitvā khau, aggivaissana, byākarauhi| na khau tai sandhiyati purimaina vā pacchimaṃ pacchimaina vā purimaṃ| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, aggivaissana, yaṃ tvaṃ aivaṃ vadaisi – ‘vaidanā mai attā’ti, vattati tai tissaṃ vaidanāyaṃ tāyaṃ vaidanāyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) vasau – aivaṃ mai vaidanā hautu, aivaṃ mai vaidanā mā ahausī’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’|

‘‘manasi karauhi, aggivaissana; manasi karitvā khau, aggivaissana, byākarauhi| na khau tai sandhiyati purimaina vā pacchimaṃ, pacchimaina vā purimaṃ| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, aggivaissana, yaṃ tvaṃ aivaṃ vadaisi – ‘sañ‍ñā mai attā’ti, vattati tai tissaṃ sañ‍ñāyaṃ vasau – aivaṃ mai sañ‍ñā hautu, aivaṃ mai sañ‍ñā mā ahausī’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’|

‘‘manasi  karauhi, aggivaissana; manasi karitvā khau, aggivaissana, byākarauhi| na khau tai sandhiyati purimaina vā pacchimaṃ, pacchimaina vā purimaṃ| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, aggivaissana, yaṃ tvaṃ aivaṃ vadaisi – ‘saṅkhārā mai attā’ti, vattati tai taisu saṅkhāraisu vasau – aivaṃ mai saṅkhārā hauntu, aivaṃ mai saṅkhārā mā ahaisu’’nti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’|

‘‘manasi karauhi, aggivaissana; manasi karitvā khau, aggivaissana, byākarauhi| na khau tai sandhiyati purimaina vā pacchimaṃ, pacchimaina vā purimaṃ| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, aggivaissana, yaṃ tvaṃ aivaṃ vadaisi – ‘viñ‍ñāṇaṃ mai attā’ti, vattati tai tasmiṃ viñ‍ñāṇai vasau – aivaṃ mai viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hautu, aivaṃ mai viñ‍ñāṇaṃ mā ahausī’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’|

‘‘manasi karauhi, aggivaissana; manasi karitvā khau, aggivaissana, byākarauhi| na khau tai sandhiyati purimaina vā pacchimaṃ, pacchimaina vā purimaṃ| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, aggivaissana, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau  mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, aggivaissana, vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā…pai॰… saṅkhārā…pai॰… taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, aggivaissana, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ  – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, aggivaissana, yau nu khau dukkhaṃ al‍līnau dukkhaṃ upagatau dukkhaṃ ajjhausitau, dukkhaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassati, api nu khau sau sāmaṃ vā dukkhaṃ parijānaiyya, dukkhaṃ vā parikkhaipaitvā viharaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘kiñhi siyā, bhau gautama? nau hidaṃ, bhau gautamā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, aggivaissana, nanu tvaṃ aivaṃ santai dukkhaṃ al‍līnau dukkhaṃ upagatau dukkhaṃ ajjhausitau, dukkhaṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘kiñhi nau siyā, bhau gautama? aivamaitaṃ bhau gautamā’’ti|

359. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, aggivaissana, purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau tiṇhaṃ kuṭhāriṃ kudhāriṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) ādāya vanaṃ pavisaiyya| sau tattha passaiyya mahantaṃ kadalikkhandhaṃ ujuṃ navaṃ akuk‍kukajātaṃ akuk‍kuṭajātaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰)| tamainaṃ mūlai chindaiyya, mūlai chaitvā aggai chindaiyya, aggai chaitvā pattavaṭṭiṃ vinibbhujaiyya vinibbhuj‍jaiyya (ka॰)| sau tattha pattavaṭṭiṃ vinibbhujantau phaiggumpi nādhigacchaiyya, kutau sāraṃ? aivamaiva khau tvaṃ, aggivaissana, mayā sakasmiṃ vādai samanuyuñ‍jiyamānau samanugāhiyamānau samanubhāsiyamānau rittau tucchau aparaddhau| bhāsitā khau pana tai aisā, aggivaissana, vaisāliyaṃ parisati vācā – ‘nāhaṃ taṃ passāmi samaṇaṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā, saṅghiṃ gaṇiṃ gaṇācariyaṃ, api arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ paṭijānamānaṃ, yau mayā vādaina vādaṃ samāraddhau na saṅkampaiyya na sampakampaiyya na  sampavaidhaiyya, yassa na kacchaihi saidā muc‍caiyyuṃ| thūṇaṃ caipāhaṃ acaitanaṃ vādaina vādaṃ samārabhaiyyaṃ sāpi mayā vādaina vādaṃ samāraddhā saṅkampaiyya sampakampaiyya sampavaidhaiyya| kau pana vādau manussabhūtassā’ti? tuyhaṃ khau pana, aggivaissana, appaikac‍cāni saidaphusitāni nalāṭā muttāni, uttarāsaṅgaṃ vinibhinditvā bhūmiyaṃ patiṭṭhitāni| mayhaṃ khau pana, aggivaissana, natthi aitarahi kāyasmiṃ saidau’’ti| iti bhagavā tasmiṃ tassaṃ (?) parisati suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ kāyaṃ vivari| aivaṃ  vuttai, sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau tuṇhībhūtau maṅkubhūtau pattakkhandhau adhaumukhau pajjhāyantau appaṭibhānau nisīdi|

360. atha khau dummukhau licchaviputtau sac‍cakaṃ nigaṇṭhaputtaṃ tuṇhībhūtaṃ maṅkubhūtaṃ pattakkhandhaṃ adhaumukhaṃ pajjhāyantaṃ appaṭibhānaṃ viditvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘upamā maṃ, bhagavā, paṭibhātī’’ti| ‘‘paṭibhātu taṃ, dummukhā’’ti bhagavā avauca| ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai, gāmassa vā nigamassa vā avidūrai paukkharaṇī| tatrāssa kak‍kaṭakau| atha khau, bhantai, sambahulā  kumārakā vā kumārikā vā tamhā gāmā vā nigamā vā nikkhamitvā yaina sā paukkharaṇī tainupasaṅkamaiyyuṃ; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ paukkharaṇiṃ augāhaitvā taṃ kak‍kaṭakaṃ udakā uddharitvā thalai patiṭṭhāpaiyyuṃ| yañ‍ñadaiva hi sau, bhantai, kak‍kaṭakau aḷaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiyya taṃ tadaiva tai kumārakā vā kumārikā vā kaṭṭhaina vā kathalaina vā sañchindaiyyuṃ sambhañ‍jaiyyuṃ  sampalibhañ‍jaiyyuṃ| aivañhi  sau, bhantai, kak‍kaṭakau sabbaihi aḷaihi sañchin‍naihi sambhaggaihi sampalibhaggaihi abhabbau taṃ paukkharaṇiṃ puna autarituṃ, saiyyathāpi pubbai| aivamaiva khau, bhantai, yāni sac‍cakassa nigaṇṭhaputtassa visūkāyitāni visaivitāni vipphanditāni tānipi sabbāni vipphanditāni kānici kānici tāni (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bhagavatā sañchin‍nāni sambhaggāni sampalibhaggāni; abhabbau ca dāni, bhantai, sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau puna bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamituṃ yadidaṃ vādādhippāyau’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau dummukhaṃ licchaviputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āgamaihi tvaṃ, dummukha, āgamaihi tvaṃ, dummukha ( ) (mukharausi tvaṃ dummukha) (syā॰ kaṃ॰) na mayaṃ tayā saddhiṃ mantaima, idha mayaṃ bhautā gautamaina saddhiṃ mantaima|

361. ‘‘tiṭṭhataisā, bhau gautama, amhākañ‍caiva añ‍ñaisañ‍ca puthusamaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ vācā| vilāpaṃ vilapitaṃ mañ‍ñai| kittāvatā ca nu khau bhautau gautamassa sāvakau sāsanakarau hauti auvādapatikarau tiṇṇavicikicchau vigatakathaṃkathau vaisāraj‍jappattau aparappac‍cayau satthusāsanai viharatī’’ti? ‘‘idha, aggivaissana, mama sāvakau yaṃ kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ rūpaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ  yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati; yā kāci vaidanā…pai॰… yā kāci sañ‍ñā…pai॰… yai kaici saṅkhārā…pai॰… yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā, yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| aittāvatā khau, aggivaissana, mama sāvakau sāsanakarau hauti auvādapatikarau tiṇṇavicikicchau vigatakathaṃkathau vaisāraj‍jappattau aparappac‍cayau satthusāsanai viharatī’’ti|

‘‘kittāvatā  pana, bhau gautama, bhikkhu arahaṃ hauti khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñā vimuttau’’ti? ‘‘idha, aggivaissana, bhikkhu yaṃ kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā sabbaṃ rūpaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na  maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā anupādā vimuttau hauti; yā kāci vaidanā…pai॰… yā kāci sañ‍ñā…pai॰… yai kaici saṅkhārā…pai॰… yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā  paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā anupādā vimuttau hauti| aittāvatā khau, aggivaissana, bhikkhu arahaṃ hauti khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñā vimuttau| aivaṃ vimuttacittau khau, aggivaissana, bhikkhu tīhi anuttariyaihi saman‍nāgatau  hauti – dassanānuttariyaina, paṭipadānuttariyaina, vimuttānuttariyaina| aivaṃ vimuttacittau khau, aggivaissana, bhikkhu tathāgatañ‍ñaiva sak‍karauti garuṃ karauti mānaiti pūjaiti – buddhau sau bhagavā baudhāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, dantau sau bhagavā damathāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, santau sau bhagavā samathāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, tiṇṇau sau bhagavā taraṇāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, parinibbutau sau bhagavā parinibbānāya dhammaṃ daisaitī’’ti|

362. aivaṃ vuttai, sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘mayamaiva, bhau gautama, dhaṃsī, mayaṃ pagabbā, yai mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ vādaina vādaṃ āsādaitabbaṃ amañ‍ñimha| siyā hi, bhau gautama, hatthiṃ pabhin‍naṃ āsaj‍ja purisassa sautthibhāvau, na tvaiva bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ āsaj‍ja siyā purisassa sautthibhāvau| siyā hi, bhau gautama, paj‍jalitaṃ jalantaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) aggikkhandhaṃ āsaj‍ja purisassa sautthibhāvau, na tvaiva bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ āsaj‍ja siyā purisassa sautthibhāvau| siyā hi, bhau gautama, āsīvisaṃ ghauravisaṃ āsaj‍ja purisassa sautthibhāvau, na tvaiva bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ āsaj‍ja siyā purisassa sautthibhāvau| mayamaiva, bhau gautama, dhaṃsī, mayaṃ pagabbā, yai mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ vādaina vādaṃ āsādaitabbaṃ amañ‍ñimha| adhivāsaitu adhivāsaitu ca (pī॰ ka॰) mai bhavaṃ gautamau svātanāya bhattaṃ saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghainā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina|

363. atha khau sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā tai licchavī āmantaisi – ‘‘suṇantu mai bhauntau licchavī, samaṇau  mai gautamau nimantitau svātanāya saddhiṃ  bhikkhusaṅghaina| taina mai abhiharaiyyātha yamassa patirūpaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyāthā’’ti| atha khau tai licchavī tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina sac‍cakassa nigaṇṭhaputtassa pañ‍camattāni thālipākasatāni bhattābhihāraṃ abhihariṃsu| atha khau nigaṇṭhaputtau sakai ārāmai paṇītaṃ khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ paṭiyādāpaitvā bhagavatau kālaṃ āraucāpaisi – ‘‘kālau, bhau gautama, niṭṭhitaṃ bhatta’’nti| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina sac‍cakassa nigaṇṭhaputtassa ārāmau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina| atha khau sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇītaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina sahatthā santappaisi sampavāraisi| atha khau sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau bhagavantaṃ bhuttāviṃ aunītapattapāṇiṃ añ‍ñataraṃ nīcaṃ āsanaṃ gahaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yamidaṃ, bhau gautama, dānai puñ‍ñañ‍ca puñ‍ñamahī ca taṃ dāyakānaṃ sukhāya hautū’’ti| ‘‘yaṃ khau, aggivaissana, tādisaṃ dakkhiṇaiyyaṃ āgamma avītarāgaṃ avītadausaṃ avītamauhaṃ, taṃ  dāyakānaṃ bhavissati| yaṃ khau, aggivaissana, mādisaṃ dakkhiṇaiyyaṃ āgamma vītarāgaṃ vītadausaṃ vītamauhaṃ, taṃ tuyhaṃ bhavissatī’’ti|

cūḷasac‍cakasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. mahāsac‍cakasuttaṃ

364. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ sunivatthau hauti pattacīvaramādāya vaisāliṃ piṇḍāya pavisitukāmau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya… pavisitukāmau hauti (sī॰)| atha khau sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau jaṅghāvihāraṃ anucaṅkamamānau anuvicaramānau yaina mahāvanaṃ kūṭāgārasālā tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau āyasmā ānandau sac‍cakaṃ nigaṇṭhaputtaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, sac‍cakau  nigaṇṭhaputtau āgacchati bhassappavādakau paṇḍitavādau sādhusammatau bahujanassa| aisau khau, bhantai, avaṇṇakāmau buddhassa, avaṇṇakāmau dhammassa, avaṇṇakāmau saṅghassa| sādhu, bhantai, bhagavā muhuttaṃ nisīdatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’ti| nisīdi bhagavā pañ‍ñattai āsanai| atha khau sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi, sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

365. ‘‘santi, bhau gautama, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā kāyabhāvanānuyaugamanuyuttā viharanti, nau cittabhāvanaṃ| phusanti hi tai, bhau gautama, sārīrikaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ| bhūtapubbaṃ, bhau gautama, sārīrikāya dukkhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhassa satau ūrukkhambhaupi nāma bhavissati, hadayampi nāma  phalissati, uṇhampi lauhitaṃ mukhatau uggamissati, ummādampi pāpuṇissati pāpuṇissanti (syā॰ kaṃ॰) cittakkhaipaṃ| tassa khau aitaṃ, bhau gautama, kāyanvayaṃ cittaṃ hauti, kāyassa vasaina vattati| taṃ kissa haitu? abhāvitattā  cittassa| santi pana, bhau gautama, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā cittabhāvanānuyaugamanuyuttā viharanti, nau kāyabhāvanaṃ| phusanti hi tai, bhau gautama, caitasikaṃ dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ| bhūtapubbaṃ, bhau gautama, caitasikāya dukkhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhassa satau ūrukkhambhaupi nāma bhavissati, hadayampi nāma phalissati, uṇhampi lauhitaṃ mukhatau uggamissati, ummādampi pāpuṇissati cittakkhaipaṃ| tassa khau aisau, bhau gautama, cittanvayau kāyau hauti, cittassa vasaina vattati| taṃ kissa haitu? abhāvitattā kāyassa| tassa mayhaṃ, bhau gautama, aivaṃ hauti – ‘addhā bhautau gautamassa sāvakā cittabhāvanānuyaugamanuyuttā viharanti, nau kāyabhāvana’’’nti|

366. ‘‘kinti pana tai, aggivaissana, kāyabhāvanā sutā’’ti? ‘‘saiyyathidaṃ – nandau vacchau, kisau saṃkic‍cau, makkhali gausālau – aitaihi, bhau gautama, acailakā muttācārā hatthāpalaikhanā naaihibhaddantikā natiṭṭhabhaddantikā naaihibhadantikā, natiṭṭhabhadantikā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) na abhihaṭaṃ na uddissakataṃ na nimantanaṃ sādiyanti, tai na kumbhimukhā  paṭiggaṇhanti na kaḷaupimukhā paṭiggaṇhanti na aiḷakamantaraṃ na daṇḍamantaraṃ na musalamantaraṃ na dvin‍naṃ bhuñ‍jamānānaṃ na gabbhiniyā na pāyamānāya na purisantaragatāya na saṅkittīsu na yattha sā upaṭṭhitau hauti na yattha makkhikā saṇḍasaṇḍacārinī, na macchaṃ na maṃsaṃ na suraṃ na mairayaṃ na thusaudakaṃ pivanti| tai aikāgārikā vā haunti aikālaupikā, dvāgārikā vā haunti dvālaupikā…pai॰… sattāgārikā vā haunti sattālaupikā| aikissāpi dattiyā yāpainti, dvīhipi dattīhi yāpainti…pai॰… sattahipi dattīhi yāpainti| aikāhikampi āhāraṃ āhārainti, dvīhikampi āhāraṃ āhārainti…pai॰… sattāhikampi āhāraṃ āhārainti| iti aivarūpaṃ addhamāsikampi pariyāyabhattabhaujanānuyaugamanuyuttā viharantī’’ti|

‘‘kiṃ pana tai, aggivaissana, tāvatakainaiva yāpaintī’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama| appaikadā, bhau gautama, uḷārāni uḷārāni khādanīyāni khādanti, uḷārāni uḷārāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍janti, uḷārāni uḷārāni sāyanīyāni sāyanti, uḷārāni uḷārāni pānāni pivanti| tai imaṃ kāyaṃ balaṃ gāhainti nāma, brūhainti nāma, maidainti nāmā’’ti|

‘‘yaṃ khau tai, aggivaissana, purimaṃ pahāya pacchā upacinanti, aivaṃ imassa kāyassa ācayāpacayau hauti| kinti pana tai, aggivaissana, cittabhāvanā sutā’’ti? cittabhāvanāya khau  sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau bhagavatā puṭṭhau samānau na sampāyāsi|

367. atha khau bhagavā sac‍cakaṃ nigaṇṭhaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yāpi khau tai aisā, aggivaissana, purimā kāyabhāvanā bhāsitā sāpi ariyassa vinayai nau dhammikā kāyabhāvanā| kāyabhāvanampi kāyabhāvanaṃ hi (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) khau tvaṃ, aggivaissana, na añ‍ñāsi, kutau pana tvaṃ cittabhāvanaṃ  jānissasi? api ca, aggivaissana, yathā abhāvitakāyau ca hauti abhāvitacittau ca, bhāvitakāyau ca hauti bhāvitacittau ca| taṃ suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

368. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, aggivaissana, abhāvitakāyau ca hauti abhāvitacittau ca? idha, aggivaissana, assutavatau puthuj‍janassa uppaj‍jati sukhā vaidanā| sau sukhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhau samānau sukhasārāgī ca hauti sukhasārāgitañ‍ca āpaj‍jati| tassa sā sukhā vaidanā nirujjhati| sukhāya vaidanāya niraudhā uppaj‍jati dukkhā vaidanā| sau dukkhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhau samānau saucati kilamati paridaivati urattāḷiṃ kandati sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| tassa khau aisā, aggivaissana, uppan‍nāpi sukhā vaidanā cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati abhāvitattā kāyassa, uppan‍nāpi dukkhā vaidanā cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati abhāvitattā cittassa| yassa kassaci, aggivaissana, aivaṃ ubhataupakkhaṃ uppan‍nāpi sukhā vaidanā cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati abhāvitattā kāyassa, uppan‍nāpi dukkhā vaidanā cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati abhāvitattā cittassa, aivaṃ khau, aggivaissana, abhāvitakāyau ca hauti abhāvitacittau ca|

369. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, aggivaissana, bhāvitakāyau ca hauti bhāvitacittau ca? idha, aggivaissana, sutavatau ariyasāvakassa uppaj‍jati sukhā vaidanā| sau sukhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhau samānau na sukhasārāgī  ca hauti, na sukhasārāgitañ‍ca āpaj‍jati| tassa sā sukhā vaidanā nirujjhati| sukhāya vaidanāya niraudhā uppaj‍jati dukkhā vaidanā| sau dukkhāya vaidanāya phuṭṭhau samānau na saucati na kilamati na paridaivati na urattāḷiṃ kandati na sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati| tassa khau aisā, aggivaissana, uppan‍nāpi sukhā vaidanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati bhāvitattā kāyassa, uppan‍nāpi dukkhā vaidanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati bhāvitattā cittassa| yassa kassaci, aggivaissana, aivaṃ ubhataupakkhaṃ uppan‍nāpi sukhā vaidanā cittaṃ  na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati bhāvitattā kāyassa, uppan‍nāpi dukkhā vaidanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati bhāvitattā cittassa| aivaṃ khau, aggivaissana, bhāvitakāyau ca hauti bhāvitacittau cā’’ti|

370. ‘‘aivaṃ pasan‍nau ahaṃ bhautau gautamassa! bhavañhi gautamau bhāvitakāyau ca hauti bhāvitacittau cā’’ti| ‘‘addhā khau tai ayaṃ, aggivaissana, āsaj‍ja upanīya vācā bhāsitā, api ca tai ahaṃ byākarissāmi| yatau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau, taṃ vata mai uppan‍nā vā sukhā vaidanā cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassati, uppan‍nā vā dukkhā vaidanā cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ naitaṃ khauṭhānaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) vij‍jatī’’ti|

‘‘na hi nūna na hanūna (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bhautau gautamassa uppaj‍jati tathārūpā sukhā vaidanā yathārūpā uppan‍nā sukhā vaidanā cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhaiyya; na hi nūna bhautau gautamassa uppaj‍jati  tathārūpā dukkhā vaidanā yathārūpā uppan‍nā dukkhā vaidanā cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhaiyyā’’ti|

371. ‘‘kiñhi nau siyā, aggivaissana? idha mai, aggivaissana, pubbaiva sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau aitadahausi – ‘sambādhau gharāvāsau rajāpathau, abbhaukāsau pabbaj‍jā| nayidaṃ sukaraṃ agāraṃ ajjhāvasatā aikantaparipuṇṇaṃ aikantaparisuddhaṃ saṅkhalikhitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajaiyya’nti| sau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, aparaina samayaina daharauva samānau, susukāḷakaisau bhadraina yaubbanaina saman‍nāgatau paṭhamaina vayasā, akāmakānaṃ mātāpitūnaṃ assumukhānaṃ rudantānaṃ, kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajiṃ| sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau kiṃkusalagavaisī anuttaraṃ santivarapadaṃ pariyaisamānau yaina āḷārau kālāmau tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā āḷāraṃ kālāmaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘icchāmahaṃ, āvusau kālāma, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai brahmacariyaṃ caritu’nti| aivaṃ vuttai, aggivaissana, āḷārau kālāmau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘viharatāyasmā, tādisau ayaṃ dhammau yattha viñ‍ñū purisau nacirassaiva sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyyā’ti| sau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, nacirassaiva khippamaiva taṃ dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇiṃ| sau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, tāvatakainaiva auṭṭhapahatamattaina lapitalāpanamattaina ñāṇavādañ‍ca  vadāmi thairavādañ‍ca, ‘jānāmi passāmī’ti ca paṭijānāmi, ahañ‍caiva añ‍ñai ca| tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, aitadahausi – ‘na khau āḷārau kālāmau imaṃ dhammaṃ kaivalaṃ saddhāmattakaina sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā  sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharāmīti pavaidaiti, addhā āḷārau kālāmau imaṃ dhammaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ viharatī’’’ti|

‘‘atha khvāhaṃ, aggivaissana, yaina āḷārau kālāmau tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā āḷāraṃ  kālāmaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘kittāvatā nau, āvusau kālāma, imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharāmīti pavaidaisī’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, aggivaissana, āḷārau kālāmau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ pavaidaisi| tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, aitadahausi – ‘na khau āḷārassaiva kālāmassa atthi saddhā, mayhaṃpatthi saddhā; na khau āḷārassaiva kālāmassa atthi vīriyaṃ, mayhaṃpatthi vīriyaṃ; na khau āḷārassaiva kālāmassa atthi sati, mayhaṃpatthi sati; na khau āḷārassaiva kālāmassa atthi samādhi, mayhaṃpatthi samādhi; na khau āḷārassaiva kālāmassa atthi pañ‍ñā, mayhaṃpatthi pañ‍ñā; yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaṃ dhammaṃ āḷārau kālāmau sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharāmīti pavaidaiti tassa dhammassa sacchikiriyāya padahaiyya’nti| sau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, nacirassaiva khippamaiva taṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ|

‘‘atha khvāhaṃ, aggivaissana, yaina āḷārau kālāmau tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā āḷāraṃ kālāmaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘aittāvatā nau, āvusau kālāma, imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja pavaidaisī’ti? ‘aittāvatā khau ahaṃ, āvusau, imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja pavaidaimī’ti| ‘ahampi khau, āvusau, aittāvatā imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharāmī’ti| ‘lābhā nau, āvusau, suladdhaṃ nau, āvusau, yai mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ tādisaṃ sabrahmacāriṃ passāma| iti yāhaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja pavaidaimi taṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharasi; yaṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharasi tamahaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja pavaidaimi| iti yāhaṃ dhammaṃ jānāmi taṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ jānāsi; yaṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ jānāsi tamahaṃ dhammaṃ jānāmi| iti yādisau ahaṃ tādisau tuvaṃ, yādisau tuvaṃ tādisau ahaṃ| aihi dāni, āvusau, ubhauva santā imaṃ gaṇaṃ pariharāmā’ti| iti  khau, aggivaissana, āḷārau kālāmau ācariyau mai samānau (attanau) ( ) natthi (sī॰ pī॰) antaivāsiṃ maṃ samānaṃ attanā samasamaṃ ṭhapaisi, uḷārāya ca maṃ pūjāya pūjaisi| tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, aitadahausi – ‘nāyaṃ dhammau nibbidāya na virāgāya na niraudhāya na upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati, yāvadaiva ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanūpapattiyā’ti| sau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, taṃ dhammaṃ analaṅkaritvā tasmā dhammā nibbij‍ja apak‍kamiṃ|

372. ‘‘sau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, kiṃkusalagavaisī anuttaraṃ santivarapadaṃ  pariyaisamānau  yaina udakau rāmaputtau tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā udakaṃ rāmaputtaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘icchāmahaṃ, āvusau passa ma॰ ni॰ 1.278 pāsarāsisuttai imasmiṃ dhammavinayai brahmacariyaṃ caritu’nti| aivaṃ vuttai, aggivaissana, udakau rāmaputtau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘viharatāyasmā, tādisau ayaṃ dhammau yattha viñ‍ñū purisau nacirassaiva sakaṃ ācariyakaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyyā’ti| sau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, nacirassaiva khippamaiva taṃ dhammaṃ pariyāpuṇiṃ| sau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, tāvatakainaiva auṭṭhapahatamattaina lapitalāpanamattaina ñāṇavādañ‍ca vadāmi thairavādañ‍ca, ‘jānāmi passāmī’ti ca paṭijānāmi, ahañ‍caiva añ‍ñai ca| tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, aitadahausi – ‘na khau rāmau imaṃ dhammaṃ kaivalaṃ saddhāmattakaina sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharāmīti pavaidaisi| addhā rāmau imaṃ dhammaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ vihāsī’ti| atha khvāhaṃ, aggivaissana, yaina udakau rāmaputtau tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā udakaṃ rāmaputtaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘kittāvatā nau, āvusau, rāmau, imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharāmīti pavaidaisī’ti? aivaṃ vuttai, aggivaissana, udakau rāmaputtau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ pavaidaisi| tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, aitadahausi – ‘na khau rāmassaiva ahausi saddhā, mayhaṃpatthi saddhā; na khau rāmassaiva ahausi vīriyaṃ, mayhaṃpatthi vīriyaṃ; na khau rāmassaiva ahausi sati, mayhaṃpatthi sati; na khau rāmassaiva ahausi samādhi, mayhaṃpatthi  samādhi; na khau rāmassaiva ahausi pañ‍ñā, mayhaṃpatthi pañ‍ñā; yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaṃ dhammaṃ rāmau sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharāmīti pavaidaisi tassa dhammassa sacchikiriyāya  padahaiyya’nti| sau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, nacirassaiva khippamaiva taṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ|

‘‘atha khvāhaṃ, aggivaissana, yaina udakau rāmaputtau tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā udakaṃ rāmaputtaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘aittāvatā nau, āvusau, rāmau imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja pavaidaisī’ti? ‘aittāvatā khau, āvusau, rāmau imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja pavaidaisī’ti| ‘ahampi khau, āvusau, aittāvatā imaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharāmī’ti| ‘lābhā nau, āvusau, suladdhaṃ nau, āvusau, yai mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ tādisaṃ sabrahmacāriṃ passāma| iti yaṃ dhammaṃ rāmau sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja pavaidaisi, taṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharasi; yaṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharasi, taṃ dhammaṃ rāmau sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja pavaidaisi| iti yaṃ dhammaṃ rāmau abhiñ‍ñāsi taṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ jānāsi; yaṃ tvaṃ dhammaṃ jānāsi taṃ dhammaṃ rāmau abhiñ‍ñāsi| iti  yādisau rāmau ahausi tādisau tuvaṃ; yādisau tuvaṃ tādisau rāmau ahausi| aihi dāni, āvusau, tuvaṃ imaṃ gaṇaṃ pariharā’ti| iti khau, aggivaissana, udakau  rāmaputtau sabrahmacārī mai samānau ācariyaṭṭhānai ca maṃ ṭhapaisi, uḷārāya ca maṃ pūjāya pūjaisi| tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, aitadahausi – ‘nāyaṃ dhammau nibbidāya na virāgāya na niraudhāya na upasamāya na abhiñ‍ñāya na sambaudhāya na nibbānāya saṃvattati, yāvadaiva naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanūpapattiyā’ti| sau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, taṃ dhammaṃ analaṅkaritvā tasmā dhammā nibbij‍ja apak‍kamiṃ|

373. ‘‘sau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, kiṃkusalagavaisī anuttaraṃ santivarapadaṃ pariyaisamānau magadhaisu anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina uruvailā sainānigamau tadavasariṃ| tatthaddasaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ bhūmibhāgaṃ, pāsādikañ‍ca vanasaṇḍaṃ, nadiñ‍ca sandantiṃ saitakaṃ supatitthaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ, samantā ca gaucaragāmaṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, aitadahausi – ‘ramaṇīyau vata, bhau, bhūmibhāgau, pāsādikau ca vanasaṇḍau, nadī ca sandati saitakā supatitthā ramaṇīyā, samantā ca gaucaragāmau| alaṃ vatidaṃ kulaputtassa padhānatthikassa  padhānāyā’ti| sau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, tatthaiva nisīdiṃ ‘alamidaṃ padhānāyā’ti|

374. ‘‘apissumaṃ, aggivaissana, tissau upamā paṭibhaṃsu anacchariyā pubbai assutapubbā| saiyyathāpi, aggivaissana, al‍laṃ kaṭṭhaṃ sasnaihaṃ udakai nikkhittaṃ| atha purisau āgacchaiyya uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya – ‘aggiṃ abhinibbattaissāmi, taijau pātukarissāmī’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, aggivaissana, api nu sau purisau amuṃ al‍laṃ kaṭṭhaṃ sasnaihaṃ, udakai nikkhittaṃ, uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya abhimanthaintau aggiṃ abhinibbattaiyya, taijau pātukaraiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘aduñhi, bhau gautama, al‍laṃ kaṭṭhaṃ sasnaihaṃ, tañ‍ca pana udakai  nikkhittaṃ| yāvadaiva ca pana sau purisau kilamathassa vighātassa bhāgī assā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, aggivaissana, yai hi kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā kāyaina caiva cittaina ca kāmaihi avūpakaṭṭhā viharanti, yau ca naisaṃ kāmaisu kāmacchandau kāmasnaihau kāmamucchā kāmapipāsā kāmapariḷāhau, sau ca ajjhattaṃ na suppahīnau hauti, na suppaṭippassaddhau, aupak‍kamikā caipi tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidayanti, abhabbāva tai ñāṇāya dassanāya anuttarāya sambaudhāya| nau caipi tai bhauntau  samaṇabrāhmaṇā aupak‍kamikā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidayanti, abhabbāva tai ñāṇāya dassanāya anuttarāya sambaudhāya| ayaṃ khau maṃ, aggivaissana, paṭhamā upamā paṭibhāsi anacchariyā pubbai assutapubbā|

375. ‘‘aparāpi khau maṃ, aggivaissana, dutiyā upamā paṭibhāsi anacchariyā pubbai assutapubbā| saiyyathāpi, aggivaissana, al‍laṃ kaṭṭhaṃ sasnaihaṃ, ārakā udakā thalai nikkhittaṃ| atha purisau āgacchaiyya uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya – ‘aggiṃ abhinibbattaissāmi, taijau pātukarissāmī’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, aggivaissana, api nu sau purisau amuṃ al‍laṃ kaṭṭhaṃ sasnaihaṃ, ārakā udakā thalai nikkhittaṃ, uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya  abhimanthaintau aggiṃ abhinibbattaiyya taijau pātukaraiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘aduñhi, bhau gautama, al‍laṃ kaṭṭhaṃ sasnaihaṃ, kiñ‍cāpi ārakā udakā thalai nikkhittaṃ| yāvadaiva ca pana sau purisau kilamathassa vighātassa  bhāgī assāti| aivamaiva khau, aggivaissana, yai hi kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā kāyaina caiva cittaina ca kāmaihi vūpakaṭṭhā viharanti, yau ca naisaṃ kāmaisu kāmacchandau kāmasnaihau kāmamucchā kāmapipāsā kāmapariḷāhau sau ca ajjhattaṃ na suppahīnau hauti, na suppaṭippassaddhau, aupak‍kamikā caipi tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidayanti, abhabbāva tai ñāṇāya dassanāya anuttarāya sambaudhāya| nau caipi tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā aupak‍kamikā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidayanti, abhabbāva tai ñāṇāya dassanāya anuttarāya sambaudhāya| ayaṃ khau maṃ, aggivaissana, dutiyā upamā paṭibhāsi anacchariyā pubbai assutapubbā’’|

376. ‘‘aparāpi khau maṃ, aggivaissana, tatiyā upamā paṭibhāsi anacchariyā  pubbai assutapubbā| saiyyathāpi, aggivaissana, sukkhaṃ kaṭṭhaṃ kauḷāpaṃ, ārakā udakā thalai nikkhittaṃ| atha purisau āgacchaiyya uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya – ‘aggiṃ abhinibbattaissāmi, taijau pātukarissāmī’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, aggivaissana, api nu sau purisau amuṃ sukkhaṃ kaṭṭhaṃ kauḷāpaṃ, ārakā udakā thalai nikkhittaṃ, uttarāraṇiṃ ādāya  abhimanthaintau aggiṃ abhinibbattaiyya, taijau pātukaraiyyā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau gautama’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘aduñhi, bhau gautama, sukkhaṃ kaṭṭhaṃ kauḷāpaṃ, tañ‍ca pana ārakā udakā thalai nikkhitta’’nti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, aggivaissana, yai hi kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā kāyaina caiva cittaina ca kāmaihi vūpakaṭṭhā viharanti, yau ca naisaṃ kāmaisu kāmacchandau kāmasnaihau kāmamucchā kāmapipāsā kāmapariḷāhau, sau ca ajjhattaṃ suppahīnau hauti suppaṭippassaddhau, aupak‍kamikā caipi tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidayanti, bhabbāva tai ñāṇāya dassanāya anuttarāya sambaudhāya| nau caipi tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā aupak‍kamikā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidayanti, bhabbāva tai ñāṇāya dassanāya anuttarāya sambaudhāya| ayaṃ khau maṃ, aggivaissana, tatiyā upamā paṭibhāsi anacchariyā pubbai assutapubbā| imā khau maṃ, aggivaissana, tissau upamā paṭibhaṃsu anacchariyā pubbai assutapubbā|’’

377. ‘‘tassa  mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, aitadahausi – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ dantaibhi dantamādhāya passa ma॰ ni॰ 1.221 vitak‍kasaṇṭhānasuttai, jivhāya tāluṃ āhac‍ca, caitasā cittaṃ abhiniggaṇhaiyyaṃ abhinippīḷaiyyaṃ abhisantāpaiyya’nti| sau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, dantaibhi dantamādhāya, jivhāya tāluṃ āhac‍ca, caitasā cittaṃ abhiniggaṇhāmi abhinippīḷaimi abhisantāpaimi| tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, dantaibhi dantamādhāya jivhāya tāluṃ āhac‍ca caitasā cittaṃ abhiniggaṇhatau abhinippīḷayatau  abhisantāpayatau kacchaihi saidā muc‍canti| saiyyathāpi, aggivaissana, balavā purisau dubbalataraṃ purisaṃ sīsai vā gahaitvā khandhai vā gahaitvā abhiniggaṇhaiyya abhinippīḷaiyya abhisantāpaiyya, aivamaiva khau mai, aggivaissana, dantaibhi dantamādhāya, jivhāya tāluṃ āhac‍ca, caitasā cittaṃ abhiniggaṇhatau abhinippīḷayatau abhisantāpayatau kacchaihi saidā muc‍canti| āraddhaṃ khau pana mai, aggivaissana, vīriyaṃ hauti asal‍līnaṃ, upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā, sāraddhau ca pana mai kāyau  hauti appaṭippassaddhau tainaiva dukkhappadhānaina padhānābhitun‍nassa satau| aivarūpāpi khau mai, aggivaissana, uppan‍nā dukkhā vaidanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati|

378. ‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, aitadahausi – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ appāṇakaṃyaiva jhānaṃ jhāyaiyya’nti| sau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, mukhatau ca nāsatau ca assāsapassāsai uparundhiṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, mukhatau ca nāsatau ca assāsapassāsaisu uparuddhaisu kaṇṇasautaihi vātānaṃ nikkhamantānaṃ adhimattau saddau hauti| saiyyathāpi nāma kammāragaggariyā dhamamānāya adhimattau saddau hauti, aivamaiva khau mai, aggivaissana, mukhatau ca nāsatau ca assāsapassāsaisu  uparuddhaisu kaṇṇasautaihi vātānaṃ nikkhamantānaṃ adhimattau saddau hauti| āraddhaṃ khau pana mai, aggivaissana, vīriyaṃ hauti asal‍līnaṃ upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā| sāraddhau ca pana mai  kāyau hauti appaṭippassaddhau tainaiva dukkhappadhānaina padhānābhitun‍nassa satau| aivarūpāpi khau mai, aggivaissana, uppan‍nā dukkhā vaidanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, aitadahausi – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ appāṇakaṃyaiva jhānaṃ jhāyaiyya’nti| sau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, mukhatau ca nāsatau ca kaṇṇatau  ca assāsapassāsai uparundhiṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, mukhatau ca nāsatau ca kaṇṇatau ca assāsapassāsaisu uparuddhaisu adhimattā vātā muddhani ūhananti ūhanti (sī॰), auhananti (syā॰ kaṃ॰), uhananti (ka॰)| saiyyathāpi, aggivaissana, balavā purisau tiṇhaina sikharaina muddhani abhimatthaiyya muddhānaṃ abhimanthaiyya (sī॰ pī॰), muddhānaṃ abhimatthaiyya (syā॰ kaṃ॰), aivamaiva khau mai, aggivaissana, mukhatau ca nāsatau ca kaṇṇatau ca assāsapassāsaisu uparuddhaisu adhimattā vātā muddhani ūhananti| āraddhaṃ khau pana mai, aggivaissana, vīriyaṃ hauti asal‍līnaṃ upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā| sāraddhau ca pana mai kāyau hauti appaṭippassaddhau tainaiva dukkhappadhānaina padhānābhitun‍nassa satau| aivarūpāpi khau mai, aggivaissana, uppan‍nā dukkhā vaidanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, aitadahausi – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ appāṇakaṃyaiva jhānaṃ jhāyaiyya’nti| sau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, mukhatau ca nāsatau ca kaṇṇatau ca assāsapassāsai uparundhiṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, mukhatau ca nāsatau ca kaṇṇatau ca assāsapassāsaisu  uparuddhaisu adhimattā sīsai sīsavaidanā haunti| saiyyathāpi, aggivaissana, balavā purisau daḷhaina  varattakkhaṇḍaina varattakabandhanaina (sī॰) sīsai sīsavaiṭhaṃ dadaiyya, aivamaiva khau mai, aggivaissana, mukhatau ca nāsatau ca kaṇṇatau ca assāsapassāsaisu uparuddhaisu adhimattā sīsai sīsavaidanā haunti| āraddhaṃ khau pana mai, aggivaissana, vīriyaṃ hauti asal‍līnaṃ upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā| sāraddhau ca pana mai kāyau hauti appaṭippassaddhau tainaiva dukkhappadhānaina padhānābhitun‍nassa satau| aivarūpāpi khau mai, aggivaissana, uppan‍nā dukkhā vaidanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, aitadahausi – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ appāṇakaṃyaiva jhānaṃ jhāyaiyya’nti| sau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, mukhatau ca nāsatau ca kaṇṇatau ca assāsapassāsai uparundhiṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, mukhatau ca nāsatau ca kaṇṇatau ca assāsapassāsaisu uparuddhaisu adhimattā  vātā kucchiṃ parikantanti| saiyyathāpi, aggivaissana, dakkhau gaughātakau vā gaughātakantaivāsī vā tiṇhaina  gauvikantanaina kucchiṃ parikantaiyya, aivamaiva khau mai, aggivaissana, mukhatau ca nāsatau ca kaṇṇatau ca assāsapassāsaisu uparuddhaisu adhimattā vātā kucchiṃ parikantanti| āraddhaṃ khau pana mai, aggivaissana, vīriyaṃ hauti asal‍līnaṃ upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā| sāraddhau ca pana mai kāyau hauti appaṭippassaddhau tainaiva dukkhappadhānaina  padhānābhitun‍nassa satau| aivarūpāpi khau mai, aggivaissana, uppan‍nā dukkhā vaidanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, aitadahausi – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ appāṇakaṃyaiva jhānaṃ jhāyaiyya’nti| sau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, mukhatau ca nāsatau ca kaṇṇatau ca assāsapassāsai uparundhiṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, mukhatau ca nāsatau ca kaṇṇatau ca assāsapassāsaisu uparuddhaisu adhimattau kāyasmiṃ ḍāhau hauti| saiyyathāpi, aggivaissana, dvai balavantau purisā dubbalataraṃ purisaṃ nānābāhāsu gahaitvā aṅgārakāsuyā santāpaiyyuṃ samparitāpaiyyuṃ, aivamaiva khau mai, aggivaissana, mukhatau ca nāsatau ca kaṇṇatau ca assāsapassāsaisu uparuddhaisu adhimattau kāyasmiṃ ḍāhau hauti| āraddhaṃ khau pana mai, aggivaissana, vīriyaṃ hauti asal‍līnaṃ upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā| sāraddhau ca pana mai kāyau hauti appaṭippassaddhau tainaiva dukkhappadhānaina padhānābhitun‍nassa satau| aivarūpāpi khau mai, aggivaissana, uppan‍nā dukkhā vaidanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati| apissu maṃ, aggivaissana, daivatā  disvā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘kālaṅkatau samaṇau gautamau’ti| aikac‍cā daivatā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘na kālaṅkatau samaṇau gautamau, api ca kālaṅkarautī’ti| aikac‍cā daivatā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘na kālaṅkatau samaṇau gautamau, napi kālaṅkarauti, arahaṃ samaṇau gautamau, vihārautvaiva sau vihārautvaivaisau (sī॰) arahatau aivarūpau hautī’ti vihārautvaivaisau arahatau’’ti (?)|

379. ‘‘tassa  mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, aitadahausi – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ sabbasau āhārupacchaidāya paṭipaj‍jaiyya’nti| atha khau maṃ, aggivaissana, daivatā upasaṅkamitvā  aitadavaucuṃ – ‘mā khau tvaṃ, mārisa, sabbasau āhārupacchaidāya paṭipaj‍ji| sacai khau tvaṃ, mārisa, sabbasau āhārupacchaidāya paṭipaj‍jissasi, tassa tai mayaṃ dibbaṃ aujaṃ laumakūpaihi ajjhauhāraissāma ajjhauharissāma (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰), tāya tvaṃ yāpaissasī’ti| tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, aitadahausi – ‘ahañ‍caiva khau pana sabbasau ajaj‍jitaṃ ajaddhukaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), jaddhukaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) paṭijānaiyyaṃ, imā ca mai daivatā dibbaṃ aujaṃ laumakūpaihi ajjhauhāraiyyuṃ ajjhauharaiyyuṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰), tāya  cāhaṃ yāpaiyyaṃ, taṃ mamassa musā’ti| sau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, tā daivatā pac‍cācikkhāmi, ‘hala’nti vadāmi|

380. ‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, aitadahausi – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ thaukaṃ thaukaṃ āhāraṃ āhāraiyyaṃ, pasataṃ pasataṃ, yadi vā muggayūsaṃ, yadi vā kulatthayūsaṃ, yadi vā kaḷāyayūsaṃ, yadi vā haraiṇukayūsa’nti| sau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, thaukaṃ thaukaṃ āhāraṃ āhāraisiṃ, pasataṃ pasataṃ, yadi vā muggayūsaṃ, yadi vā kulatthayūsaṃ, yadi vā kaḷāyayūsaṃ, yadi vā haraiṇukayūsaṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, thaukaṃ thaukaṃ āhāraṃ āhārayatau, pasataṃ pasataṃ, yadi vā muggayūsaṃ, yadi vā kulatthayūsaṃ, yadi vā kaḷāyayūsaṃ, yadi vā haraiṇukayūsaṃ, adhimattakasimānaṃ pattau kāyau hauti| saiyyathāpi nāma āsītikapabbāni vā kāḷapabbāni vā, aivamaivassu  mai aṅgapac‍caṅgāni bhavanti tāyaivappāhāratāya| saiyyathāpi nāma auṭṭhapadaṃ, aivamaivassu mai ānisadaṃ hauti tāyaivappāhāratāya| saiyyathāpi nāma vaṭṭanāvaḷī, aivamaivassu mai piṭṭhikaṇṭakau uṇṇatāvanatau hauti tāyaivappāhāratāya| saiyyathāpi nāma jarasālāya gaupāṇasiyau auluggaviluggā bhavanti, aivamaivassu mai phāsuḷiyau auluggaviluggā bhavanti tāyaivappāhāratāya| saiyyathāpi nāma gambhīrai udapānai udakatārakā gambhīragatā aukkhāyikā dissanti, aivamaivassu mai akkhikūpaisu akkhitārakā gambhīragatā aukkhāyikā dissanti tāyaivappāhāratāya| saiyyathāpi nāma tittakālābu  āmakacchin‍nau vātātapaina saṃphuṭitau hauti sammilātau, aivamaivassu mai sīsacchavi saṃphuṭitā hauti sammilātā  tāyaivappāhāratāya|

‘‘sau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, udaracchaviṃ parimasissāmīti piṭṭhikaṇṭakaṃyaiva pariggaṇhāmi, piṭṭhikaṇṭakaṃ parimasissāmīti udaracchaviṃyaiva pariggaṇhāmi, yāvassu mai, aggivaissana, udaracchavi piṭṭhikaṇṭakaṃ al‍līnā hauti tāyaivappāhāratāya| sau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, vac‍caṃ vā muttaṃ vā karissāmīti tatthaiva avakuj‍jau papatāmi tāyaivappāhāratāya| sau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, imamaiva kāyaṃ assāsaintau pāṇinā gattāni anumaj‍jāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, pāṇinā gattāni anumaj‍jatau pūtimūlāni laumāni kāyasmā papatanti tāyaivappāhāratāya| apissu maṃ, aggivaissana, manussā  disvā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘kāḷau samaṇau gautamau’ti| aikac‍cai manussā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘na kāḷau samaṇau gautamau, sāmau samaṇau gautamau’ti| aikac‍cai manussā aivamāhaṃsu – ‘na kāḷau samaṇau gautamau, napi sāmau, maṅguracchavi samaṇau gautamau’ti| yāvassu mai, aggivaissana, tāva parisuddhau chavivaṇṇau pariyaudātau upahatau hauti tāyaivappāhāratāya|

381. ‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, aitadahausi – ‘yai khau kaici atītamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā aupak‍kamikā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidayiṃsu, aitāvaparamaṃ, nayitau bhiyyau| yaipi hi kaici anāgatamaddhānaṃ samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā aupak‍kamikā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidayissanti, aitāvaparamaṃ, nayitau bhiyyau| yaipi hi kaici aitarahi samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā aupak‍kamikā dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidayanti, aitāvaparamaṃ, nayitau bhiyyau| na khau panāhaṃ imāya kaṭukāya duk‍karakārikāya adhigacchāmi uttari manussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisaṃ| siyā nu khau añ‍ñau maggau baudhāyā’ti? tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, aitadahausi – ‘abhijānāmi khau panāhaṃ pitu sak‍kassa kammantai sītāya jambucchāyāya nisin‍nau vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharitā| siyā nu khau aisau maggau baudhāyā’ti? tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, satānusāri  viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ahausi – ‘aisaiva maggau baudhāyā’ti| tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, aitadahausi – ‘kiṃ nu khau ahaṃ  tassa sukhassa bhāyāmi, yaṃ taṃ sukhaṃ añ‍ñatraiva kāmaihi añ‍ñatra  akusalaihi dhammaihī’ti? tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, aitadahausi – ‘na khau ahaṃ tassa sukhassa bhāyāmi, yaṃ taṃ sukhaṃ añ‍ñatraiva kāmaihi añ‍ñatra akusalaihi dhammaihī’ti|

382. ‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, aggivaissana, aitadahausi – ‘na khau taṃ sukaraṃ sukhaṃ adhigantuṃ aivaṃ adhimattakasimānaṃ pattakāyaina, yaṃnūnāhaṃ auḷārikaṃ āhāraṃ āhāraiyyaṃ audanakummāsa’nti| sau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, auḷārikaṃ āhāraṃ āhāraisiṃ audanakummāsaṃ| taina khau pana maṃ, aggivaissana, samayaina pañ‍ca pañ‍cavaggiyā (añ‍ñasuttaisu) bhikkhū pac‍cupaṭṭhitā haunti – ‘yaṃ khau samaṇau gautamau dhammaṃ adhigamissati, taṃ nau āraucaissatī’ti| yatau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, auḷārikaṃ āhāraṃ āhāraisiṃ audanakummāsaṃ, atha mai tai pañ‍ca bhikkhū nibbij‍ja pak‍kamiṃsu – ‘bāhul‍likau bāhulikau (sī॰ pī॰) saṃghabhaidasikkhāpadaṭīkāya samaiti samaṇau gautamau, padhānavibbhantau, āvattau bāhul‍lāyā’ti|

383. ‘‘sau  khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, auḷārikaṃ āhāraṃ āhāraitvā, balaṃ gahaitvā, vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ| aivarūpāpi khau mai, aggivaissana, uppan‍nā sukhā vaidanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati| vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ| aivarūpāpi khau mai, aggivaissana, uppan‍nā sukhā vaidanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati| pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca vihāsiṃ, satau ca sampajānau| sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaisiṃ yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ  upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ| aivarūpāpi khau mai, aggivaissana, uppan‍nā sukhā vaidanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati| sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā, pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā, adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja vihāsiṃ| aivarūpāpi khau mai, aggivaissana, uppan‍nā sukhā vaidanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati|

384. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇāya  cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaisiṃ| sau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarāmi, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarāmi| ayaṃ khau mai, aggivaissana, rattiyā paṭhamai yāmai paṭhamā vij‍jā adhigatā; avij‍jā vihatā, vij‍jā uppan‍nā; tamau vihatau, ālaukau uppan‍nau; yathā taṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau| aivarūpāpi khau mai, aggivaissana, uppan‍nā sukhā vaidanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati|

385. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai sattānaṃ cutūpapātañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaisiṃ| sau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passāmi cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāmi…pai॰… ayaṃ khau mai, aggivaissana, rattiyā majjhimai yāmai dutiyā vij‍jā adhigatā; avij‍jā vihatā, vij‍jā uppan‍nā; tamau  vihatau, ālaukau uppan‍nau; yathā taṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau| aivarūpāpi khau mai, aggivaissana, uppan‍nā sukhā vaidanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati|

386. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaisiṃ| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ| ‘imai āsavā’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ āsavasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ| tassa mai aivaṃ jānatau aivaṃ passatau kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cittha, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cittha, avij‍jāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cittha| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ ahausi| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ| ayaṃ khau mai, aggivaissana, rattiyā pacchimai yāmai tatiyā vij‍jā adhigatā; avij‍jā vihatā, vij‍jā uppan‍nā; tamau vihatau, ālaukau uppan‍nau; yathā  taṃ appamattassa ātāpinau pahitattassa viharatau| aivarūpāpi khau mai, aggivaissana, uppan‍nā sukhā vaidanā cittaṃ na pariyādāya tiṭṭhati|

387. ‘‘abhijānāmi khau panāhaṃ, aggivaissana, anaikasatāya parisāya dhammaṃ daisaitā| apissu maṃ aikamaikau aivaṃ mañ‍ñati – ‘mamaivārabbha samaṇau gautamau dhammaṃ daisaitī’ti| ‘na khau panaitaṃ, aggivaissana, aivaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ; yāvadaiva viñ‍ñāpanatthāya tathāgatau paraisaṃ dhammaṃ  daisaiti| sau khau ahaṃ, aggivaissana, tassāyaiva kathāya pariyausānai, tasmiṃyaiva purimasmiṃ samādhinimittai ajjhattamaiva cittaṃ saṇṭhapaimi san‍nisādaimi aikaudiṃ karaumi samādahāmi, yaina sudaṃ nic‍cakappaṃ viharāmī’’’ti|

‘‘aukappaniyamaitaṃ bhautau gautamassa yathā taṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| abhijānāti khau pana bhavaṃ gautamau divā supitā’’ti? ‘‘abhijānāmahaṃ, aggivaissana, gimhānaṃ pacchimai māsai pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau catugguṇaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ pañ‍ñapaitvā dakkhiṇaina passaina satau sampajānau niddaṃ auk‍kamitā’’ti| ‘‘aitaṃ khau, bhau gautama, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammauhavihārasmiṃ  vadantī’’ti? ‘‘na khau, aggivaissana, aittāvatā sammūḷhau vā hauti asammūḷhau vā| api ca, aggivaissana, yathā sammūḷhau ca hauti asammūḷhau ca, taṃ suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca –

388. ‘‘yassa kassaci, aggivaissana, yai āsavā saṃkilaisikā paunaubbhavikā sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyā appahīnā, tamahaṃ ‘sammūḷhau’ti vadāmi| āsavānañhi, aggivaissana, appahānā sammūḷhau hauti| yassa kassaci, aggivaissana, yai āsavā saṃkilaisikā paunaubbhavikā sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyā pahīnā, tamahaṃ ‘asammūḷhau’ti vadāmi| āsavānañhi, aggivaissana, pahānā asammūḷhau hauti|

‘‘tathāgatassa  khau, aggivaissana, yai āsavā saṃkilaisikā paunaubbhavikā sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyā pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| saiyyathāpi, aggivaissana, tālau matthakacchin‍nau abhabbau puna virūḷhiyā, aivamaiva khau, aggivaissana, tathāgatassa yai āsavā saṃkilaisikā paunaubbhavikā sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyā pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā’’ti|

389. aivaṃ vuttai, sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘acchariyaṃ, bhau gautama, abbhutaṃ, bhau gautama! yāvañ‍cidaṃ bhautau gautamassa aivaṃ āsaj‍ja āsaj‍ja vuc‍camānassa, upanītaihi vacanappathaihi samudācariyamānassa, chavivaṇṇau caiva pariyaudāyati, mukhavaṇṇau ca vippasīdati, yathā taṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| abhijānāmahaṃ, bhau gautama, pūraṇaṃ kassapaṃ vādaina vādaṃ samārabhitā| saupi mayā vādaina vādaṃ samāraddhau añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicari, bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmaisi, kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātvākāsi| bhautau pana bhautau khau pana (sī॰) gautamassa aivaṃ āsaj‍ja āsaj‍ja vuc‍camānassa, upanītaihi vacanappathaihi samudācariyamānassa, chavivaṇṇau caiva pariyaudāyati, mukhavaṇṇau ca vippasīdati, yathā taṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| abhijānāmahaṃ, bhau gautama, makkhaliṃ gausālaṃ…pai॰… ajitaṃ kaisakambalaṃ… pakudhaṃ kac‍cāyanaṃ… sañ‍jayaṃ bailaṭṭhaputtaṃ… nigaṇṭhaṃ nāṭaputtaṃ vādaina vādaṃ samārabhitā| saupi mayā  vādaina vādaṃ samāraddhau añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ  paṭicari, bahiddhā kathaṃ apanāmaisi, kaupañ‍ca dausañ‍ca appac‍cayañ‍ca pātvākāsi| bhautau pana gautamassa aivaṃ āsaj‍ja āsaj‍ja vuc‍camānassa, upanītaihi vacanappathaihi samudācariyamānassa, chavivaṇṇau caiva pariyaudāyati, mukhavaṇṇau ca vippasīdati, yathā taṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| handa ca dāni mayaṃ, bhau gautama, gacchāma| bahukic‍cā mayaṃ, bahukaraṇīyā’’ti| ‘‘yassadāni tvaṃ, aggivaissana, kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti|

atha khau sac‍cakau nigaṇṭhaputtau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmīti|

mahāsac‍cakasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. cūḷataṇhāsaṅkhayasuttaṃ

390. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati pubbārāmai migāramātupāsādai| atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau sak‍kau daivānamindau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, bhikkhu saṃkhittaina taṇhāsaṅkhayavimuttau hauti ac‍cantaniṭṭhau ac‍cantayaugakkhaimī ac‍cantabrahmacārī ac‍cantapariyausānau saiṭṭhau daivamanussāna’’nti?

‘‘idha, daivānaminda, bhikkhunau sutaṃ hauti – ‘sabbai dhammā nālaṃ abhinivaisāyā’ti| aivañ‍caitaṃ, daivānaminda, bhikkhunau sutaṃ hauti – ‘sabbai dhammā nālaṃ abhinivaisāyā’ti| sau sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhijānāti; sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya sabbaṃ dhammaṃ parijānāti; sabbaṃ dhammaṃ pariñ‍ñāya yaṃ kiñ‍ci vaidanaṃ vaidaiti – sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā, sau tāsu vaidanāsu anic‍cānupassī viharati, virāgānupassī viharati, niraudhānupassī viharati, paṭinissaggānupassī viharati| sau tāsu vaidanāsu anic‍cānupassī viharantau, virāgānupassī viharantau, niraudhānupassī viharantau, paṭinissaggānupassī viharantau na kiñ‍ci laukai upādiyati| anupādiyaṃ na paritassati, aparitassaṃ pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva parinibbāyati  – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti  pajānāti| aittāvatā khau, daivānaminda, bhikkhu saṃkhittaina taṇhāsaṅkhayavimuttau hauti ac‍cantaniṭṭhau ac‍cantayaugakkhaimī ac‍cantabrahmacārī ac‍cantapariyausānau saiṭṭhau daivamanussāna’’nti|

atha khau sak‍kau daivānamindau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyi|

391. taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau bhagavatau avidūrai nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa aitadahausi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau sau yakkhau bhagavatau  bhāsitaṃ abhisamaic‍ca anumaudi udāhu nau; yaṃnūnāhaṃ taṃ yakkhaṃ jānaiyyaṃ – yadi vā sau yakkhau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhisamaic‍ca  anumaudi yadi vā nau’’ti? atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaiva – pubbārāmai migāramātupāsādai antarahitau daivaisu tāvatiṃsaisu pāturahausi| taina khau pana samayaina sak‍kau daivānamindau aikapuṇḍarīkai uyyānai dibbaihi pañ‍cahi tūriyasataihi turiyasataihi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) samappitau samaṅgībhūtau paricāraiti| addasā khau sak‍kau daivānamindau āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna tāni dibbāni pañ‍ca tūriyasatāni paṭippaṇāmaitvā yaināyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘aihi khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, svāgataṃ, mārisa mauggal‍lāna! cirassaṃ khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, imaṃ pariyāyaṃ akāsi yadidaṃ idhāgamanāya| nisīda, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, idamāsanaṃ pañ‍ñatta’’nti| nisīdi khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau pañ‍ñattai āsanai| sak‍kaupi khau daivānamindau añ‍ñataraṃ nīcaṃ āsanaṃ gahaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau aitadavauca – ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana khau, kausiya, bhagavā saṃkhittaina taṇhāsaṅkhayavimuttiṃ abhāsi? sādhu mayampi aitissā kathāya bhāginau assāma savanāyā’’ti|

392. ‘‘mayaṃ khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, bahukic‍cā bahukaraṇīyā – appaiva sakaina karaṇīyaina, api ca daivānaṃyaiva tāvatiṃsānaṃ karaṇīyaina| api ca, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, sussutaṃyaiva hauti suggahitaṃ sumanasikataṃ  sūpadhāritaṃ, yaṃ nau khippamaiva antaradhāyati| bhūtapubbaṃ, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, daivāsurasaṅgāmau samupabyūḷhau samūpabyuḷhau (syā॰ kaṃ॰), samūpabbūḷhau (sī॰) ahausi| tasmiṃ khau pana, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, saṅgāmai daivā jiniṃsu, asurā parājiniṃsu| sau khau ahaṃ, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, taṃ saṅgāmaṃ abhivijinitvā vijitasaṅgāmau tatau paṭinivattitvā vaijayantaṃ nāma pāsādaṃ māpaisiṃ| vaijayantassa khau, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, pāsādassa aikasataṃ niyyūhaṃ| aikaikasmiṃ niyyūhai satta satta kūṭāgārasatāni| aikamaikasmiṃ kūṭāgārai satta satta accharāyau| aikamaikissā accharāya satta satta paricārikāyau| icchaiyyāsi nau tvaṃ, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, vaijayantassa pāsādassa rāmaṇaiyyakaṃ daṭṭhu’’nti? adhivāsaisi khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tuṇhībhāvaina|

393. atha  khau sak‍kau ca daivānamindau vaissavaṇau ca mahārājā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ purakkhatvā yaina vaijayantau pāsādau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu| addasaṃsu khau sak‍kassa daivānamindassa paricārikāyau āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ; disvā auttappamānā hirīyamānā sakaṃ sakaṃ auvarakaṃ pavisiṃsu| saiyyathāpi nāma suṇisā sasuraṃ disvā auttappati hirīyati, aivamaiva sak‍kassa daivānamindassa paricārikāyau āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ disvā auttappamānā hirīyamānā sakaṃ sakaṃ auvarakaṃ pavisiṃsu| atha khau sak‍kau ca daivānamindau vaissavaṇau ca mahārājā āyasmantaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānaṃ vaijayantai pāsādai anucaṅkamāpainti anuvicarāpainti – ‘‘idampi, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, passa vaijayantassa pāsādassa rāmaṇaiyyakaṃ; idampi, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, passa vaijayantassa pāsādassa rāmaṇaiyyaka’’nti| ‘‘saubhati idaṃ āyasmatau kausiyassa, yathā taṃ pubbai katapuñ‍ñassa| manussāpi kiñ‍cidaiva rāmaṇaiyyakaṃ disvā diṭṭhā (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) aivamāhaṃsu – ‘saubhati vata bhau yathā daivānaṃ tāvatiṃsāna’nti| tayidaṃ āyasmatau kausiyassa saubhati, yathā taṃ pubbai katapuñ‍ñassā’’ti| atha khau āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa  aitadahausi – ‘‘atibāḷhaṃ khau ayaṃ yakkhau pamattau viharati| yaṃnūnāhaṃ imaṃ yakkhaṃ saṃvaijaiyya’’nti| atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau tathārūpaṃ iddhābhisaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkhāsi abhisaṅkhāraisi (ka॰), abhisaṅkhāraiti (syā॰ kaṃ॰) yathā vaijayantaṃ pāsādaṃ pādaṅguṭṭhakaina saṅkampaisi sampakampaisi sampavaidhaisi| atha khau sak‍kau ca daivānamindau, vaissavaṇau ca mahārājā, daivā ca tāvatiṃsā acchariyabbhutacittajātā ahaisuṃ – ‘‘acchariyaṃ vata, bhau, abbhutaṃ vata, bhau, samaṇassa mahiddhikatā mahānubhāvatā, yatra hi nāma dibbabhavanaṃ pādaṅguṭṭhakaina saṅkampaissati sampakampaissati sampavaidhaissatī’’ti! atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ saṃviggaṃ laumahaṭṭhajātaṃ viditvā sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana khau, kausiya, bhagavā saṃkhittaina taṇhāsaṅkhayavimuttiṃ abhāsi? sādhu mayampi aitissā kathāya bhāginau assāma savanāyā’’ti|

394. ‘‘idhāhaṃ, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsiṃ| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau ahaṃ, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, bhikkhu saṃkhittaina taṇhāsaṅkhayavimuttau hauti ac‍cantaniṭṭhau ac‍cantayaugakkhaimī ac‍cantabrahmacārī ac‍cantapariyausānau saiṭṭhau daivamanussāna’’’nti?

‘‘aivaṃ  vuttai, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, bhagavā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘idha, daivānaminda, bhikkhunau sutaṃ hauti – sabbai dhammā nālaṃ  abhinivaisāyā’ti| aivaṃ caitaṃ daivānaminda bhikkhunau sutaṃ hauti ‘sabbai dhammā nālaṃ abhinivaisāyā’ti| sau sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhijānāti, sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya sabbaṃ dhammaṃ parijānāti, sabbaṃ dhammaṃ pariñ‍ñāya yaṃ kiñ‍ci vaidanaṃ vaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā| sau tāsu vaidanāsu anic‍cānupassī viharati, virāgānupassī viharati, niraudhānupassī viharati, paṭinissaggānupassī viharati| sau tāsu vaidanāsu anic‍cānupassī viharantau, virāgānupassī viharantau, niraudhānupassī viharantau, paṭinissaggānupassī viharantau na kiñ‍ci laukai upādiyati, anupādiyaṃ na paritassati, aparitassaṃ pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva parinibbāyati – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| aittāvatā khau, daivānaminda, bhikkhu saṃkhittaina taṇhāsaṅkhayavimuttau hauti ac‍cantaniṭṭhau ac‍cantayaugakkhaimī ac‍cantabrahmacārī ac‍cantapariyausānau saiṭṭhau daivamanussānanti| aivaṃ khau mai, mārisa mauggal‍lāna, bhagavā saṃkhittaina taṇhāsaṅkhayavimuttiṃ abhāsī’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau sak‍kassa daivānamindassa bhāsitaṃ  abhinanditvā anumauditvā – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya aivamaiva – daivaisu tāvatiṃsaisu antarahitau pubbārāmai migāramātupāsādai pāturahausi| atha khau sak‍kassa daivānamindassa paricārikāyau acirapak‍kantai āyasmantai mahāmauggal‍lānai sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ  aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘aisau nu tai, mārisa, sau bhagavā satthā’’ti? ‘‘na khau mai, mārisa, sau bhagavā satthā| sabrahmacārī mai aisau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau’’ti| ‘‘lābhā tai, mārisa, (suladdhaṃ tai, mārisa) ( ) natthi (sī॰ pī॰) yassa tai sabrahmacārī aivaṃmahiddhikau aivaṃmahānubhāvau! ahau nūna tai sau bhagavā satthā’’ti|

395. atha  khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘abhijānāti nau, bhantai, bhagavā ahu ahunañ‍ñaiva (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) ñātañ‍ñatarassa mahaisakkhassa yakkhassa saṃkhittaina taṇhāsaṅkhayavimuttiṃ bhāsitā’’ti abhāsitthāti (ka॰)? ‘‘abhijānāmahaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, idha sak‍kau daivānamindau yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau, mauggal‍lāna, sak‍kau daivānamindau maṃ aitadavauca – ‘kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, bhikkhu saṃkhittaina taṇhāsaṅkhayavimuttau hauti ac‍cantaniṭṭhau ac‍cantayaugakkhaimī ac‍cantabrahmacārī ac‍cantapariyausānau saiṭṭhau daivamanussāna’’nti|

aivaṃ vuttai ahaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ aitadavaucaṃ ‘‘idha daivānaminda bhikkhunau sutaṃ hauti ‘sabbai dhammā nālaṃ abhinivaisāyā’ti| aivaṃ caitaṃ daivānaminda bhikkhunau sutaṃ hauti ‘sabbai dhammā nālaṃ abhinivaisāyā’ti| sau sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhijānāti, sabbaṃ dhammaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya sabbaṃ dhammaṃ parijānāti, sabbaṃ dhammaṃ pariñ‍ñāya yaṃ kiñ‍ci vaidanaṃ vaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā| sau tāsu vaidanāsu anic‍cānupassī viharati, virāgānupassī viharati, niraudhānupassī viharati, paṭinissaggānupassī viharati| sau tāsu vaidanāsu anic‍cānupassī viharantau, virāgānupassī viharantau, niraudhānupassī viharantau, paṭinissaggānupassī viharantau na kiñ‍ci laukai upādiyati, anupādiyaṃ na paritassati, aparitassaṃ pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva parinibbāyati – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| aittāvatā khau, daivānaminda, bhikkhu saṃkhittaina taṇhāsaṅkhayavimuttau hauti ac‍cantaniṭṭhau ac‍cantayaugakkhaimī ac‍cantabrahmacārī ac‍cantapariyausānau saiṭṭhau daivamanussānanti| aivaṃ khau ahaṃ, mauggal‍lāna, abhijānāmi sak‍kassa daivānamindassa saṃkhittaina taṇhāsaṅkhayavimuttiṃ bhāsitā’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

cūḷataṇhāsaṅkhayasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. mahātaṇhāsaṅkhayasuttaṃ

396. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sātissa nāma bhikkhunau kaivaṭṭaputtassa aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti – ‘‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā tadaividaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ sandhāvati saṃsarati anañ‍ña’’nti| assausuṃ khau sambahulā bhikkhū – ‘‘sātissa kira nāma bhikkhunau kaivaṭṭaputtassa aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – ‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā tadaividaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ sandhāvati saṃsarati, anañ‍ña’’’nti| atha khau tai bhikkhū yaina sāti bhikkhu kaivaṭṭaputtau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā sātiṃ bhikkhuṃ kaivaṭṭaputtaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tai, āvusau sāti, aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – ‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā tadaividaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ sandhāvati saṃsarati, anañ‍ña’’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ byā khau ahaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā tadaividaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ sandhāvati saṃsarati, anañ‍ña’’nti| atha khau tai bhikkhū sātiṃ bhikkhuṃ kaivaṭṭaputtaṃ aitasmā pāpakā diṭṭhigatā vivaicaitukāmā samanuyuñ‍janti samanugāhanti samanubhāsanti – ‘‘mā aivaṃ, āvusau sāti, avaca, mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya| anaikapariyāyaināvusau  sāti, paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vuttaṃ  bhagavatā, añ‍ñatra pac‍cayā natthi viñ‍ñāṇassa sambhavau’’ti| aivampi khau sāti bhikkhu kaivaṭṭaputtau taihi bhikkhūhi samanuyuñ‍jiyamānau samanugāhiyamānau samanubhāsiyamānau tadaiva pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ thāmasā parāmāsā abhinivissa vauharati – ‘‘aivaṃ byā khau ahaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā tadaividaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ sandhāvati saṃsarati anañ‍ña’’nti|

397. yatau khau tai bhikkhū nāsakkhiṃsu sātiṃ bhikkhuṃ kaivaṭṭaputtaṃ aitasmā pāpakā diṭṭhigatā vivaicaituṃ, atha khau tai bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘sātissa nāma, bhantai, bhikkhunau kaivaṭṭaputtassa aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ  – ‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā  dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā tadaividaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ sandhāvati saṃsarati, anañ‍ña’nti| assumha khau mayaṃ, bhantai, sātissa kira nāma bhikkhunau kaivaṭṭaputtassa aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – ‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā tadaividaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ sandhāvati saṃsarati, anañ‍ña’nti| atha khau mayaṃ, bhantai, yaina sāti bhikkhu kaivaṭṭaputtau tainupasaṅkamimha; upasaṅkamitvā sātiṃ bhikkhuṃ kaivaṭṭaputtaṃ aitadavaucumha – ‘sac‍caṃ kira tai, āvusau sāti, aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – ‘‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā tadaividaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ sandhāvati saṃsarati, anañ‍ña’’nti? aivaṃ vuttai, bhantai, sāti bhikkhu kaivaṭṭaputtau amhai aitadavauca – ‘aivaṃ byā khau ahaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi  yathā tadaividaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ sandhāvati saṃsarati, anañ‍ña’nti| atha khau mayaṃ, bhantai, sātiṃ bhikkhuṃ kaivaṭṭaputtaṃ aitasmā pāpakā diṭṭhigatā vivaicaitukāmā samanuyuñ‍jimha samanugāhimha samanubhāsimha – ‘mā aivaṃ, āvusau sāti, avaca, mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi, na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhakkhānaṃ, na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya| anaikapariyāyaināvusau sāti, paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā, añ‍ñatra pac‍cayā natthi viñ‍ñāṇassa sambhavau’ti| aivampi khau, bhantai, sāti bhikkhu kaivaṭṭaputtau amhaihi samanuyuñ‍jiyamānau samanugāhiyamānau samanubhāsiyamānau tadaiva pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ thāmasā parāmasā abhinivissa vauharati – ‘aivaṃ byā khau ahaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā tadaividaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ sandhāvati saṃsarati, anañ‍ña’nti| yatau khau mayaṃ, bhantai, nāsakkhimha sātiṃ bhikkhuṃ kaivaṭṭaputtaṃ aitasmā pāpakā diṭṭhigatā vivaicaituṃ, atha mayaṃ aitamatthaṃ bhagavatau āraucaimā’’ti|
