398. atha khau bhagavā añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ  bhikkhu, mama vacanaina sātiṃ bhikkhuṃ kaivaṭṭaputtaṃ āmantaihi – ‘satthā taṃ, āvusau sāti, āmantaitī’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau paṭissutvā yaina sāti bhikkhu kaivaṭṭaputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā sātiṃ bhikkhuṃ kaivaṭṭaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘satthā taṃ, āvusau sāti, āmantaitī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau sāti bhikkhu kaivaṭṭaputtau  tassa bhikkhunau paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ  abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau sātiṃ bhikkhuṃ kaivaṭṭaputtaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira, tai, sāti, aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – ‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā tadaividaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ sandhāvati saṃsarati, anañ‍ña’’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ byā khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā tadaividaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ  sandhāvati saṃsarati, anañ‍ña’’nti| ‘‘katamaṃ taṃ, sāti, viñ‍ñāṇa’’nti? ‘‘yvāyaṃ, bhantai, vadau vaidaiyyau tatra tatra kalyāṇapāpakānaṃ kammānaṃ vipākaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaitī’’ti| ‘‘kassa nu khau nāma tvaṃ, maughapurisa, mayā aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāsi? nanu mayā, maughapurisa, anaikapariyāyaina paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vuttaṃ, añ‍ñatra pac‍cayā natthi viñ‍ñāṇassa sambhavauti? atha ca pana tvaṃ, maughapurisa, attanā duggahitaina amhai caiva abbhācikkhasi, attānañ‍ca khaṇasi, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavasi| tañhi tai, maughapurisa, bhavissati dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāyā’’ti|

399. atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, api nāyaṃ sāti bhikkhu kaivaṭṭaputtau usmīkataupi imasmiṃ dhammavinayai’’ti? ‘‘kiñhi siyā bhantai? nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, sāti bhikkhu kaivaṭṭaputtau tuṇhībhūtau maṅkubhūtau pattakkhandhau adhaumukhau pajjhāyantau appaṭibhānau nisīdi| atha khau bhagavā sātiṃ  bhikkhuṃ kaivaṭṭaputtaṃ tuṇhībhūtaṃ maṅkubhūtaṃ pattakkhandhaṃ adhaumukhaṃ pajjhāyantaṃ appaṭibhānaṃ viditvā sātiṃ bhikkhuṃ kaivaṭṭaputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pañ‍ñāyissasi khau tvaṃ, maughapurisa, aitaina sakaina pāpakaina diṭṭhigataina| idhāhaṃ bhikkhū paṭipucchissāmī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘tumhaipi mai, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānātha yathāyaṃ sāti bhikkhu kaivaṭṭaputtau  attanā duggahitaina amhai caiva abbhācikkhati, attānañ‍ca khaṇati, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavatī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai! anaikapariyāyaina hi nau, bhantai, paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā, añ‍ñatra pac‍cayā natthi viñ‍ñāṇassa sambhavau’’ti| ‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhavai! sādhu khau mai tumhai, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānātha| anaikapariyāyaina hi vau, bhikkhavai, paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vuttaṃ mayā, añ‍ñatra pac‍cayā natthi viñ‍ñāṇassa sambhavauti| atha ca panāyaṃ sāti bhikkhu kaivaṭṭaputtau attanā duggahitaina amhai caiva abbhācikkhati, attānañ‍ca khaṇati, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati  pasavati| tañhi tassa maughapurisassa bhavissati dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya|

400. ‘‘yaṃ yadaiva, bhikkhavai, pac‍cayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, taina tainaiva viñ‍ñāṇaṃtvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati saṅkhaṃ gacchati (sī॰ pī॰)| cakkhuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃtvaiva  saṅkhyaṃ gacchati; sautañ‍ca paṭic‍ca saddai ca uppaj‍jati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃtvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati; ghānañ‍ca paṭic‍ca gandhai ca uppaj‍jati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃtvaiva saṅkhyaṃ  gacchati; jivhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca rasai ca uppaj‍jati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃtvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati; kāyañ‍ca paṭic‍ca phauṭṭhabbai ca uppaj‍jati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃtvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati; manañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dhammai ca uppaj‍jati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃtvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yaṃ yadaiva pac‍cayaṃ paṭic‍ca aggi jalati taina tainaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati| kaṭṭhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca aggi jalati, kaṭṭhaggitvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati; sakalikañ‍ca paṭic‍ca aggi jalati, sakalikaggitvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati; tiṇañ‍ca paṭic‍ca aggi jalati, tiṇaggitvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati; gaumayañ‍ca paṭic‍ca aggi jalati, gaumayaggitvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati; thusañ‍ca paṭic‍ca aggi jalati, thusaggitvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati; saṅkārañ‍ca paṭic‍ca aggi jalati, saṅkāraggitvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yaṃ yadaiva pac‍cayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, taina tainaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati| cakkhuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃtvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati; sautañ‍ca paṭic‍ca saddai ca uppaj‍jati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃtvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati, ghānañ‍ca paṭic‍ca gandhai ca uppaj‍jati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ghāṇaviñ‍ñāṇaṃtvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati, jivhañ‍ca paṭic‍ca rasai ca uppaj‍jati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃtvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati| kāyañ‍ca paṭic‍ca phauṭṭhabbai ca uppaj‍jati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃtvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati| manañ‍ca paṭic‍ca dhammai ca uppaj‍jati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃtvaiva saṅkhyaṃ gacchati|

401. ‘‘bhūtamidanti, bhikkhavai, passathā’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘tadāhārasambhavanti, bhikkhavai, passathā’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘tadāhāraniraudhā  yaṃ bhūtaṃ, taṃ niraudhadhammanti, bhikkhavai, passathā’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘bhūtamidaṃ naussūti, bhikkhavai, kaṅkhatau uppaj‍jati vicikicchā’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘tadāhārasambhavaṃ naussūti, bhikkhavai, kaṅkhatau uppaj‍jati vicikicchā’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘tadāhāraniraudhā yaṃ bhūtaṃ, taṃ niraudhadhammaṃ naussūti, bhikkhavai, kaṅkhatau uppaj‍jati vicikicchā’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘bhūtamidanti, bhikkhavai, yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau yā vicikicchā sā pahīyatī’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘tadāhārasambhavanti, bhikkhavai, yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau yā vicikicchā sā pahīyatī’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘tadāhāraniraudhā yaṃ bhūtaṃ, taṃ niraudhadhammanti, bhikkhavai, yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau yā vicikicchā sā pahīyatī’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘bhūtamidanti, bhikkhavai, itipi vau aittha nibbicikicchā’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘tadāhārasambhavanti, bhikkhavai, itipi vau aittha nibbicikicchā’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘tadāhāraniraudhā yaṃ bhūtaṃ taṃ niraudhadhammanti, bhikkhavai, itipi vau aittha nibbicikicchā’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘bhūtamidanti, bhikkhavai, yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭha’’nti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘tadāhārasambhavanti, bhikkhavai, yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭha’’nti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘tadāhāraniraudhā yaṃ bhūtaṃ taṃ niraudhadhammanti, bhikkhavai, yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya sudiṭṭha’’nti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘imaṃ  cai tumhai, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhiṃ aivaṃ parisuddhaṃ aivaṃ pariyaudātaṃ al‍līyaitha kailāyaitha dhanāyaitha  mamāyaitha, api nu mai tumhai, bhikkhavai, kul‍lūpamaṃ dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānaiyyātha nittharaṇatthāya nau gahaṇatthāyā’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘imaṃ cai tumhai, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhiṃ aivaṃ parisuddhaṃ aivaṃ pariyaudātaṃ  na al‍līyaitha na kailāyaitha na dhanāyaitha na mamāyaitha, api nu mai tumhai, bhikkhavai, kul‍lūpamaṃ dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānaiyyātha nittharaṇatthāya nau gahaṇatthāyā’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

402. ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, āhārā bhūtānaṃ vā sattānaṃ ṭhitiyā, sambhavaisīnaṃ vā anuggahāya| katamai cattārau? kabaḷīkārau āhārau auḷārikau vā sukhumau vā, phassau dutiyau, manausañ‍caitanā tatiyā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ catutthaṃ|

‘‘imai ca, bhikkhavai, cattārau āhārā kiṃnidānā kiṃsamudayā kiṃjātikā kiṃpabhavā?

‘‘imai cattārau āhārā taṇhānidānā taṇhāsamudayā taṇhājātikā taṇhāpabhavā|

‘‘taṇhā cāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, kiṃnidānā kiṃsamudayā kiṃjātikā kiṃpabhavā?

‘‘taṇhā vaidanānidānā vaidanāsamudayā vaidanājātikā vaidanāpabhavā|

‘‘vaidanā cāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, kiṃnidānā kiṃsamudayā kiṃjātikā kiṃpabhavā?

‘‘vaidanā phassanidānā phassasamudayā phassajātikā phassapabhavā|

‘‘phassau cāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, kiṃnidānau kiṃsamudayau kiṃjātikau kiṃpabhavau?

‘‘phassau  saḷāyatananidānau saḷāyatanasamudayau saḷāyatanajātikau saḷāyatanapabhavau|

‘‘saḷāyatanaṃ cidaṃ, bhikkhavai, kiṃnidānaṃ kiṃsamudayaṃ  kiṃjātikaṃ kiṃpabhavaṃ?

‘‘saḷāyatanaṃ nāmarūpanidānaṃ nāmarūpasamudayaṃ nāmarūpajātikaṃ nāmarūpapabhavaṃ|

‘‘nāmarūpaṃ cidaṃ, bhikkhavai, kiṃnidānaṃ kiṃsamudayaṃ kiṃjātikaṃ kiṃpabhavaṃ?

‘‘nāmarūpaṃ viñ‍ñāṇanidānaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasamudayaṃ viñ‍ñāṇajātikaṃ viñ‍ñāṇapabhavaṃ|

‘‘viñ‍ñāṇaṃ cidaṃ, bhikkhavai, kiṃnidānaṃ kiṃsamudayaṃ kiṃjātikaṃ kiṃpabhavaṃ?

‘‘viñ‍ñāṇaṃ saṅkhāranidānaṃ saṅkhārasamudayaṃ saṅkhārajātikaṃ saṅkhārapabhavaṃ|

‘‘saṅkhārā cimai, bhikkhavai, kiṃnidānā kiṃsamudayā kiṃjātikā kiṃpabhavā?

‘‘saṅkhārā avij‍jānidānā avij‍jāsamudayā avij‍jājātikā avij‍jāpabhavā|

‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ, saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|’’’

403. ‘‘jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇanti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ; jātipac‍cayā nu khau, bhikkhavai, jarāmaraṇaṃ, nau vā, kathaṃ vā aittha kathaṃ vā vau aittha (?) hautī’’ti? ‘‘jātipac‍cayā, bhantai, jarāmaraṇaṃ; aivaṃ nau aittha hauti aivaṃ nau aittha hautīti (ka॰) – jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇa’’nti| ‘‘bhavapac‍cayā jātīti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ; bhavapac‍cayā nu khau, bhikkhavai, jāti, nau vā, kathaṃ vā aittha hautī’’ti? ‘‘bhavapac‍cayā, bhantai, jāti; aivaṃ nau aittha hauti – bhavapac‍cayā jātī’’ti| ‘‘upādānapac‍cayā  bhavauti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ; upādānapac‍cayā nu khau, bhikkhavai, bhavau, nau vā, kathaṃ vā aittha hautī’’ti? ‘‘upādānapac‍cayā, bhantai, bhavau; aivaṃ nau aittha hauti – upādānapac‍cayā bhavau’’ti| ‘‘taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānanti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ, taṇhāpac‍cayā nu khau, bhikkhavai, upādānaṃ, nau vā, kathaṃ vā aittha hautī’’ti? ‘‘taṇhāpac‍cayā, bhantai, upādānaṃ; aivaṃ nau aittha hauti – taṇhāpac‍cayā upādāna’’nti| ‘‘vaidanāpac‍cayā  taṇhāti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ; vaidanāpac‍cayā nu khau, bhikkhavai, taṇhā, nau vā, kathaṃ vā aittha hautī’’ti? ‘‘vaidanāpac‍cayā, bhantai, taṇhā; aivaṃ nau aittha hauti – vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā’’ti| ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanāti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ; phassapac‍cayā nu khau, bhikkhavai, vaidanā, nau vā, kathaṃ vā aittha hautī’’ti? ‘‘phassapac‍cayā, bhantai, vaidanā; aivaṃ nau aittha hauti – phassapac‍cayā vaidanā’’ti| ‘‘saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassauti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ; saḷāyatanapac‍cayā nu khau, bhikkhavai, phassau, nau vā, kathaṃ vā aittha hautī’’ti? ‘‘saḷāyatanapac‍cayā, bhantai, phassau; aivaṃ nau aittha hauti – saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau’’ti| ‘‘nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatananti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ; nāmarūpapac‍cayā nu khau, bhikkhavai, saḷāyatanaṃ, nau vā, kathaṃ vā aittha hautī’’ti? ‘‘nāmarūpapac‍cayā, bhantai, saḷāyatanaṃ; aivaṃ nau aittha hauti – nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatana’’nti| ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpanti  iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ; viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nu khau, bhikkhavai, nāmarūpaṃ, nau vā, kathaṃ vā aittha hautī’’ti? ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā, bhantai, nāmarūpaṃ; aivaṃ nau aittha hauti – viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpa’’nti| ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇanti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ; saṅkhārapac‍cayā nu khau, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, nau vā, kathaṃ vā aittha hautī’’ti? ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayā, bhantai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ; aivaṃ nau aittha hauti – saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇa’’nti| ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārāti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ; avij‍jāpac‍cayā nu khau, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā, nau vā, kathaṃ vā aittha hautī’’ti? ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā, bhantai, saṅkhārā; aivaṃ nau aittha hauti – avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā’’ti|

404. ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai| iti khau, bhikkhavai, tumhaipi aivaṃ vadaitha, ahampi aivaṃ vadāmi – imasmiṃ sati idaṃ hauti, imassuppādā  idaṃ uppaj‍jati, yadidaṃ – avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ, saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayā  bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

‘‘avij‍jāyatvaiva  asaisavirāganiraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau, saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhau, nāmarūpaniraudhā saḷāyatananiraudhau, saḷāyatananiraudhā phassaniraudhau, phassaniraudhā vaidanāniraudhau, vaidanāniraudhā taṇhāniraudhau, taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau, upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau, bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau, jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti|

405. ‘‘jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaniraudhauti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ; jātiniraudhā nu khau, bhikkhavai, jarāmaraṇaniraudhau, nau vā, kathaṃ vā aittha hautī’’ti? ‘‘jātiniraudhā, bhantai, jarāmaraṇaniraudhau; aivaṃ nau aittha hauti – jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaniraudhau’’ti| ‘‘bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhauti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ; bhavaniraudhā nu khau, bhikkhavai, jātiniraudhau, nau vā, kathaṃ vā aittha hautī’’ti? ‘‘bhavaniraudhā, bhantai, jātiniraudhau; aivaṃ nau aittha hauti – bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau’’ti| ‘‘upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhauti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ; upādānaniraudhā nu khau, bhikkhavai, bhavaniraudhau, nau vā, kathaṃ vā aittha hautī’’ti? ‘‘upādānaniraudhā, bhantai, bhavaniraudhau; aivaṃ nau aittha hauti – upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau’’ti| ‘‘taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhauti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ; taṇhāniraudhā nu khau, bhikkhavai, upādānaniraudhau, nau vā, kathaṃ vā aittha hautī’’ti? ‘‘taṇhāniraudhā, bhantai, upādānaniraudhau; aivaṃ nau aittha hauti – taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau’’ti| ‘‘vaidanāniraudhā  taṇhāniraudhauti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ; vaidanāniraudhā nu khau, bhikkhavai, taṇhāniraudhau, nau vā, kathaṃ vā aittha hautī’’ti? ‘‘vaidanāniraudhā, bhantai, taṇhāniraudhau; aivaṃ nau aittha hauti – vaidanāniraudhā taṇhāniraudhau’’ti| ‘‘phassaniraudhā vaidanāniraudhauti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ; phassaniraudhā  nu khau, bhikkhavai, vaidanāniraudhau, nau vā, kathaṃ vā aittha hautī’’ti? ‘‘phassaniraudhā, bhantai, vaidanāniraudhau; aivaṃ nau aittha hauti – phassaniraudhā vaidanāniraudhau’’ti| ‘‘saḷāyatananiraudhā phassaniraudhauti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ; saḷāyatananiraudhā nu khau, bhikkhavai, phassaniraudhau, nau vā, kathaṃ vā aittha hautīti? saḷāyatananiraudhā, bhantai, phassaniraudhau; aivaṃ nau aittha hauti – saḷāyatananiraudhā  phassaniraudhau’’ti| ‘‘nāmarūpaniraudhā saḷāyatananiraudhauti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ; nāmarūpaniraudhā nu khau, bhikkhavai, saḷāyatananiraudhau, nau vā, kathaṃ vā aittha hautī’’ti? ‘‘nāmarūpaniraudhā, bhantai, saḷāyatananiraudhau; aivaṃ nau aittha hauti – nāmarūpaniraudhā saḷāyatananiraudhau’’ti| ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhauti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ; viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā nu khau, bhikkhavai, nāmarūpaniraudhau, nau vā, kathaṃ vā aittha hautī’’ti? ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā, bhantai, nāmarūpaniraudhau; aivaṃ nau aittha hauti – viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhau’’ti| ‘‘saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhauti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ; saṅkhāraniraudhā nu khau, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau, nau vā, kathaṃ vā aittha hautī’’ti? ‘‘saṅkhāraniraudhā, bhantai, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau; aivaṃ nau aittha hauti – saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau’’ti| ‘‘avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhauti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ; avij‍jāniraudhā nu khau, bhikkhavai, saṅkhāraniraudhau, nau vā, kathaṃ vā aittha hautī’’ti? ‘‘avij‍jāniraudhā, bhantai, saṅkhāraniraudhau; aivaṃ nau aittha hauti – avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau’’ti|

406. ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai| iti khau, bhikkhavai, tumhaipi aivaṃ vadaitha, ahampi aivaṃ vadāmi – imasmiṃ asati idaṃ na hauti, imassa niraudhā idaṃ nirujjhati, yadidaṃ – avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau, saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhau, nāmarūpaniraudhā saḷāyatananiraudhau, saḷāyatananiraudhā phassaniraudhau, phassaniraudhā vaidanāniraudhau, vaidanāniraudhā taṇhāniraudhau, taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau, upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau, bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau, jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti|

407. ‘‘api nu tumhai, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ jānantā aivaṃ passantā pubbantaṃ  vā paṭidhāvaiyyātha – ‘ahaisumha nu khau mayaṃ atītamaddhānaṃ, nanu khau ahaisumha atītamaddhānaṃ, kiṃ nu khau ahaisumha atītamaddhānaṃ, kathaṃ nu khau ahaisumha atītamaddhānaṃ, kiṃ hutvā kiṃ ahaisumha nu khau mayaṃ atītamaddhāna’’’nti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘api  nu tumhai, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ jānantā aivaṃ passantā aparantaṃ  vā paṭidhāvaiyyātha – bhavissāma nu khau mayaṃ anāgatamaddhānaṃ, nanu khau bhavissāma anāgatamaddhānaṃ, kiṃ nu khau bhavissāma anāgatamaddhānaṃ, kathaṃ nu khau bhavissāma anāgatamaddhānaṃ, kiṃ hutvā kiṃ bhavissāma nu khau mayaṃ anāgatamaddhāna’’nti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘api nu tumhai, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ jānantā aivaṃ passantā aitarahi vā pac‍cuppan‍namaddhānaṃ ajjhattaṃ kathaṃkathī assatha – ahaṃ nu khausmi, nau nu khausmi, kiṃ nu khausmi, kathaṃ nu khausmi, ayaṃ nu khau sattau kutau āgatau, sau kuhiṃgāmī bhavissatī’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘api nu tumhai, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ jānantā aivaṃ passantā aivaṃ vadaiyyātha – satthā nau garu, satthugāravaina ca mayaṃ aivaṃ vadaimā’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘api nu tumhai, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ jānantā aivaṃ passantā aivaṃ vadaiyyātha – samaṇau aivamāha, samaṇā ca nāma mayaṃ aivaṃ vadaimā’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘api nu tumhai, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ jānantā aivaṃ passantā añ‍ñaṃ satthāraṃ  uddisaiyyāthā’’ti?

‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘api nu tumhai, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ jānantā aivaṃ passantā yāni tāni puthusamaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ vata kautūhalamaṅgalāni tāni sāratau pac‍cāgacchaiyyāthā’’ti?

‘‘nau  haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘nanu, bhikkhavai, yadaiva tumhākaṃ sāmaṃ ñātaṃ sāmaṃ diṭṭhaṃ sāmaṃ viditaṃ, tadaiva tumhai vadaithā’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai, upanītā khau mai tumhai, bhikkhavai, iminā sandiṭṭhikaina dhammaina akālikaina aihipassikaina aupanaiyyikaina pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbaina viñ‍ñūhi| sandiṭṭhikau ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, dhammau akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhi – iti yantaṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vutta’’nti|

408. ‘‘tiṇṇaṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, san‍nipātā gabbhassāvak‍kanti hauti| idha mātāpitarau ca san‍nipatitā haunti, mātā ca na utunī hauti, gandhabbau ca na pac‍cupaṭṭhitau hauti, naiva tāva gabbhassāvak‍kanti  hauti| idha mātāpitarau ca san‍nipatitā haunti, mātā ca utunī hauti, gandhabbau ca na pac‍cupaṭṭhitau hauti, naiva tāva gabbhassāvak‍kanti hauti| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, mātāpitarau ca san‍nipatitā haunti, mātā ca utunī hauti, gandhabbau ca pac‍cupaṭṭhitau hauti – aivaṃ tiṇṇaṃ san‍nipātā gabbhassāvak‍kanti hauti| tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, mātā nava vā dasa vā māsai gabbhaṃ kucchinā pariharati mahatā saṃsayaina garubhāraṃ garumbhāraṃ (sī॰ pī॰)| tamainaṃ, bhikkhavai, mātā  navan‍naṃ vā  dasan‍naṃ vā māsānaṃ ac‍cayaina vijāyati mahatā saṃsayaina garubhāraṃ| tamainaṃ jātaṃ samānaṃ sakaina lauhitaina pausaiti| lauhitañhaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyassa vinayai yadidaṃ mātuthañ‍ñaṃ| sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, kumārau vuddhimanvāya indriyānaṃ paripākamanvāya yāni tāni kumārakānaṃ kīḷāpanakāni taihi kīḷati, saiyyathidaṃ – vaṅkakaṃ ghaṭikaṃ maukkhacikaṃ ciṅgulakaṃ pattāḷhakaṃ rathakaṃ dhanukaṃ| sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, kumārau vuddhimanvāya indriyānaṃ paripākamanvāya pañ‍cahi kāmaguṇaihi samappitau samaṅgībhūtau paricāraiti – cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaihi rūpaihi iṭṭhaihi kantaihi manāpaihi piyarūpaihi kāmūpasaṃhitaihi rajanīyaihi, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyaihi saddaihi… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyaihi gandhaihi… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyaihi rasaihi… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi iṭṭhaihi kantaihi manāpaihi piyarūpaihi kāmūpasaṃhitaihi rajanīyaihi|

409. ‘‘sau  cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā piyarūpai rūpai sāraj‍jati, appiyarūpai rūpai byāpaj‍jati, anupaṭṭhitakāyasati ca viharati parittacaitasau| tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti – yatthassa tai pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti| sau aivaṃ anuraudhaviraudhaṃ samāpan‍nau yaṃ kiñ‍ci vaidanaṃ vaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā, sau taṃ vaidanaṃ abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ vaidanaṃ abhinandatau abhivadatau ajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau uppaj‍jati nandī| yā vaidanāsu nandī tadupādānaṃ, tassupādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā…pai॰… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā…pai॰… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya piyarūpai dhammai  sāraj‍jati, appiyarūpai dhammai byāpaj‍jati, anupaṭṭhitakāyasati ca viharati parittacaitasau| tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti – yatthassa tai pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti| sau aivaṃ anuraudhaviraudhaṃ samāpan‍nau yaṃ kiñ‍ci vaidanaṃ vaidaiti sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā, sau taṃ vaidanaṃ abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ vaidanaṃ abhinandatau abhivadatau ajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau uppaj‍jati nandī| yā vaidanāsu nandī tadupādānaṃ, tassupādānapac‍cayā  bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

410. ‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā  sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ; kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| taṃ dhammaṃ suṇāti gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ vā kulai pac‍cājātau| sau taṃ dhammaṃ sutvā tathāgatai saddhaṃ paṭilabhati| sau taina saddhāpaṭilābhaina saman‍nāgatau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘sambādhau gharāvāsau rajāpathau, abbhaukāsau pabbaj‍jā| nayidaṃ sukaraṃ agāraṃ ajjhāvasatā aikantaparipuṇṇaṃ aikantaparisuddhaṃ saṅkhalikhitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ kaisamassuṃ  auhāraitvā, kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā, agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajaiyya’’’nti| sau aparaina samayaina appaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ pahāya, mahantaṃ vā bhaugakkhandhaṃ pahāya, appaṃ vā ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya, mahantaṃ vā ñātiparivaṭṭaṃ pahāya, kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā, kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā, agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati|

411. ‘‘sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāsājīvasamāpan‍nau pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, nihitadaṇḍau nihitasatthau laj‍jī dayāpan‍nau sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharati|

‘‘adin‍nādānaṃ pahāya adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau  hauti, din‍nādāyī din‍napāṭikaṅkhī athainaina sucibhūtaina attanā viharati|

‘‘abrahmacariyaṃ pahāya brahmacārī hauti, ārācārī viratau maithunā gāmadhammā|

‘‘musāvādaṃ  pahāya musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, sac‍cavādī sac‍casandhau  thaitau pac‍cayikau avisaṃvādakau laukassa|

‘‘pisuṇaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti – itau sutvā na amutra akkhātā imaisaṃ bhaidāya, amutra vā sutvā na imaisaṃ akkhātā amūsaṃ bhaidāya| iti bhin‍nānaṃ vā sandhātā, sahitānaṃ vā anuppadātā samaggārāmau samaggaratau samagganandī, samaggakaraṇiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti|

‘‘pharusaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti – yā sā vācā nailā kaṇṇasukhā paimanīyā hadayaṅgamā paurī bahujanakantā bahujanamanāpā tathārūpiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti|

‘‘samphappalāpaṃ pahāya samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti, kālavādī bhūtavādī atthavādī dhammavādī vinayavādī, nidhānavatiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā kālaina, sāpadaisaṃ pariyantavatiṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ|

‘‘sau  bījagāmabhūtagāmasamārambhā paṭiviratau hauti, aikabhattikau hauti rattūparatau, viratau vikālabhaujanā| nac‍cagītavāditavisūkadassanā paṭiviratau hauti, mālāgandhavilaipanadhāraṇamaṇḍanavibhūsanaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau hauti, uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanā paṭiviratau hauti, jātarūparajatapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti, āmakadhañ‍ñapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti, āmakamaṃsapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti, itthikumārikapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti, dāsidāsapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti, ajaiḷakapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti, kuk‍kuṭasūkarapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti, hatthigavāssavaḷavapaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau  hauti, khaittavatthupaṭiggahaṇā paṭiviratau hauti, dūtaiyyapahiṇagamanānuyaugā paṭiviratau hauti, kayavik‍kayā paṭiviratau hauti, tulākūṭakaṃsakūṭamānakūṭā paṭiviratau hauti, uk‍kauṭanavañ‍cana-nikati-sāciyaugā paṭiviratau hauti, chaidana-vadhabandhanaviparāmausa-ālaupa-sahasākārā paṭiviratau hauti passa ma॰ ni॰ 1.293 cūḷahatthipadaupamai|

‘‘sau santuṭṭhau hauti kāyaparihārikaina cīvaraina kucchiparihārikaina piṇḍapātaina| sau yaina yainaiva pak‍kamati samādāyaiva pak‍kamati| saiyyathāpi nāma pakkhī sakuṇau yaina yainaiva ḍaiti sapattabhārauva ḍaiti, aivamaiva bhikkhu santuṭṭhau hauti kāyaparihārikaina cīvaraina, kucchiparihārikaina piṇḍapātaina| sau yaina yainaiva pak‍kamati samādāyaiva pak‍kamati| sau iminā ariyaina  sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau ajjhattaṃ anavaj‍jasukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

‘‘sau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā…pai॰… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā…pai॰… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati manindriyaṃ manindriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sau iminā ariyaina indriyasaṃvaraina saman‍nāgatau ajjhattaṃ abyāsaikasukhaṃ  paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

‘‘sau abhik‍kantai paṭik‍kantai sampajānakārī hauti, ālaukitai vilaukitai sampajānakārī hauti, samiñ‍jitai pasāritai sampajānakārī hauti, saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇai sampajānakārī hauti, asitai pītai khāyitai sāyitai sampajānakārī hauti, uc‍cārapassāvakammai sampajānakārī hauti, gatai ṭhitai nisin‍nai suttai jāgaritai bhāsitai tuṇhībhāvai sampajānakārī hauti|

412. ‘‘sau iminā ca ariyaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau, (imāya ca ariyāya santuṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau) passa ma॰ ni॰ 1.296 cūḷahatthipadaupamai, iminā ca ariyaina indriyasaṃvaraina saman‍nāgatau, iminā ca ariyaina satisampajañ‍ñaina saman‍nāgatau, vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajati – arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ palālapuñ‍jaṃ| sau pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā, ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya, parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau abhijjhaṃ laukai pahāya vigatābhijjhaina caitasā viharati, abhijjhāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; byāpādapadausaṃ pahāya  abyāpan‍nacittau viharati, sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī, byāpādapadausā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; thīnamiddhaṃ pahāya vigatathīnamiddhau viharati ālaukasañ‍ñī, satau sampajānau, thīnamiddhā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ pahāya anuddhatau viharati ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittau, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; vicikicchaṃ pahāya tiṇṇavicikicchau viharati akathaṃkathī kusalaisu dhammaisu, vicikicchāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti|

413. ‘‘sau  imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai, vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

414. ‘‘sau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā piyarūpai rūpai na sāraj‍jati, appiyarūpai rūpai na byāpaj‍jati, upaṭṭhitakāyasati ca viharati appamāṇacaitasau| tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti – yatthassa tai pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti| sau aivaṃ anuraudhaviraudhavippahīnau  yaṃ kiñ‍ci vaidanaṃ vaidaiti, sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā, sau taṃ vaidanaṃ nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ vaidanaṃ anabhinandatau anabhivadatau anajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau yā vaidanāsu nandī sā nirujjhati| tassa nandīniraudhā upādānaniraudhau, upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau, bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau, jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā…pai॰… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā…pai॰… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya piyarūpai  dhammai na sāraj‍jati, appiyarūpai dhammai na byāpaj‍jati, upaṭṭhitakāyasati ca viharati appamāṇacaitasau, tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti – yatthassa tai pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti| sau aivaṃ anuraudhaviraudhavippahīnau yaṃ kiñ‍ci vaidanaṃ vaidaiti, sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā, sau taṃ vaidanaṃ nābhinandati nābhivadati  nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa taṃ vaidanaṃ anabhinandatau anabhivadatau anajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau yā vaidanāsu nandī sā nirujjhati| tassa nandīniraudhā upādānaniraudhau, upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau, bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau, jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti| imaṃ khau mai tumhai, bhikkhavai, saṃkhittaina taṇhāsaṅkhayavimuttiṃ dhāraitha, sātiṃ pana bhikkhuṃ  kaivaṭṭaputtaṃ mahātaṇhājālataṇhāsaṅghāṭappaṭimuk‍ka’’nti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

mahātaṇhāsaṅkhayasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. mahāassapurasuttaṃ

415. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā aṅgaisu viharati assapuraṃ nāma aṅgānaṃ nigamau| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘samaṇā samaṇāti vau, bhikkhavai, janau sañ‍jānāti| tumhai ca pana ‘kai tumhai’ti puṭṭhā samānā ‘samaṇāmhā’ti paṭijānātha; taisaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, aivaṃsamañ‍ñānaṃ sataṃ aivaṃpaṭiñ‍ñānaṃ sataṃ ‘yai dhammā samaṇakaraṇā ca brāhmaṇakaraṇā ca tai dhammai samādāya vattissāma, aivaṃ nau ayaṃ amhākaṃ samañ‍ñā ca sac‍cā bhavissati paṭiñ‍ñā ca bhūtā| yaisañ‍ca mayaṃ cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ paribhuñ‍jāma, taisaṃ tai kārā amhaisu mahapphalā bhavissanti mahānisaṃsā, amhākañ‍caivāyaṃ pabbaj‍jā avañjhā bhavissati saphalā saudrayā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ|

416. ‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā samaṇakaraṇā ca brāhmaṇakaraṇā ca? ‘hirauttappaina saman‍nāgatā bhavissāmā’ti aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ| siyā khau pana, bhikkhavai, tumhākaṃ aivamassa – ‘hirauttappainamha saman‍nāgatā, alamaittāvatā katamaittāvatā, anuppattau nau sāmañ‍ñatthau, natthi nau kiñ‍ci uttariṃ karaṇīya’nti tāvatakainaiva tuṭṭhiṃ āpaj‍jaiyyātha| āraucayāmi vau, bhikkhavai, paṭivaidayāmi vau, bhikkhavai – ‘mā vau sāmañ‍ñatthikānaṃ sataṃ sāmañ‍ñatthau parihāyi, sati uttariṃ karaṇīyai’|

417. ‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, uttariṃ karaṇīyaṃ? ‘parisuddhau nau kāyasamācārau  bhavissati uttānau vivaṭau na ca chiddavā saṃvutau ca| tāya ca pana parisuddhakāyasamācāratāya naivattānuk‍kaṃsaissāma na paraṃ vambhaissāmā’ti naivattānuk‍kaṃsissāma na paraṃ vambhissāmāti (sabbattha) aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ| siyā khau pana, bhikkhavai, tumhākaṃ aivamassa – ‘hirauttappainamha saman‍nāgatā, parisuddhau nau kāyasamācārau; alamaittāvatā katamaittāvatā, anuppattau nau sāmañ‍ñatthau, natthi nau  kiñ‍ci uttariṃ karaṇīya’nti tāvatakainaiva tuṭṭhiṃ āpaj‍jaiyyātha| āraucayāmi vau, bhikkhavai, paṭivaidayāmi vau, bhikkhavai – ‘mā vau sāmañ‍ñatthikānaṃ sataṃ sāmañ‍ñatthau parihāyi, sati uttariṃ karaṇīyai’|

418. ‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, uttariṃ karaṇīyaṃ? ‘parisuddhau nau vacīsamācārau bhavissati uttānau vivaṭau na ca chiddavā saṃvutau ca| tāya ca pana parisuddhavacīsamācāratāya naivattānuk‍kaṃsaissāma na paraṃ vambhaissāmā’ti aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ| siyā khau pana, bhikkhavai, tumhākaṃ aivamassa – ‘hirauttappainamha saman‍nāgatā, parisuddhau nau kāyasamācārau, parisuddhau vacīsamācārau; alamaittāvatā katamaittāvatā, anuppattau nau sāmañ‍ñatthau, natthi nau kiñ‍ci uttariṃ karaṇīya’nti tāvatakainaiva tuṭṭhiṃ āpaj‍jaiyyātha| āraucayāmi vau, bhikkhavai, paṭivaidayāmi vau, bhikkhavai – ‘mā vau sāmañ‍ñatthikānaṃ sataṃ sāmañ‍ñatthau parihāyi, sati uttariṃ karaṇīyai’|

419. ‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, uttariṃ karaṇīyaṃ? ‘parisuddhau nau manausamācārau bhavissati uttānau vivaṭau na ca chiddavā saṃvutau ca| tāya ca pana  parisuddhamanausamācāratāya naivattānuk‍kaṃsaissāma na paraṃ vambhaissāmā’ti aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ| siyā khau pana, bhikkhavai, tumhākaṃ aivamassa – ‘hirauttappainamha saman‍nāgatā, parisuddhau nau kāyasamācārau, parisuddhau vacīsamācārau, parisuddhau manausamācārau; alamaittāvatā katamaittāvatā, anuppattau nau sāmañ‍ñatthau, natthi nau kiñ‍ci uttariṃ karaṇīya’nti tāvatakainaiva tuṭṭhiṃ āpaj‍jaiyyātha| āraucayāmi vau, bhikkhavai, paṭivaidayāmi vau, bhikkhavai – ‘mā vau sāmañ‍ñatthikānaṃ sataṃ sāmañ‍ñatthau parihāyi, sati uttariṃ karaṇīyai’|

420. ‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, uttariṃ karaṇīyaṃ? ‘parisuddhau nau ājīvau bhavissati uttānau vivaṭau na ca chiddavā saṃvutau ca| tāya ca pana parisuddhājīvatāya naivattānuk‍kaṃsaissāma na paraṃ vambhaissāmā’ti aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ| siyā khau pana, bhikkhavai, tumhākaṃ aivamassa – ‘hirauttappainamha saman‍nāgatā, parisuddhau nau kāyasamācārau, parisuddhau vacīsamācārau, parisuddhau manausamācārau, parisuddhau ājīvau; alamaittāvatā  katamaittāvatā, anuppattau nau sāmañ‍ñatthau, natthi nau kiñ‍ci uttariṃ karaṇīya’nti  tāvatakainaiva tuṭṭhiṃ āpaj‍jaiyyātha| āraucayāmi vau, bhikkhavai, paṭivaidayāmi vau, bhikkhavai – ‘mā vau sāmañ‍ñatthikānaṃ sataṃ sāmañ‍ñatthau parihāyi, sati uttariṃ karaṇīyai’|

421. ‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, uttariṃ karaṇīyaṃ? ‘indriyaisu guttadvārā bhavissāma; cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jissāma, rakkhissāma cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jissāma| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā…pai॰… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā…pai॰… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya na nimittaggāhī nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jissāma, rakkhissāma manindriyaṃ, manindriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jissāmā’ti aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ| siyā khau pana, bhikkhavai, tumhākaṃ aivamassa – ‘hirauttappainamha  saman‍nāgatā, parisuddhau nau kāyasamācārau, parisuddhau vacīsamācārau, parisuddhau manausamācārau, parisuddhau ājīvau, indriyaisumha guttadvārā; alamaittāvatā katamaittāvatā, anuppattau nau sāmañ‍ñatthau, natthi nau kiñ‍ci uttariṃ karaṇīya’nti tāvatakainaiva tuṭṭhiṃ  āpaj‍jaiyyātha| āraucayāmi vau, bhikkhavai, paṭivaidayāmi vau, bhikkhavai – ‘mā vau sāmañ‍ñatthikānaṃ sataṃ sāmañ‍ñatthau parihāyi, sati uttariṃ karaṇīyai’|

422. ‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, uttariṃ karaṇīyaṃ? ‘bhaujanai mattañ‍ñunau bhavissāma, paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau āhāraṃ āharissāma, naiva davāya na madāya na maṇḍanāya na vibhūsanāya yāvadaiva imassa kāyassa ṭhitiyā yāpanāya, vihiṃsūparatiyā, brahmacariyānuggahāya, iti purāṇañ‍ca vaidanaṃ paṭihaṅkhāma navañ‍ca vaidanaṃ na uppādaissāma, yātrā ca nau bhavissati, anavaj‍jatā ca, phāsu vihārau cā’ti aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ| siyā khau pana, bhikkhavai, tumhākaṃ aivamassa – ‘hirauttappainamha saman‍nāgatā, parisuddhau nau kāyasamācārau, parisuddhau vacīsamācārau, parisuddhau manausamācārau, parisuddhau ājīvau, indriyaisumha guttadvārā, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñunau; alamaittāvatā katamaittāvatā, anuppattau nau sāmañ‍ñatthau, natthi nau kiñ‍ci uttariṃ karaṇīya’nti tāvatakainaiva tuṭṭhiṃ āpaj‍jaiyyātha| āraucayāmi vau, bhikkhavai, paṭivaidayāmi vau, bhikkhavai – ‘mā vau, sāmañ‍ñatthikānaṃ sataṃ sāmañ‍ñatthau parihāyi sati uttariṃ karaṇīyai’|

423. ‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, uttariṃ karaṇīyaṃ? ‘jāgariyaṃ anuyuttā bhavissāma, divasaṃ caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaissāma| rattiyā paṭhamaṃ yāmaṃ caṅkamaina  nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi  cittaṃ parisaudhaissāma| rattiyā majjhimaṃ yāmaṃ dakkhiṇaina passaina sīhasaiyyaṃ kappaissāma pādai pādaṃ ac‍cādhāya, satau sampajānau uṭṭhānasañ‍ñaṃ manasi karitvā| rattiyā pacchimaṃ yāmaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya caṅkamaina nisaj‍jāya āvaraṇīyaihi dhammaihi cittaṃ parisaudhaissāmā’ti, aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ| siyā khau pana, bhikkhavai, tumhākaṃ aivamassa – ‘hirauttappainamha saman‍nāgatā, parisuddhau nau kāyasamācārau, parisuddhau vacīsamācārau, parisuddhau  manausamācārau, parisuddhau ājīvau, indriyaisumha guttadvārā, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñunau, jāgariyaṃ anuyuttā; alamaittāvatā katamaittāvatā, anuppattau nau sāmañ‍ñatthau, natthi nau kiñ‍ci uttariṃ karaṇīya’nti, tāvatakainaiva tuṭṭhiṃ āpaj‍jaiyyātha| āraucayāmi vau, bhikkhavai, paṭivaidayāmi vau, bhikkhavai – ‘mā vau, sāmañ‍ñatthikānaṃ sataṃ sāmañ‍ñatthau parihāyi sati uttariṃ karaṇīyai’|

424. ‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, uttariṃ karaṇīyaṃ? ‘satisampajañ‍ñaina saman‍nāgatā bhavissāma, abhik‍kantai paṭik‍kantai sampajānakārī, ālaukitai vilaukitai sampajānakārī, samiñ‍jitai pasāritai sampajānakārī, saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇai sampajānakārī, asitai pītai khāyitai sāyitai sampajānakārī, uc‍cārapassāvakammai sampajānakārī, gatai ṭhitai nisin‍nai suttai jāgaritai bhāsitai tuṇhībhāvai sampajānakārī’ti, aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ| siyā khau pana, bhikkhavai, tumhākaṃ aivamassa  – ‘hirauttappainamha saman‍nāgatā, parisuddhau nau kāyasamācārau, parisuddhau vacīsamācārau, parisuddhau manausamācārau, parisuddhau ājīvau, indriyaisumha guttadvārā, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñunau, jāgariyaṃ anuyuttā, satisampajañ‍ñaina saman‍nāgatā; alamaittāvatā katamaittāvatā, anuppattau nau sāmañ‍ñatthau, natthi nau kiñ‍ci uttariṃ karaṇīya’nti tāvatakainaiva tuṭṭhiṃ āpaj‍jaiyyātha| āraucayāmi vau, bhikkhavai, paṭivaidayāmi vau, bhikkhavai – ‘mā vau, sāmañ‍ñatthikānaṃ sataṃ sāmañ‍ñatthau parihāyi sati uttariṃ karaṇīyai’|

425. ‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, uttariṃ karaṇīyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajati – arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanappatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ palālapuñ‍jaṃ| sau pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā, ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya  parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā| sau abhijjhaṃ laukai pahāya vigatābhijjhaina caitasā viharati, abhijjhāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; byāpādapadausaṃ pahāya abyāpan‍nacittau viharati, sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī, byāpādapadausā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; thīnamiddhaṃ pahāya vigatathīnamiddhau viharati, ālaukasañ‍ñī satau sampajānau, thīnamiddhā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ pahāya anuddhatau viharati, ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittau, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; vicikicchaṃ pahāya tiṇṇavicikicchau viharati, akathaṃkathī kusalaisu dhammaisu, vicikicchāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti|

426. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau iṇaṃ ādāya kammantai payaujaiyya| tassa tai kammantā samijjhaiyyuṃ sampaj‍jaiyyuṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰)| sau yāni ca paurāṇāni iṇamūlāni tāni ca byantī byantiṃ (ka॰), byanti (pī॰) karaiyya, siyā cassa uttariṃ avasiṭṭhaṃ dārabharaṇāya| tassa aivamassa – ‘ahaṃ khau pubbai iṇaṃ ādāya kammantai payaujaisiṃ, tassa mai tai kammantā samijjhiṃsu| sauhaṃ yāni ca paurāṇāni iṇamūlāni tāni ca byantī akāsiṃ, atthi ca mai uttariṃ avasiṭṭhaṃ dārabharaṇāyā’ti| sau tataunidānaṃ labhaitha pāmauj‍jaṃ, adhigacchaiyya saumanassaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau ābādhikau assa dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau, bhattañ‍cassa nacchādaiyya, na cassa kāyai balamattā| sau aparaina samayaina tamhā ābādhā muc‍caiyya, bhattañ‍cassa chādaiyya, siyā cassa kāyai balamattā| tassa aivamassa – ‘ahaṃ khau pubbai ābādhikau ahausiṃ dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau, bhattañ‍ca mai nacchādaisi, na ca mai āsi kāyai balamattā, saumhi aitarahi tamhā ābādhā muttau, bhattañ‍ca mai chādaiti, atthi ca mai kāyai balamattā’ti| sau tataunidānaṃ labhaitha pāmauj‍jaṃ, adhigacchaiyya saumanassaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau bandhanāgārai baddhau assa| sau aparaina samayaina tamhā bandhanā muc‍caiyya sautthinā abbhayaina abyayaina (sī॰ pī॰), na cassa kiñ‍ci bhaugānaṃ vayau| tassa aivamassa – ‘ahaṃ khau pubbai bandhanāgārai baddhau ahausiṃ, saumhi aitarahi  tamhā bandhanā muttau, sautthinā abbhayaina, natthi ca mai kiñ‍ci bhaugānaṃ vayau’ti| sau tataunidānaṃ labhaitha pāmauj‍jaṃ, adhigacchaiyya saumanassaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau dāsau assa anattādhīnau parādhīnau na yainakāmaṃgamau| sau aparaina samayaina tamhā dāsabyā muc‍caiyya attādhīnau aparādhīnau bhujissau yainakāmaṃgamau| tassa aivamassa – ‘ahaṃ khau pubbai dāsau ahausiṃ  anattādhīnau parādhīnau na yainakāmaṃgamau, saumhi aitarahi tamhā dāsabyā muttau attādhīnau aparādhīnau  bhujissau yainakāmaṃgamau’ti| sau tataunidānaṃ labhaitha pāmauj‍jaṃ, adhigacchaiyya saumanassaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau sadhanau sabhaugau kantāraddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jaiyya sīlakkhandhavaggapāḷiyā kiñ‍ci visadisaṃ| sau aparaina samayaina tamhā kantārā nittharaiyya sautthinā abbhayaina, na cassa kiñ‍ci bhaugānaṃ vayau| tassa aivamassa – ‘ahaṃ khau pubbai sadhanau sabhaugau kantāraddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jiṃ| saumhi aitarahi tamhā kantārā nitthiṇṇau sautthinā abbhayaina, natthi ca mai kiñ‍ci bhaugānaṃ vayau’ti| sau tataunidānaṃ labhaitha pāmauj‍jaṃ, adhigacchaiyya saumanassaṃ|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yathā iṇaṃ yathā raugaṃ yathā bandhanāgāraṃ yathā dāsabyaṃ yathā kantāraddhānamaggaṃ, imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai appahīnai attani samanupassati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, āṇaṇyaṃ yathā āraugyaṃ yathā bandhanāmaukkhaṃ yathā bhujissaṃ yathā khaimantabhūmiṃ; aivamaiva bhikkhu imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai  pahīnai attani samanupassati|

427. ‘‘sau imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai, vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi, savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ vivaikajaina pītisukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa vivaikajaina pītisukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, dakkhau nhāpakau nahāpakau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vā nhāpakantaivāsī vā kaṃsathālai nhānīyacuṇṇāni nahānīyacuṇṇāni (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ākiritvā udakaina paripphausakaṃ paripphausakaṃ san‍naiyya| sāyaṃ nhānīyapiṇḍi snaihānugatā snaihaparaitā santarabāhirā, phuṭā snaihaina na ca pagghariṇī| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ vivaikajaina pītisukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa vivaikajaina pītisukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti|

428. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ  caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ samādhijaina pītisukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa samādhijaina pītisukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, udakarahadau ubbhidaudakau  ubbhitaudakau (ka॰)| tassa  naivassa puratthimāya disāya udakassa āyamukhaṃ, na pacchimāya disāya udakassa āyamukhaṃ, na uttarāya disāya udakassa āyamukhaṃ, na dakkhiṇāya disāya udakassa āyamukhaṃ, daivau ca na kālaina kālaṃ sammādhāraṃ anuppavaicchaiyya| atha khau tamhāva udakarahadā sītā vāridhārā ubbhij‍jitvā tamaiva udakarahadaṃ sītaina vārinā abhisandaiyya parisandaiyya paripūraiyya parippharaiyya, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau udakarahadassa sītaina vārinā apphuṭaṃ assa| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ samādhijaina pītisukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa samādhijaina pītisukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti|

429. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati, satau ca sampajānau, sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ nippītikaina sukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa nippītikaina sukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, uppaliniyaṃ vā paduminiyaṃ vā puṇḍarīkiniyaṃ vā appaikac‍cāni uppalāni vā padumāni vā puṇḍarīkāni vā udakai jātāni udakai saṃvaḍḍhāni udakānuggatāni antaunimuggapausīni, tāni yāva caggā yāva ca mūlā  sītaina vārinā abhisan‍nāni parisan‍nāni paripūrāni paripphuṭāni, nāssa na naisaṃ (sī॰) kiñ‍ci sabbāvataṃ uppalānaṃ vā padumānaṃ vā puṇḍarīkānaṃ vā sītaina vārinā apphuṭaṃ assa| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ nippītikaina sukhaina abhisandaiti parisandaiti paripūraiti parippharati, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa nippītikaina sukhaina apphuṭaṃ hauti|

430. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā, pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā, adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau imamaiva kāyaṃ parisuddhaina caitasā pariyaudātaina pharitvā nisin‍nau hauti, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa parisuddhaina caitasā pariyaudātaina apphuṭaṃ hauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau audātaina vatthaina sasīsaṃ pārupaitvā nisin‍nau assa, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau  kāyassa audātaina vatthaina apphuṭaṃ assa| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imamaiva kāyaṃ parisuddhaina caitasā pariyaudātaina pharitvā nisin‍nau hauti, nāssa kiñ‍ci sabbāvatau kāyassa parisuddhaina caitasā pariyaudātaina apphuṭaṃ hauti|

431. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ  anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ, dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau sakamhā gāmā añ‍ñaṃ gāmaṃ gacchaiyya, tamhāpi gāmā añ‍ñaṃ gāmaṃ gacchaiyya, sau tamhā gāmā sakaṃyaiva gāmaṃ pac‍cāgacchaiyya| tassa aivamassa – ‘ahaṃ khau sakamhā gāmā amuṃ gāmaṃ agacchiṃ agacchiṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), tatrapi aivaṃ aṭṭhāsiṃ aivaṃ nisīdiṃ aivaṃ abhāsiṃ aivaṃ tuṇhī ahausiṃ; tamhāpi gāmā amuṃ gāmaṃ agacchiṃ, tatrapi aivaṃ aṭṭhāsiṃ aivaṃ nisīdiṃ aivaṃ abhāsiṃ aivaṃ tuṇhī ahausiṃ; saumhi tamhā gāmā sakaṃyaiva gāmaṃ pac‍cāgatau’ti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati|

432. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai sattānaṃ cutūpapātañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai  suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai, yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti…pai॰…  saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, dvai agārā sadvārā san‍nadvārā (ka॰)| tattha cakkhumā purisau majjhai ṭhitau passaiyya manussai gaihaṃ pavisantaipi nikkhamantaipi, anucaṅkamantaipi anuvicarantaipi| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti…pai॰…|

433. ‘‘sau aivaṃ samāhitai cittai parisuddhai pariyaudātai anaṅgaṇai vigatūpak‍kilaisai mudubhūtai kammaniyai  ṭhitai ānaiñ‍jappattai āsavānaṃ khayañāṇāya cittaṃ abhinin‍nāmaiti| sau ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ‘imai āsavā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tassa aivaṃ jānatau aivaṃ passatau kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, avij‍jāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, pabbatasaṅkhaipai udakarahadau acchau vippasan‍nau anāvilau| tattha cakkhumā purisau tīrai ṭhitau passaiyya sippisambukampi sippikasambukampi (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) sakkharakathalampi macchagumbampi, carantampi tiṭṭhantampi| tassa aivamassa – ‘ayaṃ khau udakarahadau acchau vippasan‍nau anāvilau| tatrimai sippisambukāpi  sakkharakathalāpi macchagumbāpi carantipi tiṭṭhantipīti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāti|

434. ‘‘ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘samaṇau’ itipi ‘brāhmaṇau’itipi ‘nhātakau’itipi ‘vaidagū’itipi ‘sauttiyau’itipi ‘ariyau’itipi ‘arahaṃ’itipi| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu samaṇau hauti? samitāssa haunti pāpakā akusalā dhammā, saṃkilaisikā, paunaubbhavikā, sadarā, dukkhavipākā, āyatiṃ, jātijarāmaraṇiyā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu samaṇau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu brāhmaṇau hauti? bāhitāssa haunti pāpakā akusalā dhammā, saṃkilaisikā, paunaubbhavikā, sadarā, dukkhavipākā, āyatiṃ, jātijarāmaraṇiyā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu brāhmaṇau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu nhātakau nahātakau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) hauti? nhātāssa haunti pāpakā akusalā dhammā, saṃkilaisikā, paunaubbhavikā, sadarā, dukkhavipākā, āyatiṃ, jātijarāmaraṇiyā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu nhātakau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaidagū hauti? viditāssa haunti pāpakā akusalā dhammā, saṃkilaisikā, paunaubbhavikā, sadarā, dukkhavipākā, āyatiṃ, jātijarāmaraṇiyā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaidagū hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sauttiyau hauti? nissutāssa haunti pāpakā akusalā dhammā, saṃkilaisikā, paunaubbhavikā, sadarā, dukkhavipākā, āyatiṃ, jātijarāmaraṇiyā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sauttiyau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyau hauti? ārakāssa haunti pāpakā akusalā dhammā, saṃkilaisikā, paunaubbhavikā, sadarā, dukkhavipākā, āyatiṃ, jātijarāmaraṇiyā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ariyau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arahaṃ hauti? ārakāssa haunti pāpakā akusalā dhammā, saṃkilaisikā, paunaubbhavikā, sadarā, dukkhavipākā, āyatiṃ, jātijarāmaraṇiyā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arahaṃ hautī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

mahāassapurasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|

10. cūḷaassapurasuttaṃ

435. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā aṅgaisu viharati assapuraṃ nāma aṅgānaṃ nigamau| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘samaṇā samaṇāti vau, bhikkhavai, janau sañ‍jānāti| tumhai ca pana ‘kai tumhai’ti puṭṭhā samānā ‘samaṇāmhā’ti paṭijānātha| taisaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, aivaṃsamañ‍ñānaṃ sataṃ aivaṃpaṭiñ‍ñānaṃ sataṃ – ‘yā samaṇasāmīcippaṭipadā taṃ paṭipaj‍jissāma; aivaṃ nau ayaṃ amhākaṃ samañ‍ñā ca sac‍cā bhavissati paṭiñ‍ñā ca bhūtā; yaisañ‍ca mayaṃ cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ paribhuñ‍jāma, taisaṃ tai kārā amhaisu mahapphalā bhavissanti mahānisaṃsā, amhākañ‍caivāyaṃ pabbaj‍jā avañjhā bhavissati saphalā saudrayā’ti| aivañhi vau, bhikkhavai, sikkhitabbaṃ|

436. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na samaṇasāmīcippaṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti? yassa kassaci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau abhijjhālussa abhijjhā appahīnā hauti, byāpan‍nacittassa byāpādau appahīnau hauti, kaudhanassa kaudhau appahīnau hauti, upanāhissa upanāhau appahīnau hauti, makkhissa makkhau appahīnau hauti, paḷāsissa paḷāsau appahīnau hauti, issukissa issā appahīnā hauti, maccharissa macchariyaṃ appahīnaṃ hauti, saṭhassa sāṭhaiyyaṃ appahīnaṃ hauti, māyāvissa māyā appahīnā hauti, pāpicchassa pāpikā icchā appahīnā hauti, micchādiṭṭhikassa micchādiṭṭhi appahīnā hauti – imaisaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, samaṇamalānaṃ samaṇadausānaṃ samaṇakasaṭānaṃ āpāyikānaṃ ṭhānānaṃ duggativaidaniyānaṃ appahānā ‘na samaṇasāmīcippaṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nau’ti vadāmi| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, matajaṃ nāma āvudhajātaṃ ubhataudhāraṃ pītanisitaṃ| tadassa saṅghāṭiyā sampārutaṃ sampalivaiṭhitaṃ| tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imassa bhikkhunau pabbaj‍jaṃ vadāmi|

437. ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṅghāṭikassa saṅghāṭidhāraṇamattaina sāmañ‍ñaṃ vadāmi| nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, acailakassa acailakamattaina sāmañ‍ñaṃ vadāmi| nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, rajaujal‍likassa rajaujal‍likamattaina sāmañ‍ñaṃ vadāmi| nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, udakaurauhakassa udakaurauhaṇamattaina udakaurauhakamattaina (sī॰ pī॰) sāmañ‍ñaṃ vadāmi| nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, rukkhamūlikassa rukkhamūlikamattaina  sāmañ‍ñaṃ vadāmi| nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, abbhaukāsikassa abbhaukāsikamattaina sāmañ‍ñaṃ vadāmi| nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ubbhaṭṭhakassa ubbhaṭṭhakamattaina sāmañ‍ñaṃ vadāmi| nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, pariyāyabhattikassa pariyāyabhattikamattaina sāmañ‍ñaṃ vadāmi| nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, mantajjhāyakassa mantajjhāyakamattaina sāmañ‍ñaṃ vadāmi| nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, jaṭilakassa jaṭādhāraṇamattaina sāmañ‍ñaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘saṅghāṭikassa cai, bhikkhavai, saṅghāṭidhāraṇamattaina abhijjhālussa  abhijjhā pahīyaitha, byāpan‍nacittassa byāpādau pahīyaitha, kaudhanassa kaudhau pahīyaitha, upanāhissa upanāhau pahīyaitha, makkhissa makkhau pahīyaitha, paḷāsissa paḷāsau pahīyaitha, issukissa issā pahīyaitha, maccharissa macchariyaṃ pahīyaitha, saṭhassa sāṭhaiyyaṃ pahīyaitha, māyāvissa māyā pahīyaitha, pāpicchassa pāpikā icchā pahīyaitha, micchādiṭṭhikassa micchādiṭṭhi pahīyaitha, tamainaṃ mittāmac‍cā ñātisālauhitā jātamaiva naṃ saṅghāṭikaṃ karaiyyuṃ, saṅghāṭikattamaiva saṃghāṭīkattai caiva (ka॰) samādapaiyyuṃ – ‘aihi tvaṃ, bhadramukha, saṅghāṭikau hauhi, saṅghāṭikassa tai satau saṅghāṭidhāraṇamattaina abhijjhālussa abhijjhā pahīyissati, byāpan‍nacittassa byāpādau pahīyissati, kaudhanassa kaudhau pahīyissati, upanāhissa upanāhau pahīyissati, makkhissa makkhau pahīyissati, paḷāsissa paḷāsau pahīyissati, issukissa issā pahīyissati, maccharissa macchariyaṃ pahīyissati, saṭhassa sāṭhaiyyaṃ pahīyissati, māyāvissa māyā pahīyissati, pāpicchassa pāpikā icchā pahīyissati, micchādiṭṭhikassa micchādiṭṭhi pahīyissatī’ti| yasmā ca khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṅghāṭikampi idhaikac‍caṃ passāmi abhijjhāluṃ byāpan‍nacittaṃ kaudhanaṃ upanāhiṃ makkhiṃ paḷāsiṃ issukiṃ macchariṃ saṭhaṃ māyāviṃ pāpicchaṃ micchādiṭṭhikaṃ, tasmā na saṅghāṭikassa saṅghāṭidhāraṇamattaina sāmañ‍ñaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘acailakassa cai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… rajaujal‍likassa cai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… udakaurauhakassa cai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… rukkhamūlikassa  cai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… abbhaukāsikassa cai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… ubbhaṭṭhakassa cai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… pariyāyabhattikassa cai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… mantajjhāyakassa cai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… jaṭilakassa  cai, bhikkhavai, jaṭādhāraṇamattaina abhijjhālussa abhijjhā pahīyaitha, byāpan‍nacittassa byāpādau pahīyaitha, kaudhanassa kaudhau pahīyaitha, upanāhissa upanāhau pahīyaitha, makkhissa makkhau pahīyaitha, paḷāsissa paḷāsau pahīyaitha, issukissa issā pahīyaitha, maccharissa macchariyaṃ pahīyaitha, saṭhassa sāṭhaiyyaṃ pahīyaitha, māyāvissa māyā pahīyaitha, pāpicchassa pāpikā icchā pahīyaitha, micchādiṭṭhikassa micchādiṭṭhi pahīyaitha, tamainaṃ mittāmac‍cā ñātisālauhitā jātamaiva naṃ jaṭilakaṃ karaiyyuṃ, jaṭilakattamaiva jaṭilakattai caiva (ka॰) samādapaiyyuṃ – ‘aihi tvaṃ, bhadramukha, jaṭilakau hauhi, jaṭilakassa tai satau jaṭādhāraṇamattaina abhijjhālussa abhijjhā pahīyissati byāpan‍nacittassa  byāpādau pahīyissati, kaudhanassa kaudhau pahīyissati…pai॰… pāpicchassa pāpikā icchā pahīyissati micchādiṭṭhikassa micchādiṭṭhi pahīyissatī’ti| yasmā ca khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, jaṭilakampi idhaikac‍caṃ passāmi abhijjhāluṃ byāpan‍nacittaṃ kaudhanaṃ upanāhiṃ makkhiṃ palāsiṃ issukiṃ macchariṃ saṭhaṃ māyāviṃ pāpicchaṃ micchādiṭṭhiṃ, tasmā  na jaṭilakassa jaṭādhāraṇamattaina sāmañ‍ñaṃ vadāmi|

438. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu samaṇasāmīcippaṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nau hauti? yassa kassaci, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau abhijjhālussa abhijjhā pahīnā hauti, byāpan‍nacittassa byāpādau pahīnau hauti, kaudhanassa kaudhau pahīnau hauti, upanāhissa upanāhau pahīnau hauti, makkhissa makkhau pahīnau hauti, paḷāsissa paḷāsau pahīnau hauti, issukissa issā pahīnā hauti, maccharissa macchariyaṃ pahīnaṃ hauti, saṭhassa sāṭhaiyyaṃ pahīnaṃ hauti, māyāvissa māyā pahīnā hauti, pāpicchassa pāpikā icchā pahīnā hauti, micchādiṭṭhikassa micchādiṭṭhi pahīnā hauti – imaisaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, samaṇamalānaṃ samaṇadausānaṃ samaṇakasaṭānaṃ āpāyikānaṃ ṭhānānaṃ duggativaidaniyānaṃ pahānā ‘samaṇasāmīcippaṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nau’ti vadāmi| sau sabbaihi imaihi pāpakaihi akusalaihi dhammaihi visuddhamattānaṃ samanupassati ( ) (vimuttamattānaṃ samanupassati) (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| tassa sabbaihi imaihi pāpakaihi akusalaihi dhammaihi visuddhamattānaṃ samanupassatau  ( ) (vimuttamattānaṃ samanupassatau) (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati, pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati|

‘‘sau maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina  avairaina abyābajjhaina pharitvā viharati| karuṇāsahagataina  caitasā…pai॰… muditāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyābajjhaina pharitvā viharati| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, paukkharaṇī acchaudakā sātaudakā sītaudakā saitakā supatitthā ramaṇīyā| puratthimāya  caipi disāya purisau āgacchaiyya ghammābhitattau ghammaparaitau kilantau tasitau pipāsitau| sau taṃ paukkharaṇiṃ āgamma vinaiyya udakapipāsaṃ vinaiyya ghammapariḷāhaṃ…pai॰… pacchimāya caipi disāya purisau āgacchaiyya…pai॰… uttarāya caipi disāya purisau āgacchaiyya…pai॰… dakkhiṇāya caipi disāya purisau āgacchaiyya| yatau kutau caipi naṃ purisau āgacchaiyya ghammābhitattau ghammaparaitau, kilantau tasitau pipāsitau| sau taṃ paukkharaṇiṃ āgamma vinaiyya udakapipāsaṃ, vinaiyya ghammapariḷāhaṃ| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, khattiyakulā caipi agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti, sau ca tathāgatappavaiditaṃ dhammavinayaṃ āgamma, aivaṃ maittaṃ karuṇaṃ muditaṃ upaikkhaṃ bhāvaitvā labhati ajjhattaṃ tamahaṃ (ka॰) vūpasamaṃ tamahaṃ (ka॰)| ajjhattaṃ vūpasamā ‘samaṇasāmīcippaṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nau’ti vadāmi| brāhmaṇakulā caipi…pai॰… vaissakulā caipi…pai॰… suddakulā caipi…pai॰… yasmā kasmā caipi kulā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti, sau ca tathāgatappavaiditaṃ dhammavinayaṃ āgamma, aivaṃ maittaṃ karuṇaṃ muditaṃ upaikkhaṃ bhāvaitvā labhati ajjhattaṃ vūpasamaṃ| ajjhattaṃ vūpasamā ‘samaṇasāmīcippaṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nau’ti vadāmi|

‘‘khattiyakulā  caipi agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti| sau ca āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| āsavānaṃ khayā samaṇau hauti| brāhmaṇakulā caipi…pai॰… vaissakulā caipi… suddakulā caipi… yasmā kasmā caipi kulā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau hauti, sau ca āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ  diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| āsavānaṃ khayā samaṇau hautī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

cūḷaassapurasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|

mahāyamakavaggau niṭṭhitau catutthau|


tassuddānaṃ –

giñ‍jakasālavanaṃ pariharituṃ, pañ‍ñavatau puna sac‍cakanisaidhau|

mukhavaṇṇapasīdanatāpindau, kaivaṭṭaassapurajaṭilaina||


5. cūḷayamakavaggau

1. sālaiyyakasuttaṃ

439. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausalaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ yaina sālā nāma kausalānaṃ brāhmaṇagāmau tadavasari| assausuṃ khau sālaiyyakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā – ‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau kausalaisu cārikaṃ caramānau mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ sālaṃ anuppattau| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ; kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti’| sādhu khau pana tathārūpānaṃ arahataṃ dassanaṃ hautī’’ti|

atha khau sālaiyyakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā appaikac‍cai bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu; appaikac‍cai bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu, sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ  sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu; appaikac‍cai yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu; appaikac‍cai bhagavatau santikai nāmagauttaṃ sāvaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu; appaikac‍cai tuṇhībhūtā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau sālaiyyakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhau gautama, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍janti? kau pana, bhau gautama, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jantī’’ti?

‘‘adhammacariyāvisamacariyāhaitu khau, gahapatayau, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍janti| dhammacariyāsamacariyāhaitu  khau, gahapatayau, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jantī’’ti|

‘‘na khau mayaṃ imassa bhautau gautamassa saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa, vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa, vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāma| sādhu nau bhavaṃ gautamau tathā dhammaṃ daisaitu, yathā mayaṃ imassa bhautau gautamassa saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa, vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa, vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānaiyyāmā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, gahapatayau, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau sālaiyyakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā  aitadavauca –

440. ‘‘tividhaṃ khau, gahapatayau, kāyaina adhammacariyāvisamacariyā hauti, catubbidhaṃ vācāya adhammacariyāvisamacariyā hauti, tividhaṃ manasā adhammacariyāvisamacariyā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapatayau, tividhaṃ kāyaina adhammacariyāvisamacariyā hauti? idha, gahapatayau, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātī hauti, luddau luddau dāruṇau (ka॰) ṭīkā aulaukaitabbā lauhitapāṇi hatappahatai niviṭṭhau adayāpan‍nau pāṇabhūtaisu sabbapāṇabhūtaisu (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰)|

‘‘adin‍nādāyī khau pana hauti| yaṃ taṃ parassa paravittūpakaraṇaṃ, gāmagataṃ vā arañ‍ñagataṃ vā, taṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādātā hauti|

‘‘kāmaisumicchācārī khau pana hauti| yā tā māturakkhitā piturakkhitā mātāpiturakkhitā bhāturakkhitā bhaginirakkhitā ñātirakkhitā gauttarakkhitā dhammarakkhitā sassāmikā saparidaṇḍā antamasau mālāguḷaparikkhittāpi, tathārūpāsu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jitā hauti| aivaṃ khau, gahapatayau, tividhaṃ kāyaina adhammacariyāvisamacariyā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapatayau, catubbidhaṃ vācāya adhammacariyāvisamacariyā hauti? idha, gahapatayau, aikac‍cau musāvādī hauti| sabhāgatau vā parisāgatau vā, ñātimajjhagatau vā pūgamajjhagatau vā rājakulamajjhagatau vā, abhinītau sakkhipuṭṭhau – ‘aihambhau purisa, yaṃ jānāsi taṃ vadaihī’ti, sau ajānaṃ vā āha – ‘jānāmī’ti, jānaṃ vā āha – ‘na jānāmī’ti, apassaṃ vā āha – ‘passāmī’ti, passaṃ vā āha – ‘na passāmī’ti sau āha ajānaṃ vā ahaṃ jānāmīti jānaṃ vā ahaṃ na jānāmīti apassaṃ vā ahaṃ passāmīti passaṃ vā ahaṃ na passāmīti (ka॰)| iti attahaitu vā parahaitu vā āmisakiñ‍cikkhahaitu vā sampajānamusā bhāsitā hauti|

‘‘pisuṇavācau khau pana hauti| itau sutvā amutra akkhātā  imaisaṃ bhaidāya, amutra vā sutvā imaisaṃ akkhātā amūsaṃ bhaidāya| iti samaggānaṃ vā bhaittā bhaidakā (ka॰), bhaidaitā (syā॰ kaṃ॰), tadaṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana bhaittāti dissati, bhin‍nānaṃ vā anuppadātā, vaggārāmau vaggaratau vagganandī vaggakaraṇiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti|

‘‘pharusavācau khau pana hauti| yā sā vācā aṇḍakā kaṇḍakā (ka॰) kak‍kasā parakaṭukā parābhisaj‍janī kaudhasāmantā asamādhisaṃvattanikā, tathārūpiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti|

‘‘samphappalāpī khau pana hauti| akālavādī abhūtavādī anatthavādī adhammavādī avinayavādī| anidhānavatiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti akālaina anapadaisaṃ apariyantavatiṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ| aivaṃ khau, gahapatayau, catubbidhaṃ vācāya adhammacariyāvisamacariyā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapatayau, tividhaṃ manasā adhammacariyāvisamacariyā hauti? idha, gahapatayau, aikac‍cau abhijjhālu hauti, yaṃ taṃ parassa paravittūpakaraṇaṃ taṃ abhijjhātā hauti – ‘ahau vata yaṃ parassa taṃ mamassā’’’ti!

‘‘byāpan‍nacittau khau pana hauti paduṭṭhamanasaṅkappau – ‘imai sattā hañ‍ñantu vā vajjhantu vā ucchij‍jantu vā vinassantu vā mā vā ahaisu’’’nti mā vā ahaisuṃ iti vāti (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰)|

‘‘micchādiṭṭhikau khau pana hauti viparītadassanau – ‘natthi din‍naṃ natthi yiṭṭhaṃ natthi hutaṃ, natthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau, natthi ayaṃ laukau natthi parau laukau, natthi mātā natthi pitā, natthi sattā aupapātikā, natthi laukai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipan‍nā  yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’ti| aivaṃ khau, gahapatayau, tividhaṃ manasā adhammacariyāvisamacariyā  hauti|

‘‘aivaṃ adhammacariyāvisamacariyāhaitu khau, gahapatayau, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍janti|

441. ‘‘tividhaṃ khau, gahapatayau, kāyaina dhammacariyāsamacariyā hauti, catubbidhaṃ vācāya dhammacariyāsamacariyā hauti, tividhaṃ manasā dhammacariyāsamacariyā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapatayau, tividhaṃ kāyaina dhammacariyāsamacariyā hauti? idha, gahapatayau, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, nihitadaṇḍau nihitasatthau laj‍jī dayāpan‍nau sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharati|

‘‘adin‍nādānaṃ pahāya adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti| yaṃ taṃ parassa paravittūpakaraṇaṃ, gāmagataṃ vā arañ‍ñagataṃ vā, taṃ nādin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādātā hauti|

‘‘kāmaisumicchācāraṃ pahāya kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti| yā tā māturakkhitā piturakkhitā mātāpiturakkhitā bhāturakkhitā bhaginirakkhitā ñātirakkhitā gauttarakkhitā dhammarakkhitā sassāmikā saparidaṇḍā antamasau mālāguḷaparikkhittāpi, tathārūpāsu na cārittaṃ āpaj‍jitā hauti| aivaṃ khau, gahapatayau, tividhaṃ kāyaina dhammacariyāsamacariyā  hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapatayau, catubbidhaṃ vācāya dhammacariyāsamacariyā hauti? idha, gahapatayau, aikac‍cau musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti| sabhāgatau vā parisāgatau vā, ñātimajjhagatau vā pūgamajjhagatau vā rājakulamajjhagatau vā, abhinītau sakkhipuṭṭhau – ‘aihambhau purisa, yaṃ jānāsi taṃ vadaihī’ti, sau  ajānaṃ vā āha – ‘na jānāmī’ti, jānaṃ vā āha – ‘jānāmī’ti, apassaṃ vā āha – ‘na passāmī’ti, passaṃ vā āha  – ‘passāmī’ti| iti attahaitu vā parahaitu vā āmisakiñ‍cikkhahaitu vā na sampajānamusā bhāsitā hauti|

‘‘pisuṇaṃ  vācaṃ pahāya pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, itau sutvā na amutra akkhātā imaisaṃ bhaidāya, amutra vā sutvā na imaisaṃ akkhātā amūsaṃ bhaidāya| iti bhin‍nānaṃ vā sandhātā, sahitānaṃ vā anuppadātā, samaggārāmau samaggaratau samagganandī samaggakaraṇiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti|

‘‘pharusaṃ vācaṃ pahāya pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti| yā sā vācā nailā kaṇṇasukhā paimanīyā hadayaṅgamā paurī bahujanakantā bahujanamanāpā – tathārūpiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti|

‘‘samphappalāpaṃ pahāya samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti| kālavādī bhūtavādī atthavādī dhammavādī vinayavādī nidhānavatiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti kālaina sāpadaisaṃ pariyantavatiṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ| aivaṃ khau, gahapatayau, catubbidhaṃ vācāya dhammacariyāsamacariyā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapatayau, tividhaṃ manasā dhammacariyāsamacariyā hauti? idha, gahapatayau, aikac‍cau anabhijjhālu hauti, yaṃ taṃ parassa paravittūpakaraṇaṃ taṃ nābhijjhātā hauti – ‘ahau vata yaṃ parassa taṃ mamassā’ti!

‘‘abyāpan‍nacittau khau pana hauti appaduṭṭhamanasaṅkappau – ‘imai sattā avairā abyābajjhā anīghā sukhī attānaṃ pariharantū’ti|

‘‘sammādiṭṭhikau khau pana hauti aviparītadassanau – ‘atthi din‍naṃ atthi yiṭṭhaṃ  atthi hutaṃ, atthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau, atthi ayaṃ laukau atthi parau laukau, atthi mātā atthi pitā, atthi sattā aupapātikā, atthi laukai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipan‍nā yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’ti| aivaṃ khau, gahapatayau, tividhaṃ manasā dhammacariyāsamacariyā hauti|

‘‘aivaṃ dhammacariyāsamacariyāhaitu khau, gahapatayau, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti|

442. ‘‘ākaṅkhaiyya  cai, gahapatayau, dhammacārī samacārī – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā khattiyamahāsālānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti; ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati, yaṃ sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā khattiyamahāsālānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi sau dhammacārī samacārī|

‘‘ākaṅkhaiyya cai, gahapatayau, dhammacārī samacārī – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ…pai॰… gahapatimahāsālānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti; ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati, yaṃ sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi sau dhammacārī samacārī|

‘‘ākaṅkhaiyya cai, gahapatayau, dhammacārī samacārī – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā cātumahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapajjaiyya’nti; ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ  vij‍jati, yaṃ sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā cātumahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi sau dhammacārī samacārī|

‘‘ākaṅkhaiyya cai, gahapatayau, dhammacārī samacārī – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā tāvatiṃsānaṃ daivānaṃ…pai॰… yāmānaṃ daivānaṃ… tusitānaṃ daivānaṃ… nimmānaratīnaṃ daivānaṃ… paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ daivānaṃ… brahmakāyikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti; ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati, yaṃ sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā brahmakāyikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi sau dhammacārī samacārī|

‘‘ākaṅkhaiyya cai, gahapatayau, dhammacārī samacārī – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā ābhānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti; ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati, yaṃ sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā ābhānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi sau dhammacārī samacārī|

‘‘ākaṅkhaiyya cai, gahapatayau, dhammacārī samacārī – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā parittābhānaṃ daivānaṃ…pai॰… appamāṇābhānaṃ daivānaṃ… ābhassarānaṃ daivānaṃ… parittasubhānaṃ  daivānaṃ… appamāṇasubhānaṃ daivānaṃ… subhakiṇhānaṃ daivānaṃ… vaihapphalānaṃ daivānaṃ… avihānaṃ  daivānaṃ… atappānaṃ daivānaṃ… sudassānaṃ daivānaṃ… sudassīnaṃ daivānaṃ… akaniṭṭhānaṃ daivānaṃ… ākāsānañ‍cāyatanūpagānaṃ daivānaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanūpagānaṃ daivānaṃ … ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanūpagānaṃ daivānaṃ… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanūpagānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti; ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati, yaṃ sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanūpagānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi sau dhammacārī samacārī|

‘‘ākaṅkhaiyya cai, gahapatayau, dhammacārī samacārī – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti; ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati, yaṃ sau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi sau dhammacārī samacārī’’ti|

443. aivaṃ  vuttai, sālaiyyakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama! saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya, cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti| aivamaivaṃ bhautā gautamaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aitai mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāma dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakai nau bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitai pāṇupaitaṃ (ka॰) saraṇaṃ gatai’’ti|

sālaiyyakasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

2. vairañ‍jakasuttaṃ

444. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina vairañ‍jakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā sāvatthiyaṃ paṭivasanti kainacidaiva karaṇīyaina| assausuṃ  khau vairañ‍jakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā – ‘‘samaṇau khalu, bhau, gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā| sau imaṃ laukaṃ sadaivakaṃ samārakaṃ sabrahmakaṃ sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiṃ pajaṃ sadaivamanussaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaiti| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ; kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti’| sādhu khau pana tathārūpānaṃ arahataṃ dassanaṃ hautī’’ti|

atha khau vairañ‍jakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā appaikac‍cai bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu; appaikac‍cai bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu, sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ  sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu; appaikac‍cai yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu; appaikac‍cai bhagavatau santikai  nāmagauttaṃ sāvaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu; appaikac‍cai tuṇhībhūtā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau vairañ‍jakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhau gautama, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍janti? kau pana, bhau gautama, haitu, kau pac‍cayau yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jantī’’ti?

‘‘adhammacariyāvisamacariyāhaitu khau, gahapatayau, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍janti| dhammacariyāsamacariyāhaitu khau, gahapatayau, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jantī’’ti|

‘‘na khau mayaṃ imassa bhautau gautamassa saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa, vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa, vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāma| sādhu nau bhavaṃ gautamau tathā dhammaṃ daisaitu yathā mayaṃ imassa bhautau gautamassa saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa, vitthāraina atthaṃ avibhattassa, vitthāraina atthaṃ  ājānaiyyāmā’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, gahapatayau, suṇātha sādhukaṃ manasi karautha, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ bhau’’ti khau vairañ‍jakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

445. ‘‘tividhaṃ khau, gahapatayau, kāyaina adhammacārī visamacārī hauti, catubbidhaṃ vācāya adhammacārī visamacārī hauti, tividhaṃ manasā adhammacārī visamacārī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapatayau, tividhaṃ kāyaina adhammacārī visamacārī hauti? idha, gahapatayau, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātī hauti| luddau lauhitapāṇi hatappahatai niviṭṭhau adayāpan‍nau pāṇabhūtaisu| adin‍nādāyī khau pana hauti| yaṃ taṃ parassa paravittūpakaraṇaṃ… taṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādātā hauti| kāmaisumicchācārī khau pana hauti| yā tā māturakkhitā… tathārūpāsu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jitā hauti| aivaṃ khau, gahapatayau, tividhaṃ kāyaina adhammacārī visamacārī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapatayau, catubbidhaṃ vācāya adhammacārī visamacārī hauti? idha, gahapatayau, aikac‍cau musāvādī hauti| sabhāgatau vā… sampajānamusā bhāsitā hauti| pisuṇavācau khau pana hauti| itau sutvā amutra akkhātā… vaggakaraṇiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti| pharusavācau khau pana hauti| yā sā vācā aṇḍakā kak‍kasā… tathārūpiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti| samphappalāpī khau pana hauti| akālavādī… apariyantavatiṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ| aivaṃ khau, gahapatayau, catubbidhaṃ vācāya adhammacārī visamacārī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapatayau, tividhaṃ manasā adhammacārī visamacārī hauti? idha, gahapatayau, aikac‍cau abhijjhālu hauti…pai॰… taṃ mamassā’ti| byāpan‍nacittau khau pana hauti paduṭṭhamanasaṅkappau – imai sattā hañ‍ñantu vā… mā vā ahaisu’nti| micchādiṭṭhikau khau pana  hauti viparītadassanau – ‘natthi din‍naṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ… sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’ti| aivaṃ  khau, gahapatayau, tividhaṃ manasā adhammacārī visamacārī hauti|

‘‘aivaṃ adhammacariyāvisamacariyāhaitu khau, gahapatayau, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍janti|

446. ‘‘tividhaṃ  khau, gahapatayau, kāyaina dhammacārī samacārī hauti, catubbidhaṃ vācāya dhammacārī samacārī hauti, tividhaṃ manasā dhammacārī samacārī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapatayau, tividhaṃ kāyaina dhammacārī samacārī hauti? idha, gahapatayau, aikac‍cau pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, nihitadaṇḍau nihitasatthau laj‍jī dayāpan‍nau sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharati| adin‍nādānaṃ pahāya adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, yaṃ taṃ parassa… taṃ nādin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādātā hauti| kāmaisumicchācāraṃ pahāya… tathārūpāsu na cārittaṃ āpaj‍jitā hauti| aivaṃ khau, gahapatayau, tividhaṃ kāyaina dhammacārī samacārī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapatayau, catubbidhaṃ vācāya dhammacārī samacārī hauti? idha, gahapatayau, aikac‍cau musāvādaṃ pahāya musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti| sabhāgatau vā…pai॰… na sampajānamusā bhāsitā hauti| pisuṇaṃ vācaṃ pahāya… samaggakaraṇiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti| pharusaṃ vācaṃ pahāya… tathārūpiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā hauti| samphappalāpaṃ pahāya… kālaina sāpadaisaṃ pariyantavatiṃ atthasaṃhitaṃ| aivaṃ khau, gahapatayau, catubbidhaṃ vācāya dhammacārī samacārī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapatayau, tividhaṃ manasā dhammacārī samacārī hauti? idha, gahapatayau, aikac‍cau anabhijjhālu hauti| yaṃ taṃ parassa paravittūpakaraṇaṃ taṃ nābhijjhātā hauti ‘ahau vata yaṃ parassa, taṃ mamassā’ti| abyāpan‍nacittau khau pana hauti appaduṭṭhamanasaṅkappau – ‘imai sattā avairā abyābajjhā anīghā sukhī attānaṃ pariharantū’ti| sammādiṭṭhikau khau pana hauti aviparītadassanau – ‘atthi din‍naṃ, atthi yiṭṭhaṃ… sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintī’ti| aivaṃ khau, gahapatayau, tividhaṃ manasā dhammacārī samacārī hauti|

‘‘aivaṃ  dhammacariyāsamacariyāhaitu khau, gahapatayau, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti|

447. ‘‘ākaṅkhaiyya cai, gahapatayau, dhammacārī samacārī – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā khattiyamahāsālānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti; ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati, yaṃ sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā khattiyamahāsālānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi sau dhammacārī samacārī|

‘‘ākaṅkhaiyya cai, gahapatayau, dhammacārī samacārī – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā brāhmaṇamahāsālānaṃ gahapatimahāsālānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti; ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati, yaṃ sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā gahapatimahāsālānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi sau dhammacārī samacārī|

‘‘ākaṅkhaiyya cai, gahapatayau, dhammacārī samacārī – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā cātumahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti; ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati, yaṃ sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā cātumahārājikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi sau dhammacārī samacārī|

‘‘ākaṅkhaiyya cai, gahapatayau, dhammacārī samacārī – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā tāvatiṃsānaṃ daivānaṃ… yāmānaṃ daivānaṃ… tusitānaṃ daivānaṃ… nimmānaratīnaṃ daivānaṃ… paranimmitavasavattīnaṃ daivānaṃ… brahmakāyikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti; ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati, yaṃ sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā brahmakāyikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi sau dhammacārī samacārī|

‘‘ākaṅkhaiyya cai, gahapatayau, dhammacārī samacārī – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā ābhānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti; ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati, yaṃ sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā ābhānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi sau dhammacārī samacārī|
