‘‘ākaṅkhaiyya  cai, gahapatayau, dhammacārī samacārī ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā parittābhānaṃ daivānaṃ…pai॰… appamāṇābhānaṃ daivānaṃ… ābhassarānaṃ daivānaṃ… parittasubhānaṃ daivānaṃ… appamāṇasubhānaṃ daivānaṃ… subhakiṇhānaṃ daivānaṃ … vaihapphalānaṃ daivānaṃ… avihānaṃ daivānaṃ… atappānaṃ daivānaṃ… sudassānaṃ daivānaṃ… sudassīnaṃ daivānaṃ… akaniṭṭhānaṃ daivānaṃ… ākāsānañ‍cāyatanūpagānaṃ daivānaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanūpagānaṃ  daivānaṃ… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanūpagānaṃ daivānaṃ… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanūpagānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti; ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati, yaṃ sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanūpagānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi sau dhammacārī samacārī|

‘‘ākaṅkhaiyya cai gahapatayau dhammacārī samacārī – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti; ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati, ‘yaṃ sau āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi sau dhammacārī samacārī’’’ti|

448. aivaṃ vuttai, vairañ‍jakā brāhmaṇagahapatikā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘abhik‍kantaṃ, bhau gautama, abhik‍kantaṃ bhau gautama! saiyyathāpi, bhau gautama, nik‍kuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya, paṭicchan‍naṃ vā vivaraiyya, mūḷhassa vā maggaṃ ācikkhaiyya, andhakārai vā tailapaj‍jautaṃ dhāraiyya, cakkhumantau rūpāni dakkhantīti; aivamaivaṃ bhautā gautamaina anaikapariyāyaina dhammau pakāsitau| aitai mayaṃ bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāma  dhammañ‍ca bhikkhusaṅghañ‍ca| upāsakai nau bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitu aj‍jataggai pāṇupaitai saraṇaṃ gatai’’ti|

vairañ‍jakasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dutiyaṃ|

3. mahāvaidal‍lasuttaṃ

449. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā sāriputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca  –

‘‘‘duppañ‍ñau duppañ‍ñau’ti, āvusau, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau, duppañ‍ñauti vuc‍catī’’ti?

‘‘‘nappajānāti nappajānātī’ti khau, āvusau, tasmā duppañ‍ñauti vuc‍cati|

‘‘kiñ‍ca nappajānāti? ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti nappajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti nappajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti nappajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti nappajānāti| ‘nappajānāti nappajānātī’ti khau, āvusau, tasmā duppañ‍ñauti vuc‍catī’’ti|

‘‘‘sādhāvusau’ti khau āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau āyasmatau sāriputtassa bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ uttariṃ pañhaṃ apucchi –

‘‘‘pañ‍ñavā pañ‍ñavā’ti, āvusau, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau, pañ‍ñavāti vuc‍catī’’ti?

‘‘‘pajānāti pajānātī’ti khau, āvusau, tasmā pañ‍ñavāti vuc‍cati|

‘‘kiñ‍ca pajānāti? ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti  pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti pajānāti| ‘pajānāti pajānātī’ti khau, āvusau, tasmā pañ‍ñavāti vuc‍catī’’ti|

‘‘‘viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti, āvusau, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau, viñ‍ñāṇanti vuc‍catī’’ti?

‘‘‘vijānāti vijānātī’ti khau, āvusau, tasmā viñ‍ñāṇanti vuc‍cati|

‘‘kiñ‍ca vijānāti? sukhantipi vijānāti, dukkhantipi vijānāti, adukkhamasukhantipi vijānāti| ‘vijānāti vijānātī’ti khau, āvusau, tasmā viñ‍ñāṇanti vuc‍catī’’ti|

‘‘yā cāvusau, pañ‍ñā yañ‍ca viñ‍ñāṇaṃ – imai dhammā saṃsaṭṭhā udāhu visaṃsaṭṭhā? labbhā ca panimaisaṃ dhammānaṃ vinibbhujitvā vinibbhuj‍jitvā vinibbhuj‍jitvā (ka॰) vinibbhujitvā nānākaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāpaitu’’nti? ‘‘yā cāvusau, pañ‍ñā yañ‍ca viñ‍ñāṇaṃ – imai dhammā saṃsaṭṭhā, nau visaṃsaṭṭhā| na ca labbhā imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ vinibbhujitvā vinibbhujitvā nānākaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāpaituṃ| yaṃ hāvusau yañ‍cāvusau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), pajānāti taṃ vijānāti, yaṃ vijānāti taṃ pajānāti| tasmā  imai dhammā saṃsaṭṭhā, nau visaṃsaṭṭhā| na ca labbhā imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ vinibbhujitvā vinibbhujitvā nānākaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāpaitu’’nti|

‘‘yā cāvusau, pañ‍ñā yañ‍ca viñ‍ñāṇaṃ – imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ saṃsaṭṭhānaṃ nau visaṃsaṭṭhānaṃ kiṃ nānākaraṇa’’nti? ‘‘yā cāvusau, pañ‍ñā yañ‍ca viñ‍ñāṇaṃ – imaisaṃ  dhammānaṃ saṃsaṭṭhānaṃ nau visaṃsaṭṭhānaṃ pañ‍ñā bhāvaitabbā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ| idaṃ naisaṃ nānākaraṇa’’nti|

450. ‘‘‘vaidanā vaidanā’ti, āvusau, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau, vaidanāti vuc‍catī’’ti?

‘‘‘vaidaiti vaidaitī’ti khau, āvusau, tasmā vaidanāti vuc‍cati|

‘‘kiñ‍ca  vaidaiti? sukhampi vaidaiti, dukkhampi vaidaiti, adukkhamasukhampi vaidaiti| ‘vaidaiti vaidaitī’ti khau, āvusau, tasmā vaidanāti vuc‍catī’’ti|

‘‘‘sañ‍ñā sañ‍ñā’ti, āvusau, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau, sañ‍ñāti vuc‍catī’’ti?

‘‘‘sañ‍jānāti sañ‍jānātī’ti khau, āvusau, tasmā sañ‍ñāti vuc‍cati|

‘‘kiñ‍ca sañ‍jānāti? nīlakampi sañ‍jānāti, pītakampi sañ‍jānāti, lauhitakampi sañ‍jānāti, audātampi sañ‍jānāti| ‘sañ‍jānāti sañ‍jānātī’ti khau, āvusau, tasmā sañ‍ñāti vuc‍catī’’ti|

‘‘yā cāvusau, vaidanā yā ca sañ‍ñā yañ‍ca viñ‍ñāṇaṃ – imai dhammā saṃsaṭṭhā udāhu visaṃsaṭṭhā? labbhā ca panimaisaṃ dhammānaṃ vinibbhujitvā vinibbhujitvā nānākaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāpaitu’’nti? ‘‘yā cāvusau, vaidanā yā ca sañ‍ñā yañ‍ca viñ‍ñāṇaṃ – imai dhammā saṃsaṭṭhā, nau visaṃsaṭṭhā| na ca labbhā imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ vinibbhujitvā vinibbhujitvā nānākaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāpaituṃ| yaṃ hāvusau yañ‍cāvusau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), vaidaiti taṃ sañ‍jānāti, yaṃ sañ‍jānāti taṃ vijānāti| tasmā imai dhammā saṃsaṭṭhā nau visaṃsaṭṭhā| na ca labbhā imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ vinibbhujitvā vinibbhujitvā nānākaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāpaitu’’nti|

451. ‘‘nissaṭṭhaina hāvusau nissaṭṭhaina panāvusau (?), pañ‍cahi indriyaihi parisuddhaina manauviñ‍ñāṇaina kiṃ naiyya’’nti?

‘‘nissaṭṭhaina āvusau, pañ‍cahi indriyaihi parisuddhaina manauviñ‍ñāṇaina ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ  naiyyaṃ, ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ naiyyaṃ, ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ naiyya’’nti|

‘‘naiyyaṃ panāvusau, dhammaṃ kaina pajānātī’’ti?

‘‘naiyyaṃ  khau, āvusau, dhammaṃ pañ‍ñācakkhunā pajānātī’’ti|

‘‘pañ‍ñā panāvusau, kimatthiyā’’ti?

‘‘pañ‍ñā khau, āvusau, abhiñ‍ñatthā pariñ‍ñatthā pahānatthā’’ti|

452. ‘‘kati  panāvusau, pac‍cayā sammādiṭṭhiyā uppādāyā’’ti?

‘‘dvai khau, āvusau, pac‍cayā sammādiṭṭhiyā uppādāya – paratau ca ghausau, yaunisau ca manasikārau| imai khau, āvusau, dvai pac‍cayā sammādiṭṭhiyā uppādāyā’’ti|

‘‘katihi panāvusau, aṅgaihi anuggahitā sammādiṭṭhi caitauvimuttiphalā ca hauti caitauvimuttiphalānisaṃsā ca, pañ‍ñāvimuttiphalā ca hauti pañ‍ñāvimuttiphalānisaṃsā cā’’ti?

‘‘pañ‍cahi khau, āvusau, aṅgaihi anuggahitā sammādiṭṭhi caitauvimuttiphalā ca hauti caitauvimuttiphalānisaṃsā ca, pañ‍ñāvimuttiphalā ca hauti pañ‍ñāvimuttiphalānisaṃsā ca| idhāvusau, sammādiṭṭhi sīlānuggahitā ca hauti, sutānuggahitā ca hauti, sākacchānuggahitā ca hauti, samathānuggahitā ca hauti, vipassanānuggahitā ca hauti| imaihi khau, āvusau, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi anuggahitā sammādiṭṭhi caitauvimuttiphalā ca hauti caitauvimuttiphalānisaṃsā ca, pañ‍ñāvimuttiphalā ca hauti pañ‍ñāvimuttiphalānisaṃsā cā’’ti|

453. ‘‘kati panāvusau, bhavā’’ti?

‘‘tayaumai, āvusau, bhavā – kāmabhavau, rūpabhavau, arūpabhavau’’ti|

‘‘kathaṃ panāvusau, āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti hautī’’ti?

‘‘avij‍jānīvaraṇānaṃ  khau, āvusau, sattānaṃ taṇhāsaṃyaujanānaṃ tatratatrābhinandanā – aivaṃ āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti hautī’’ti|

‘‘kathaṃ panāvusau, āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti na hautī’’ti?

‘‘avij‍jāvirāgā khau, āvusau, vij‍juppādā taṇhāniraudhā – aivaṃ āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti na hautī’’ti|

454. ‘‘katamaṃ panāvusau, paṭhamaṃ jhāna’’nti?

‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati – idaṃ vuc‍cati, āvusau, paṭhamaṃ jhāna’’nti|

‘‘paṭhamaṃ panāvusau, jhānaṃ katiaṅgika’’nti?

‘‘paṭhamaṃ khau, āvusau, jhānaṃ pañ‍caṅgikaṃ| idhāvusau, paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa bhikkhunau vitak‍kau ca vattati, vicārau ca pīti ca sukhañ‍ca cittaikaggatā ca| paṭhamaṃ khau, āvusau, jhānaṃ aivaṃ pañ‍caṅgika’’nti|

‘‘paṭhamaṃ panāvusau, jhānaṃ kataṅgavippahīnaṃ kataṅgasaman‍nāgata’’nti?

‘‘paṭhamaṃ khau, āvusau, jhānaṃ pañ‍caṅgavippahīnaṃ, pañ‍caṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ| idhāvusau, paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa bhikkhunau kāmacchandau pahīnau  hauti, byāpādau pahīnau hauti, thīnamiddhaṃ pahīnaṃ hauti, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ pahīnaṃ  hauti, vicikicchā pahīnā hauti; vitak‍kau ca vattati, vicārau ca pīti ca sukhañ‍ca cittaikaggatā ca| paṭhamaṃ khau, āvusau, jhānaṃ aivaṃ pañ‍caṅgavippahīnaṃ pañ‍caṅgasaman‍nāgata’’nti|

455. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, āvusau, indriyāni nānāvisayāni nānāgaucarāni, na añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa  gaucaravisayaṃ pac‍canubhaunti, saiyyathidaṃ – cakkhundriyaṃ, sautindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ| imaisaṃ khau, āvusau, pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ nānāvisayānaṃ nānāgaucarānaṃ, na añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa gaucaravisayaṃ pac‍canubhauntānaṃ, kiṃ paṭisaraṇaṃ, kau ca naisaṃ gaucaravisayaṃ pac‍canubhautī’’ti?

‘‘pañ‍cimāni, āvusau, indriyāni nānāvisayāni nānāgaucarāni, na añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa gaucaravisayaṃ pac‍canubhaunti, saiyyathidaṃ – cakkhundriyaṃ, sautindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ| imaisaṃ khau, āvusau, pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ nānāvisayānaṃ nānāgaucarānaṃ, na añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa gaucaravisayaṃ pac‍canubhauntānaṃ, manau paṭisaraṇaṃ, manau ca naisaṃ gaucaravisayaṃ pac‍canubhautī’’ti|

456. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, āvusau, indriyāni, saiyyathidaṃ – cakkhundriyaṃ, sautindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ| imāni khau, āvusau, pañ‍cindriyāni kiṃ paṭic‍ca tiṭṭhantī’’ti?

‘‘pañ‍cimāni, āvusau, indriyāni, saiyyathidaṃ – cakkhundriyaṃ, sautindriyaṃ, ghānindriyaṃ, jivhindriyaṃ, kāyindriyaṃ| imāni khau, āvusau, pañ‍cindriyāni āyuṃ paṭic‍ca tiṭṭhantī’’ti|

‘‘āyu panāvusau, kiṃ paṭic‍ca tiṭṭhatī’’ti?

‘‘āyu usmaṃ paṭic‍ca tiṭṭhatī’’ti|

‘‘usmā panāvusau, kiṃ paṭic‍ca tiṭṭhatī’’ti?

‘‘usmā āyuṃ paṭic‍ca tiṭṭhatī’’ti|

‘‘idānaiva khau mayaṃ, āvusau, āyasmatau sāriputtassa bhāsitaṃ aivaṃ ājānāma – ‘āyu  usmaṃ paṭic‍ca tiṭṭhatī’ti| idānaiva pana mayaṃ, āvusau, āyasmatau  sāriputtassa bhāsitaṃ aivaṃ ājānāma – ‘usmā āyuṃ paṭic‍ca tiṭṭhatī’ti|

‘‘yathā kathaṃ panāvusau, imassa bhāsitassa atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti?

‘‘taina hāvusau, upamaṃ tai karissāmi; upamāyapidhaikac‍cai viñ‍ñū purisā bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānanti| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, tailappadīpassa jhāyatau ac‍ciṃ paṭic‍ca ābhā pañ‍ñāyati, ābhaṃ paṭic‍ca ac‍ci pañ‍ñāyati; aivamaiva khau, āvusau, āyu usmaṃ paṭic‍ca tiṭṭhati, usmā āyuṃ paṭic‍ca tiṭṭhatī’’ti|

457. ‘‘taiva  nu khau, āvusau, āyusaṅkhārā, tai vaidaniyā dhammā udāhu añ‍ñai āyusaṅkhārā añ‍ñai vaidaniyā dhammā’’ti? ‘‘na khau, āvusau, taiva āyusaṅkhārā tai vaidaniyā dhammā| tai ca hāvusau, āyusaṅkhārā abhaviṃsu tai vaidaniyā dhammā, na yidaṃ sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpan‍nassa bhikkhunau vuṭṭhānaṃ pañ‍ñāyaitha| yasmā ca khau, āvusau, añ‍ñai āyusaṅkhārā añ‍ñai vaidaniyā dhammā, tasmā sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpan‍nassa bhikkhunau vuṭṭhānaṃ pañ‍ñāyatī’’ti|

‘‘yadā nu khau, āvusau, imaṃ kāyaṃ kati dhammā jahanti; athāyaṃ kāyau ujjhitau avakkhittau saiti, yathā kaṭṭhaṃ acaitana’’nti?

‘‘yadā khau, āvusau, imaṃ kāyaṃ tayau dhammā jahanti – āyu usmā ca viñ‍ñāṇaṃ; athāyaṃ kāyau ujjhitau avakkhittau saiti, yathā kaṭṭhaṃ acaitana’’nti|

‘‘yvāyaṃ, āvusau, matau kālaṅkatau, yau cāyaṃ bhikkhu sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpan‍nau – imaisaṃ kiṃ nānākaraṇa’’nti?

‘‘yvāyaṃ, āvusau, matau kālaṅkatau tassa kāyasaṅkhārā niruddhā paṭippassaddhā, vacīsaṅkhārā niruddhā paṭippassaddhā, cittasaṅkhārā niruddhā paṭippassaddhā, āyu parikkhīṇau, usmā vūpasantā, indriyāni paribhin‍nāni| yau cāyaṃ bhikkhu sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpan‍nau tassapi kāyasaṅkhārā niruddhā paṭippassaddhā, vacīsaṅkhārā niruddhā paṭippassaddhā, cittasaṅkhārā niruddhā paṭippassaddhā, āyu na parikkhīṇau, usmā avūpasantā, indriyāni vippasan‍nāni| yvāyaṃ, āvusau, matau kālaṅkatau, yau cāyaṃ bhikkhu sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpan‍nau – idaṃ naisaṃ nānākaraṇa’’nti|

458. ‘‘kati  panāvusau, pac‍cayā adukkhamasukhāya caitauvimuttiyā samāpattiyā’’ti?

‘‘cattārau khau, āvusau, pac‍cayā adukkhamasukhāya caitauvimuttiyā samāpattiyā| idhāvusau, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| imai khau, āvusau, cattārau pac‍cayā adukkhamasukhāya caitauvimuttiyā samāpattiyā’’ti|

‘‘kati panāvusau, pac‍cayā animittāya caitauvimuttiyā samāpattiyā’’ti?

‘‘dvai khau, āvusau, pac‍cayā animittāya caitauvimuttiyā samāpattiyā  – sabbanimittānañ‍ca amanasikārau, animittāya ca dhātuyā manasikārau| imai khau, āvusau, dvai pac‍cayā animittāya caitauvimuttiyā samāpattiyā’’ti|

‘‘kati panāvusau, pac‍cayā animittāya caitauvimuttiyā ṭhitiyā’’ti?

‘‘tayau khau, āvusau, pac‍cayā animittāya caitauvimuttiyā  ṭhitiyā  – sabbanimittānañ‍ca amanasikārau, animittāya ca dhātuyā manasikārau, pubbai ca abhisaṅkhārau| imai khau, āvusau, tayau pac‍cayā animittāya caitauvimuttiyā ṭhitiyā’’ti|

‘‘kati panāvusau, pac‍cayā animittāya caitauvimuttiyā vuṭṭhānāyā’’ti?

‘‘dvai khau, āvusau, pac‍cayā animittāya caitauvimuttiyā vuṭṭhānāya – sabbanimittānañ‍ca manasikārau, animittāya ca dhātuyā amanasikārau| imai khau, āvusau, dvai pac‍cayā animittāya caitauvimuttiyā vuṭṭhānāyā’’ti|

459. ‘‘yā cāyaṃ, āvusau, appamāṇā caitauvimutti, yā ca ākiñ‍cañ‍ñā caitauvimutti, yā ca suñ‍ñatā caitauvimutti, yā ca animittā caitauvimutti – imai dhammā nānātthā caiva nānābyañ‍janā ca udāhu aikatthā byañ‍janamaiva nāna’’nti?

‘‘yā cāyaṃ, āvusau, appamāṇā caitauvimutti, yā ca ākiñ‍cañ‍ñā caitauvimutti, yā ca suñ‍ñatā caitauvimutti, yā ca animittā caitauvimutti – atthi khau, āvusau, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma imai dhammā nānātthā caiva nānābyañ‍janā ca; atthi ca khau, āvusau, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma imai dhammā aikatthā, byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ’’|

‘‘katamau  cāvusau, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma imai dhammā nānātthā caiva nānābyañ‍janā ca’’?

‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina  caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyābajjhaina pharitvā viharati| karuṇāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… muditāsahagataina caitasā… upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau  tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyābajjhaina pharitvā viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, appamāṇā caitauvimutti’’|

‘‘katamā cāvusau, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñā caitauvimutti’’?

‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma natthi kiñ‍cīti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñā caitauvimutti’’|

‘‘katamā cāvusau, suñ‍ñatā caitauvimutti’’?

‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘suñ‍ñamidaṃ attaina vā attaniyaina vā’ti| ayaṃ  vuc‍catāvusau, suñ‍ñatā caitauvimutti’’|

‘‘katamā cāvusau, animittā caitauvimutti’’?

‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu sabbanimittānaṃ amanasikārā animittaṃ caitausamādhiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, animittā caitauvimutti| ayaṃ khau, āvusau, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma imai dhammā nānātthā caiva nānābyañ‍janā ca’’|

‘‘katamau cāvusau, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma imai dhammā aikatthā byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ’’?

‘‘rāgau khau, āvusau, pamāṇakaraṇau, dausau pamāṇakaraṇau, mauhau pamāṇakaraṇau| tai khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau pahīnā  ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| yāvatā khau, āvusau, appamāṇā caitauvimuttiyau, akuppā tāsaṃ caitauvimutti aggamakkhāyati| sā khau panākuppā caitauvimutti suñ‍ñā rāgaina, suñ‍ñā dausaina, suñ‍ñā mauhaina| rāgau khau, āvusau, kiñ‍canau, dausau kiñ‍canau, mauhau kiñ‍canau| tai khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| yāvatā khau, āvusau, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñā caitauvimuttiyau, akuppā tāsaṃ caitauvimutti aggamakkhāyati| sā khau panākuppā caitauvimutti suñ‍ñā rāgaina, suñ‍ñā dausaina, suñ‍ñā mauhaina| rāgau khau, āvusau, nimittakaraṇau, dausau nimittakaraṇau, mauhau nimittakaraṇau| tai  khīṇāsavassa bhikkhunau pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| yāvatā khau, āvusau, animittā caitauvimuttiyau, akuppā tāsaṃ caitauvimutti aggamakkhāyati| sā khau panākuppā caitauvimutti suñ‍ñā rāgaina, suñ‍ñā dausaina, suñ‍ñā mauhaina| ayaṃ khau, āvusau, pariyāyau yaṃ pariyāyaṃ āgamma imai dhammā aikatthā byañ‍janamaiva nāna’’nti|

idamavaucāyasmā sāriputtau| attamanau āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau āyasmatau sāriputtassa bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

mahāvaidal‍lasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ tatiyaṃ|

4. cūḷavaidal‍lasuttaṃ

460. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| atha khau visākhau upāsakau yaina dhammadin‍nā bhikkhunī tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā dhammadin‍naṃ bhikkhuniṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau visākhau upāsakau dhammadin‍naṃ bhikkhuniṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘sak‍kāyau sak‍kāyau’ti, ayyai, vuc‍cati| katamau nu khau, ayyai, sak‍kāyau vuttau bhagavatā’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍ca khau imai, āvusau visākha, upādānakkhandhā sak‍kāyau vuttau bhagavatā, saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau, vaidanupādānakkhandhau, sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhau, saṅkhārupādānakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| imai khau, āvusau visākha, pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā sak‍kāyau vuttau bhagavatā’’ti|

‘‘sādhayyai’’ti khau visākhau upāsakau dhammadin‍nāya bhikkhuniyā bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā dhammadin‍naṃ bhikkhuniṃ uttariṃ pañhaṃ apucchi – ‘‘‘sak‍kāyasamudayau sak‍kāyasamudayau’ti, ayyai, vuc‍cati| katamau nu khau, ayyai, sak‍kāyasamudayau vuttau bhagavatā’’ti? ‘‘yāyaṃ, āvusau visākha, taṇhā paunaubbhavikā nandīrāgasahagatā tatratatrābhinandinī, saiyyathidaṃ – kāmataṇhā bhavataṇhā vibhavataṇhā; ayaṃ khau, āvusau visākha, sak‍kāyasamudayau vuttau bhagavatā’’ti|

‘‘‘sak‍kāyaniraudhau  sak‍kāyaniraudhau’ti, ayyai, vuc‍cati| katamau nu khau, ayyai, sak‍kāyaniraudhau vuttau bhagavatā’’ti?

‘‘yau  khau, āvusau visākha, tassāyaiva taṇhāya asaisavirāganiraudhau cāgau paṭinissaggau mutti anālayau; ayaṃ khau, āvusau visākha, sak‍kāyaniraudhau vuttau bhagavatā’’ti|

‘‘‘sak‍kāyaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā sak‍kāyaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti, ayyai, vuc‍cati| katamā nu khau, ayyai, sak‍kāyaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā vuttā bhagavatā’’ti?

‘‘ayamaiva khau, āvusau visākha, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau sak‍kāyaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā vuttā bhagavatā, saiyyathidaṃ  – sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhī’’ti|

‘‘tañ‍ñaiva nu khau, ayyai, upādānaṃ tai taiva (sī॰) pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā udāhu añ‍ñatra pañ‍cahupādānakkhandhaihi upādāna’’nti? ‘‘na khau, āvusau visākha, tañ‍ñaiva upādānaṃ tai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā, nāpi  añ‍ñatra pañ‍cahupādānakkhandhaihi upādānaṃ| yau khau, āvusau visākha, pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu chandarāgau taṃ tattha upādāna’’nti|

461. ‘‘kathaṃ panāyyai, sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi hautī’’ti? ‘‘idhāvusau visākha, assutavā puthuj‍janau, ariyānaṃ adassāvī ariyadhammassa akauvidau ariyadhammai avinītau, sappurisānaṃ adassāvī sappurisadhammassa akauvidau sappurisadhammai avinītau, rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, attani vā rūpaṃ, rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| vaidanaṃ…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati, viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, āvusau visākha, sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi hautī’’ti|

‘‘kathaṃ panāyyai, sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi na hautī’’ti?

‘‘idhāvusau visākha, sutavā ariyasāvakau, ariyānaṃ dassāvī ariyadhammassa kauvidau ariyadhammai suvinītau, sappurisānaṃ dassāvī sappurisadhammassa kauvidau sappurisadhammai suvinītau, na rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, na rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, na attani vā rūpaṃ, na rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| na vaidanaṃ…pai॰… na sañ‍ñaṃ… na saṅkhārai…pai॰… na viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati, na viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, na attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, na viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, āvusau visākha, sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi na hautī’’ti|

462. ‘‘katamau panāyyai, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau’’ti?

‘‘ayamaiva khau, āvusau visākha, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau  sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhī’’ti| ‘‘ariyau panāyyai, aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau saṅkhatau udāhu asaṅkhatau’’ti?

‘‘ariyau khau, āvusau visākha, aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau saṅkhatau’’ti|

‘‘ariyaina nu khau, ayyai, aṭṭhaṅgikaina maggaina tayau khandhā saṅgahitā udāhu tīhi khandhaihi ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau saṅgahitau’’ti?

‘‘na khau, āvusau visākha, ariyaina aṭṭhaṅgikaina maggaina tayau khandhā saṅgahitā; tīhi ca khau, āvusau visākha, khandhaihi ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau saṅgahitau| yā cāvusau visākha, sammāvācā yau ca sammākammantau yau ca sammāājīvau imai dhammā  sīlakkhandhai saṅgahitā| yau ca sammāvāyāmau yā ca sammāsati yau ca sammāsamādhi imai dhammā samādhikkhandhai saṅgahitā| yā ca sammādiṭṭhi yau ca sammāsaṅkappau, imai dhammā pañ‍ñākkhandhai saṅgahitā’’ti|

‘‘katamau panāyyai, samādhi, katamai dhammā samādhinimittā, katamai dhammā samādhiparikkhārā, katamā samādhibhāvanā’’ti?

‘‘yā khau, āvusau visākha, cittassa aikaggatā ayaṃ samādhi; cattārau satipaṭṭhānā samādhinimittā; cattārau sammappadhānā samādhiparikkhārā| yā taisaṃyaiva dhammānaṃ āsaivanā bhāvanā bahulīkammaṃ, ayaṃ aittha samādhibhāvanā’’ti|

463. ‘‘kati panāyyai, saṅkhārā’’ti?

‘‘tayaumai, āvusau visākha, saṅkhārā – kāyasaṅkhārau, vacīsaṅkhārau, cittasaṅkhārau’’ti|

‘‘katamau panāyyai, kāyasaṅkhārau, katamau vacīsaṅkhārau, katamau cittasaṅkhārau’’ti?

‘‘assāsapassāsā khau, āvusau visākha, kāyasaṅkhārau, vitak‍kavicārā vacīsaṅkhārau, sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca cittasaṅkhārau’’ti|

‘‘kasmā panāyyai, assāsapassāsā kāyasaṅkhārau, kasmā vitak‍kavicārā vacīsaṅkhārau, kasmā sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca cittasaṅkhārau’’ti?

‘‘assāsapassāsā khau, āvusau  visākha, kāyikā aitai dhammā kāyappaṭibaddhā, tasmā assāsapassāsā kāyasaṅkhārau| pubbai khau, āvusau visākha, vitak‍kaitvā vicāraitvā pacchā vācaṃ bhindati, tasmā vitak‍kavicārā vacīsaṅkhārau| sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca caitasikā aitai dhammā  cittappaṭibaddhā, tasmā sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca cittasaṅkhārau’’ti|

464. ‘‘kathaṃ  panāyyai, sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpatti hautī’’ti?

‘‘na khau, āvusau visākha, sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jantassa bhikkhunau aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jissa’nti vā, ‘ahaṃ sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jāmī’ti vā, ‘ahaṃ sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpan‍nau’ti vā| atha khvāssa pubbaiva tathā cittaṃ bhāvitaṃ hauti yaṃ taṃ tathattāya upanaitī’’ti|

‘‘sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jantassa  panāyyai, bhikkhunau katamai dhammā paṭhamaṃ nirujjhanti – yadi vā kāyasaṅkhārau, yadi vā vacīsaṅkhārau, yadi vā cittasaṅkhārau’’ti? ‘‘sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jantassa khau, āvusau visākha, bhikkhunau paṭhamaṃ nirujjhati vacīsaṅkhārau, tatau kāyasaṅkhārau, tatau cittasaṅkhārau’’ti|

‘‘kathaṃ panāyyai, sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpattiyā vuṭṭhānaṃ hautī’’ti?

‘‘na khau, āvusau visākha, sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpattiyā vuṭṭhahantassa bhikkhunau aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpattiyā vuṭṭhahissa’nti vā, ‘ahaṃ sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpattiyā vuṭṭhahāmī’ti vā, ‘ahaṃ sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpattiyā vuṭṭhitau’ti vā| atha khvāssa pubbaiva tathā cittaṃ bhāvitaṃ hauti yaṃ taṃ tathattāya upanaitī’’ti|

‘‘sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpattiyā vuṭṭhahantassa panāyyai, bhikkhunau katamai dhammā paṭhamaṃ uppaj‍janti – yadi vā kāyasaṅkhārau, yadi vā vacīsaṅkhārau, yadi  vā cittasaṅkhārau’’ti? ‘‘sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpattiyā vuṭṭhahantassa khau, āvusau visākha, bhikkhunau paṭhamaṃ uppaj‍jati cittasaṅkhārau, tatau kāyasaṅkhārau, tatau vacīsaṅkhārau’’ti|

‘‘sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpattiyā vuṭṭhitaṃ panāyyai, bhikkhuṃ kati phassā phusantī’’ti? ‘‘sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpattiyā vuṭṭhitaṃ khau, āvusau  visākha, bhikkhuṃ tayau phassā phusanti – suñ‍ñatau phassau, animittau phassau, appaṇihitau phassau’’ti|

‘‘sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpattiyā vuṭṭhitassa panāyyai, bhikkhunau kiṃnin‍naṃ cittaṃ hauti kiṃpauṇaṃ kiṃpabbhāra’’nti? ‘‘sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasamāpattiyā vuṭṭhitassa khau, āvusau visākha, bhikkhunau vivaikanin‍naṃ cittaṃ hauti, vivaikapauṇaṃ vivaikapabbhāra’’nti|

465. ‘‘kati  panāyyai, vaidanā’’ti?

‘‘tissau khau imā, āvusau visākha, vaidanā – sukhā vaidanā, dukkhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā’’ti|

‘‘katamā panāyyai, sukhā vaidanā, katamā dukkhā vaidanā, katamā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā’’ti?

‘‘yaṃ khau, āvusau visākha, kāyikaṃ vā caitasikaṃ vā sukhaṃ sātaṃ vaidayitaṃ – ayaṃ sukhā vaidanā| yaṃ khau, āvusau visākha, kāyikaṃ vā caitasikaṃ vā dukkhaṃ asātaṃ vaidayitaṃ – ayaṃ dukkhā vaidanā| yaṃ khau, āvusau visākha, kāyikaṃ vā caitasikaṃ vā naiva sātaṃ nāsātaṃ vaidayitaṃ – ayaṃ adukkhamasukhā  vaidanā’’ti|

‘‘sukhā panāyyai, vaidanā kiṃsukhā kiṃdukkhā, dukkhā vaidanā kiṃsukhā kiṃdukkhā, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā kiṃsukhā kiṃdukkhā’’ti?

‘‘sukhā khau, āvusau visākha, vaidanā ṭhitisukhā vipariṇāmadukkhā; dukkhā vaidanā ṭhitidukkhā vipariṇāmasukhā; adukkhamasukhā vaidanā ñāṇasukhā añ‍ñāṇadukkhā’’ti|

‘‘sukhāya panāyyai, vaidanāya kiṃ anusayau anusaiti, dukkhāya vaidanāya kiṃ anusayau anusaiti, adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya kiṃ anusayau anusaitī’’ti?

‘‘sukhāya khau, āvusau visākha, vaidanāya rāgānusayau anusaiti, dukkhāya vaidanāya paṭighānusayau anusaiti, adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya avij‍jānusayau anusaitī’’ti|

‘‘sabbāya nu khau, ayyai, sukhāya vaidanāya rāgānusayau anusaiti, sabbāya dukkhāya vaidanāya paṭighānusayau anusaiti, sabbāya adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya avij‍jānusayau anusaitī’’ti?

‘‘na khau, āvusau visākha, sabbāya sukhāya vaidanāya rāgānusayau anusaiti, na sabbāya dukkhāya vaidanāya paṭighānusayau anusaiti, na sabbāya adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya avij‍jānusayau anusaitī’’ti|

‘‘sukhāya  panāyyai, vaidanāya kiṃ pahātabbaṃ, dukkhāya vaidanāya kiṃ pahātabbaṃ, adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya kiṃ pahātabba’’nti?

‘‘sukhāya khau, āvusau visākha, vaidanāya rāgānusayau pahātabbau, dukkhāya vaidanāya paṭighānusayau pahātabbau, adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya avij‍jānusayau pahātabbau’’ti|

‘‘sabbāya  nu khau, ayyai, sukhāya vaidanāya rāgānusayau pahātabbau, sabbāya dukkhāya vaidanāya paṭighānusayau pahātabbau, sabbāya adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya avij‍jānusayau pahātabbau’’ti?

‘‘na khau, āvusau visākha, sabbāya sukhāya vaidanāya rāgānusayau pahātabbau, na sabbāya dukkhāya vaidanāya paṭighānusayau pahātabbau, na sabbāya adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya avij‍jānusayau pahātabbau| idhāvusau visākha, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| rāgaṃ taina pajahati, na tattha rāgānusayau anusaiti| idhāvusau visākha, bhikkhu iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘kudāssu nāmāhaṃ tadāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharissāmi yadariyā aitarahi āyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharantī’ti? iti anuttaraisu vimaukkhaisu pihaṃ upaṭṭhāpayatau  uppaj‍jati pihāppac‍cayā daumanassaṃ| paṭighaṃ taina pajahati, na tattha paṭighānusayau anusaiti| idhāvusau visākha, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā, dukkhassa ca pahānā, pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā, adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| avij‍jaṃ taina pajahati, na tattha avij‍jānusayau anusaitī’’ti|

466. ‘‘sukhāya panāyyai, vaidanāya kiṃ paṭibhāgau’’ti?

‘‘sukhāya khau, āvusau visākha, vaidanāya dukkhā vaidanā paṭibhāgau’’ti|

‘‘dukkhāya panāyyai, vaidanāya kiṃ paṭibhāgau’’ti?

‘‘dukkhāya khau, āvusau visākha, vaidanāya sukhā vaidanā paṭibhāgau’’ti|

‘‘adukkhamasukhāya panāyyai, vaidanāya kiṃ paṭibhāgau’’ti?

‘‘adukkhamasukhāya khau, āvusau visākha, vaidanāya avij‍jā paṭibhāgau’’ti|

‘‘avij‍jāya panāyyai, kiṃ paṭibhāgau’’ti?

‘‘avij‍jāya khau, āvusau visākha, vij‍jā paṭibhāgau’’ti|

‘‘vij‍jāya panāyyai, kiṃ paṭibhāgau’’ti?

‘‘vij‍jāya khau, āvusau visākha, vimutti paṭibhāgau’’ti|

‘‘vimuttiyā panāyyai, kiṃ paṭibhāgau’’ti?

‘‘vimuttiyā khau, āvusau visākha, nibbānaṃ paṭibhāgau’’ti|

‘‘nibbānassa panāyyai, kiṃ paṭibhāgau’’ti? ‘‘ac‍cayāsi, āvusau ac‍casarāvusau (sī॰ pī॰), ac‍cassarāvusau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) visākha, pañhaṃ, nāsakkhi pañhānaṃ pariyantaṃ gahaituṃ| nibbānaugadhañhi, āvusau visākha, brahmacariyaṃ, nibbānaparāyanaṃ nibbānapariyausānaṃ| ākaṅkhamānau ca tvaṃ, āvusau visākha, bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyyāsi, yathā ca tai bhagavā byākarauti tathā naṃ dhāraiyyāsī’’ti|

467. atha khau visākhau upāsakau dhammadin‍nāya bhikkhuniyā bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā dhammadin‍naṃ bhikkhuniṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau visākhau upāsakau yāvatakau ahausi dhammadin‍nāya bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ kathāsal‍lāpau taṃ sabbaṃ bhagavatau āraucaisi| aivaṃ vuttai, bhagavā visākhaṃ upāsakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘paṇḍitā, visākha, dhammadin‍nā bhikkhunī, mahāpañ‍ñā, visākha, dhammadin‍nā bhikkhunī| maṃ caipi tvaṃ, visākha, aitamatthaṃ pucchaiyyāsi, ahampi taṃ aivamaivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ, yathā  taṃ dhammadin‍nāya bhikkhuniyā byākataṃ| aisau caivaitassa aisauvaitassa (syā॰ kaṃ॰) atthau| aivañ‍ca naṃ aivamaitaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) dhāraihī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanau visākhau upāsakau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti|

cūḷavaidal‍lasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ catutthaṃ|

5. cūḷadhammasamādānasuttaṃ

468. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, dhammasamādānāni| katamāni cattāri? atthi, bhikkhavai, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhaṃ āyatiṃ dukkhavipākaṃ; atthi, bhikkhavai, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadukkhañ‍caiva āyatiñ‍ca dukkhavipākaṃ; atthi, bhikkhavai, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadukkhaṃ āyatiṃ sukhavipākaṃ; atthi, bhikkhavai, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhañ‍caiva āyatiñ‍ca sukhavipākaṃ’’|

469. ‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhaṃ āyatiṃ dukkhavipākaṃ? santi, bhikkhavai, aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau – ‘natthi kāmaisu dausau’ti| tai kāmaisu pātabyataṃ āpaj‍janti| tai khau mauḷibaddhāhi mauḷibandhāhi (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) paribbājikāhi paricārainti| tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘kiṃsu nāma tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kāmaisu anāgatabhayaṃ sampassamānā kāmānaṃ pahānamāhaṃsu, kāmānaṃ pariñ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñapainti? sukhau imissā paribbājikāya taruṇāya mudukāya laumasāya bāhāya samphassau’ti tai kāmaisu pātabyataṃ āpaj‍janti| tai kāmaisu pātabyataṃ āpaj‍jitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ  duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍janti| tai tattha dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidayanti| tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘idaṃ khau tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kāmaisu anāgatabhayaṃ sampassamānā kāmānaṃ pahānamāhaṃsu, kāmānaṃ pariñ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñapainti, imai hi mayaṃ kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ  dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidayāmā’ti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gimhānaṃ pacchimai māsai māluvāsipāṭikā phalaiyya| atha khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, māluvābījaṃ añ‍ñatarasmiṃ sālamūlai nipataiyya| atha khau, bhikkhavai, yā tasmiṃ sālai adhivatthā daivatā sā bhītā saṃviggā santāsaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya| atha khau, bhikkhavai, tasmiṃ sālai adhivatthāya daivatāya mittāmac‍cā ñātisālauhitā ārāmadaivatā vanadaivatā rukkhadaivatā ausadhitiṇavanappatīsu adhivatthā daivatā saṅgamma samāgamma aivaṃ samassāsaiyyuṃ – ‘mā bhavaṃ bhāyi, mā bhavaṃ bhāyi; appaiva nāmaitaṃ māluvābījaṃ  maurau vā gilaiyya maurau vā gilaiyya, gaudhā vā khādaiyya (ka॰), magau vā khādaiyya, davaḍāhau vanadāhau (ka॰) vā ḍahaiyya, vanakammikā vā uddharaiyyuṃ, upacikā vā uṭṭhahaiyyuṃ udrabhaiyyuṃ (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰), abījaṃ vā panassā’ti| atha khau taṃ, bhikkhavai, māluvābījaṃ naiva maurau gilaiyya, na magau khādaiyya, na davaḍāhau ḍahaiyya, na vanakammikā uddharaiyyuṃ, na upacikā uṭṭhahaiyyuṃ, bījañ‍ca panassa taṃ pāvussakaina maighaina abhippavuṭṭhaṃ  sammadaiva viruhaiyya| sāssa māluvālatā taruṇā mudukā laumasā vilambinī, sā taṃ sālaṃ upanisaivaiyya| atha khau, bhikkhavai, tasmiṃ sālai adhivatthāya daivatāya aivamassa – ‘kiṃsu nāma tai  bhauntau mittāmac‍cā ñātisālauhitā ārāmadaivatā vanadaivatā rukkhadaivatā ausadhitiṇavanappatīsu adhivatthā daivatā māluvābījai anāgatabhayaṃ sampassamānā saṅgamma samāgamma aivaṃ samassāsaisuṃ samassāsaiyyuṃ (ka॰) – ‘‘mā bhavaṃ bhāyi mā bhavaṃ bhāyi, appaiva nāmaitaṃ māluvābījaṃ maurau vā gilaiyya, magau vā khādaiyya, davaḍāhau vā ḍahaiyya, vanakammikā vā uddharaiyyuṃ, upacikā vā uṭṭhahaiyyuṃ, abījaṃ vā panassā’’ti; sukhau imissā māluvālatāya taruṇāya mudukāya laumasāya vilambiniyā samphassau’ti| sā taṃ sālaṃ anupariharaiyya| sā taṃ sālaṃ anupariharitvā upari viṭabhiṃ viṭapaṃ (syā॰ ṭṭha॰) karaiyya| upari viṭabhiṃ karitvā aughanaṃ janaiyya| aughanaṃ janaitvā yai tassa sālassa mahantā mahantā khandhā tai padālaiyya| atha khau, bhikkhavai, tasmiṃ sālai adhivatthāya daivatāya aivamassa – ‘idaṃ khau tai bhauntau mittāmac‍cā ñātisālauhitā ārāmadaivatā vanadaivatā rukkhadaivatā ausadhitiṇavanappatīsu adhivatthā daivatā māluvābījai anāgatabhayaṃ sampassamānā saṅgamma samāgamma aivaṃ samassāsaisuṃ samassāsaiyyuṃ (ka॰) – ‘‘mā bhavaṃ bhāyi mā bhavaṃ bhāyi, appaiva nāmaitaṃ māluvābījaṃ maurau vā gilaiyya, magau  vā khādaiyya, davaḍāhau vā ḍahaiyya, vanakammikā vā uddharaiyyuṃ, upacikā vā uṭṭhahaiyyuṃ abījaṃ vā panassā’’ti| yañ‍cāhaṃ yaṃ vāhaṃ (ka॰), svāhaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) māluvābījahaitu dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidayāmī’ti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, santi aikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau ‘natthi kāmaisu dausau’ti| tai kāmaisu pātabyataṃ āpaj‍janti| tai mauḷibaddhāhi paribbājikāhi paricārainti| tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘kiṃsu nāma tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kāmaisu anāgatabhayaṃ sampassamānā kāmānaṃ pahānamāhaṃsu, kāmānaṃ pariñ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñapainti? sukhau imissā paribbājikāya taruṇāya mudukāya laumasāya bāhāya samphassau’ti| tai kāmaisu pātabyataṃ āpaj‍janti| tai kāmaisu pātabyataṃ āpaj‍jitvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍janti| tai tattha dukkhā tibbā kharā  kaṭukā vaidanā vaidayanti| tai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘idaṃ khau tai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā kāmaisu anāgatabhayaṃ sampassamānā kāmānaṃ pahānamāhaṃsu, kāmānaṃ pariñ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñapainti| imai hi  mayaṃ kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidayāmā’ti| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhaṃ āyatiṃ dukkhavipākaṃ|

470. ‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadukkhañ‍caiva āyatiñ‍ca dukkhavipākaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau acailakau hauti muttācārau hatthāpalaikhanau, naaihibhaddantikau, natiṭṭhabhaddantikau, nābhihaṭaṃ, na uddissakataṃ, na nimantanaṃ sādiyati, sau na kumbhimukhā paṭiggaṇhāti, na kaḷaupimukhā paṭiggaṇhāti, na aiḷakamantaraṃ, na daṇḍamantaraṃ, na musalamantaraṃ, na dvin‍naṃ bhuñ‍jamānānaṃ, na gabbhiniyā, na pāyamānāya, na purisantaragatāya, na saṅkittīsu, na yattha sā upaṭṭhitau  hauti, na yattha makkhikā saṇḍasaṇḍacārinī, na macchaṃ, na maṃsaṃ, na suraṃ, na mairayaṃ, na thusaudakaṃ pivati| sau aikāgārikau vā hauti aikālaupikau, dvāgārikau vā hauti dvālaupikau…pai॰… sattāgārikau vā hauti sattālaupikau| aikissāpi dattiyā yāpaiti, dvīhipi dattīhi yāpaiti… sattahipi dattīhi yāpaiti| aikāhikampi āhāraṃ āhāraiti, dvīhikampi āhāraṃ āhāraiti… sattāhikampi āhāraṃ āhāraiti| iti aivarūpaṃ addhamāsikampi pariyāyabhattabhaujanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati| sau sākabhakkhau vā hauti, sāmākabhakkhau vā hauti, nīvārabhakkhau vā hauti, daddulabhakkhau vā hauti, haṭabhakkhau vā hauti, kaṇabhakkhau vā hauti, ācāmabhakkhau vā hauti, piñ‍ñākabhakkhau vā hauti, tiṇabhakkhau vā hauti, gaumayabhakkhau vā hauti, vanamūlaphalāhārau yāpaiti pavattaphalabhaujī| sau sāṇānipi dhāraiti, masāṇānipi dhāraiti, chavadussānipi dhāraiti, paṃsukūlānipi dhāraiti, tirīṭānipi dhāraiti, ajinampi dhāraiti, ajinakkhipampi dhāraiti, kusacīrampi dhāraiti, vākacīrampi dhāraiti, phalakacīrampi dhāraiti, kaisakambalampi dhāraiti, vāḷakambalampi dhāraiti, ulūkapakkhampi dhāraiti, kaisamassulaucakaupi hauti, kaisamassulaucanānuyaugamanuyuttau, ubbhaṭṭhakaupi hauti, āsanapaṭikkhittau, uk‍kuṭikaupi hauti uk‍kuṭikappadhānamanuyuttau, kaṇṭakāpassayikaupi hauti, kaṇṭakāpassayai saiyyaṃ  kappaiti passa ma॰ ni॰ 1.155 mahāsīhanādasuttai, sāyatatiyakampi udakaurauhanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati| iti aivarūpaṃ anaikavihitaṃ kāyassa ātāpanaparitāpanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadukkhañ‍caiva āyatiñ‍ca dukkhavipākaṃ|

471. ‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadukkhaṃ āyatiṃ sukhavipākaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pakatiyā tibbarāgajātikau hauti, sau abhikkhaṇaṃ rāgajaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; pakatiyā tibbadausajātikau hauti, sau abhikkhaṇaṃ dausajaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; pakatiyā tibbamauhajātikau hauti, sau abhikkhaṇaṃ mauhajaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| sau sahāpi dukkhaina, sahāpi daumanassaina, assumukhaupi rudamānau paripuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ carati| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadukkhaṃ āyatiṃ sukhavipākaṃ|

472. ‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhañ‍caiva āyatiñ‍ca sukhavipākaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau pakatiyā na tibbarāgajātikau hauti, sau na abhikkhaṇaṃ rāgajaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; pakatiyā na tibbadausajātikau hauti, sau na abhikkhaṇaṃ dausajaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; pakatiyā na tibbamauhajātikau hauti, sau na abhikkhaṇaṃ mauhajaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| sau vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ  pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhañ‍caiva āyatiñ‍ca sukhavipākaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri dhammasamādānānī’’ti|

idamavauca  bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

cūḷadhammasamādānasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

6. mahādhammasamādānasuttaṃ

473. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘yaibhuyyaina, bhikkhavai, sattā aivaṃkāmā aivaṃchandā aivaṃadhippāyā – ‘ahau vata aniṭṭhā akantā amanāpā dhammā parihāyaiyyuṃ, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā dhammā abhivaḍḍhaiyyu’nti| taisaṃ, bhikkhavai, sattānaṃ aivaṃkāmānaṃ aivaṃchandānaṃ aivaṃadhippāyānaṃ aniṭṭhā akantā amanāpā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā dhammā parihāyanti| tatra tumhai, bhikkhavai, kaṃ haituṃ pac‍caithā’’ti? ‘‘bhagavaṃmūlakā  nau, bhantai, dhammā, bhagavaṃnaittikā, bhagavaṃpaṭisaraṇā| sādhu vata, bhantai, bhagavantañ‍ñaiva paṭibhātu aitassa bhāsitassa atthau; bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

474. ‘‘idha, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau, ariyānaṃ adassāvī ariyadhammassa akauvidau ariyadhammai avinītau, sappurisānaṃ adassāvī sappurisadhammassa akauvidau sappurisadhammai avinītau, saivitabbai dhammai na jānāti asaivitabbai dhammai na jānāti, bhajitabbai  dhammai na jānāti abhajitabbai dhammai na jānāti| sau saivitabbai dhammai ajānantau asaivitabbai dhammai ajānantau, bhajitabbai dhammai ajānantau abhajitabbai dhammai ajānantau, asaivitabbai dhammai saivati saivitabbai dhammai na saivati, abhajitabbai dhammai bhajati bhajitabbai dhammai na bhajati| tassa asaivitabbai dhammai saivatau saivitabbai dhammai asaivatau, abhajitabbai dhammai bhajatau bhajitabbai dhammai abhajatau aniṭṭhā akantā amanāpā dhammā  abhivaḍḍhanti, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā dhammā parihāyanti| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, hauti yathā taṃ aviddasunau|

‘‘sutavā ca khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau, ariyānaṃ dassāvī ariyadhammassa kauvidau ariyadhammai  suvinītau, sappurisānaṃ dassāvī sappurisadhammassa kauvidau sappurisadhammai suvinītau, saivitabbai dhammai jānāti asaivitabbai dhammai jānāti, bhajitabbai dhammai jānāti abhajitabbai dhammai jānāti| sau saivitabbai dhammai jānantau asaivitabbai dhammai jānantau, bhajitabbai dhammai jānantau abhajitabbai dhammai jānantau, asaivitabbai dhammai na saivati saivitabbai dhammai saivati, abhajitabbai dhammai na bhajati bhajitabbai dhammai bhajati| tassa asaivitabbai dhammai asaivatau saivitabbai dhammai saivatau, abhajitabbai dhammai abhajatau bhajitabbai dhammai bhajatau, aniṭṭhā akantā amanāpā dhammā parihāyanti, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti| taṃ  kissa haitu? aivañhaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, hauti yathā taṃ viddasunau|

475. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, dhammasamādānāni| katamāni cattāri? atthi, bhikkhavai, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadukkhañ‍caiva āyatiñ‍ca dukkhavipākaṃ; atthi, bhikkhavai, dhammasamādānaṃ  pac‍cuppan‍nasukhaṃ āyatiṃ dukkhavipākaṃ; atthi, bhikkhavai, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadukkhaṃ āyatiṃ sukhavipākaṃ; atthi, bhikkhavai, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhañ‍caiva āyatiñ‍ca sukhavipākaṃ|

476. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yamidaṃ yadidaṃ (sī॰) dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadukkhañ‍caiva āyatiñ‍ca dukkhavipākaṃ, taṃ avidvā avij‍jāgatau yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti – ‘idaṃ khau dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadukkhañ‍caiva āyatiñ‍ca dukkhavipāka’nti| taṃ avidvā avij‍jāgatau yathābhūtaṃ appajānantau taṃ saivati, taṃ na parivaj‍jaiti| tassa taṃ saivatau, taṃ aparivaj‍jayatau, aniṭṭhā akantā amanāpā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā dhammā parihāyanti| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, hauti yathā taṃ aviddasunau|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yamidaṃ dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhaṃ āyatiṃ dukkhavipākaṃ taṃ avidvā avij‍jāgatau yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti – ‘idaṃ khau dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhaṃ  āyatiṃ dukkhavipāka’nti| taṃ avidvā avij‍jāgatau yathābhūtaṃ appajānantau taṃ saivati, taṃ na parivaj‍jaiti| tassa taṃ saivatau, taṃ  aparivaj‍jayatau, aniṭṭhā akantā amanāpā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā dhammā parihāyanti| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, hauti yathā taṃ aviddasunau|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yamidaṃ dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadukkhaṃ āyatiṃ sukhavipākaṃ, taṃ avidvā avij‍jāgatau yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti – ‘idaṃ khau dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadukkhaṃ āyatiṃ sukhavipāka’nti| taṃ avidvā avij‍jāgatau yathābhūtaṃ appajānantau taṃ na saivati, taṃ parivaj‍jaiti| tassa taṃ asaivatau, taṃ parivaj‍jayatau, aniṭṭhā  akantā amanāpā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā dhammā parihāyanti| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, hauti yathā taṃ aviddasunau|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yamidaṃ dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhañ‍caiva āyatiñ‍ca sukhavipākaṃ, taṃ avidvā avij‍jāgatau yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti – ‘idaṃ khau dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhañ‍caiva āyatiñ‍ca sukhavipāka’nti| taṃ avidvā avij‍jāgatau yathābhūtaṃ appajānantau taṃ na saivati, taṃ parivaj‍jaiti| tassa taṃ asaivatau, taṃ parivaj‍jayatau, aniṭṭhā akantā amanāpā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā dhammā parihāyanti| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, hauti yathā taṃ aviddasunau|

477. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yamidaṃ dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadukkhañ‍caiva āyatiñ‍ca dukkhavipākaṃ taṃ vidvā vij‍jāgatau yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti – ‘idaṃ  khau dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadukkhañ‍caiva āyatiñ‍ca dukkhavipāka’nti| taṃ vidvā vij‍jāgatau yathābhūtaṃ pajānantau taṃ na saivati, taṃ parivaj‍jaiti| tassa taṃ asaivatau, taṃ parivaj‍jayatau, aniṭṭhā akantā amanāpā dhammā parihāyanti, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, hauti yathā taṃ viddasunau|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yamidaṃ dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhaṃ āyatiṃ dukkhavipākaṃ taṃ vidvā vij‍jāgatau yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti – ‘idaṃ khau dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhaṃ āyatiṃ dukkhavipāka’nti| taṃ vidvā vij‍jāgatau yathābhūtaṃ pajānantau taṃ na saivati, taṃ parivaj‍jaiti| tassa taṃ asaivatau, taṃ parivaj‍jayatau, aniṭṭhā akantā amanāpā dhammā parihāyanti, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, hauti yathā taṃ viddasunau|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yamidaṃ dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadukkhaṃ āyatiṃ sukhavipākaṃ taṃ vidvā vij‍jāgatau yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti – ‘idaṃ khau dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadukkhaṃ āyatiṃ sukhavipāka’nti| taṃ vidvā vij‍jāgatau yathābhūtaṃ pajānantau taṃ saivati, taṃ na parivaj‍jaiti| tassa taṃ saivatau, taṃ aparivaj‍jayatau, aniṭṭhā akantā amanāpā dhammā parihāyanti, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, hauti yathā taṃ viddasunau|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, yamidaṃ dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhañ‍caiva āyatiñ‍ca sukhavipākaṃ  taṃ vidvā vij‍jāgatau yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti – ‘idaṃ khau dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhañ‍caiva āyatiñ‍ca sukhavipāka’nti| taṃ vidvā vij‍jāgatau yathābhūtaṃ pajānantau taṃ saivati, taṃ na parivaj‍jaiti| tassa taṃ saivatau, taṃ aparivaj‍jayatau, aniṭṭhā akantā amanāpā dhammā parihāyanti, iṭṭhā kantā manāpā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, hauti yathā taṃ viddasunau|

478. ‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadukkhañ‍caiva āyatiñ‍ca dukkhavipākaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau sahāpi dukkhaina sahāpi daumanassaina pāṇātipātī hauti, pāṇātipātapac‍cayā ca dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi dukkhaina sahāpi daumanassaina adin‍nādāyī hauti, adin‍nādānapac‍cayā ca dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi dukkhaina sahāpi daumanassaina kāmaisu micchācārī hauti, kāmaisu micchācārapac‍cayā ca dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi dukkhaina sahāpi daumanassaina musāvādī hauti, musāvādapac‍cayā ca dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi dukkhaina sahāpi daumanassaina pisuṇavācau hauti, pisuṇavācāpac‍cayā ca dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi dukkhaina sahāpi daumanassaina pharusavācau hauti, pharusavācāpac‍cayā ca dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi dukkhaina sahāpi daumanassaina samphappalāpī hauti, samphappalāpapac‍cayā ca dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi  dukkhaina sahāpi daumanassaina abhijjhālu  hauti, abhijjhāpac‍cayā ca dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi dukkhaina sahāpi daumanassaina byāpan‍nacittau hauti, byāpādapac‍cayā ca dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi dukkhaina sahāpi daumanassaina micchādiṭṭhi hauti, micchādiṭṭhipac‍cayā ca dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| sau  kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadukkhañ‍caiva āyatiñ‍ca dukkhavipākaṃ|

479. ‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhaṃ āyatiṃ dukkhavipākaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau sahāpi sukhaina sahāpi saumanassaina pāṇātipātī hauti, pāṇātipātapac‍cayā ca sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi sukhaina sahāpi saumanassaina adin‍nādāyī hauti, adin‍nādānapac‍cayā ca sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi sukhaina sahāpi saumanassaina kāmaisumicchācārī hauti, kāmaisumicchācārapac‍cayā ca sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi sukhaina sahāpi saumanassaina musāvādī hauti, musāvādapac‍cayā ca sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi sukhaina sahāpi saumanassaina pisuṇavācau hauti, pisuṇavācāpac‍cayā ca sukhaṃ  saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi sukhaina sahāpi saumanassaina pharusavācau hauti, pharusavācāpac‍cayā ca sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi sukhaina sahāpi saumanassaina samphappalāpī hauti, samphappalāpapac‍cayā ca sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi  sukhaina sahāpi saumanassaina abhijjhālu hauti, abhijjhāpac‍cayā ca sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi sukhaina sahāpi saumanassaina byāpan‍nacittau hauti, byāpādapac‍cayā ca sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi sukhaina sahāpi saumanassaina micchādiṭṭhi hauti, micchādiṭṭhipac‍cayā ca sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhaṃ āyatiṃ dukkhavipākaṃ|

480. ‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadukkhaṃ āyatiṃ sukhavipākaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau sahāpi dukkhaina sahāpi daumanassaina pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, pāṇātipātā vairamaṇīpac‍cayā ca dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi dukkhaina sahāpi daumanassaina adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā vairamaṇīpac‍cayā ca dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi dukkhaina sahāpi daumanassaina kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇīpac‍cayā ca dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi dukkhaina sahāpi daumanassaina musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, musāvādā vairamaṇīpac‍cayā ca dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi dukkhaina sahāpi daumanassaina pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, pisuṇāya vācāya vairamaṇīpac‍cayā ca dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi dukkhaina sahāpi daumanassaina pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, pharusāya vācāya vairamaṇīpac‍cayā ca dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi dukkhaina sahāpi daumanassaina samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti, samphappalāpā vairamaṇīpac‍cayā ca dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi dukkhaina sahāpi daumanassaina anabhijjhālu hauti, anabhijjhāpac‍cayā ca dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi dukkhaina sahāpi daumanassaina abyāpan‍nacittau hauti, abyāpādapac‍cayā ca dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi dukkhaina  sahāpi daumanassaina sammādiṭṭhi hauti, sammādiṭṭhipac‍cayā ca dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadukkhaṃ āyatiṃ sukhavipākaṃ|

481. ‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhañ‍caiva āyatiñ‍ca sukhavipākaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau sahāpi sukhaina sahāpi saumanassaina pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau hauti, pāṇātipātā vairamaṇīpac‍cayā ca sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi sukhaina sahāpi saumanassaina adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau hauti, adin‍nādānā vairamaṇīpac‍cayā ca sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi sukhaina sahāpi saumanassaina kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau hauti, kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇīpac‍cayā ca sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi sukhaina  sahāpi saumanassaina musāvādā paṭiviratau hauti, musāvādā vairamaṇīpac‍cayā ca sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi sukhaina sahāpi saumanassaina pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, pisuṇāya vācāya  vairamaṇīpac‍cayā ca sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi sukhaina sahāpi saumanassaina pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau hauti, pharusāya vācāya vairamaṇīpac‍cayā ca sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi sukhaina sahāpi saumanassaina samphappalāpā paṭiviratau hauti, samphappalāpā vairamaṇīpac‍cayā ca sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi sukhaina sahāpi saumanassaina anabhijjhālu hauti, anabhijjhāpac‍cayā ca sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi sukhaina sahāpi saumanassaina abyāpan‍nacittau hauti, abyāpādapac‍cayā ca sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti; sahāpi sukhaina sahāpi saumanassaina sammādiṭṭhi hauti, sammādiṭṭhipac‍cayā ca sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| idaṃ, vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhañ‍caiva  āyatiñ‍ca sukhavipākaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri dhammasamādānāni|

482. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, tittakālābu visaina saṃsaṭṭhau| atha purisau āgacchaiyya jīvitukāmau amaritukāmau sukhakāmau dukkhappaṭikūlau| tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘ambhau purisa, ayaṃ tittakālābu visaina saṃsaṭṭhau, sacai ākaṅkhasi piva pipa (sī॰ pī॰)| tassa  tai pivatau pipatau (sī॰ pī॰) caiva nacchādaissati  vaṇṇainapi gandhainapi rasainapi, pivitvā pītvā (sī॰) ca pana maraṇaṃ vā nigacchasi maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkha’nti| sau taṃ appaṭisaṅkhāya pivaiyya, nappaṭinissaj‍jaiyya| tassa taṃ pivatau caiva nacchādaiyya vaṇṇainapi gandhainapi rasainapi, pivitvā ca pana maraṇaṃ vā nigacchaiyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ| tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ dhammasamādānaṃ vadāmi, yamidaṃ dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadukkhañ‍caiva āyatiñ‍ca dukkhavipākaṃ|

483. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, āpānīyakaṃsau vaṇṇasampan‍nau gandhasampan‍nau rasasampan‍nau| sau ca khau visaina saṃsaṭṭhau| atha purisau āgacchaiyya jīvitukāmau amaritukāmau sukhakāmau dukkhappaṭikūlau| tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘ambhau purisa, ayaṃ āpānīyakaṃsau vaṇṇasampan‍nau gandhasampan‍nau rasasampan‍nau| sau ca khau visaina saṃsaṭṭhau, sacai ākaṅkhasi piva| tassa tai pivatauhi pivataupi (ka॰) khau chādaissati vaṇṇainapi gandhainapi rasainapi, pivitvā ca pana maraṇaṃ vā nigacchasi maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkha’nti| sau taṃ appaṭisaṅkhāya  pivaiyya, nappaṭinissaj‍jaiyya| tassa taṃ pivatauhi khau chādaiyya vaṇṇainapi gandhainapi rasainapi, pivitvā ca pana maraṇaṃ vā nigacchaiyya maraṇamattaṃ vā dukkhaṃ| tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ dhammasamādānaṃ vadāmi, yamidaṃ dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhaṃ āyatiṃ dukkhavipākaṃ|

484. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, pūtimuttaṃ nānābhaisaj‍jaihi saṃsaṭṭhaṃ| atha  purisau āgacchaiyya paṇḍukaraugī| tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘ambhau purisa, idaṃ pūtimuttaṃ nānābhaisaj‍jaihi saṃsaṭṭhaṃ, sacai ākaṅkhasi piva| tassa tai pivatauhi khau nacchādaissati vaṇṇainapi gandhainapi rasainapi, pivitvā ca pana sukhī bhavissasī’ti| sau taṃ paṭisaṅkhāya pivaiyya, nappaṭinissaj‍jaiyya| tassa taṃ pivatauhi khau nacchādaiyya vaṇṇainapi gandhainapi rasainapi, pivitvā  ca pana sukhī assa| tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ dhammasamādānaṃ vadāmi, yamidaṃ dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadukkhaṃ āyatiṃ sukhavipākaṃ|

485. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, dadhi ca madhu ca sappi ca phāṇitañ‍ca aikajjhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhaṃ| atha purisau āgacchaiyya lauhitapakkhandikau| tamainaṃ aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ – ‘ambhau purisa, idaṃ  dadhiṃ ca madhuṃ ca sappiṃ ca phāṇitañ‍ca aikajjhaṃ saṃsaṭṭhaṃ, sacai ākaṅkhasi piva| tassa tai pivatau caiva chādaissati vaṇṇainapi gandhainapi rasainapi, pivitvā ca pana sukhī bhavissasī’ti| sau taṃ paṭisaṅkhāya pivaiyya, nappaṭinissaj‍jaiyya| tassa taṃ pivatau caiva chādaiyya vaṇṇainapi gandhainapi rasainapi, pivitvā ca pana sukhī assa| tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ dhammasamādānaṃ vadāmi, yamidaṃ dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhañ‍caiva āyatiñ‍ca sukhavipākaṃ|

486. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, vassānaṃ pacchimai māsai saradasamayai viddhai vigatavalāhakai daivai ādic‍cau nabhaṃ abbhussak‍kamānau sabbaṃ ākāsagataṃ tamagataṃ abhivihac‍ca bhāsatai ca tapatai ca viraucatai ca; aivamaiva  khau, bhikkhavai, yamidaṃ dhammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhañ‍caiva āyatiñ‍ca sukhavipākaṃ tadañ‍ñai puthusamaṇabrāhmaṇaparappavādai abhivihac‍ca bhāsatai ca tapatai ca viraucatai cā’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

mahādhammasamādānasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. vīmaṃsakasuttaṃ

487. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘vīmaṃsakaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā parassa caitaupariyāyaṃ ajānantaina ājānantaina (pī॰ ka॰), ajānantaina kinti (?) tathāgatai saman‍naisanā kātabbā ‘sammāsambuddhau vā nau vā’ iti viñ‍ñāṇāyā’’ti| ‘‘bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā, bhagavaṃnaittikā bhagavaṃpaṭisaraṇā; sādhu vata, bhantai, bhagavantaṃyaiva paṭibhātu aitassa bhāsitassa atthau; bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha, bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

488. ‘‘vīmaṃsakaina, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā parassa caitaupariyāyaṃ ajānantaina dvīsu dhammaisu tathāgatau saman‍naisitabbau cakkhusautaviñ‍ñaiyyaisu dhammaisu – ‘yai saṃkiliṭṭhā cakkhusautaviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā, saṃvij‍janti vā tai tathāgatassa nau vā’ti? tamainaṃ saman‍naisamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘yai saṃkiliṭṭhā cakkhusautaviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā, na tai tathāgatassa saṃvij‍jantī’ti|

‘‘yatau naṃ saman‍naisamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘yai saṃkiliṭṭhā cakkhusautaviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā, na tai tathāgatassa saṃvij‍jantī’ti, tatau naṃ uttariṃ saman‍naisati – ‘yai vītimissā cakkhusautaviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā, saṃvij‍janti vā tai tathāgatassa nau  vā’ti? tamainaṃ saman‍naisamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘yai vītimissā cakkhusautaviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā, na tai tathāgatassa saṃvij‍jantī’ti|

‘‘yatau naṃ saman‍naisamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘yai vītimissā cakkhusautaviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā, na tai tathāgatassa saṃvij‍jantī’ti, tatau naṃ uttariṃ saman‍naisati – ‘yai vaudātā cakkhusautaviñ‍ñaiyyā  dhammā, saṃvij‍janti vā tai tathāgatassa nau vā’ti? tamainaṃ saman‍naisamānau aivaṃ  jānāti – ‘yai vaudātā cakkhusautaviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā, saṃvij‍janti tai tathāgatassā’ti|

‘‘yatau naṃ saman‍naisamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘yai vaudātā cakkhusautaviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā, saṃvij‍janti tai tathāgatassā’ti, tatau naṃ uttariṃ saman‍naisati – ‘dīgharattaṃ samāpan‍nau ayamāyasmā imaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ, udāhu ittarasamāpan‍nau’ti? tamainaṃ saman‍naisamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘dīgharattaṃ samāpan‍nau ayamāyasmā imaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ, nāyamāyasmā ittarasamāpan‍nau’ti|

‘‘yatau naṃ saman‍naisamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘dīgharattaṃ samāpan‍nau ayamāyasmā imaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ, nāyamāyasmā ittarasamāpan‍nau’ti, tatau naṃ uttariṃ saman‍naisati – ‘ñattajjhāpan‍nau ayamāyasmā bhikkhu yasappattau, saṃvij‍jantassa idhaikac‍cai ādīnavā’ti? na tāva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau idhaikac‍cai ādīnavā saṃvij‍janti yāva na ñattajjhāpan‍nau hauti yasappattau| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ñattajjhāpan‍nau hauti yasappattau, athassa idhaikac‍cai ādīnavā saṃvij‍janti| tamainaṃ saman‍naisamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘ñattajjhāpan‍nau ayamāyasmā bhikkhu yasappattau, nāssa idhaikac‍cai ādīnavā saṃvij‍jantī’ti|

‘‘yatau naṃ saman‍naisamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘ñattajjhāpan‍nau ayamāyasmā  bhikkhu yasappattau, nāssa idhaikac‍cai ādīnavā saṃvij‍jantī’ti, tatau naṃ uttariṃ saman‍naisati – ‘abhayūparatau ayamāyasmā, nāyamāyasmā bhayūparatau; vītarāgattā kāmai na saivati khayā rāgassā’ti? tamainaṃ saman‍naisamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘abhayūparatau ayamāyasmā, nāyamāyasmā bhayūparatau; vītarāgattā kāmai na saivati khayā rāgassā’ti| tañ‍cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuṃ parai aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘kai panāyasmatau ākārā, kai anvayā, yaināyasmā aivaṃ vadaisi – abhayūparatau ayamāyasmā, nāyamāyasmā bhayūparatau; vītarāgattā kāmai na saivati khayā rāgassā’ti| sammā byākaramānau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘tathā hi pana ayamāyasmā saṅghai vā viharantau aikau vā viharantau, yai ca tattha sugatā yai ca tattha duggatā, yai ca tattha gaṇamanusāsanti, yai ca idhaikac‍cai āmisaisu saṃdissanti, yai ca idhaikac‍cai āmisaina anupalittā, nāyamāyasmā taṃ taina avajānāti| sammukhā khau pana maitaṃ bhagavatau  sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – abhayūparatauhamasmi, nāhamasmi bhayūparatau, vītarāgattā kāmai na saivāmi khayā rāgassā’ti|

489. ‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, tathāgatauva uttariṃ paṭipucchitabbau – ‘yai saṃkiliṭṭhā cakkhusautaviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā, saṃvij‍janti vā tai tathāgatassa nau vā’ti? byākaramānau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau aivaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘yai saṃkiliṭṭhā cakkhusautaviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā, na tai tathāgatassa saṃvij‍jantī’’’ti|

‘‘yai vītimissā cakkhusautaviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā, saṃvij‍janti vā tai tathāgatassa nau vāti? byākaramānau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau aivaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘yai vītimissā cakkhusautaviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā, na tai tathāgatassa saṃvij‍jantī’ti|

‘‘yai vaudātā cakkhusautaviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā, saṃvij‍janti vā tai tathāgatassa nau vāti? byākaramānau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau aivaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘yai vaudātā cakkhusautaviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā, saṃvij‍janti tai tathāgatassa; aitaṃpathauhamasmi, aitaṃgaucarau aitapathauhamasmi aitagaucarau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), nau ca taina tammayau’ti|

‘‘aivaṃvādiṃ khau, bhikkhavai, satthāraṃ arahati sāvakau upasaṅkamituṃ dhammassavanāya| tassa satthā dhammaṃ daisaiti uttaruttariṃ paṇītapaṇītaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kasappaṭibhāgaṃ| yathā yathā khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau satthā dhammaṃ daisaiti uttaruttariṃ paṇītapaṇītaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kasappaṭibhāgaṃ tathā tathā sau tasmiṃ dhammai abhiñ‍ñāya idhaikac‍caṃ dhammaṃ dhammaisu  niṭṭhaṃ gacchati, satthari pasīdati – ‘sammāsambuddhau bhagavā, svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau, suppaṭipan‍nau saṅghau’ti| tañ‍cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuṃ parai aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘kai panāyasmatau ākārā, kai anvayā, yaināyasmā aivaṃ vadaisi – sammāsambuddhau bhagavā, svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau, suppaṭipan‍nau saṅghau’ti? sammā byākaramānau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu aivaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃ dhammassavanāya| tassa mai bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaiti uttaruttariṃ paṇītapaṇītaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kasappaṭibhāgaṃ| yathā yathā mai, āvusau, bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaiti uttaruttariṃ paṇītapaṇītaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kasappaṭibhāgaṃ tathā tathāhaṃ tasmiṃ dhammai abhiñ‍ñāya idhaikac‍caṃ dhammaṃ dhammaisu niṭṭhamagamaṃ, satthari pasīdiṃ – sammāsambuddhau bhagavā, svākkhātau bhagavatā, dhammau, suppaṭipan‍nau saṅghau’ti|

490. ‘‘yassa  kassaci, bhikkhavai, imaihi ākāraihi imaihi padaihi imaihi byañ‍janaihi tathāgatai saddhā niviṭṭhā hauti mūlajātā patiṭṭhitā, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ākāravatī saddhā dassanamūlikā, daḷhā; asaṃhāriyā samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmiṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatai dhammasaman‍naisanā hauti| aivañ‍ca pana tathāgatau dhammatāsusaman‍niṭṭhau hautī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

vīmaṃsakasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ sattamaṃ|

8. kausambiyasuttaṃ

491. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina kausambiyaṃ bhikkhū bhaṇḍanajātā kalahajātā vivādāpan‍nā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ mukhasattīhi vitudantā viharanti| tai na caiva añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sañ‍ñāpainti na ca sañ‍ñattiṃ upainti, na ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nijjhāpainti, na ca nijjhattiṃ upainti| atha khau añ‍ñatarau  bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idha, bhantai, kausambiyaṃ bhikkhū bhaṇḍanajātā kalahajātā vivādāpan‍nā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ mukhasattīhi vitudantā viharanti, tai na caiva añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sañ‍ñāpainti, na ca sañ‍ñattiṃ upainti, na ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nijjhāpainti, na ca nijjhattiṃ upaintī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mama vacanaina tai bhikkhū āmantaihi – ‘satthā vau āyasmantai āmantaitī’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau paṭissutvā yaina tai bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā tai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘satthā āyasmantai āmantaitī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū tassa bhikkhunau paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nai khau tai bhikkhū bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tumhai, bhikkhavai, bhaṇḍanajātā kalahajātā vivādāpan‍nā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ mukhasattīhi vitudantā viharatha, tai na caiva añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sañ‍ñāpaitha, na ca sañ‍ñattiṃ upaitha, na ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nijjhāpaitha, na ca nijjhattiṃ upaithā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, yasmiṃ tumhai samayai bhaṇḍanajātā kalahajātā vivādāpan‍nā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ  mukhasattīhi vitudantā viharatha, api nu tumhākaṃ tasmiṃ samayai maittaṃ kāyakammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sabrahmacārīsu āvi caiva rahau ca, maittaṃ vacīkammaṃ…pai॰… maittaṃ manaukammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sabrahmacārīsu āvi caiva rahau cā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘iti kira, bhikkhavai, yasmiṃ tumhai samayai bhaṇḍanajātā kalahajātā vivādāpan‍nā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ mukhasattīhi vitudantā viharatha, naiva tumhākaṃ  tasmiṃ samayai maittaṃ kāyakammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sabrahmacārīsu āvi caiva rahau ca, na maittaṃ vacīkammaṃ…pai॰… na maittaṃ manaukammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sabrahmacārīsu āvi caiva rahau ca| atha kiñ‍carahi tumhai, maughapurisā, kiṃ jānantā kiṃ passantā bhaṇḍanajātā kalahajātā vivādāpan‍nā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ mukhasattīhi vitudantā viharatha, tai na caiva añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ sañ‍ñāpaitha, na ca sañ‍ñattiṃ upaitha, na ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nijjhāpaitha, na ca nijjhattiṃ upaitha? tañhi tumhākaṃ, maughapurisā, bhavissati dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāyā’’ti|

492. atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘chayimai, bhikkhavai, dhammā sāraṇīyā piyakaraṇā garukaraṇā saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattanti| katamai cha? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau maittaṃ kāyakammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sabrahmacārīsu āvi caiva rahau ca| ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau maittaṃ vacīkammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sabrahmacārīsu āvi caiva rahau ca| ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau  garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikibhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau maittaṃ manaukammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sabrahmacārīsu āvi caiva rahau ca| ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yai tai lābhā dhammikā dhammaladdhā antamasau pattapariyāpan‍namattampi, tathārūpaihi lābhaihi appaṭivibhattabhaugī hauti sīlavantaihi sabrahmacārīhi sādhāraṇabhaugī| ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yāni tāni sīlāni akhaṇḍāni acchiddāni asabalāni akammāsāni bhujissāni viñ‍ñuppasatthāni  aparāmaṭṭhāni samādhisaṃvattanikāni tathārūpaisu sīlaisu sīlasāmañ‍ñagatau viharati sabrahmacārīhi āvi caiva rahau ca| ayampi dhammau  sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yāyaṃ diṭṭhi ariyā niyyānikā niyyāti tak‍karassa sammā dukkhakkhayāya tathārūpāya diṭṭhiyā diṭṭhisāmañ‍ñagatau viharati sabrahmacārīhi āvi caiva rahau ca| ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

‘‘imai khau, bhikkhavai, cha sāraṇīyā dhammā piyakaraṇā garukaraṇā saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattanti| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, chan‍naṃ sāraṇīyānaṃ dhammānaṃ aitaṃ aggaṃ aitaṃ saṅgāhikaṃ saṅgāhakaṃ (?) aitaṃ saṅghāṭanikaṃ – yadidaṃ yāyaṃ diṭṭhi ariyā niyyānikā niyyāti tak‍karassa sammā dukkhakkhayāya| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kūṭāgārassa aitaṃ aggaṃ aitaṃ saṅgāhikaṃ aitaṃ saṅghāṭanikaṃ yadidaṃ kūṭaṃ; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ chan‍naṃ sāraṇīyānaṃ dhammānaṃ aitaṃ aggaṃ aitaṃ saṅgāhikaṃ aitaṃ saṅghāṭanikaṃ  yadidaṃ yāyaṃ diṭṭhi ariyā niyyānikā niyyāti tak‍karassa sammā dukkhakkhayāya|

493. ‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, yāyaṃ diṭṭhi ariyā niyyānikā niyyāti tak‍karassa sammā dukkhakkhayāya? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘atthi nu  khau mai taṃ pariyuṭṭhānaṃ ajjhattaṃ appahīnaṃ, yaināhaṃ pariyuṭṭhānaina pariyuṭṭhitacittau yathābhūtaṃ nappajānaiyyaṃ na passaiyya’nti? sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhitau hauti, pariyuṭṭhitacittauva hauti| sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu byāpādapariyuṭṭhitau hauti, pariyuṭṭhitacittauva hauti| sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu thīnamiddhapariyuṭṭhitau hauti, pariyuṭṭhitacittauva hauti| sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍capariyuṭṭhitau hauti, pariyuṭṭhitacittauva hauti| sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhitau hauti, pariyuṭṭhitacittauva hauti| sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu idhalaukacintāya pasutau hauti, pariyuṭṭhitacittauva hauti| sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu paralaukacintāya pasutau hauti, pariyuṭṭhitacittauva hauti| sacai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu bhaṇḍanajātau kalahajātau vivādāpan‍nau añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ mukhasattīhi vitudantau viharati, pariyuṭṭhitacittauva hauti| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘natthi khau mai taṃ pariyuṭṭhānaṃ ajjhattaṃ appahīnaṃ, yaināhaṃ pariyuṭṭhānaina pariyuṭṭhitacittau yathābhūtaṃ nappajānaiyyaṃ na passaiyyaṃ| suppaṇihitaṃ mai mānasaṃ sac‍cānaṃ baudhāyā’ti| idamassa paṭhamaṃ ñāṇaṃ adhigataṃ hauti ariyaṃ laukuttaraṃ asādhāraṇaṃ puthuj‍janaihi|

494. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘imaṃ nu khau ahaṃ diṭṭhiṃ āsaivantau bhāvaintau bahulīkarauntau labhāmi pac‍cattaṃ samathaṃ, labhāmi pac‍cattaṃ nibbuti’nti? sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘imaṃ khau ahaṃ diṭṭhiṃ āsaivantau bhāvaintau bahulīkarauntau labhāmi pac‍cattaṃ  samathaṃ, labhāmi pac‍cattaṃ nibbuti’nti| idamassa dutiyaṃ ñāṇaṃ adhigataṃ hauti ariyaṃ laukuttaraṃ asādhāraṇaṃ puthuj‍janaihi|

495. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yathā rūpāyāhaṃ diṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau, atthi nu khau itau bahiddhā añ‍ñau samaṇau  vā brāhmaṇau vā  tathārūpāya diṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau’ti? sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘yathārūpāyāhaṃ diṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau, natthi itau bahiddhā añ‍ñau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā tathārūpāya diṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau’ti| idamassa tatiyaṃ ñāṇaṃ adhigataṃ hauti ariyaṃ laukuttaraṃ asādhāraṇaṃ puthuj‍janaihi|

496. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yathārūpāya dhammatāya diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau saman‍nāgatau, ahampi tathārūpāya dhammatāya saman‍nāgatau’ti| kathaṃrūpāya ca, bhikkhavai, dhammatāya diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau saman‍nāgatau? dhammatā aisā, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhisampan‍nassa puggalassa – ‘kiñ‍cāpi tathārūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati, yathārūpāya āpattiyā vuṭṭhānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, atha khau naṃ khippamaiva satthari vā viñ‍ñūsu vā sabrahmacārīsu daisaiti vivarati uttānīkarauti; daisaitvā vivaritvā uttānīkatvā āyatiṃ saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati’| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, daharau kumārau mandau uttānasaiyyakau hatthaina vā pādaina vā aṅgāraṃ ak‍kamitvā khippamaiva paṭisaṃharati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, dhammatā aisā diṭṭhisampan‍nassa puggalassa – ‘kiñ‍cāpi tathārūpiṃ  āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati yathārūpāya āpattiyā vuṭṭhānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, atha khau naṃ khippamaiva satthari vā viñ‍ñūsu vā sabrahmacārīsu daisaiti vivarati  uttānīkarauti; daisaitvā vivaritvā uttānīkatvā āyatiṃ saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati’| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘yathārūpāya dhammatāya diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau saman‍nāgatau, ahampi tathārūpāya dhammatāya saman‍nāgatau’ti| idamassa catutthaṃ ñāṇaṃ adhigataṃ hauti ariyaṃ laukuttaraṃ asādhāraṇaṃ puthuj‍janaihi|

497. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yathārūpāya dhammatāya diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau saman‍nāgatau, ahampi tathārūpāya dhammatāya saman‍nāgatau’ti| kathaṃrūpāya ca, bhikkhavai, dhammatāya diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau saman‍nāgatau? dhammatā aisā, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhisampan‍nassa puggalassa – ‘kiñ‍cāpi yāni tāni sabrahmacārīnaṃ uc‍cāvacāni kiṃkaraṇīyāni tattha ussuk‍kaṃ āpan‍nau hauti, atha khvāssa tibbāpaikkhā hauti adhisīlasikkhāya adhicittasikkhāya adhipañ‍ñāsikkhāya’| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gāvī taruṇavacchā thambañ‍ca ālumpati vacchakañ‍ca apacinati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, dhammatā aisā diṭṭhisampan‍nassa puggalassa – ‘kiñ‍cāpi yāni tāni sabrahmacārīnaṃ uc‍cāvacāni kiṃkaraṇīyāni tattha ussuk‍kaṃ āpan‍nau hauti, atha khvāssa tibbāpaikkhā hauti adhisīlasikkhāya adhicittasikkhāya adhipañ‍ñāsikkhāya’| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘yathārūpāya dhammatāya diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau  saman‍nāgatau, ahampi tathārūpāya dhammatāya saman‍nāgatau’ti| idamassa pañ‍camaṃ ñāṇaṃ adhigataṃ hauti ariyaṃ laukuttaraṃ asādhāraṇaṃ puthuj‍janaihi|

498. ‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yathārūpāya balatāya diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau saman‍nāgatau, ahampi tathārūpāya balatāya saman‍nāgatau’ti| kathaṃrūpāya ca, bhikkhavai, balatāya diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau saman‍nāgatau? balatā aisā, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhisampan‍nassa puggalassa yaṃ tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai daisiyamānai aṭṭhiṃkatvā manasikatvā sabbacaitasā sabbacaitasau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), sabbaṃ caitasā (ka॰) saman‍nāharitvā auhitasautau dhammaṃ suṇāti| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘yathārūpāya balatāya diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau saman‍nāgatau, ahampi tathārūpāya balatāya saman‍nāgatau’ti| idamassa chaṭṭhaṃ ñāṇaṃ adhigataṃ hauti ariyaṃ laukuttaraṃ asādhāraṇaṃ puthuj‍janaihi|

499. ‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yathārūpāya balatāya  diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau saman‍nāgatau, ahampi tathārūpāya balatāya saman‍nāgatau’ti| kathaṃrūpāya ca, bhikkhavai, balatāya diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau saman‍nāgatau? balatā aisā, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhisampan‍nassa puggalassa yaṃ tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai daisiyamānai labhati atthavaidaṃ, labhati dhammavaidaṃ, labhati dhammūpasaṃhitaṃ pāmauj‍jaṃ| sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘yathārūpāya balatāya diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau  saman‍nāgatau, ahampi tathārūpāya balatāya saman‍nāgatau’ti| idamassa sattamaṃ ñāṇaṃ adhigataṃ hauti ariyaṃ laukuttaraṃ asādhāraṇaṃ puthuj‍janaihi|

500. ‘‘aivaṃ sattaṅgasaman‍nāgatassa khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa dhammatā susaman‍niṭṭhā hauti sautāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya| aivaṃ sattaṅgasaman‍nāgatau  khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau sautāpattiphalasaman‍nāgatau hautī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti|

kausambiyasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. brahmanimantanikasuttaṃ

501. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, samayaṃ uk‍kaṭṭhāyaṃ viharāmi subhagavanai sālarājamūlai| taina khau pana, bhikkhavai, samayaina bakassa brahmunau aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti – ‘idaṃ nic‍caṃ, idaṃ dhuvaṃ, idaṃ sassataṃ, idaṃ kaivalaṃ, idaṃ acavanadhammaṃ, idañhi na jāyati na jīyati na mīyati na cavati na upapaj‍jati, itau ca panañ‍ñaṃ uttari nissaraṇaṃ natthī’ti| atha khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, bakassa brahmunau caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya, pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya, aivamaiva – uk‍kaṭṭhāyaṃ subhagavanai sālarājamūlai antarahitau tasmiṃ brahmalaukai pāturahausiṃ| addasā khau maṃ, bhikkhavai, bakau brahmā dūratauva āgacchantaṃ; disvāna maṃ aitadavauca – ‘aihi khau, mārisa, svāgataṃ, mārisa! cirassaṃ khau, mārisa, imaṃ pariyāyamakāsi yadidaṃ idhāgamanāya| idañhi, mārisa, nic‍caṃ, idaṃ dhuvaṃ, idaṃ sassataṃ, idaṃ kaivalaṃ, idaṃ acavanadhammaṃ, idañhi na jāyati na jīyati na mīyati na cavati na upapaj‍jati| itau ca panañ‍ñaṃ uttari nissaraṇaṃ natthī’’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, bakaṃ brahmānaṃ aitadavaucaṃ  – ‘‘avij‍jāgatau vata, bhau, bakau brahmā; avij‍jāgatau vata, bhau, bakau brahmā; yatra hi nāma anic‍caṃyaiva samānaṃ nic‍canti vakkhati, addhuvaṃyaiva samānaṃ dhuvanti vakkhati, asassataṃyaiva samānaṃ sassatanti vakkhati, akaivalaṃyaiva samānaṃ  kaivalanti vakkhati, cavanadhammaṃyaiva samānaṃ acavanadhammanti vakkhati; yattha ca pana jāyati jīyati mīyati cavati upapaj‍jati tañ‍ca vakkhati – ‘idañhi na jāyati na jīyati na mīyati na cavati na upapaj‍jatī’ti; santañ‍ca panañ‍ñaṃ uttari nissaraṇaṃ ‘natthañ‍ñaṃ uttari nissaraṇa’nti vakkhatī’’ti|

502. ‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, mārau pāpimā añ‍ñataraṃ brahmapārisaj‍jaṃ anvāvisitvā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘bhikkhu, bhikkhu, maitamāsadau maitamāsadau, aisau hi, bhikkhu, brahmā mahābrahmā  abhibhū anabhibhūtau añ‍ñadatthudasau vasavattī issarau kattā nimmātā saiṭṭhau sajitā saj‍jitā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰), sañ‍jitā (sī॰ pī॰) vasī pitā bhūtabhabyānaṃ| ahaisuṃ khau yai, bhikkhu, tayā pubbai samaṇabrāhmaṇā laukasmiṃ pathavīgarahakā pathavījigucchakā, āpagarahakā āpajigucchakā, taijagarahakā taijajigucchakā, vāyagarahakā vāyajigucchakā, bhūtagarahakā bhūtajigucchakā, daivagarahakā daivajigucchakā, pajāpatigarahakā pajāpatijigucchakā, brahmagarahakā brahmajigucchakā – tai kāyassa bhaidā pāṇupacchaidā hīnai kāyai patiṭṭhitā ahaisuṃ| yai pana, bhikkhu, tayā pubbai samaṇabrāhmaṇā laukasmiṃ pathavīpasaṃsakā pathavābhinandinau, āpapasaṃsakā āpābhinandinau, taijapasaṃsakā taijābhinandinau, vāyapasaṃsakā  vāyābhinandinau, bhūtapasaṃsakā bhūtābhinandinau, daivapasaṃsakā daivābhinandinau, pajāpatipasaṃsakā pajāpatābhinandinau, brahmapasaṃsakā brahmābhinandinau – tai kāyassa bhaidā pāṇupacchaidā paṇītai kāyai patiṭṭhitā| taṃ tāhaṃ, bhikkhu, aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘iṅgha tvaṃ, mārisa, yadaiva tai brahmā āha tadaiva tvaṃ karauhi, mā tvaṃ brahmunau vacanaṃ upātivattitthau’| sacai khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, brahmunau vacanaṃ upātivattissasi, saiyyathāpi nāma purisau siriṃ āgacchantiṃ daṇḍaina paṭippaṇāmaiyya, saiyyathāpi vā pana, bhikkhu, purisau narakappapātai papatantau hatthaihi ca pādaihi ca pathaviṃ virādhaiyya, aivaṃ sampadamidaṃ, bhikkhu, tuyhaṃ bhavissati| ‘iṅghaṃ tvaṃ, mārisa, yadaiva tai brahmā āha tadaiva tvaṃ karauhi, mā tvaṃ brahmunau vacanaṃ upātivattitthau| nanu tvaṃ, bhikkhu, passasi brahmaparisaṃ san‍nipatita’nti? iti khau maṃ, bhikkhavai, mārau pāpimā brahmaparisaṃ upanaisi|

‘‘aivaṃ  vuttai, ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, māraṃ pāpimantaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘jānāmi khau tāhaṃ, pāpima; mā tvaṃ mañ‍ñitthau – na maṃ jānātī’ti| mārau tvamasi, pāpima| yau caiva, pāpima, brahmā, yā ca brahmaparisā, yai ca brahmapārisaj‍jā, sabbaiva tava hatthagatā sabbaiva tava vasaṃgatā| tuyhañhi, pāpima, aivaṃ hauti – ‘aisaupi mai assa hatthagatau, aisaupi mai assa vasaṃgatau’ti| ahaṃ khau pana, pāpima, naiva tava hatthagatau naiva tava vasaṃgatau’’ti|

503. ‘‘aivaṃ  vuttai, bhikkhavai, bakau brahmā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘ahañhi, mārisa, nic‍caṃyaiva  samānaṃ nic‍canti vadāmi, dhuvaṃyaiva  samānaṃ dhuvanti vadāmi, sassataṃyaiva samānaṃ sassatanti vadāmi, kaivalaṃyaiva samānaṃ kaivalanti vadāmi, acavanadhammaṃyaiva samānaṃ acavanadhamma’nti vadāmi, yattha ca pana na jāyati na jīyati na mīyati na cavati na upapaj‍jati tadaivāhaṃ vadāmi – ‘idañhi na jāyati na jīyati na mīyati na cavati na upapaj‍jatī’ti| asantañ‍ca panañ‍ñaṃ uttari nissaraṇaṃ ‘natthañ‍ñaṃ uttari nissaraṇa’nti vadāmi| ahaisuṃ khau, bhikkhu, tayā pubbai samaṇabrāhmaṇā laukasmiṃ yāvatakaṃ tuyhaṃ kasiṇaṃ āyu tāvatakaṃ taisaṃ tapaukammamaiva ahausi| tai khau aivaṃ jānaiyyuṃ – ‘santañ‍ca panañ‍ñaṃ uttari nissaraṇaṃ atthañ‍ñaṃ uttari nissaraṇanti, asantaṃ vā añ‍ñaṃ uttari nissaraṇaṃ natthañ‍ñaṃ uttari nissaraṇa’nti| taṃ tāhaṃ, bhikkhu, aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘na caivañ‍ñaṃ uttari nissaraṇaṃ dakkhissasi, yāvadaiva ca pana kilamathassa vighātassa bhāgī bhavissasi| sacai khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, pathaviṃ ajjhausissasi, aupasāyikau mai bhavissasi vatthusāyikau, yathākāmakaraṇīyau bāhitaiyyau| sacai āpaṃ… taijaṃ… vāyaṃ… bhūtai… daivai… pajāpatiṃ… brahmaṃ ajjhausissasi, aupasāyikau mai bhavissasi vatthusāyikau, yathākāmakaraṇīyau bāhitaiyyau’ti|

‘‘ahampi khau aivaṃ, brahmai, jānāmi – sacai pathaviṃ ajjhausissāmi, aupasāyikau tai bhavissāmi vatthusāyikau, yathākāmakaraṇīyau  bāhitaiyyau| ‘sacai āpaṃ… taijaṃ… vāyaṃ… bhūtai… daivai… pajāpatiṃ… brahmaṃ ajjhausissāmi, aupasāyikau tai bhavissāmi vatthusāyikau, yathākāmakaraṇīyau bāhitaiyyau’ti api ca tai ahaṃ, brahmai, gatiñ‍ca pajānāmi, jutiñ‍ca pajānāmi – aivaṃ mahiddhikau bakau brahmā, aivaṃ mahānubhāvau bakau brahmā, aivaṃ mahaisakkhau bakau brahmā’’ti|

‘‘yathākathaṃ  pana mai tvaṃ, mārisa, gatiñ‍ca pajānāsi, jutiñ‍ca pajānāsi – ‘aivaṃ mahiddhikau bakau brahmā, aivaṃ mahānubhāvau bakau brahmā, aivaṃ mahaisakkhau bakau brahmā’ti?

‘‘yāvatā candimasūriyā, pariharanti disā bhanti viraucanā|

tāva sahassadhā laukau, aittha tai vattatai vattatī (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vasau||

‘‘parauparañ‍ca  parauvarañ‍ca (sī॰ pī॰) jānāsi, athau rāgavirāginaṃ|

itthabhāvañ‍ñathābhāvaṃ, sattānaṃ āgatiṃ gati’’nti||

‘‘aivaṃ khau tai ahaṃ, brahmai, gatiñ‍ca pajānāmi jutiñ‍ca pajānāmi – ‘aivaṃ mahiddhikau bakau brahmā, aivaṃ mahānubhāvau bakau  brahmā, aivaṃ mahaisakkhau bakau brahmā’ti|
