504. ‘‘atthi khau, brahmai, añ‍ñau kāyau, taṃ tvaṃ na jānāsi na passasi; tamahaṃ jānāmi passāmi| atthi khau, brahmai, ābhassarā nāma kāyau yatau tvaṃ cutau idhūpapan‍nau| tassa tai aticiranivāsaina sā sati pamuṭṭhā, taina taṃ tvaṃ na jānāsi na passasi; tamahaṃ jānāmi passāmi| aivampi khau ahaṃ, brahmai, naiva tai samasamau abhiñ‍ñāya, kutau nīcaiyyaṃ? atha  khau ahamaiva tayā bhiyyau| atthi khau, brahmai, subhakiṇhau nāma kāyau, vaihapphalau nāma kāyau, abhibhū nāma kāyau, taṃ tvaṃ na jānāsi na passasi; tamahaṃ jānāmi passāmi| aivampi khau ahaṃ, brahmai, naiva tai samasamau abhiñ‍ñāya, kutau nīcaiyyaṃ? atha khau ahamaiva tayā bhiyyau| pathaviṃ khau ahaṃ, brahmai, pathavitau abhiñ‍ñāya yāvatā pathaviyā pathavattaina ananubhūtaṃ tadabhiñ‍ñāya pathaviṃ nāpahausiṃ, pathaviyā nāpahausiṃ, pathavitau nāpahausiṃ, pathaviṃ maiti nāpahausiṃ, pathaviṃ nābhivadiṃ| aivampi khau ahaṃ, brahmai, naiva tai samasamau abhiñ‍ñāya, kutau nīcaiyyaṃ? atha khau ahamaiva tayā bhiyyau| āpaṃ khau ahaṃ, brahmai…pai॰… taijaṃ khau ahaṃ, brahmai…pai॰… vāyaṃ khau ahaṃ, brahmai…pai॰… bhūtai khau ahaṃ, brahmai…pai॰… daivai khau ahaṃ, brahmai…pai॰… pajāpatiṃ khau ahaṃ, brahmai…pai॰… brahmaṃ khau ahaṃ, brahmai…pai॰… ābhassarai khau ahaṃ, brahmai…pai॰… subhakiṇhai khau ahaṃ, brahmai…  …pai॰… vaihapphalai khau ahaṃ, brahmai…pai॰… abhibhuṃ khau ahaṃ, brahmai…pai॰… sabbaṃ khau ahaṃ, brahmai, sabbatau abhiñ‍ñāya yāvatā sabbassa sabbattaina ananubhūtaṃ tadabhiñ‍ñāya sabbaṃ nāpahausiṃ sabbasmiṃ nāpahausiṃ sabbatau nāpahausiṃ sabbaṃ maiti nāpahausiṃ, sabbaṃ nābhivadiṃ| aivampi khau ahaṃ, brahmai, naiva tai samasamau abhiñ‍ñāya, kutau nīcaiyyaṃ? atha khau ahamaiva tayā bhiyyau’’ti|

‘‘sacai  khau, mārisa, sabbassa sabbattaina ananubhūtaṃ, tadabhiñ‍ñāya mā haiva tai rittakamaiva ahausi, tucchakamaiva ahausī’’ti|

‘‘‘viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anidassanaṃ anantaṃ sabbatau pabhaṃ’, taṃ pathaviyā pathavattaina ananubhūtaṃ, āpassa āpattaina ananubhūtaṃ, taijassa taijattaina ananubhūtaṃ, vāyassa vāyattaina ananubhūtaṃ, bhūtānaṃ bhūtattaina ananubhūtaṃ, daivānaṃ daivattaina ananubhūtaṃ, pajāpatissa pajāpatittaina ananubhūtaṃ, brahmānaṃ brahmattaina ananubhūtaṃ, ābhassarānaṃ ābhassarattaina ananubhūtaṃ, subhakiṇhānaṃ subhakiṇhattaina ananubhūtaṃ, vaihapphalānaṃ vaihapphalattai  ananubhūtaṃ, abhibhussa abhibhuttaina ananubhūtaṃ, sabbassa sabbattaina ananubhūtaṃ’’|

‘‘handa carahi handa ca hi (sī॰ pī॰) tai, mārisa, passa antaradhāyāmī’’ti| ‘handa carahi mai tvaṃ, brahmai, antaradhāyassu, sacai visahasī’ti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, bakau brahmā ‘antaradhāyissāmi samaṇassa gautamassa, antaradhāyissāmi samaṇassa gautamassā’ti naivassu mai sak‍kauti antaradhāyituṃ|

‘‘aivaṃ vuttai, ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, bakaṃ brahmānaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘handa carahi tai brahmai antaradhāyāmī’ti| ‘handa carahi mai tvaṃ, mārisa, antaradhāyassu sacai visahasī’ti| atha khau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathārūpaṃ iddhābhisaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkhāsiṃ – ‘aittāvatā brahmā ca brahmaparisā ca brahmapārisaj‍jā ca saddañ‍ca mai saussanti saddamaiva suyyanti (ka॰), na ca maṃ dakkhantī’ti| antarahitau imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsiṃ –

‘‘bhavaivāhaṃ bhayaṃ disvā, bhavañ‍ca vibhavaisinaṃ|

bhavaṃ nābhivadiṃ kiñ‍ci, nandiñ‍ca na upādiyi’’nti||

‘‘atha  khau, bhikkhavai, brahmā ca brahmaparisā ca brahmapārisaj‍jā ca acchariyabbhutacittajātā ahaisuṃ – ‘acchariyaṃ vata bhau, abbhutaṃ vata bhau! samaṇassa gautamassa mahiddhikatā mahānubhāvatā, na ca vata nau itau pubbai diṭṭhau vā, sutau vā, añ‍ñau  samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā aivaṃ mahiddhikau aivaṃ mahānubhāvau yathāyaṃ samaṇau gautamau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau| bhavarāmāya vata, bhau, pajāya bhavaratāya bhavasammuditāya samūlaṃ bhavaṃ udabbahī’ti|

505. ‘‘atha khau, bhikkhavai, mārau pāpimā añ‍ñataraṃ brahmapārisaj‍jaṃ anvāvisitvā maṃ aitadavauca – ‘sacai khau tvaṃ, mārisa, aivaṃ pajānāsi, sacai tvaṃ aivaṃ anubuddhau, mā sāvakai upanaisi, mā pabbajitai; mā sāvakānaṃ dhammaṃ daisaisi, mā pabbajitānaṃ; mā sāvakaisu gaidhimakāsi, mā pabbajitaisu| ahaisuṃ khau, bhikkhu, tayā pubbai samaṇabrāhmaṇā laukasmiṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā paṭijānamānā| tai sāvakai upanaisuṃ pabbajitai, sāvakānaṃ dhammaṃ daisaisuṃ pabbajitānaṃ, sāvakaisu gaidhimakaṃsu pabbajitaisu, tai sāvakai upanaitvā pabbajitai, sāvakānaṃ dhammaṃ daisaitvā pabbajitānaṃ, sāvakaisu gaidhitacittā pabbajitaisu, kāyassa bhaidā pāṇupacchaidā hīnai kāyai patiṭṭhitā| ahaisuṃ yai pana, bhikkhu, tayā pubbai samaṇabrāhmaṇā laukasmiṃ arahantau sammāsambuddhā paṭijānamānā| tai na sāvakai upanaisuṃ na pabbajitai, na sāvakānaṃ dhammaṃ daisaisuṃ na pabbajitānaṃ, na sāvakaisu  gaidhimakaṃsu na pabbajitaisu, tai na sāvakai upanaitvā na pabbajitai, na sāvakānaṃ dhammaṃ daisaitvā na pabbajitānaṃ, na sāvakaisu gaidhitacittā na pabbajitaisu, kāyassa bhaidā pāṇupacchaidā paṇītai kāyai patiṭṭhitā| taṃ tāhaṃ, bhikkhu, aivaṃ vadāmi – iṅgha tvaṃ, mārisa, appaussuk‍kau diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāramanuyuttau viharassu, anakkhātaṃ kusalañhi, mārisa, mā paraṃ auvadāhī’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ vuttai, ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, māraṃ pāpimantaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘jānāmi khau tāhaṃ, pāpima, mā tvaṃ mañ‍ñitthau – na maṃ jānātī’ti| mārau tvamasi, pāpima| na maṃ tvaṃ, pāpima, hitānukampī aivaṃ vadaisi; ahitānukampī maṃ tvaṃ, pāpima, aivaṃ vadaisi| tuyhañhi, pāpima, aivaṃ hauti – ‘yaisaṃ samaṇau gautamau dhammaṃ daisaissati, tai mai visayaṃ upātivattissantī’ti| asammāsambuddhāva pana tai, pāpima, samānā sammāsambuddhāmhāti paṭijāniṃsu| ahaṃ khau pana, pāpima, sammāsambuddhauva samānau sammāsambuddhaumhīti paṭijānāmi| daisaintaupi hi, pāpima, tathāgatau sāvakānaṃ dhammaṃ tādisauva adaisaintaupi hi, pāpima, tathāgatau sāvakānaṃ dhammaṃ tādisauva| upanaintaupi hi, pāpima, tathāgatau sāvakai  tādisauva, anupanaintaupi hi, pāpima, tathāgatau sāvakai tādisauva| taṃ kissa haitu? tathāgatassa, pāpima, yai āsavā saṃkilaisikā paunaubbhavikā sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyā – tai pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ  anuppādadhammā| saiyyathāpi, pāpima, tālau matthakacchin‍nau abhabbau puna virūḷhiyā; aivamaiva khau, pāpima, tathāgatassa yai āsavā saṃkilaisikā paunaubbhavikā sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyā – tai pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammāti|

‘‘iti hidaṃ mārassa ca anālapanatāya brahmunau ca abhinimantanatāya, tasmā imassa vaiyyākaraṇassa brahmanimantanikaṃtaiva adhivacana’’nti|

brahmanimantanikasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ navamaṃ|

10. mārataj‍janīyasuttaṃ

506. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau bhaggaisu viharati susumāragirai bhaisakaḷāvanai migadāyai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau abbhaukāsai caṅkamati| taina khau pana samayaina mārau pāpimā āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa kucchigatau hauti kauṭṭhamanupaviṭṭhau| atha khau āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa aitadahausi – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau mai kucchi garugarau viya garu garu viya (sī॰ pī॰ ṭīkāyaṃ pāṭhantaraṃ)? māsācitaṃ mañ‍ñai’’ti| atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau caṅkamā aurauhitvā vihāraṃ pavisitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau pac‍cattaṃ yaunisau manasākāsi| addasā khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau māraṃ pāpimantaṃ kucchigataṃ kauṭṭhamanupaviṭṭhaṃ| disvāna  māraṃ pāpimantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘nikkhama, pāpima; nikkhama, pāpima! mā tathāgataṃ vihaisaisi, mā tathāgatasāvakaṃ| mā tai ahausi dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāyā’’ti| atha khau mārassa pāpimatau aitadahausi – ‘‘ajānamaiva khau maṃ ayaṃ samaṇau apassaṃ aivamāha – ‘nikkhama, pāpima; nikkhama, pāpima! mā tathāgataṃ vihaisaisi, mā tathāgatasāvakaṃ| mā tai ahausi dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāyā’ti| yaupissa sau satthā saupi maṃ naiva khippaṃ jānaiyya, kutau pana kutau ca pana (syā॰) maṃ ayaṃ sāvakau jānissatī’’ti? atha khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau  māraṃ pāpimantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aivampi khau tāhaṃ, pāpima, jānāmi, mā tvaṃ mañ‍ñitthau – ‘na maṃ jānātī’ti| mārau tvamasi, pāpima; tuyhañhi, pāpima, aivaṃ hauti – ‘ajānamaiva khau maṃ ayaṃ samaṇau apassaṃ aivamāha – nikkhama, pāpima; nikkhama, pāpima! mā tathāgataṃ vihaisaisi, mā tathāgatasāvakaṃ| mā tai ahausi dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāyāti| yaupissa sau satthā saupi maṃ naiva khippaṃ jānaiyya, kutau pana maṃ ayaṃ sāvakau jānissatī’’’ti?

atha khau mārassa pāpimatau aitadahausi – ‘‘jānamaiva khau maṃ ayaṃ samaṇau passaṃ aivamāha – ‘nikkhama, pāpima; nikkhama, pāpima! mā tathāgataṃ vihaisaisi, mā tathāgatasāvakaṃ| mā tai ahausi dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāyā’’’ti| atha khau mārau pāpimā āyasmatau  mahāmauggal‍lānassa mukhatau uggantvā pac‍caggaḷai aṭṭhāsi|

507. addasā  khau āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau māraṃ pāpimantaṃ pac‍caggaḷai ṭhitaṃ; disvāna māraṃ pāpimantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘aitthāpi khau tāhaṃ, pāpima, passāmi; mā tvaṃ mañ‍ñitthau ‘‘na maṃ passatī’’ti| aisau tvaṃ, pāpima, pac‍caggaḷai ṭhitau| bhūtapubbāhaṃ, pāpima, dūsī nāma mārau ahausiṃ, tassa mai kāḷī nāma bhaginī| tassā tvaṃ puttau| sau mai tvaṃ bhāginaiyyau ahausi| taina khau pana, pāpima, samayaina kakusandhau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau laukai uppan‍nau hauti| kakusandhassa khau pana, pāpima, bhagavatau arahatau  sammāsambuddhassa vidhurasañ‍jīvaṃ nāma sāvakayugaṃ ahausi aggaṃ bhaddayugaṃ| yāvatā khau pana, pāpima, kakusandhassa bhagavatau  arahatau sammāsambuddhassa sāvakā| taisu na ca kauci āyasmatā vidhuraina samasamau hauti yadidaṃ dhammadaisanāya| iminā khau aivaṃ aitaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), pāpima, pariyāyaina āyasmatau vidhurassa vidhurautaiva vidhurassa vidhurau vidhurautvaiva (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) samañ‍ñā udapādi|

‘‘āyasmā pana, pāpima, sañ‍jīvau arañ‍ñagataupi rukkhamūlagataupi suñ‍ñāgāragataupi appakasirainaiva sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jati| bhūtapubbaṃ, pāpima, āyasmā sañ‍jīvau añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpan‍nau nisin‍nau hauti| addasaṃsu khau, pāpima, gaupālakā pasupālakā kassakā pathāvinau āyasmantaṃ sañ‍jīvaṃ añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpan‍naṃ nisin‍naṃ; disvāna taisaṃ aitadahausi – ‘acchariyaṃ vata, bhau, abbhutaṃ vata, bhau! ayaṃ samaṇau nisin‍nakauva kālaṅkatau! handa naṃ dahāmā’ti| atha khau tai, pāpima, gaupālakā pasupālakā kassakā pathāvinau tiṇañ‍ca kaṭṭhañ‍ca gaumayañ‍ca saṃkaḍḍhitvā āyasmatau sañ‍jīvassa kāyai upacinitvā aggiṃ datvā pak‍kamiṃsu| atha khau, pāpima, āyasmā sañ‍jīvau tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina tāya samāpattiyā vuṭṭhahitvā cīvarāni papphauṭaitvā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| addasaṃsu khau tai, pāpima, gaupālakā pasupālakā kassakā pathāvinau āyasmantaṃ sañ‍jīvaṃ piṇḍāya carantaṃ; disvāna naisaṃ  aitadahausi – ‘acchariyaṃ vata, bhau, abbhutaṃ vata, bhau! ayaṃ samaṇau nisin‍nakauva kālaṅkatau, svāyaṃ paṭisañ‍jīvitau’ti| iminā khau aivaṃ, pāpima, pariyāyaina āyasmatau sañ‍jīvassa sañ‍jīvautaiva sañ‍jīvau sañ‍jīvautvaiva (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) samañ‍ñā udapādi|

508. ‘‘atha khau, pāpima, dūsissa mārassa aitadahausi – ‘imaisaṃ khau ahaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ sīlavantānaṃ kalyāṇadhammānaṃ naiva jānāmi āgatiṃ vā gatiṃ vā| yaṃnūnāhaṃ brāhmaṇagahapatikai anvāvisaiyyaṃ  – aitha, tumhai bhikkhū sīlavantai kalyāṇadhammai ak‍kausatha paribhāsatha rausaitha vihaisaitha| appaiva nāma tumhaihi ak‍kausiyamānānaṃ paribhāsiyamānānaṃ rausiyamānānaṃ vihaisiyamānānaṃ siyā cittassa añ‍ñathattaṃ, yathā taṃ dūsī  mārau labhaitha autāra’nti| atha khau tai, pāpima, dūsī mārau brāhmaṇagahapatikai anvāvisi – ‘aitha, tumhai bhikkhū sīlavantai kalyāṇadhammai ak‍kausatha paribhāsatha rausaitha vihaisaitha| appaiva nāma tumhaihi ak‍kausiyamānānaṃ paribhāsiyamānānaṃ rausiyamānānaṃ vihaisiyamānānaṃ siyā cittassa añ‍ñathattaṃ, yathā taṃ dūsī mārau labhaitha autāra’nti|

‘‘atha khau tai, pāpima, brāhmaṇagahapatikā anvāvisiṭṭhā dūsinā māraina bhikkhū sīlavantai kalyāṇadhammai ak‍kausanti paribhāsanti rausainti vihaisainti – ‘imai pana muṇḍakā samaṇakā ibbhā kiṇhā kaṇhā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) bandhupādāpac‍cā ‘‘jhāyinausmā jhāyinausmā’’ti pattakkhandhā adhaumukhā madhurakajātā jhāyanti pajjhāyanti nijjhāyanti apajjhāyanti| saiyyathāpi nāma ulūkau  rukkhasākhāyaṃ mūsikaṃ maggayamānau jhāyati pajjhāyati nijjhāyati apajjhāyati; aivamaivimai muṇḍakā samaṇakā ibbhā kiṇhā bandhupādāpac‍cā ‘‘jhāyinausmā jhāyinausmā’’ti pattakkhandhā adhaumukhā madhurakajātā jhāyanti pajjhāyanti nijjhāyanti apajjhāyanti| saiyyathāpi nāma kautthu nadītīrai macchai maggayamānau jhāyati pajjhāyati nijjhāyati apajjhāyati; aivamaivimai muṇḍakā samaṇakā ibbhā kiṇhā bandhupādāpac‍cā ‘‘jhāyinausmā jhāyinausmā’’ti pattakkhandhā adhaumukhā madhurakajātā jhāyanti pajjhāyanti nijjhāyanti apajjhāyanti| saiyyathāpi nāma biḷārau sandhisamalasaṅkaṭīrai mūsikaṃ maggayamānau jhāyati pajjhāyati nijjhāyati apajjhāyati; aivamaivimai muṇḍakā samaṇakā ibbhā kiṇhā bandhupādāpac‍cā ‘‘jhāyinausmā jhāyinausmā’’ti pattakkhandhā adhaumukhā madhurakajātā jhāyanti pajjhāyanti nijjhāyanti apajjhāyanti| saiyyathāpi nāma gadrabhau vahacchin‍nau sandhisamalasaṅkaṭīrai jhāyati pajjhāyati nijjhāyati apajjhāyati, aivamaivimai muṇḍakā samaṇakā ibbhā kiṇhā bandhupādāpac‍cā ‘‘jhāyinausmā jhāyinausmā’’ti pattakkhandhā adhaumukhā madhurakajātā jhāyanti pajjhāyanti nijjhāyanti apajjhāyantī’’ti|

‘‘yai khau pana, pāpima, taina samayaina manussā kālaṅkaraunti yaibhuyyaina kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍janti|

509. ‘‘atha  khau, pāpima, kakusandhau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau bhikkhū  āmantaisi – ‘anvāviṭṭhā khau, bhikkhavai, brāhmaṇagahapatikā dūsinā māraina – aitha, tumhai bhikkhū sīlavantai kalyāṇadhammai ak‍kausatha paribhāsatha rausaitha vihaisaitha, appaiva nāma tumhaihi ak‍kausiyamānānaṃ paribhāsiyamānānaṃ rausiyamānānaṃ vihaisiyamānānaṃ siyā cittassa añ‍ñathattaṃ, yathā taṃ dūsī mārau labhaitha autāra’nti| aitha, tumhai, bhikkhavai, maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharatha, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyābajjhaina pharitvā viharatha| karuṇāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… muditāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharatha, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyābajjhaina pharitvā viharathā’ti|

‘‘atha khau tai, pāpima, bhikkhū kakusandhaina bhagavatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina aivaṃ auvadiyamānā aivaṃ anusāsiyamānā arañ‍ñagatāpi rukkhamūlagatāpi suñ‍ñāgāragatāpi maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā vihariṃsu, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyābajjhaina pharitvā vihariṃsu| karuṇāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… muditāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā vihariṃsu, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyābajjhaina pharitvā vihariṃsu|

510. ‘‘atha khau, pāpima, dūsissa mārassa aitadahausi – ‘aivampi khau ahaṃ karauntau imaisaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ sīlavantānaṃ kalyāṇadhammānaṃ naiva jānāmi āgatiṃ vā gatiṃ vā, yaṃnūnāhaṃ brāhmaṇagahapatikai anvāvisaiyyaṃ – aitha, tumhai bhikkhū sīlavantai kalyāṇadhammai sak‍karautha garuṃ karautha mānaitha  pūjaitha, appaiva nāma tumhaihi sak‍kariyamānānaṃ garukariyamānānaṃ māniyamānānaṃ pūjiyamānānaṃ siyā cittassa añ‍ñathattaṃ, yathā taṃ dūsī mārau labhaitha autāra’nti| atha khau tai, pāpima, dūsī mārau brāhmaṇagahapatikai anvāvisi – ‘aitha, tumhai bhikkhū sīlavantai kalyāṇadhammai sak‍karautha garuṃ karautha mānaitha pūjaitha, appaiva nāma tumhaihi sak‍kariyamānānaṃ garukariyamānānaṃ māniyamānānaṃ pūjiyamānānaṃ siyā cittassa añ‍ñathattaṃ, yathā taṃ dūsī mārau labhaitha autāra’nti| atha khau tai, pāpima, brāhmaṇagahapatikā anvāviṭṭhā dūsinā māraina bhikkhū sīlavantai kalyāṇadhammai sak‍karaunti garuṃ karaunti mānainti pūjainti|

‘‘yai khau pana, pāpima, taina samayaina manussā kālaṅkaraunti yaibhuyyaina kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti|

511. ‘‘atha  khau, pāpima, kakusandhau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘anvāviṭṭhā khau, bhikkhavai, brāhmaṇagahapatikā dūsinā māraina – aitha, tumhai bhikkhū sīlavantai kalyāṇadhammai sak‍karautha garuṃ karautha mānaitha pūjaitha, appaiva nāma tumhaihi sak‍kariyamānānaṃ garukariyamānānaṃ māniyamānānaṃ pūjiyamānānaṃ siyā cittassa añ‍ñathattaṃ, yathā taṃ dūsī mārau labhaitha autāranti| aitha, tumhai, bhikkhavai, asubhānupassinau kāyai viharatha, āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñinau, sabbalaukai anabhiratisañ‍ñinau anabhiratasañ‍ñīnau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), sabbasaṅkhāraisu anic‍cānupassinau’ti|

‘‘atha khau tai, pāpima, bhikkhū kakusandhaina bhagavatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina aivaṃ auvadiyamānā aivaṃ anusāsiyamānā arañ‍ñagatāpi rukkhamūlagatāpi suñ‍ñāgāragatāpi asubhānupassinau kāyai vihariṃsu, āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñinau, sabbalaukai anabhiratisañ‍ñinau, sabbasaṅkhāraisu anic‍cānupassinau|

512. ‘‘atha khau, pāpima, kakusandhau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya āyasmatā vidhuraina pacchāsamaṇaina gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| atha khau, pāpima, dūsī mārau añ‍ñataraṃ kumārakaṃ  kumāraṃ (sī॰ pī॰) anvāvisitvā sakkharaṃ gahaitvā āyasmatau vidhurassa sīsai pahāramadāsi; sīsaṃ vaubhindi sīsaṃ tai bhindissāmīti (ka॰)| atha khau, pāpima, āyasmā vidhurau bhin‍naina sīsaina lauhitaina gaḷantaina kakusandhaṃyaiva bhagavantaṃ  arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhi| atha khau, pāpima, kakusandhau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau nāgāpalaukitaṃ apalaukaisi  – ‘na vāyaṃ dūsī mārau mattamañ‍ñāsī’ti| sahāpalaukanāya ca pana, pāpima, dūsī mārau tamhā ca ṭhānā cavi mahānirayañ‍ca upapaj‍ji|

‘‘tassa khau pana, pāpima, mahānirayassa tayau nāmadhaiyyā haunti – chaphassāyatanikau itipi, saṅkusamāhatau itipi, pac‍cattavaidaniyau itipi| atha khau maṃ, pāpima, nirayapālā upasaṅkamitvā aitadavaucuṃ – yadā khau tai yatau tai (ka॰), mārisa, saṅkunā saṅku hadayai samāgacchaiyya| atha naṃ tvaṃ jānaiyyāsi – ‘vassasahassaṃ mai nirayai pac‍camānassā’ti| sau khau ahaṃ, pāpima, bahūni vassāni bahūni vassasatāni bahūni vassasahassāni tasmiṃ mahānirayai apac‍ciṃ| dasavassasahassāni tassaiva mahānirayassa ussadai apac‍ciṃ vuṭṭhānimaṃ nāma vaidanaṃ vaidiyamānau| tassa mayhaṃ, pāpima, aivarūpau kāyau hauti, saiyyathāpi manussassa| aivarūpaṃ sīsaṃ hauti, saiyyathāpi macchassa|

513.

 ‘‘kīdisau nirayau āsi, yattha dūsī apac‍catha|

vidhuraṃ sāvakamāsaj‍ja, kakusandhañ‍ca brāhmaṇaṃ||

‘‘sataṃ āsi ayausaṅkū, sabbai pac‍cattavaidanā|

īdisau nirayau āsi, yattha dūsī apac‍catha|

vidhuraṃ sāvakamāsaj‍ja, kakusandhañ‍ca brāhmaṇaṃ||

‘‘yau aitamabhijānāti, bhikkhu buddhassa sāvakau|

tādisaṃ  bhikkhumāsaj‍ja, kaṇha dukkhaṃ nigacchasi||

‘‘majjhai sarassa tiṭṭhanti, vimānā kappaṭṭhāyinau|

vaiḷuriyavaṇṇā rucirā, ac‍cimantau pabhassarā|

accharā tattha nac‍canti, puthu nānattavaṇṇiyau||

‘‘yau  aitamabhijānāti, bhikkhu buddhassa sāvakau|

tādisaṃ bhikkhumāsaj‍ja, kaṇha dukkhaṃ nigacchasi||

‘‘yau vai buddhaina cauditau, bhikkhu saṅghassa paikkhatau|

migāramātupāsādaṃ, pādaṅguṭṭhaina kampayi||

‘‘yau aitamabhijānāti, bhikkhu buddhassa sāvakau|

tādisaṃ bhikkhumāsaj‍ja, kaṇha dukkhaṃ nigacchasi||

‘‘yau vaijayantaṃ pāsādaṃ, pādaṅguṭṭhaina kampayi|

iddhibalainupatthaddhau, saṃvaijaisi ca daivatā||

‘‘yau aitamabhijānāti, bhikkhu buddhassa sāvakau|

tādisaṃ bhikkhumāsaj‍ja, kaṇha dukkhaṃ nigacchasi||

‘‘yau vaijayantapāsādai, sak‍kaṃ sau paripucchati|

api vāsava jānāsi, taṇhākkhayavimuttiyau|

tassa sak‍kau viyākāsi, pañhaṃ puṭṭhau yathātathaṃ||

‘‘yau aitamabhijānāti, bhikkhu buddhassa sāvakau|

tādisaṃ bhikkhumāsaj‍ja, kaṇha dukkhaṃ nigacchasi||

‘‘yau brahmaṃ paripucchati, sudhammāyābhitau sabhaṃ|

aj‍jāpi  tyāvusau diṭṭhi, yā tai diṭṭhi purai ahu|

passasi vītivattantaṃ, brahmalaukai pabhassaraṃ||

‘‘tassa  brahmā viyākāsi, anupubbaṃ yathātathaṃ|

na mai mārisa sā diṭṭhi, yā mai diṭṭhi purai ahu||

‘‘passāmi vītivattantaṃ, brahmalaukai pabhassaraṃ|

sauhaṃ aj‍ja kathaṃ vaj‍jaṃ, ahaṃ nic‍caumhi sassatau||

‘‘yau aitamabhijānāti, bhikkhu buddhassa sāvakau|

tādisaṃ bhikkhumāsaj‍ja, kaṇha dukkhaṃ nigacchasi||

‘‘yau mahāmairunau kūṭaṃ, vimaukkhaina aphassayi|

vanaṃ pubbavidaihānaṃ, yai ca bhūmisayā narā||

‘‘yau aitamabhijānāti, bhikkhu buddhassa sāvakau|

tādisaṃ bhikkhumāsaj‍ja, kaṇha dukkhaṃ nigacchasi||

‘‘na  vai aggi caitayati vaiṭhayati (sī॰), ‘ahaṃ bālaṃ ḍahāmī’ti|

bālau ca jalitaṃ aggiṃ, āsaj‍ja naṃ sa ḍayhati||

‘‘aivamaiva tuvaṃ māra, āsaj‍ja naṃ tathāgataṃ|

sayaṃ ḍahissasi attānaṃ, bālau aggiṃva saṃphusaṃ||

‘‘apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavī mārau, āsaj‍ja naṃ tathāgataṃ|

kin‍nu mañ‍ñasi pāpima, na mai pāpaṃ vipac‍cati||

‘‘karautau cīyati pāpaṃ, cirarattāya antaka|

māra nibbinda buddhamhā, āsaṃ mākāsi bhikkhusu||

‘‘iti  māraṃ ataj‍jaisi, bhikkhu bhaisakaḷāvanai|

tatau sau dummanau yakkhau, tatthaivantaradhāyathā’’ti||

mārataj‍janīyasuttaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ dasamaṃ|

cūḷayamakavaggau niṭṭhitau pañ‍camau|

tassuddānaṃ  –

sālaiyya vairañ‍jaduvai ca tuṭṭhi, cūḷamahādhammasamādānañ‍ca|

vīmaṃsakā kausambi ca brāhmaṇau, dūsī ca mārau dasamau ca vaggau||

sālaiyyavaggau niṭṭhitau pañ‍camau|


idaṃ vaggānamuddānaṃ –

mūlapariyāyau caiva, sīhanādau ca uttamau|

kakacau caiva gausiṅgau, sālaiyyau ca imai pañ‍ca||

mūlapaṇṇāsakaṃ samattaṃ|
